## the_lonely_bot_category_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Athe_lonely_bot_category_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

有迹象表明，[Root.GetName]曾是某个古代文明的家园。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:4`

### English

[Root.GetName] exhibits signs of once having been home to an ancient civilization.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:5`

### English

## NANO_SWARM_CAT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ANANO_SWARM_CAT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]有显著的水体，同时其大陆部分是了无生机的荒漠。已授权进一步研究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:6`

### English

[Root.GetName] has significant bodies of water, and yet its landmasses are lifeless deserts. Further study is warranted.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:7`

### English

## STAR_RESONANCE_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASTAR_RESONANCE_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的科学专员发现，在[root.GetName]的日冕附近出现的疑似传感器故障在重启之后仍然留存在。已批准进一步研究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:8`

### English

Our scientists have discovered what first appeared to be a sensor malfunction near the corona of [root.GetName], however it's still there after rebooting. A closer look is warranted.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:9`

## ALPHA_CENTAURI_CAT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AALPHA_CENTAURI_CAT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]环绕红矮星的奇特轨道使得这颗行星处于潮汐锁定的状态，具有进一步调查的价值。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:10`

### English

[From.GetName]'s peculiar orbit around its red dwarf star renders the planet tidally locked, and potentially worthy of further investigation.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:11`

## FROZEN_SPRINGS_CAT

### 中文

神秘热源

### English

## FROZEN_SPRINGS_CAT_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AFROZEN_SPRINGS_CAT_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

科研团队在调查[From.GetName]的时候发现了一处来自行星的冰层之下的异常热信号。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:12`

### English

Whilst surveying [From.GetName], the science team detected unusual heat signatures coming from below the frozen surface of the planet.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:13`

## d_house_turtle_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_house_turtle_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一只由我们繁育的年轻的尼玛，现在已经长大到足以承载部分我们的产业了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:21`

### English

## d_turtle_miner_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_turtle_miner_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

小心地平衡药物和痛疼抑制器，我们就能在其上居住的同时钻入尼玛。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:23`

### English

The careful balance of drugs and pain inhibitors allows us to drill into the Nemmas while still residing on them.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:26`

## d_turtle_corpse_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_turtle_corpse_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

曾在此生活的硕大尼玛尸体漂浮于此，甚至从轨道中也清晰可见。这只两栖爬行动物现在仅仅只是一座漂浮着的岛屿。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:25`

### English

The floating remains of the gargantuan Nemma that once lived here can still be seen from orbit. The reptilian amphibian is now naught but a floating island.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:28`

## d_nano_corpses_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_nano_corpses_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

曾在帕尔乌斯上演化并覆于其上的庞大的纳米虫群所留下的极细粉末。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:27`

### English

A fine powder is all that remains of the massive nanite swarm that evolved on - and once covered - Parvus III.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:31`

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:36`

### English

## LONELY_BOT_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ALONELY_BOT_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]上的这座工厂已荒废颓败了数个世纪，仿佛随时都要垮塌得支离破碎。它在行星的殖民地建立之初曾扮演过重要的作用，我们的殖民家出面并要求我们进入这座工厂，并使其修复如新，恢复往日荣光。\n\n回报：£alloys£§G合金§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:49`

### English

The factory on [Root.GetName] has been decaying for centuries already, and is reaching a point where it might fall apart at any moment. As it has played an instrumental role in the foundation of the colony on the planet, our colonists have reached out and asked us to step in and make sure the building will be restored to former glory.\n\nReward: £alloys£ §GAlloys§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:54`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ALONELY_BOT_MISSING_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

在这座星球上生活的机器人最近消失了，其他的许多殖民者也是如此。我们的工程师正在试图搜寻它们最近的信号以溯源，来看看我们能不能找到这些殖民者和机器人。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:51`

### English

The robot inhabiting the planet has recently disappeared - as has a large number of our colonists. Our engineers are looking into tracking its remote signals back to the source, to see if we can find the colonists and the robot.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:57`

## breed_turtle_world_project

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abreed_turtle_world_project&lang=en)

### 中文

开始尼玛育种计划

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:52`

### English

Start a Nemma Breeding Program

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:59`

## breed_turtle_world_project_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abreed_turtle_world_project_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

通过对尼玛的初步研究，并结合我们的动物学知识，现在可以鼓励这一温顺驯服的物种在它们栖息地的承载范围内欣欣向荣地发展。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:53`

### English

By first studying the Nemmas and then applying our zoological expertise, we can encourage docile and obedient specimens to flourish within the carrying capacity of their habitat.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:60`

## enslave_turtle_world_project

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenslave_turtle_world_project&lang=en)

### 中文

开始奴役尼玛

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:54`

### English

Start Enslaving the Nemmas

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:61`

## enslave_turtle_world_project_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenslave_turtle_world_project_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

先进的药物和义体植入体可以将尼玛变得更加温顺和易于操控。这一以来它们就可以成为负载能力强大的巨兽。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:55`

### English

Advanced drugs and cybernetic implants will render the Nemmas more docile and impressionable. In time they shall become gigantic beasts of burden.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:62`

## mine_turtle_world_project

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amine_turtle_world_project&lang=en)

### 中文

开始尼玛矿业

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:56`

### English

Start the Nemma Mining Operation

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:63`

## mine_turtle_world_project_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amine_turtle_world_project_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

在小心地研究了尼玛的生物学构成后，我们的[Root.owner.GetScientistPlural]可以有效地才这些巨星爬行动物的外壳中收获有用的矿物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:57`

### English

After carefully studying the Nemmas' biology, our [Root.owner.GetScientistPlural] can successfully harvest useful minerals from the shells of these gigantic reptiles.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:64`

## kill_turtle_world_project

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Akill_turtle_world_project&lang=en)

宰杀尼玛

### English

Slaughter the Nemmas

## kill_turtle_world_project_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Akill_turtle_world_project_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

我们将不择手段从这个星球上将尼玛的威胁清除干净。

### English

We will expunge the Nemma menace from this planet by any means necessary.

研究尼玛

### English

Study the Nemma

## study_turtle_world_project_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astudy_turtle_world_project_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

应该让尼玛处于在我们最好的[Owner.GetScientistPlural]的保护之下，然后试图寻找解决方案。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:61`

### English

We shall put the fate of the Nemma in the care of our best [Owner.GetScientistPlural] and see what solutions can be found.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:68`

## STUDY_NANITE_SWARM

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASTUDY_NANITE_SWARM&lang=en)

### 中文

研究纳米虫群

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:62`

### English

Study Nanite Swarm

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:70`

## STUDY_NANITE_SWARM_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASTUDY_NANITE_SWARM_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

[nanite_swarm_planet.GetName]上的纳米虫群既是一种科学奇迹，又是一个危险。溯其起源及它们与这颗星球独特生态系统的联系对我们的[Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural]来说是一个使其兴奋的挑战，当然也可潜在地促进我们对机械智能的理解。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:63`

### English

The nanite swarm on [nanite_swarm_planet.GetName] is both a technological marvel and a danger. Charting the origin of the nanites as well as their relation to the planet's unique ecology provides an exciting challenge for our [Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural] while also potentially advancing our understanding of machine intelligence.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:71`

## DESTROY_SWARM

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ADESTROY_SWARM&lang=en)

### 中文

摧毁虫群

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:64`

### English

Destroy the Swarm

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:72`

## DESTROY_SWARM_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ADESTROY_SWARM_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

下令对虫群进行轨道轰炸。如果有任何值得发现的秘密，我们可以从这些害虫的尸体里寻找。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:65`

### English

Order an orbital bombardment of the swarm. If there are any secrets worth discovering, we will harvest them from the shells of these gnats.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:73`

## TAME_THE_SWARM

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATAME_THE_SWARM&lang=en)

### 中文

驯服虫群

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:66`

### English

Tame the Swarm

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:75`

## TAME_THE_SWARM_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATAME_THE_SWARM_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

我们对人工智能的进阶科技可以用于重编程并驯服这些纳米机器人。他们可以在这个行星上建造建筑时贡献巨大的力量，而他们与这颗星球的生态系统之间的关联，则可能可以利用来创造我们的第二母星。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:67`

### English

Our advanced knowledge of Artificial Intelligence can be used to reprogram and tame the nanites. They could be a great asset in building structures on the planet - and with their link to the world's ecosystem, it should be possible to instruct the nanites to create a second homeworld.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:76`

## TAME_THE_SWARM_DESC_ARTIFICIAL

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATAME_THE_SWARM_DESC_ARTIFICIAL&lang=en)

### 中文

我们对人工智能的进阶科技可以用于重编程并驯服这些纳米机器人。他们可以在这个行星上建造建筑时贡献巨大的力量，而他们与这颗星球的生态系统之间的关联，则可能可以利用来创造一个天堂。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:68`

### English

Our advanced knowledge of Artificial Intelligence can be used to reprogram and tame the nanites. They could be a great asset in building structures on the planet - and with their link to the world's ecosystem, it should be possible to instruct the nanites to create a paradise.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:77`

## ENLIGHTEN_RADIO

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AENLIGHTEN_RADIO&lang=en)

### 中文

启蒙辐合植物

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:69`

### English

Enlighten the Radiotrophic

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:79`

## ENLIGHTEN_RADIO_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AENLIGHTEN_RADIO_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

这些可以在极高辐射强度的星球下存活并繁衍兴盛的植物可以作为我们帝国绝佳的补充。不过在此之前，我们需要提升他们的心智并在数月内教会它们我们数千年来的进展。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:70`

### English

These plants that can live and thrive on worlds with more radiation than atmosphere could be an excellent addition to our empire. But before then, we will need to enhance their minds and teach them thousands of years of progress in a few months.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:80`

## GAS_RESTORATION

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AGAS_RESTORATION&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]恢复计划

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:71`

### English

[From.GetName] Restoration Project

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:82`

## GAS_RESTORATION_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AGAS_RESTORATION_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

为错误赎罪是一项强权者的特权，在此情此景下，我们必须深切地赎罪。[From.GetName]上的灾难令人震愕，而我们唯一的愿望是我们的科学家们能够反转我们深层探测器的极性，寄希望于能以此重新点燃这颗气态巨行星内部的生命之火。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:72`

### English

Atoning for your mistakes is a privilege for those with power, and in this scenario, we must atone dearly. The disaster on [From.GetName] was horrifying, and our only hope is that our scientists can reverse the polarity of our deep scanners and potentially reignite the spark of life inside the gas giant.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:83`

## RESCUE_STORM

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ARESCUE_STORM&lang=en)

### 中文

解救[Root.GetName]

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:73`

### English

Free the [Root.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:85`

## RESCUE_STORM_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ARESCUE_STORM_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]被[FromFrom.GetName]上的风暴围困。他们唯一的希望在于我们发射一艘新飞船过去，然后远距离重调制他们的护盾频率。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:74`

### English

The [Root.GetName] is trapped in a storm on [FromFrom.GetName]. Their only hope may rest in our sending a new ship there, and remotely modulating their shield frequencies.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:86`

## STUDY_SLEEPING_CRYSTAL

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASTUDY_SLEEPING_CRYSTAL&lang=en)

### 中文

研究奇怪的小行星

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:75`

### English

Study the Strange Asteroid

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:88`

## STUDY_SLEEPING_CRYSTAL_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASTUDY_SLEEPING_CRYSTAL_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]是一颗奇怪的小行星，它似乎可以通过未知的方式旋转并躲开我们的开采站。我们需要委派一艘科研船去研究它。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:76`

### English

[From.GetName] is a strange asteroid that seems to be able to rotate and avoid our mining station through unknown means. We should dispatch a science ship to discover how.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:89`

## CRACK_THE_EGG

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ACRACK_THE_EGG&lang=en)

### 中文

破壳

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:77`

### English

Crack the Egg

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:91`

## CRACK_THE_EGG_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ACRACK_THE_EGG_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

在[Solar_System.GetName]发现的这颗巨型的蛋刚刚开始产生裂缝。这是一次难以置信的好机会，能够近距离研究这种巨大生命体的繁育循环。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:78`

### English

The great egg we have discovered in [Solar_System.GetName] is just begging to be dissected. It's an incredible opportunity to get to study such a gargantuan creature's reproductive cycle this close.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:92`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adecision_crystalline_refugees&lang=en)

$NAME_Central_Crystal_Species$被从他们的家园$NAME_Central_Crystal_Country$放逐了，为他们寻找一个新家。

Find a home for the $NAME_Central_Crystal_Species$ refugees, who have been banished from their homeland in the $NAME_Central_Crystal_Country$.

晶态建设

Crystalline Construction

## decision_crystalline_architecture_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adecision_crystalline_architecture_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一支由$NAME_Central_Crystal_Country$派出的工程师将能改进栖息地的布局。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:84`

### English

A team of engineers sent by the $NAME_Central_Crystal_Country$ will improve the layout of the habitat.

## decision_expand_dayside

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adecision_expand_dayside&lang=en)

### 中文

扩建昼半球基础设施

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:85`

### English

Expand Dayside Infrastructure

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:101`

## decision_expand_dayside_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adecision_expand_dayside_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

得益于[Root.GetName]的独特情况，我们可以在其昼半球设立无数的太阳能电池板，极大地增强我们的能量产出。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:86`

### English

By taking advantage of [Root.GetName]'s unique situation, we can set up solar panels all over the dayside of this world, expanding our energy production immensely.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:102`

## decision_introduce_chaos

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adecision_introduce_chaos&lang=en)

### 中文

引入混沌

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:87`

### English

Introduce Chaos

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:104`

## decision_introduce_chaos_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adecision_introduce_chaos_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这颗星球对我们而言简直完美过头了——我们的殖民者在街道上精神崩溃。我们唯一有效的选择是引入一些不完美成分。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:88`

### English

This world is too perfect for us - our colonists are having mental breakdowns in the streets. Our only real option is to introduce some imperfections.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:105`

## anomaly.2080.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.2080.name&lang=en)

### 中文

神秘机器人

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:90`

### English

The Enigmatic Robot

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:109`

## anomaly.2580.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.2580.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的科学家在调查[From.GetName]上的早先文明的过程中，发现了一个神秘的小机器人试图隐藏自己。在对这个小东西经过数周的接近和示好之后，它终于将我们的科学家们带回到一个自它的创造者离去后起就由它照看的还在运作中的工厂。\n\n被带到这座建筑之后，我们的人获赠了一大批可以带回飞船上的资源。尽管稀奇古怪难以沟通，但这个小机器人似乎把我们的科学家当成了自己的朋友。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:91`

### English

As our scientists were looking into the earlier civilization on [From.GetName], they came across a small enigmatic robot trying to hide from them. Following weeks of approaching and befriending the little thing, it finally brought them back to a still-functional factory that it has been supervising since the demise of its creators.\n\nAfter being guided to the building, our people were given a large quantity of resources they could bring back to their ship. Though weird and hard to communicate with, the little robot seems to think of our scientists as its friends.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:110`

## anomaly.6700.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6700.name&lang=en)

### 中文

死里逃生

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:93`

### English

Narrow Escape

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:114`

## anomaly.6700.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6700.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]的船员在§H[nanite_swarm_planet.GetName]§!上的荒芜沙丘上探索时，第一个汇报并不出乎意料：这是一片不自然的废土，在水面以上没有任何的生命迹象。然而，这座行星的水生生命已经远远进化过了我们预期可以涉足陆地的水准。\n\n此外，船员们还发现了一些被黄沙覆盖、外观老旧的似乎是建筑一样的东西。就在他们刚开始向这些表面上的建筑内挖掘时，一小群纳米虫群突触出现，并开始袭击研究人员。似乎这座建筑不是由沙子，而是由无数的微小纳米机器构成的！\n\n启动以后这些虫群很快开始自我复制、唤醒越来越多的休眠群体。[Root.GetLeaderName]下令从行星上紧急撤离，[Root.GetName]才能在最后一刻从这个纳米机器人风暴的穷追不舍中逃离大气层。其他的虫群也被激活了，而且没有停息的迹象。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:94`

### English

As the [Root.GetName]s' crew explored the barren dunes of §H[nanite_swarm_planet.GetName]§!, the first reports were much as expected: this is an unnatural wasteland with no signs of life above the water's surface. Yet the planet's aquatic life has evolved well past the point we would expect some species to make forays onto dry land.\n\nAdditionally, the crew discovered what originally appeared to be various ancient-looking, sand-covered structures. Shortly after they began digging into these apparent buildings, small swarms of nanites emerged and began harassing the researchers. It seems the buildings are formed not from sand, but by billions of microscopic nanomachines!\n\nOnce mobilized, these swarms quickly multiplied, awakening more and more of the dormant whole. [Root.GetLeaderName] ordered a swift evacuation of the planet, allowing the [Root.GetName] just enough time to escape the atmosphere with a swarming storm of nanites in hot pursuit. The remaining swarm now seems agitated, and it's not calming down.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:115`

## anomaly.6700.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6700.a&lang=en)

### 中文

查查到底发生了什么事。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:95`

### English

Figure out what happened here.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:116`

## anomaly.6705.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6705.name&lang=en)

### 中文

纳米机器人的起源

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:96`

### English

On the Origin of Nanites

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:118`

## anomaly.6705.desc.start

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6705.desc.start&lang=en)

### 中文

[root.GetName]已经完成了对§H[nanite_swarm_planet.GetName]§!原生纳米机器人的研究，结果令人难以置信。数千年前，一艘来自先进文明的舰船在此坠毁。那艘船上有几十种不同设计的机器人。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:97`

### English

The [root.GetName] has completed its study of the nanites native to §H[nanite_swarm_planet.GetName]§!, and the results are incredible. Thousands of years ago, a ship hailing from an advanced civilization crashed here. Aboard that vessel were robots of dozens different designs.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:119`

## anomaly.6705.desc.L

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6705.desc.L&lang=en)

### 中文

令人好奇的是，这些机器人似乎是神秘的“建门者”的工具，比所有我们已知的L-星门都更古老。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:98`

### English

Curiously, it seems these machines might have been a tool of the mysterious Gate Builders, though older than any L-Gate we have found.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:120`

## anomaly.6705.desc.noL

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6705.desc.noL&lang=en)

### 中文

不管是谁，建造这些机器的人确实掌握了更先进的科技，而且远远地早于我们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:99`

### English

Whoever built these machines was in possession of truly advanced technology, long before our time.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:121`

## anomaly.6705.desc.end

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6705.desc.end&lang=en)

### 中文

\n这些机器人自主行动，并尝试按他们的程序保护行星。失去了创造者的规制，一场战斗很快就在这些机器之中爆发了。\n\n这场冲突中胜出的是一群具有超适应性、可自我复制的纳米系统，它们迅速演化适应了行星的生态系统。这些机器将自己变成了行星表面的主宰，将一切当地种群和其他的机器对手全部化为碎屑。\n\n如今我们所见即是这场大战的余波：一个并非了无生机的星球，而是承载着它最顶尖的猎食者。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:100`

### English

\nThe robots operated autonomously and attempted to secure the planet according to their programming. Without their creators to rein them in, a battle soon broke out among the machines.\n\nThe emerging victor of this conflict was a hyper-adaptive, self-replicating nanite system that rapidly evolved to fit the planet's biosphere. This machine made itself the master of the surface world, breaking down anything from local flora to its former, robotic rivals.\n\nWe bear witness to the aftermath of this great war: a planet not devoid of life, but instead supporting its evolved apex predator.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:122`

## anomaly.6705.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6705.a&lang=en)

### 中文

驯服虫群

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:101`

### English

Tame the Swarm.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:123`

## anomaly.6705.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6705.b&lang=en)

### 中文

进一步研究虫群。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:102`

### English

Study the Swarm further.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:124`

## anomaly.6705.b.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6705.b.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

指挥我们的[Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural]解开[nanite_swarm_planet.GetName]上演化出的纳米机器人的秘密。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:103`

### English

Direct our [Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural] to unlock the secrets of the evolved nanites on [nanite_swarm_planet.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:125`

## anomaly.6705.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6705.c&lang=en)

### 中文

摧毁虫群。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:104`

### English

Destroy the Swarm.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:126`

## anomaly.6710.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6710.name&lang=en)

### 中文

人造免疫系统

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:105`

### English

Artificial Immune System

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:128`

## anomaly.6710.desc.start

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6710.desc.start&lang=en)

### 中文

§H[nanite_swarm_planet.GetName]§! 的居民令人印象深刻。我们发送到大气层的任何探测器都会虫群迅速分析并拆散，此后不久，虫群的其余部分也学会了如何最好地摧毁我们的无人机——即使是在行星的远端。虫群对我们的技术的理解显着增加。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:106`

### English

The denizens of §H[nanite_swarm_planet.GetName]§! keep impressing. Any probe we send down into the atmosphere is quickly analyzed and taken apart by the swarm, and shortly thereafter the rest of its hive has learned how to best consume our drones as well - even on the far side of the planet. The swarm demonstrates a measurable increase in its understanding of our technology.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:129`

## anomaly.6710.desc.normal

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6710.desc.normal&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的一些[Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural]认为，这种行为证明了该虫群是一个智慧的存在，或是某种生物，甚至可能被认为是活着的。\n\n是时候做出决定了：我们是放生它们，还是摧毁它们，还是试图为了我们自己的利益而占据这些存在呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:107`

### English

Some of our [Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural] argue that this behavior is proof that the swarm is a sapient being - or beings - and might even be considered to be alive.\n\nIt is time to make a decision: do we let them be, destroy them, or else attempt to appropriate these beings for our own gain?

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:130`

## anomaly.6710.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6710.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

这些是正在发育的蜂群行为，如果任其发展，纳米虫群可能会实现智慧。我们是让它保持这种状态，还是在它成为威胁之前将其摧毁？我们能否以某种方式利用这个实体来谋取利益？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:108`

### English

These are the behaviors of a budding hivemind, and if left to its own devices, the nanite swarm may achieve sapience. Do we leave it in this state, or do we destroy it before it become a threat? Could we somehow appropriate this entity for our gain?

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:131`

## anomaly.6710.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6710.a&lang=en)

### 中文

驯服虫群。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:109`

### English

Tame the Swarm.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:133`

## anomaly.6710.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6710.b&lang=en)

### 中文

摧毁虫群。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:110`

### English

Destroy the Swarm.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:134`

## anomaly.6710.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6710.c&lang=en)

### 中文

这些不仅仅是自动机器人，而是活物！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:111`

### English

These are not mere automatons, but living beings!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:135`

## anomaly.6715.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6715.name&lang=en)

### 中文

虫群沃土

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:112`

### English

Fertilization of Nanites

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:137`

## anomaly.6715.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6715.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管§H[nanite_swarm_planet.GetName]§!的虫群在成为集体时所向披靡，但其个体却相当脆弱。从该行星的大气中抽取出一小部分，并使用我们的先进AI科技，我们成功地重写了这些机器人的通讯系统。当我们将我们重编程过的机器人放回虫群后，这些修改在一瞬间席卷全球。\n\n获得网络的控制后，我们第一要务是为§H[nanite_swarm_planet.GetName]§!带回稳定的大气，并且要对[Root.Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural]而言最为舒适的那种。这颗行星现在已经充分准备好等待我们的到来，而纳米虫群则会对我们殖民者的需求无所不应。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:113`

### English

While the nanite swarm of §H[nanite_swarm_planet.GetName]§! was impressive as a collective, as individuals they were quite vulnerable. Extracting a few from the planet's atmosphere and using our advanced AI technology, we have managed to reprogram the communication systems of the machines. Then when we reintroduced our reprogrammed nanites into the swarm, the modifications spread across the globe in a flash.\n\nWith control of the network secured, our first order was to reintroduce a stable biosphere onto §H[nanite_swarm_planet.GetName]§! - one which is optimized for [Root.Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural]. The planet is now ready and waiting for us, and the nanite swarm is prepared to help with any task our colonists may give it.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:138`

## anomaly.6715.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6715.a&lang=en)

### 中文

殖民船！起飞！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:114`

### English

Start up the colony ships' engines!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:139`

## anomaly.6720.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6720.name&lang=en)

### 中文

轨道轰炸胜过雄辩

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:115`

### English

No One Outsmarts Orbital Bombardment

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:141`

## anomaly.6720.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6720.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在[root.fleet.GetName]完成对§H[nanite_swarm_planet.GetName]§!的轨道轰炸后，尘埃逐渐落定。尽管混合使用了强劲的爆炸物和试验性EMP，轰炸的过程仍然持续了很长，不过最终，这里再也没有任何机械生命活动的迹象了。\n\n我们未来的殖民者可以获取这些害虫的尸体，并有望能从中收获一些有用的东西。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:116`

### English

The dust is settling after the [root.fleet.GetName] finished orbital bombardment of §H[nanite_swarm_planet.GetName]§!. Despite using a mix of powerful explosives and experimental EMPs, the process took a long time - but at last, there is no longer any trace of machine life down there.\n\nOur future colonists will have access to these gnats' remains and will, hopefully, manage to harvest something valuable from them.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:142`

## anomaly.6725.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6725.name&lang=en)

### 中文

准备不足

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:117`

### English

Underprepared

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:144`

## anomaly.6725.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6725.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的预防措施和特种设备仍不充分。我们在§H[nanite_swarm_planet.GetName]§!建立殖民地之后很快就跟地面的团队失去了联络。从落脚点收到的最后一条通讯显示，天空被微小的淹没了前哨的黑色物体遮蔽。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:118`

### English

Our precautions and special equipment were not enough. Shortly after establishing a colony on §H[nanite_swarm_planet.GetName]§!, we lost contact with our teams on the ground. The last communication we received from the settlement showed a sky darkened by small black bodies swarming the outpost.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:145`

## anomaly.6725.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6725.a&lang=en)

### 中文

该死的纳米机器人！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:119`

### English

Damn those nanites!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:146`

## parvus_III_chain_title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparvus_III_chain_title&lang=en)

### 中文

帕尔乌斯III的居民

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:120`

### English

The Inhabitants of Parvus III

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:148`

## parvus_III_chain_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparvus_III_chain_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

帕尔乌斯III没有有机生命，但它仍然充满了活动。这些生物是什么，它们能够被合理解释吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:121`

### English

Parvus III is devoid of organic life, yet it still swarms with activity. What are these creatures and can they be reasoned with?

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:149`

## anomaly.6740.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6740.name&lang=en)

### 中文

科学之色

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:123`

### English

The Color of Science

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:153`

## anomaly.6740.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6740.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

造成§Y[From.GetName]§!日冕发现的恒星辐射共振的原因证明是一种可以耐受高温的异常晶体阵列。\n\n在把这些晶体带到船上后，我们的[Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural]震惊地发现这些怪异的共振是可以复现的。首先，他们将这些晶体暴露在与[From.GetName]相同的恒星辐射中，产生了与最初记录的现象完全一致的结果。它也似乎制造了我们发送给它的所有能量波的共振，形成了[Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural]没有观测过的新的色彩和声音！\n\n这种频率可以改变我们对宇宙的认知方式。换句话说，我们可以用一个晶态结构开发研究设备，使其能承受恒星级的超高热量。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:124`

### English

The cause of the solar radiation resonance observed around the corona of §Y[From.GetName]§! turned out to be an unusual crystal formation capable of enduring the heat!\n\nHaving brought the crystal aboard, our [Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural] were startled to realize that the strange resonance was reproducible. First, they exposed the crystal to the same solar radiation from [From.GetName], producing an identical result to the initially recorded phenomenon. It also seemed to create resonances with all energy waves we sent through it, forming new colors and sounds never before observed by [Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural]!\n\nSuch frequencies could change the way we view the universe. Alternatively, we can develop research applications for a crystalline structure capable of surviving the intense heat of a star.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:154`

## anomaly.6740.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6740.a&lang=en)

### 中文

将新的频率发回给我们的艺术家们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:125`

### English

Send the new frequencies back to our artists.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:155`

## anomaly.6740.a.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6740.a.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

获取这些新的频率以备进一步分析。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:126`

### English

Retrieve these new frequencies for further analysis.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:156`

## anomaly.6740.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6740.b&lang=en)

### 中文

采集这些晶体用在我们能量武器的隔热上。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:127`

### English

Harvest the crystal to insulate our energy weapons.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:157`

## anomaly.6745.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6745.name&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:129`

### English

[From.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:161`

## anomaly.6745.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6745.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

自[Root.Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural]开始仰望星空以来，[From.GetName]就一直是一个纯真的梦幻，一个充满了我们的期待的地方，一个新的家园。可悲的是，随着我们的望远镜的发展，我们意识到这颗行星可能是一个潮汐锁定、万物冰封的地狱——这一假说已由我们今天的发现而彻底证实。\n\n然而希望仍然存在，这个世界存在着作为一个宜居行星而言所必需的一切因素。我们或许只需要轻轻推上一把……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:130`

### English

For as long as we [Root.Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural] have been able to look to the stars for new homes, [From.GetName] has been a naive dream: a place we would look to with optimism; a new home. Sadly as our telescopes got better, we realized that the planet would probably be a tidally locked, frozen hellscape - a theory which we have confirmed today.\n\nThere is hope, however; this world has all the building blocks necessary for a habitable planet. It might just need a slight push...

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:162`

## anomaly.6745.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6745.a&lang=en)

### 中文

谢谢你给了我们灵感，[From.GetName]。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:131`

### English

Thank you for inspiring us, [From.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:163`

## anomaly.6745.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6745.b&lang=en)

### 中文

在我们的荣耀榜上的又一颗外行星。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:132`

### English

Another exoplanet added to our growing list.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:164`

## anomaly.6750.name

[From.GetName]

[From.GetName]

## anomaly.6750.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6750.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这颗潮汐锁定的行星似乎具有各种宜居行星的建筑模块。也许在上面能建造一些东西。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:134`

### English

This tidally locked planet appears to have all the building blocks for a habitable world. Perhaps there is something that can be built here.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:166`

## anomaly.6800.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6800.name&lang=en)

### 中文

打靶训练

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:136`

### English

Target Practice

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:170`

## anomaly.6800.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6800.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的传感器发现了[From.GetName]上的舰船残骸是通过某种罕见的方式抵达那里的。这些舰船似乎是从大约数光年以外故意地径直开向小行星，用以测试设计者推进器的精确度。在对残骸进行深度扫描时，我们找到了那么一艘推进装置相当完好无损的舰船。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:137`

### English

Our sensors have discovered that the ship remnants on [From.GetName] got there in a peculiar way. The ships seem to have been intentionally driven straight into the asteroid, probably from light-years away, to test the accuracy of the designers' thrusters. As we deep-scan the remains, we pick out one ship in particular, which seems to have reasonably intact propulsion.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:171`

## anomaly.6800.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6800.a&lang=en)

### 中文

取回那个推进器！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:138`

### English

Recover that thruster!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:172`

## anomaly.6800.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6800.b&lang=en)

### 中文

我们不会浪费那些合金的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:139`

### English

We won't waste those alloys.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:173`

## anomaly.6805.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6805.name&lang=en)

### 中文

小行星敏感中继器

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:141`

### English

Asteroid Sensor Relay

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:177`

## anomaly.6805.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6805.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的[Owner.GetScientistPlural]期望在[From.GetName]内部发现稀有资源，结果偶然撞见了一样极其不寻常的东西。这个看起来像是普通小行星的物体事实上是一个长期休眠的中继站，里面有复杂的电子元件和先进的传感器，用以向某个古老的帝国发送信号。这些撞击也损毁了小行星的一部分内部构造，我们只能从中提取到一个恒星系。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:142`

### English

Hoping to discover rare resources inside [From.GetName], our [Owner.GetScientistPlural] stumbled upon something most unusual. What had appeared to be a simple asteroid was in fact a long-dormant relay station with complex electronics and advanced sensors set up to send signals back to an ancient empire. The impact also has damaged some of the asteroid's internal structure, and we can only extract one system from it.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:178`

## anomaly.6805.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6805.a&lang=en)

### 中文

取回传感器。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:143`

### English

Retrieve the Sensors.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:179`

## anomaly.6805.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6805.b&lang=en)

### 中文

破译他们的加密软件。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:144`

### English

Extrapolate their encryption software.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:180`

## anomaly.6810.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6810.name&lang=en)

### 中文

感染

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:145`

### English

Infestation

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:182`

## anomaly.6810.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6810.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们错了。这座围绕[From.GetName]的空间站并非无人居住；相反，它上面爬满了巨大的外星节肢动物！我们部署进空间站的传感器在信号中断前只回传了一些行动迅捷的阴影和巨大颚骨的画面。在对空间站进行深度扫描后，我们发现了一处可能是无比复杂的数据库的地方。为了抵达这座数据库，我们需要穿过这些一团团的、有好多腿儿的守护者。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:146`

### English

We were wrong. The station orbiting [From.GetName] is not uninhabited; instead, it is crawling with giant alien arthropoids! The probes we deployed into the station only returned skittering shadows and large mandibles before signal was lost. Deep scans of the station have also revealed what we postulate to be an incredibly complex databank. To reach this databank, we would have to go through that cluster of many-legged defenders.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:183`

## anomaly.6810.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6810.a&lang=en)

### 中文

送客，巨大的外星节肢动物可不是闹着玩的！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:147`

### English

Send an away party, giant alien arthropoids are no joke!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:184`

## anomaly.6810.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6810.b&lang=en)

### 中文

也许我们最好还是不要打扰他们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:148`

### English

It's probably for the best that we leave them alone.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:185`

## anomaly.6810.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6810.c&lang=en)

### 中文

§B尝试与这些节肢动物小伙伴联络。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:149`

### English

§BAttempt to contact our fellow arthropoids.§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:186`

## anomaly.6810.d

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6810.d&lang=en)

§B把他们泵出去。§!

### English

§BVent them out.§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:187`

## anomaly.6810.d.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6810.d.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

用我们对居住站的先进知识让空间站的大气层裂一个口子。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:151`

### English

Use our advance knowledge of habitats to breach the station's atmosphere.

## anomaly.6810.e.evopred

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6810.e.evopred&lang=en)

### English

§BAquire their genomes!§!

## anomaly.6815.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6815.name&lang=en)

### 中文

巨大外星节肢动物

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:153`

### English

The Giant Alien Arthropoids

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:190`

## anomaly.6815.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6815.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

噢，你们好！很抱歉之前摧毁了你们的探测器。那只是因为我们在这做一些非常精密的实验，而我们担心一些外在的干扰会影响实验结果——我相信你能理解。那么，我们能帮上什么忙吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:154`

### English

Oh, hello there! Sorry about destroying your probe earlier. It's just that we are conducting some very delicate experiments here, and we were worried that outside interference could alter the results - I'm sure you understand. Now, how can we help you?

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:191`

## anomaly.6815.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6815.a&lang=en)

### 中文

慢着，所以你们并不是带有敌意的害虫？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:155`

### English

Wait, so you are not a hostile infestation?

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:192`

## anomaly.6820.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6820.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

究竟是什么原因会让你这么想啊？我们是一支策展人分遣队，派驻到这里研究这个恒星系独特的特征。如果你是想分享我们机构通常给予的那些福利，我建议你和我们的主研究实验室联系。我可以提供你一份目前我们在此地所发现的东西的拷贝。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:156`

### English

What would ever give you that impression? We are a Curator detachment sent here to study this system's unique properties. If you wish to partake of our order's usual offerings, I suggest contacting the primary research lab. I can provide you with a copy of our findings so far.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:193`

## anomaly.6820.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6820.a&lang=en)

### 中文

祝你的实验一切顺利。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:157`

### English

Good luck with your experiments.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:194`

## anomaly.6825.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6825.name&lang=en)

### 中文

生长的水晶

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:159`

### English

Crystal Growths

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:198`

## anomaly.6825.desc.start

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6825.desc.start&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]上生活的生命体形式和我们预想的完全不一样。我们的[owner.GetScientistPlural]汇报到，小行星内有不断生长的水晶。这些生命体利用[From.System.GetName]散发的辐射，并结合小行星内的矿物元素，被证实能够完成自给自足的繁衍生息。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:160`

### English

The lifeforms living on [From.GetName] were nothing like we expected. Our [owner.GetScientistPlural] report back that the asteroid has crystal growths. These growths have turned out to be self-sustaining and caused by the crystals taking in the ambient radiation of [From.System.GetName] and combining it with the mineral composition of the asteroid.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:199`

## anomaly.6825.desc.nofood

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6825.desc.nofood&lang=en)

### 中文

这些水晶主要是由蔗糖构成的，十分易碎。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:161`

### English

The crystals themselves are primarily made out of sucrose and are quite brittle.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:200`

## anomaly.6825.desc.food

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6825.desc.food&lang=en)

### 中文

这些水晶主要是由蔗糖构成的，想必具有潜在的食用价值。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:162`

### English

The crystals themselves are primarily made out of sucrose and could potentially even be edible.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:201`

## anomaly.6825.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6825.b&lang=en)

### 中文

啥，你说能吃？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:163`

### English

Edible you say?

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:202`

## anomaly.6835.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6835.name&lang=en)

### 中文

水晶的呼唤

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:164`

### English

Call for Crystals

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:203`

## anomaly.6835.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6835.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在食用了太空生长的水晶糖数年之后，很明显，我们的人民对这一美食的需求愈发旺盛。我们的[Root.GetScientistPlural]已经列了一份表，上面是能够建造水晶糖农场的潜在候选地。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:165`

### English

After eating space-based crystal sugars for a few years now, it has become apparent that our population would like to see it more readily available. Our [Root.GetScientistPlural] have come up with a list of potential candidates where we could construct more crystal farms.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:204`

## anomaly.6835.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6835.a&lang=en)

### 中文

寻找更多的小行星来拓建水晶糖农场。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:166`

### English

Search for more asteroids to expand the crystal farms.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:205`

## anomaly.6835.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6835.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

最多§Y3§!个随机小行星将会生产§G3§!£food£食物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:167`

### English

Up to §Y3§! random asteroids will now produce §G3§! £food£ food.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:206`

## anomaly.6835.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6835.b&lang=en)

### 中文

还是不要拓建水晶糖农场了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:168`

### English

Let's not expand the crystal farms.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:207`

## anomaly.6840.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6840.name&lang=en)

### 中文

羊羊太少

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:170`

### English

Too Few Sheep

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:211`

## anomaly.6840.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6840.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]是一颗独特的气态巨行星。我们对天体物理的知识告诉我们，这样的行星应该有一个行星环，然而它并没有。在我们的科研船近距离观察了那颗诡异的卫星之后，这一现象的原因已经十分显然了。这颗星球被一些“牧羊人”卫星所环绕，这些卫星全部拥有富得难以置信的矿物，并且每年还在积累更多。我们可以用精准控制的爆炸爆破这些卫星，然后将它们的矿物一次性收集起来；也可以让它们继续留在环带，持续地收集它们积累下来的太空残骸。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:171`

### English

[From.GetName] is a peculiar gas giant. Our knowledge of astrophysics tells us that the planet should have a planetary ring, yet it doesn't. The cause of this became apparent after our science ship took a closer look at the intriguing moon. The planet is surrounded by shepherd moons, all incredibly rich in minerals and accumulating more every year. We could blast the moons with precise and controlled explosions and harvest them or keep them inside the ring zone and harvest the space debris they accrue.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:212`

## anomaly.6840.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6840.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们现在就需要那些矿物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:172`

### English

We need those minerals now.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:213`

## anomaly.6840.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6840.b&lang=en)

心不急才能喝到热粥。

### English

Good things come to those who wait.

## anomaly.6845.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6845.name&lang=en)

### 中文

连锁灾难

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:175`

### English

Cascading Catastrophe

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:218`

## anomaly.6845.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6845.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们确实在[From.GetName]上发现了生命，但也是因为我们的行动，这一生命已经灭绝了。这些微生生物住在气态巨行星的外层边缘，对于我们的深度扫描表现出了极大的负面反应。扫描的频率同生物的细胞结构产生了共振——这一效应如同瀑布倾泻而下般飞速传开，从一个气态小虫子传播到另一只气态小虫。所有生物都在收到影响之后不久就不再活动了。\n\n我们现在正环绕着全宇宙最大的坟场。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:176`

### English

We did indeed discover life on [From.GetName], but by our actions, it has since been eradicated. The microbial creatures which lived in the outer edges of the gas giant's atmosphere reacted poorly to our deep scans. These frequencies caused each creature's cellular structure to resonate - an effect that then cascaded and spread from gas mite to gas mite. Each was rendered inert soon after.\n\nWe are now in orbit over the largest graveyard in the universe.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:219`

## anomaly.6845.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6845.a&lang=en)

### 中文

还有什么能做的吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:177`

### English

Can anything be done?

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:220`

## anomaly.6850.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6850.name&lang=en)

### 中文

反转深度扫描极性

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:178`

### English

Reversing Deep Scan Polarities

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:221`

## anomaly.6850.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6850.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

完成了！如同奇迹一般，当我们的[Owner.GetScientistPlural]对我们的飞船进行了合适的改造之后，它们能够重现最初的瀑布效应。这一次效果不一样，[FromFromFrom.GetName]上的生命体如同一波巨大的浪潮一样又重新涌动了起来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:179`

### English

It is done! Miraculously, once our [Owner.GetScientistPlural] installed the proper modifications on our vessel, they were able to recreate the initial cascade. This time the effect was different, and life started springing back up all over [FromFromFrom.GetName] in a great wave.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:222`

## anomaly.6850.desc.valuesothers

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6850.desc.valuesothers&lang=en)

### 中文

这一过程教会了我们许多关于宇宙的事情，但更多的可能是关于我们自己的事情。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:180`

### English

This process has taught us a lot about the universe but perhaps even more about ourselves.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:223`

## anomaly.6850.desc.novaluesothers

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6850.desc.novaluesothers&lang=en)

### 中文

在未来，当我们拿出扫描仪的时候，我们必须得更加小心谨慎。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:181`

### English

In the future we will have to be more careful where we point our scanners.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:224`

## anomaly.6850.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6850.a&lang=en)

### 中文

极好的消息！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:182`

### English

Fantastic news!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:225`

## anomaly.6855.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6855.name&lang=en)

### 中文

肉眼捕捉

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:184`

### English

Caught in the Eye

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:229`

## anomaly.6855.desc.regular

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6855.desc.regular&lang=en)

### 中文

我们从[Root.GetName]收到的最后一束信号无异于混乱的静电音。他们一直在研究[From.GetName]上奇怪的区域风暴，并且一直发回令人难以置信的前期报告。这股风暴会对发送过去的探测器做出反应，制造出真空口袋来避开它们，再缓慢地往回渗透进探测器。在我们失去联络之前，[Root.GetName]正计划在风暴处测试许多不同的扫描频率。科研船看上去已经沉没到了气态巨行星深处，现在已经陷入了沉寂。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:185`

### English

The last signal received from [Root.GetName] was not much more than garbled static. They had been studying the strange localized storms on [From.GetName] and were sending back incredible preliminary reports. The tempest reacted to the probes being sent in, creating vacuum pockets to avoid them and then slowly seeping back in towards the drones. [Root.GetName] was planning on testing out numerous scanning frequencies at the storm before we lost contact. The science ship seems to have sunken into the gas giant and appears to be dormant.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:230`

## anomaly.6855.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6855.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

来自[Root.GetName]的信号突然从主脑意识中被切断了。[Owner.GetScientistPlural]看上去并没有死亡，只是无法与我们交流。们一直在研究[From.GetName]上奇怪的区域风暴，并且一直发回令人难以置信的前期报告。这股风暴会对发送过去的探测器做出反应，制造出真空口袋来避开它们，再缓慢地往回渗透进探测器。在联络被切断之前，[Root.GetName]的计划是在风暴处测试许多不同的扫描频率。科研船看上去已经沉没到了气态巨行星深处，现在已经陷入了沉寂。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:186`

### English

The signals from [Root.GetName] were suddenly cut off from the consciousness. The [Owner.GetScientistPlural] do not appear to be dead, just unable to communicate with us. They had been studying the strange localized storms on [From.GetName] and were sending back incredible preliminary reports. The tempest reacted to the probes being sent in, creating vacuum pockets to avoid them and then slowly seeping back in towards the drones. The plan for [Root.GetName] was to test out numerous scanning frequencies at the storm before contact was cut off. The science ship seems to have sunk into the gas giant and now appears dormant.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:231`

## anomaly.6855.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6855.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们需要营救[Root.GetName]！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:187`

### English

We need to rescue [Root.GetName]!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:232`

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:188`

### English

Wait, [From.GetName] is even MORE interesting now.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:233`

## anomaly.6855.b.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6855.b.tooltip&lang=en)

放弃[Root.GetName]，研究反应。

### English

Abandon [Root.GetName] and study the reactions.

## anomaly.6860.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6860.name&lang=en)

### 中文

涨落的护盾频率

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:190`

### English

Fluctuating Shield Frequencies

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:235`

## anomaly.6860.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6860.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在几个月的工作之后，我们的[Owner.GetScientistPlural]终于设法调整[FromFrom.GetName]护盾的工作方式，使得[FromFromFrom.GetName]风暴放开了对落难舰船的钳制。同[trapped_in_storm.GetName]的通讯很快重新建立了起来，十分难以置信的是，[FromFrom.GetName]上没有组员记得他们被风暴困住的这段时间。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:191`

### English

After months of work, our [Owner.GetScientistPlural] finally managed to alternate [FromFrom.GetName] shields in such a way that [FromFromFrom.GetName] tempest released its grip on the marooned ship. Communications with [trapped_in_storm.GetName] was quickly re-established, and incredibly none onboard [FromFrom.GetName] seem to remember their time trapped inside the storm.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:236`

## anomaly.6860.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6860.a&lang=en)

### 中文

回去工作。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:192`

### English

Back to work.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:237`

## anomaly.6865.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6865.name&lang=en)

### 中文

快邮

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:194`

### English

Express Mail

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:241`

## anomaly.6865.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6865.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

当[Root.GetName]靠的足够近得以扫描货舱的时候，似乎所有的传感器都功能失常了。这个舱室使用的[Owner.GetBioAlloy]同我们自己建造太空结构时使用的是完全一样的。这种某些外星生命体发明出了同我们完全一致的复合材料的可能性，根本不可能成立，但在校准了扫描仪之后，我们依然得到了同样的结果。\n\n在打开舱室之后，迎接我们的唯一一样东西是一份文档集，里面详细地阐述了我们应当如何指派我们的每一位[Owner.GetScientistPlural]，以及他们今后的研究计划。该指引在一个特定日期结束，也就是从现在算起的整整10年。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:195`

### English

Once [Root.GetName] got close enough to scan the cargo pod it was assumed that the sensors were malfunctioning. The pod is built out of the exact same [Owner.GetBioAlloy] we ourselves use for spacebound structures. The likelihood that some alien beings would come up with this exact mixture is beyond unlikely, yet after recalibrating the scanners the same results appeared.\n\nUpon opening the pod, the only thing greeting us was a collection of documents detailing exactly where we should assign each of our [Owner.GetScientistPlural] and what they should be asked to study. The instructions end on a fixed date, one that is scheduled to fall exactly 10 years from now.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:242`

## anomaly.6865.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6865.a&lang=en)

### 中文

将这些笔记发回[Owner.Capital.GetName]。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:196`

### English

Send the notes back to [Owner.Capital.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:243`

## anomaly.6865.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6865.b&lang=en)

### 中文

这有点瘆人了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:197`

### English

This has rattled us.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:244`

## anomaly.6870.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6870.name&lang=en)

### 中文

关闭循环

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:198`

### English

Closing the Loop

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:245`

## anomaly.6870.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6870.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在经过几年不顺坦的发展之后，[Root.Capital.GetName]的上方出现了一道时空织构裂口。令人心惊胆颤的是，这一天的日期和我们十年前收到的怪异指示上面所说的日期是同一天。我们现在只有一件需要做的事情：把收到的那些笔记发射到裂口里面，关闭这个循环。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:199`

### English

After years of uneasy development, a rip in the fabric of space has appeared above [Root.Capital.GetName]. Unnervingly this is the exact date as indicated in the strange instructions we received a decade ago. There is only one thing to do: send the documents we received back into the tear and close the loop.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:246`

## anomaly.6870.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6870.a&lang=en)

### 中文

让过去的我们自己收拾这个烂摊子吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:200`

### English

Let our past selves deal with this.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:247`

## anomaly.6875.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6875.name&lang=en)

### 中文

便利的采矿

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:202`

### English

Convenient Mining

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:251`

## anomaly.6875.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6875.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]附近小行星的轨道是如此完美无暇地稳定，也不难理解为什么我们的[Owner.GetScientistPlural]会认为它是被人工放置在这个地方了。然而，那里也没有任何推进系统或者其他人工航行的迹象。唯一的自然演化结论是，由于某种几乎荒谬到不可能成真的几率，这颗小行星被[From.GetName]的重力场捕获了。我们在这颗小行星的表面还探测到了几种十分稀有的水晶。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:203`

### English

The asteroid around [From.GetName] has such a perfectly stable orbit that it's easy to understand why our [Owner.GetScientistPlural] thought it was placed there artificially. Yet, there are no conclusive indications of any propulsion systems or any other artificial marring. The only natural conclusion is that, by some absurdly improbable chance, the asteroid got caught in [From.GetName]'s gravity field. We have also detected some rare crystals on the surface of the asteroid.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:252`

## anomaly.6875.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6875.a&lang=en)

### 中文

真是贴心啊。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:204`

### English

How thoughtful.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:253`

## anomaly.6880.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6880.name&lang=en)

### 中文

无法解释的旋转

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:205`

### English

Unexplainable Rotations

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:254`

## anomaly.6880.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6880.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

正当我们的采矿站在这颗被捕获的小行星上完工几天之后，这枚岩石突然开始旋转起来，在这个过程中摧毁了采矿站，但同时又有更多的稀有水晶矿藏暴露在了我们的传感器面前！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:206`

### English

Just a day after finishing our mining station on the trapped asteroid, the rock abruptly rotated, destroying the mining station. In the process of doing this, even more rare crystals deposits were exposed to our sensors!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:255`

## anomaly.6880.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6880.a&lang=en)

### 中文

再建造一遍！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:207`

### English

Build it again!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:256`

## anomaly.6880.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6880.b&lang=en)

### 中文

为什么它要那么做？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:208`

### English

Why did it do that?

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:257`

## anomaly.6885.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6885.name&lang=en)

### 中文

发怒的晶体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:209`

### English

Annoyed Crystal

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:258`

## anomaly.6885.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6885.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

警报的笛声响彻整个[Solar_System.GetName]，大量的晶化野兽从小行星庇护所里面冲了出来，正是我们在钻孔并打算提取晶体的那颗小行星。它看上去§R生气了§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:210`

### English

Warning sirens blared across [Solar_System.GetName] as the massive crystalline beast left the shelter of the asteroid we had been drilling into and extracting crystals from. It seems §Rangry§!.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:259`

## anomaly.6890.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6890.name&lang=en)

### 中文

沉睡的晶巢

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:211`

### English

Sleeping Crystal Nest

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:260`

## anomaly.6890.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6890.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个被捕捉的小行星，实际上，是某种晶态生命体的巢穴。很庆幸我们没有继续挖掘，因为看上去我们似乎激怒了在里面休眠的生物。如果我们什么都不做，只是从远处研究它，这些生物应该会平静下来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:212`

### English

The trapped asteroid is, in fact, a crystalline being's nest. It is good that we didn't keep digging, for it seems like we agitated the creature hibernating inside. If we do nothing for a while and study it from afar, it should settle down.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:261`

## anomaly.6890.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6890.a&lang=en)

## anomaly.6895.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6895.name&lang=en)

### 中文

有毒的生物工业

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:215`

### English

Toxic Biological Industry

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:266`

## anomaly.6895.desc.catano

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6895.desc.catano&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[From.GetName]的有毒大气层内观测到的生命信号，是体型庞大的半有机机械体！这个外星工业体似乎正是星球上乌烟瘴气的罪魁祸首，而且它依旧在运转。这个星球上没有其他的生命信号，这些机械体的造物主肯定抛下它们自己跑掉了。我们目前还不能确定它们的意图，但是它们建造的建筑想必能够帮助我们更好地理解工程学。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:216`

### English

The life signs observed within the toxic atmosphere of [From.GetName] are giant semi-organic machines! This alien industry seems to have been the cause of the noxious atmosphere on the planet, yet it's still operational. No other life signs remain on the world, and the creators of these machines must have left them running. We can't quite ascertain their purpose yet, but their construction could surely help our understanding of engineering.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:267`

## anomaly.6895.desc.catayes

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6895.desc.catayes&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[From.GetName]的有毒大气层内观测到的生命信号，是体型庞大的半有机机械体！正如我们一样，看上去这个设施的创造者也很青睐生物和机械的融合之美。这个外星工业体似乎正是星球上乌烟瘴气的罪魁祸首，而且它依旧在运转。这个星球上没有其他的生命信号，这些机械体的造物主肯定抛下它们自己跑掉了。我们目前还不能确定它们的意图，但是它们建造的建筑想必能够帮助我们更好地理解工程学。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:217`

### English

The life signs observed within the toxic atmosphere of [From.GetName] are giant semi-organic machines! Like us, it seems like the creators of this facility appreciated a merging of the biological and mechanical. This alien industry appears to have been the cause of the noxious atmosphere on the planet, yet it's still operational. No other life signs remain on the world, and the creators of these machines must have left them running. We can't quite ascertain their purpose yet, but their construction could surely help our understanding of engineering.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:268`
