## anomaly.6900.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6900.name&lang=en)

### 中文

冷酷的思绪

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:222`

### English

Cool Thoughts

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:275`

## anomaly.6900.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6900.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们并不是在地表之下检测到了生命形式，事实上，是整个星球都检测到了生命！这颗冰封的星球内部布满了微细的管道，相互连接，就像一台原始的计算机。尽管结构很粗糙，它通天的规模意味着，其算力甚至可以匹敌[Owner.Capital.GetName]的某些超级计算机。即使是现在，这台电脑还在活动——我们的[Owner.GetScientistPlural]可以同其互动，但是由于它运转十分缓慢，我们只能询问其一个简单的问题。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:223`

### English

We didn't detect life forms below the surface. We detected them in the entire world! The frozen planet is interspersed with micro-thin tubes linked like a rudimentary computer. While the structure is primitive, its gigantic scale means it can probably rival some of [Owner.Capital.GetName]'s supercomputers in output. Even now, the computer seems to be active - our [Owner.GetScientistPlural] can interface with it, but since it's so slow, we would only be able to ask it a single question.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:276`

## anomaly.6905.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6905.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的[Owner.GetScientistPlural]有了更加惊怖的发现。他们早前扫描到的山脉，实际上是某个巨型蛋的卵壳膜。我们也不知道究竟是什么巨兽诞下了这么一枚蛋，更不知道何等的怖兽会从中孵化出来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:233`

### English

Our [Owner.GetScientistPlural] have discovered something rather monstrous. The mountain range they scanned earlier was in actuality the outer membrane of a gigantic egg. It's uncertain what behemoth could lay such an egg and what horror would hatch from it.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:288`

### English

## anomaly.6910.desc.start

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6910.desc.start&lang=en)

### 中文

[FromFromFrom.GetName]的内部有什么东西超出我们所料。我们并没有发现什么巨兽生命的幼体，取而代之的只是一团基因浆。整个行星的内部填满了基因物质和资源。全部这些物质最终会变成什么还不明晰，但是如果不打破砂锅问到底，我们的星系可能会更加安全。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:238`

### English

The insides of [FromFromFrom.GetName] were not what we expected. Instead of some infant titanic lifeform, we've simply uncovered genetic slop. The entire planet is filled with genetic material and resources. What all this material would have become is unclear, but our galaxy is probably safer, not knowing.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:293`

## anomaly.6910.desc.hatchling

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6910.desc.hatchling&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管这个蛋同我们文明之前遭遇到的虚空孶孽很相似，但是它孕育了更加伟大的存在。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:239`

### English

While similar to the voidspawn our civilization encountered before, this egg would have created something much grander.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:294`

## anomaly.6910.b.evopred

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6910.b.evopred&lang=en)

### 中文

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:240`

### English

Feast on the yolk!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:295`

## anomaly.6915.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6915.name&lang=en)

### 中文

感恩

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:241`

### English

Gratitude

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:296`

## anomaly.6915.desc.regular

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6915.desc.regular&lang=en)

### 中文

§R“它活了！它又活过来了！同如此至伟的造物相比，我们不过是微不足道的凡尘；面对如此伟大之物，繁星帝国之间的鸡毛琐事又可称几——”§!\n\n[Root.GetName]最后的传输信号在此中断，当我们发送探测器去扫描[FromFromFrom.GetName]残骸的时候，我们只看到了一个空壳。看上去这个生物对自己的解放者并没有多少感激之情。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:242`

### English

§R"IT LIVES! IT LIVES AGAIN! We are nothing but insignificant specks compared to such a creature; what does the ephemeral squabbling between empires matter in the face of such great-"§!\n\nThe last transmission of the [Root.GetName] cuts off there, and when we use our probes to scan the remains of [FromFromFrom.GetName], we find a hollow shell. It seems the creature had little gratitude to show its liberators.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:297`

## anomaly.6915.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6915.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

我们派遣去往[Solar_System.GetName]星系的[Owner.GetScientistPlural]全部拉响了警报，但是没过多久就系数同[Owner.GetBrainName]失去了联系，只告诉了我们§R“它松动了。”§!当蛋壳开始裂开的时候，[Root.GetName]最后的传输信号被切断；当我们发射探测器去扫描[FromFromFrom.GetName]的残骸的时候，我们只看到了一个空壳。看上去这个生物对自己的解放者并没有多少感激之情。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:243`

### English

Our [Owner.GetScientistPlural] dispatched to the [Solar_System.GetName] system all reacted in alarm shortly before losing contact with the [Owner.GetBrainName], only telling us that §R"It's loose."§! The last transmission of the [Root.GetName] cuts off as the egg begins to crack open, and when we use our probes to scan the remains of [FromFromFrom.GetName], we find a hollow shell. It seems the creature had little gratitude for its liberator.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:298`

## anomaly.6915.b.evopred

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6915.b.evopred&lang=en)

### 中文

收集残余物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:244`

### English

Gather what is left.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:299`

## anomaly.3140.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3140.name&lang=en)

### 中文

地下热泉

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:246`

### English

Heat from Below

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:303`

## anomaly.3140.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3140.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在探察冰面下方之后我们得出结论，这处位于[From.GetName]的异常热信号是由热液泉引起的。类似的地方这个星球上似乎还有更多，但是我们尚未发现。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:247`

### English

After probing beneath the surface, we have concluded the unusual heat signatures on [From.GetName] are caused by hydrothermal springs. There seems to be more to this planet than meets the eye...

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:304`

## anomaly.3140.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3140.a&lang=en)

### 中文

真是棒极了。但我们没必要继续在这上面耗费精力了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:248`

### English

Remarkable. But we need not waste any more resources on this.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:305`

## anomaly.3141.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3141.name&lang=en)

### 中文

外星生命的深度

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:249`

### English

The Depths of Alien Life

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:306`

## anomaly.3141.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3141.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]的船员的进一步探索揭示出热水来源的行星地壳深处竟然密布着微观生命体！我们一度以为空无一物的冰封星球事实上孕育着一个欣欣向荣的水下生物圈。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:250`

### English

Continued exploration by the crew of [From.GetName] has revealed the hot water coming from deep within the planet's crust is teeming with microscopic life! What we once thought of as an empty, frozen planet, actually contains a thriving underwater biosphere!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:307`

## colony.5000.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.5000.name&lang=en)

### 中文

意外之友

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:251`

### English

An Unexpected Friend

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:309`

## colony.5000.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.5000.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]上的神秘机器人对我们的殖民者从一落足于行星上起就非常欢迎。我们的许多定居者一开始不信任这个小机器人，但它对我们建立殖民地的过程难以置信地有帮助，并且在其中扮演了重要角色。\n\n在头几年的殖民过程中，这个小东西在[Root.GetName]上的第一座城市诞生了衍生形象，并成为了整颗行星的吉祥物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:252`

### English

The enigmatic creature on [Root.GetName] has been very welcoming to our colonists after they settled down on the planet. At first many of our settlers were distrustful of the small robot, but it has shown itself to be incredibly helpful and has played a key role in establishing the colony.\n\nFollowing the first years of the colony, the small thing has become something of a pet figure in the first cities on [Root.GetName], and a mascot for the planet as a whole.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:310`

## colony.5000.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.5000.a&lang=en)

### 中文

这可爱的小机器人帮助了我们真多。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:253`

### English

How nice of the little creature to help us out.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:311`

## colony.5005.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.5005.name&lang=en)

### 中文

古代工厂

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:254`

### English

The Ancient Factory

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:313`

## colony.5005.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.5005.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这座由[Root.GetName]上的小机器人监管的工厂在殖民地建立的最初几年起到了重要作用。现在这些定居者们终于得以有时间了解他们的小吉祥物以及理解它不寻常的行为，他们了解到它看护了数个世纪的这座老旧的建筑已经残破不堪，并需要比它所能提供的更多的帮助。\n\n尽管它的远古工程和机械对我们而言仍很陌生，但我们的工程师认为他们应该可以修好，只是需要一点时间。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:255`

### English

The factory supervised by the little robot on [Root.GetName] has been instrumental for the colony in its early years. Now that the settlers have had time to get to know their mascot and understand its uncommon mannerisms, they have learnt that the old building it has taken care of for centuries is falling apart and needs more help than the creature can give it on its own.\n\nThough its ancient engineering and machinery are still foreign to us, our engineers think they should be able to fix it, given time.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:314`

## colony.5005.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.5005.a&lang=en)

### 中文

那就让我们看看能不能帮上它。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:256`

### English

Let us see if we can help it out then.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:315`

## colony.5006.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.5006.name&lang=en)

### 中文

工厂崩塌

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:257`

### English

Factory Collapse

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:317`

## colony.5006.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.5006.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在数年的缓慢剥蚀中，[Root.GetName]上的这座巨大工厂终于彻底瓦解。在清理完这座老建筑的废墟后，我们的殖民者发现了一个巨大的金属堆隐藏在原来建筑物中可能是地下室的位置。在它的正中央静静地躺着那个过去快乐地欢迎着我们来到这颗星球的机器人，它在这座它负责监管的工厂的倒塌过程中被压毁了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:258`

### English

After years of slow decline, the great factory on [Root.GetName] has finally fallen apart. After clearing out all the debris from the old building, our colonists came across large piles of metal hidden away in what would have been the basement when it was still standing. In the middle of it all lies the robot that once so happily welcomed our settlers to the planet, crushed by the factory it was responsible for supervising.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:318`

## colony.5006.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.5006.a&lang=en)

### 中文

吉祥物没了……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:259`

### English

A mascot no more, then.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:319`

## colony.5010.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.5010.name&lang=en)

### 中文

工厂复工

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:260`

### English

The Factory Restored

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:321`

## colony.5010.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.5010.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

据报告，伴随着所有设备的突然停转，我们的工程师确信他们未能在[Root.GetName]上修复这座古老的工厂。由于未能找到失败的原因，他们开始放下工具离开大楼。就在这时引擎突然轰鸣起来。\n\n不仅发动机重新工作了，而且我们的工程师似乎也意外地修复了工厂的其余部分——尽管还不清楚这是如何发生的。他们的报告最后还提到了一个小机器人，它看到工厂重新开始运作后跳起了奇怪的舞蹈。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:261`

### English

Our engineers were certain they had failed at fixing the ancient factory on [Root.GetName], according to their report, when everything suddenly stopped. Unsure how they had managed to fail they began putting aside their tools and exiting the building, when suddenly the engine roared to life.\n\nNot only is the engine working again, but our engineers seem to have accidentally managed to fix other parts of the factory as well - though it is unclear how that happened. Their report ends with a small description of the weird dance the little robot did when it saw its factory working again.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:322`

## colony.5010.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.5010.a&lang=en)

### 中文

结果好才是真的好。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:262`

### English

All's well that ends well.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:323`

## colony.5010.tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.5010.tt&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y$d_hab_lonely_bot_deposit$§!殖民地特征现在产出§G1§!£volatile_motes£§Y易爆微粒§!和§G1§!£exotic_gases£§Y异星天然气§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:263`

### English

The §Y$d_hab_lonely_bot_deposit$§! Colony Feature now also produces §G1§! £volatile_motes£ §YVolatile Mote§! and §G1§! £exotic_gases£ §YExotic Gas§!.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:324`

## colony.5015.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.5015.name&lang=en)

### 中文

失踪的机器人

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:264`

### English

A Missing Robot

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:326`

## colony.5015.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.5015.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们收到了[Root.GetName]上的人员的报告，似乎他们行星上的神秘机器人又带来了麻烦。最近它失踪了，它失踪的同时，恰好一些殖民者被报道从该行星上的一个城市消失。\n\n虽然这两件事最初被认为是无关的，但人们已经开始将他们朋友和家人的失踪归咎于这个机器人。我们的工程师正在扫描该机器人所在的工厂自事故发生以来收到的几个信号，看看是否这些信号能发回这个机器人。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:265`

### English

We have received a report from the people of [Root.GetName], as it seems once again they are having trouble with the enigmatic robot on their planet. Recently it has gone missing, and its disappearance coincides with the reports of several colonists vanishing from one of the cities on the planet.\n\nThough they were originally assumed to be unrelated, people have started blaming the creature for their missing friends and family. Our engineers are looking into scanning several signals that the robot's factory has received since the incident, to see if it leads back to the creature itself.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:327`

## colony.5015.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.5015.a&lang=en)

### 中文

希望我们能解决这个问题，越快越好。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:266`

### English

Let us hope we can solve this as quickly as possible.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:328`

## colony.5020.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.5020.name&lang=en)

### 中文

遗骸

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:267`

### English

The Remains

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:330`

## colony.5020.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.5020.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在花费数周尝试追踪[Root.GetName]上的机器人的信号后，我们的工程师终于发现了一座深入地下的远古竖井。在地面下的深处，他们看到了一座远古的建筑，多数的失踪人口都在锁在其中，同时还有小机器人的残留物。\n\n在唤醒了这些殖民者之后，我们发现他们都是某个教团的成员，意外地落入了这座废墟，当小机器人来试图解救他们的时候一切都开始崩塌。我们的工程师和这些人自己都不清楚到底是怎么陷入这个地方的，也不知道是什么东西停止了建筑的崩塌。\n\n我们的工程师对深入研究那个已经损坏的神秘机器很感兴趣，想知道能从中获得什么信息。而另一方面，[Root.GetName]的人们则期望我们可以将这个小东西修复，变回原来的样子。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:268`

### English

After spending weeks trying to track down the signals from the robot on [Root.GetName], our engineers have finally found an ancient shaft leading deep into the ground. Far below ground they came across an ancient structure with most of the missing people locked in some form of stasis, as well as the remains of the little robot.\n\nAfter waking the colonists, we learn that all of them are part of some cult that managed to accidentally make their way into the ruins, and that everything was about to collapse on them when the little robot came to their rescue. It seems unclear to both our engineers and the people themselves exactly how they found themselves in a stasis, or what the creature did to stop the collapse of the building.\n\nOur engineers are interested in looking into the enigmatic machine now that it is no longer functional, to see what we can learn from it. The people of [Root.GetName], on the other hand, are pleading with us to see if we can restore the little creature back to its former self.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:331`

## colony.5020.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.5020.a&lang=en)

那就把大家的小吉祥物变回来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:269`

### English

Let the people have their little mascot back, then.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:332`

## colony.5020.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.5020.b&lang=en)

让工程师把它拆了看看里面有什么秘密。

### English

Have the engineers break it apart and see what mysteries it holds.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:334`

## ancrel.11000.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.11000.name&lang=en)

### 中文

第一层

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:272`

### English

The First Layer

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:336`

## ancrel.11000.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.11000.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这座废墟看起来至少有成百上千年那么陈旧了。起初它看起来只是一个大型露天建筑，不过随着对外部结构发掘的深入，我们很快发现这只是一座城市中被废弃的贫民窟。有一座巨大的大门阻挡了我们深入城市的去路，我们的科学家猜想这个社会分为了数个阶层，而建筑的每一层分别居住着这座城市一个阶层的人。\n\n我们的科学家很快就在这座地下废墟破碎的混凝土地面下发现了外星人的遗骸。然而在找到第一具遗骸过后，我们很快发现贫民窟里到处都是遗骸，有的横尸街头，有的死在家中，有的从天花板上高高地悬挂下来。虽然不知道是谁干的，但真的是一副相当令人反胃的场景。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:273`

### English

The ruins seem to be centuries, if not millennia, old. At first it seems to be only a large open-air dome, but as we are excavating the outermost parts we quickly find ourselves in what appears to be the derelict slums of a city. As a large gate blocks our progress deeper into the city, our scientists speculate that it is split into a hierarchy, each layer holding one class of the people of the city.\n\nIt does not take long before one of our scientists finds alien remains in subterranean ruins under the broken concrete floor. After the first corpse is found, however, we start finding remains all over the slums, be it in the streets, in what would have been their homes, or hanging from the most remote parts of the ceiling. It is unclear what killed them, but it is a grisly sight.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:337`

## ancrel.11000.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.11000.a&lang=en)

### 中文

那么其他层里又为我们隐藏着什么呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:274`

### English

What do the other layers have in store for us?

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:338`

## ancrel.11005.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.11005.name&lang=en)

### 中文

第二层

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:275`

### English

The Second Layer

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:340`

## ancrel.11005.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.11005.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

第一个圈子似乎是由流浪汉和贫民们摇摇欲坠的窝棚组成，而第二个圈子则与[Root.GetAdj]市政规划观念有很多一致之处。\n\n我们发现了几个我们的科学家认为曾经是集市的开放广场，但因为年深日久，所有东西都早已腐朽到无法辨认它可能是什么的地步。\n\n之后不久，我们也开始在圆顶的这一部分发现值得注意的遗迹。此处居民尸骸遍地，其死亡方式似乎与他们那些穷邻居差不多。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:276`

### English

Whereas the first circle seemingly consisted of the ramshackle dwellings of the outcast and destitute, this second circle has more in common with the [Root.GetAdj] notion of proper urban planning.\n\nWe find several open squares our scientists assume used to be marketplaces, though anything from back then has long since decayed to the point where it is impossible to make out what it might have been.\n\nIt does not take long before we start finding unusual remains in this part of the dome too. They seem to have perished in much the same way as their poorer neighbors, and we find the dead strewn all over.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:341`

## ancrel.11005.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.11005.a&lang=en)

### 中文

又是惨烈的一幕。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:277`

### English

Yet another brutal sight.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:342`

## ancrel.11010.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.11010.name&lang=en)

### 中文

第三层

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:278`

### English

The Third Layer

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:344`

## ancrel.11010.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.11010.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

当进入第三层的大门最终打开后，我们的科学家们——带着对下一步的发现的好奇——鱼贯而入，而这里很快被确认为是该穹顶古建筑中的一个更富有的区域。我们可就此对从这座城市可能获得的发现做出更积极的期望。\n\n进入这一层后不久，我们中的一个人突然转身，野蛮地袭击了一位同事。当该袭击者被制服时，受害者已经死在地上。此后，那位发疯的科学家一直胡言乱语，我们完全无法理解他们身上到底发生了什么。\n\n这一层比前两层都小，很可能是我们到达圆顶中心之前的最后一层。只剩下一扇门了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:279`

### English

When the gate into the third layer finally opens, our scientists - curious about what they will find next - flood into what is soon identified as a yet wealthier region of the old dome. At this point we can make confident assumptions about what to expect from this city.\n\nShortly after we enter the layer, one of our people suddenly turns around and savagely attacks a colleague. The victim is already dead on the ground by the time the unexpected assailant is restrained. In the aftermath we are completely unable to get anything sensible out of the crazed scientist, and get no better understanding of what happened to them.\n\nThis layer is significantly smaller than either of the previous two, and it is most likely the final one before we reach the center of the dome. Only one gate remains.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:345`

## ancrel.11010.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.11010.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的科学家随时准备探索最后一层。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:280`

### English

Our scientists stand ready to check the final layer.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:346`

## ancrel.11015.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.11015.name&lang=en)

### 中文

最后一层

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:281`

### English

The Final Layer

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:348`

## ancrel.11015.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.11015.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

当最后一扇门开启时，强烈的灵能波冲击着我们的科学家。他们中的大多数在剧烈的痛苦中崩溃了，最终只有少数人进入了这一层。\n\n这一层似乎是一座大寺庙，中间有一个巨大的祭台或祭坛。墙上到处都是描绘不同恒星系的巨大图表，一个重复的图表似乎围绕着[Root.System.GetName]本身。\n\n幸存为数不多的的几位科学家向中央靠拢，他们发现这实际上是一台打造成古老祭坛形式的雄伟机器。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:282`

### English

As soon as the final gate opens up, waves of intense psionic energy wash upon our scientists. Most of them collapse in apparent agony, leaving only a few to make their way into the actual layer itself.\n\nIt seems to be a large temple, with a massive dais or altar in the middle of it. All across the walls there are huge diagrams of different star systems, and a repeated one seems to center on [Root.System.GetName] itself.\n\nMaking their way closer to the middle, the few scientists that remain find that it is in fact an imposing machine fashioned in the form of an old altar.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:349`

## ancrel.11015.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.11015.a&lang=en)

### 中文

让我们修一下这台机器，看看能发现什么。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:283`

### English

Let us tinker with this machine and see what we can find.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:350`

## ancrel.11020.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.11020.name&lang=en)

### 中文

长者

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:284`

### English

The Elder One

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:352`

## ancrel.11020.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.11020.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

当修理工作开始时，他们突然被一种共同的幻觉所困扰——一个正被挖出并解封的存在，尖啸着发出命令。尖啸越发刺耳，一个生物撕开[From.System.GetName]星系的景象在他们的脑海中反复上演。几位科学家不省人事，无法应对这种持续折磨他们身体疼痛。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:285`

### English

As they start tinkering with the machine, they are suddenly beset by a shared auditory hallucination - a screaming command by something to be unearthed, unshackled. Visions of a creature ripping apart the [From.System.GetName] system plays repeatedly in their heads as the creature's demands grow shriller. Several scientists fall unconscious, unable to deal with the unabating torrents of pain that rack their bodies.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:353`

## ancrel.11020.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.11020.a&lang=en)

拆了这台机器，以免造成更多的伤害！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:286`

### English

Rip the machine apart before it can cause any more harm!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:354`

## ancrel.11025.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.11025.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

终于……\n\n凡人，跪下！在我面前卑躬屈膝，我将赐予你佑护和智慧。\n\n若敢不从，我将为你带来终结！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:289`

### English

FINALLY...\n\nBOW DOWN MORTALS, GROVEL BEFORE ME, AND I SHALL GRANT YOU PROTECTION AND WISDOM.\n\nOPPOSE ME, AND I SHALL BE YOUR END!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:359`

### English

O Great One! Embrace your wayward flock!

在[eldritch_location.GetName]损失舰队

### English

Lost Fleet in [eldritch_location.GetName]

## ancrel.11035.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.11035.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们与我们穿越[eldritch_location.GetName]星系的舰队失去了联系。随后，我们又收到了其中恒星坍缩和一切毁灭的消息。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:299`

### English

We have lost all contact with our fleet as it was traveling through the [eldritch_location.GetName] system. Shortly afterwards, we receive news about the collapse of the star and the destruction of everything around it.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:372`

一场悲剧。

### English

## central.5.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acentral.5.name&lang=en)

### 中文

来自星系中心的信号

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:301`

### English

Signal from the Center of the Galaxy

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:375`

## central.5.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acentral.5.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一个强大的信号从通常被认为是没有生命的星系中心发出，传向星系。没过多久，我们又收到了来自同一地点的信号传输。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:302`

### English

A powerful signal is bounced through the galaxy from the center, a place often thought to be empty of life. Shortly afterwards, we received a transmission from the very same location.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:376`

## central.5.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acentral.5.a&lang=en)

### 中文

把它们接上来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:303`

### English

Put them through.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:377`

## central.10.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acentral.10.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

你好，星系的年轻人。\n\n我们这些来自天堂中心的人再次离开了我们的庇护所，去看看你们这些住在外围的人必须为我们提供什么。希望你不会像在你之前的人那样试图给国家带来恐怖。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:304`

### English

Hail, you who are still young and new to the galaxy.\n\nWe who come from the paradise of the center have once more left our sanctuary to see what you who live on the outer circles have to offer. Hopefully, you will not try to bring terror upon the empire like those who came before you.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:379`

## central.10.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acentral.10.a&lang=en)

### 中文

你好，呃……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:305`

### English

Hello the-

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:380`

### English

Did they just cut the feed?

### English

$NAME_Central_Crystal_Country$

## central.20.1.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acentral.20.1.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

你们还有什么想和我们讨论的吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:309`

### English

What else did you want to discuss with us?

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:386`

## central.20.2.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acentral.20.2.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

你现在站在晶态帝国外环舰队指挥官、内陆之守护者的面前。有什么是你想与我们讨论的吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:310`

### English

You stand in front of the Commander of the Outer Circle Armada of the Crystalline Empire, Protector of the Inner Lands. What is it you wish to discuss with us?

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:387`

## central.20.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acentral.20.a&lang=en)

### 中文

能不能告诉我更多关于你们家园的事？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:311`

### English

Could you tell us more about your homeland?

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:388`

## central.20.a.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acentral.20.a.response&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的家园如泡沫幻影，如果我们告诉你太多，将会给它带来末日。你们这些年轻的种族在被允许进入那座圣所之前还有很多需要学习的。\n\n如果你们证明了自己的价值，有朝一日我们会将向你们揭示一切的真理。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:312`

### English

Our homeland is like a dream, and if we were to tell you much more about it, that would be the end of it. You younger races still have much to learn before you can be allowed into the sanctum.\n\nOne day, if you prove worthy, we will be able to show you the truth of everything.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:389`

## central.20.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acentral.20.b&lang=en)

### 中文

你们为何降临？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:313`

### English

Why have you arrived now?

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:390`

## central.20.b.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acentral.20.b.response&lang=en)

### 中文

我们长期以来观察着星系中的其他种族，并且我们在现在的邻居中看到了潜能。当我们收到信息，这些年轻种族中有一个就是我们在等待的那个，我们立刻就准备好了找到他们。\n\n我们还不确定你们这些年轻种族中到底谁是我们正在寻找的，但是我们相信你们都有能力向我们证明自己。希望你们不要辜负我们的期望，而我们可以找到我们都能够接受的条件。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:314`

### English

We have long watched over the other races in the galaxy, and we see potential in our current neighbors. When the messenger arrived with news that one of the younger races was the one we had waited for, we immediately prepared to track them down.\n\nWe are not certain which one of you younger races is the one we are looking for, yet, but we believe all of you can prove useful for us. Hopefully you will be able to live up to the expectations we have for you, and we can find terms that both of us will be happy with.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:391`

## central.20.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acentral.20.c&lang=en)

### 中文

有什么你们在找的，或者需要我们帮忙的吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:315`

### English

Is there anything you are looking for, or need our help with?

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:392`

## central.20.c.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acentral.20.c.response&lang=en)

### 中文

没有，时机还没成熟。我们晚些时候再回来，希望那时我们向你能分享更多消息。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:316`

### English

No, the time for that is not now. Come back later, and hopefully we will be able to share more news with you.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:393`

## central.20.d

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acentral.20.d&lang=en)

就这样。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:317`

### English

That is all.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:394`

## central.21.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acentral.21.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

再见，恐怖分子，我们和你们没有什么好谈的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:318`

### English

Leave, terrorist, we have nothing more to say to you.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:396`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acentral.21.a&lang=en)

## central.50.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acentral.50.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在我们社会中一些不守规矩的份子倡议在我们整个家园松绑一些规制，在首都造成了不小的骚乱。伟大的存在正计划把这些份子转移到外部世界，这样它们就不能对我们的边界和我们人民的和谐再构成任何威胁。\n\n如果你们能接纳这些被流放者将会对我们有很大帮助。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:320`

### English

Several unruly elements in our society are advocating for easing the restrictions on entering our homeland, causing quite a ruckus in the capital. The Great One is looking to move these elements into foreign lands, so they will no longer pose a threat to our borders or the harmony of our people.\n\nIf you would offer refuge for the displaced, that would be incredibly helpful to us.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:399`

## central.50.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acentral.50.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们会试着为他们找到容身之所的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:321`

### English

We will try to find some place for them to stay.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:400`

## central.50.a.tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acentral.50.a.tt&lang=en)

### 中文

在加来的§Y10年§!内，我们可以在一个§Y居住站§!上发起§Y$decision_crystalline_refugees$§!决议。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:322`

### English

For the next §Y10 years§! we will be able to enact the §Y$decision_crystalline_refugees$§! decision on a §YHabitat§!.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:401`

## central.50.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acentral.50.b&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的国土内没有空间了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:323`

### English

There is no room in our lands.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:402`

### English

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:404`

### English

£trigger_no£ §Y$NAME_Central_Crystal_Country$§! is hostile to us.

## central.51.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acentral.51.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

很高兴听到这个消息。我希望他们可以在你们的国土上过得好，并成为你们帝国的佑护。\n\n$central.quest.transmission.end$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:326`

### English

That is good to hear. We hope they will thrive in your lands, and be a blessing for your empire.\n\n$central.quest.transmission.end$

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:407`

## central.51.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acentral.51.a&lang=en)

### 中文

是的，我们还有别的事情。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:327`

### English

Yes, there was something else we had in mind.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:408`

## central.51.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acentral.51.b&lang=en)

### 中文

没，就这样。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:328`

### English

No, that is all.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:409`

## central.52.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acentral.52.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们很遗憾。那我们去别的地方为他们找容身之地了。\n\n$central.quest.transmission.end$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:329`

### English

We are sorry to hear that. We will look elsewhere for our people then.\n\n$central.quest.transmission.end$

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:411`

## central.60.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acentral.60.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们最近在我们最大的城市之一发生了一长小型灾难，并迅速制造了难以言说的毁坏。为了将其修复如初，我们正寻找愿意向我们贡献材料的人，让我们开始重建工作。\n\n当然，我们会慷慨地想愿意帮助我们的人支付酬劳。不仅如此，我们还可以派出一队工程师与你们的人民分享我们的一部分秘密。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:330`

### English

We have recently seen a small calamity happen in one of our greatest cities, leaving untold destruction in its wake. To restore it to former glory, we are looking for people willing to send materials our way, so we can begin the reconstruction.\n\nWe will, of course, pay quite handsomely to anyone willing to help us out. Not only that, but we can send a team of engineers to share some of our secrets with your people.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:413`

## central.60.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acentral.60.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们可以为你们的人民拨出一部分资源。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:331`

### English

We will set aside some materials for your people.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:414`

## central.60.a.tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acentral.60.a.tt&lang=en)

### 中文

在加来的§Y10年§!内，我们可以在一个§Y居住站§!上发起§Y$decision_crystalline_architecture$§!决议。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:332`

### English

For the next §Y10 years§! we will be able to enact the §Y$decision_crystalline_architecture$§! decision on a §YHabitat§!.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:415`

## central.60.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acentral.60.b&lang=en)

### 中文

我们没有余钱。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:333`

### English

We have none to spare.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:416`

## central.61.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acentral.61.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们很高兴知道你们愿意提供帮助。你们的礼物可以帮助数以百万计的$NAME_Central_Crystal_Species$。\n\n$central.quest.transmission.end$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:334`

### English

We are happy to hear you are willing to help us out. Your gift will help millions of lost $NAME_Central_Crystal_Species$.\n\n$central.quest.transmission.end$

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:418`

## central.62.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acentral.62.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们很遗憾你们不愿意提供帮助。我们只能再去其他地方寻找帮助了。\n\n$central.quest.transmission.end$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:335`

### English

We are sorry to hear that you are not willing to help us out. We will look elsewhere for help, then.\n\n$central.quest.transmission.end$

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:420`

## central.70.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acentral.70.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一位我们的首席科学官对你们的帝国产生了浓厚的兴趣，虽然很难说你们到底能提供什么我们帝国所没有的。但无论如何，他们希望能宜居你们国家并向你们中最杰出的人学习。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:336`

### English

One of our foremost scientists has taken a particular interest in your empire, though it is hard to say what you could offer that we of the Empire could not. In any case, they seek to move to your country and learn from your best.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:422`

## central.70.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acentral.70.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们对他们的位置有所打算了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:337`

### English

We have a position in mind for them.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:423`

## central.70.a.tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acentral.70.a.tt&lang=en)

### 中文

§H$NAME_Central_Crystal_Species$§!§Y科学官§!将会迁移到§Y[Root.GetName]§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:338`

### English

A §H$NAME_Central_Crystal_Species$§! §YScientist§! will move to §Y[Root.GetName]§!.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:424`

## central.70.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acentral.70.b&lang=en)

### 中文

这没有他们的位置。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:339`

### English

There are no positions available for them.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:425`

## central.71.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acentral.71.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

大好消息。希望他们可以对你们的帝国做出比在我们这更大的贡献。\n\n$central.quest.transmission.end$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:340`

### English

Great news. Hopefully they will prove to be more helpful for your empire than they were for ours.\n\n$central.quest.transmission.end$

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:427`

## central.72.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acentral.72.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

那他们可得找点别的事情干了。\n\n$central.quest.transmission.end$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:341`

### English

They will simply have to find something else to occupy their mind then.\n\n$central.quest.transmission.end$

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:429`

## colony.9000.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9000.name&lang=en)

### 中文

不稳地面

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:342`

### English

Unsteady Ground

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:431`

## colony.9000.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9000.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[Root.GetName]遇到了奇怪的情况：我们显然定居到了某种巨大的两栖爬行动物身上，他们的骨质外壳由多种奇特的矿物构成。许多我们认为是当地岛屿的其实都是这种生物，我们的殖民者则将他们称为“§Y尼玛§!”。\n\n他们看起来人畜无害，由于我们并没有深入他们的外壳，他们应该几乎没注意到我们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:343`

### English

Our colony on [Root.GetName] has encountered an odd situation: we have apparently settled on the back of a gargantuan amphibian reptile, whose bony shell is formed of various exotic minerals. Most of what we thought were local islands have turned out to be these creatures; our colonists have begun to refer to them as '§YNemmas§!'.\n\nThey seem harmless enough, and as long as we don't dig too deep into their shell, they should barely notice us.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:432`

## colony.9000.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9000.a&lang=en)

### 中文

继续吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:344`

### English

Carry on.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:433`

## colony.9000.a.turtle

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9000.a.turtle&lang=en)

### 中文

他们看起来……好亲切。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:345`

### English

They seem... familiar.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:434`

## colony.9005.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9005.name&lang=en)

### 中文

勇敢的发现

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:346`

### English

A Daring Discovery

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:436`

## colony.9005.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9005.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[Root.GetName]的殖民地正在繁荣发展，这个新殖民地的青少年当然要为自己创造一些娱乐，就像所有年轻人都会做的一样。一个团体发明了一种互相比拼悄悄接近当地尼玛头部的游戏。\n\n不幸的是，这一大胆的行动导致了悲剧：在惊吓到了这只巨兽之后，一些年轻的[Root.Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural]从这只巨大生物的颈部坠落，造成多名人口受伤，其中一位不治身亡。\n\n烦躁的殖民者现在要求实施一些举措来应对这些“残暴的巨兽”。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:347`

### English

As our colony at [Root.GetName] has been flourishing, the adolescents of this new world have sought to create entertainment for themselves - as youths are wont to do. Some groups have made a game of sneaking up as close as they dare to their local Nemma's head.\n\nUnfortunately, this daring activity has led to tragedy: after startling the beast, several young [Root.Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural] fell off the neck of the massive creature, resulting in injuries and one fatality.\n\nDistraught colonists are now asking for something to be done about these "brutish behemoths."

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:437`

## colony.9005.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9005.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

在我们在[Root.GetName]的殖民地繁荣发展时，显然尼玛对我们前往其头部的无人机感到非常地不适。特别是在这只野兽畏缩并从它的脖子上甩下了许多[Root.Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural]，造成明显损失时尤为明显。\n\n如果不进行一些举措处理这些巨兽我们的进展将会受到影响。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:348`

### English

While our [Root.GetName] colony has been flourishing, it has become apparent that Nemmas have a strong aversion to our drones venturing near their head. This became starkly apparent when one such beast flinched and dislodged several [Root.Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural] from its neck, causing severe damage.\n\nOur progress will be restrained unless we do something to handle these behemoths.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:438`

## colony.9005.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9005.a&lang=en)

### 中文

必须采取行动。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:349`

### English

Something must be done.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:439`

### English

Allow the youths their fun.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:440`

## colony.9005.b.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9005.b.gestalt&lang=en)

我们之后尽量避免再刺激到这些生物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:351`

### English

We will avoid agitating the creatures further.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:441`

## colony.9010.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9010.name&lang=en)

### 中文

尼玛特遣队

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:352`

### English

Nemma Taskforce

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:443`

## colony.9010.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9010.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

派遣我们最好的生物学家和工程师为[Root.GetName]上的尼玛困境寻找潜在的解决方案，许多提案现已经完成可以呈交。\n\n我们考虑了从最普通到最极端的所有想法。这一情况的紧迫程度要求我们直接跳到现场调研查中。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:353`

### English

Having tasked our best biologists and engineers with devising potential solutions for the Nemma dilemma on [Root.GetName], several proposals are now ready to be presented.\n\nWe've considered everything from the most rational to the most extreme of ideas. The urgency of this situation demands we must skip straight to live field tests.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:444`

## colony.9010.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9010.a&lang=en)

### 中文

繁育更多尼玛，并保护他们的安全以获得更多生存空间。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:354`

### English

Breed more Nemmas and safeguard them for more living space.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:445`

## colony.9010.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9010.b&lang=en)

### 中文

奴役这些尼玛，让他们为我们所用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:355`

### English

Enslave the Nemmas so that they become deferential and compliant.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:446`

## colony.9010.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9010.c&lang=en)

### 中文

够了！摁住这些尼玛，钻取他们的外壳。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:356`

### English

Enough! Sedate the Nemmas, and start drilling their shells.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:447`

## colony.9010.d

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9010.d&lang=en)

### 中文

把这些脏东西清理掉。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:357`

### English

Exterminate the filth.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:448`

## colony.9015.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9015.name&lang=en)

### 中文

拓展群岛

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:358`

### English

Expand the Archipelago

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:450`

## colony.9015.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9015.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在一些对成年个体的初步试验之后，我们的动物学家已经在[Root.GetName]上设立了一个尼玛繁育系统，以期为海洋带来更多的巨大尼玛。繁育者提示，这不可能无限地进行下去，因为这颗行星只能再承载额外三只或者四只尼玛。我们得到下一只尼玛的时间大约需要§Y十年§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:359`

### English

After initial trials with the adult specimen, our zoologists have set up a Nemma breeding system on [Root.GetName] in the hopes of introducing more giant Nemmas to our oceans. The breeders warn that this cannot continue indefinitely, and that the planet can only support three, perhaps four more Nemmas. The estimated time until we have our next Nemma should be about §Yten years§!.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:451`

## colony.9015.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9015.a&lang=en)

### 中文

那么我们开始繁育吧！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:360`

### English

We'd best commence breeding, then!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:452`

## colony.9020.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9020.name&lang=en)

### 中文

生命的奇迹

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:361`

### English

The Miracle of Life

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:454`

## colony.9020.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9020.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[Root.GetName]上的繁育计划结出了硕果，另一只尼玛加入了我们的行列！这个幼年个体很快适应了我们的存在，而我们发现它是一座稳定的栖息地。这只尼玛现在已经准备好承载我们的产业了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:362`

### English

Our breeding program on [Root.GetName] bears fruit as we add another Nemma to our ranks! This juvenile has quickly acclimated to our presence, and we have found it a stable habitat. The Nemma should now be ready to bear our industry.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:455`

## colony.9020.desc.full

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9020.desc.full&lang=en)

### 中文

$colony.9020.desc$\n\n然而，由于已经达到了生态承载上限，这将会是这个星球上最后一只新增的尼玛。我们的牧尼玛人必须在繁育尼玛之外寻找新的着力点，就是说他们需要更好地维护已有的尼玛。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:363`

### English

$colony.9020.desc$\n\nThis will, however, have to be the last Nemma added to this world, as their habitat has reached capacity. Our Nemma wranglers will have to refocus their efforts not on breeding more Nemmas, but instead on maintaining what they already have created.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:456`

## colony.9020.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9020.a&lang=en)

### 中文

叹为观止！能再表演一遍那个吗！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:364`

### English

Magnificent! Start on another one!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:457`

## colony.9020.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9020.b&lang=en)

### 中文

大家都干得漂亮。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:365`

### English

Great work everyone.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:458`

## colony.9020.b.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9020.b.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

对这个项目而言真是令人满意的结果。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:366`

### English

This is a satisfactory conclusion to the project.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:459`

## colony.9025.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9025.name&lang=en)

### 中文

环境过载

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:367`

### English

Overpopulation

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:461`

## colony.9025.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9025.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

难过的是我们最近尝试在[Root.GetName]增添另一只尼玛的努力失败了；我们无法为这么巨大的生物寻找到足够的栖息空间。但[Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural]并没有沮丧，并且承诺未来会取得更好的结果。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:368`

### English

Sadly the latest effort to add another Nemma to [Root.GetName] has failed; we could find no habitat spacious enough for such a massive beast to thrive. The [Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural] are not dismayed, however, and promise better results in the future.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:462`

## colony.9025.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9025.a&lang=en)

### 中文

让我们再试一次。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:369`

### English

Let's try again.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:463`

## colony.9030.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9030.name&lang=en)

### 中文

强制服从

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:370`

### English

Enforced Obedience

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:465`

## colony.9030.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9030.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的外星生物工程师[Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural]成功驯服了尼玛威胁！由于我们不懈的努力，这些生命现在向我们臣服了。\n\n这些尼玛获得了新的用途：最高级的驮畜。他们庞大的躯体使得他们可以搭载难以计量的材料，由神经植入体引导选择最优的路线。我们的殖民活动也由此得到了加速。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:371`

### English

Our xeno-bioengineering [Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural] have curtailed the Nemma menace! Thanks to our superb efforts, the creatures are now subservient to us.\n\nThe Nemmas have been granted renewed purpose as superlative beasts of burden. Their colossal bulk allows them to carry immense quantities of materiel, guided down optimal routes by way of their cybernetic implants. Our colonization effort has accelerated accordingly.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:466`

## colony.9030.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9030.a&lang=en)

### 中文

棒极了，现在我们可以开始干点正事了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:372`

### English

Excellent work, now our true work can begin.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:467`

## colony.9035.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9035.name&lang=en)

### 中文

尼玛拥抱者

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:373`

### English

Nemma Huggers

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:469`

## colony.9035.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9035.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

今天在[Root.GetName]上爆发了一些骚乱，部分人口奋起抗议我们对尼玛的对待方式。这些抗议者在围着尼玛的头部也就是殖民首府的所在地排成了人墙。抗议者要求我们立刻将尼玛放归自由，并致力维系这些物种而不是奴役他们。\n\n这一运动很快扩散到了其他尼玛身上的其他地方——然而，利于我们目前计划的反抗议也在形成，公共矛盾逐渐升级，并有爆发出暴力冲突的危险。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:374`

### English

Today riots broke out on [Root.GetName] as parts of the population have risen in protest against our treatment of the Nemmas. The protestors formed a picket line around the head of the Nemma on which the colony's capital lies. The protestors demanded that we immediately set all the Nemmas free, working to conserve their species rather than enslave them.\n\nThe movement has quickly spread to sites around the other Nemmas - however, counter-protests have formed in favor of our existing programs, escalating public tension and risking outbursts of violence.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:470`

## colony.9035.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9035.a&lang=en)

### 中文

改为实行繁育计划。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:375`

### English

Commence a breeding program instead.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:471`

## colony.9035.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9035.b&lang=en)

### 中文

逮捕煽动者然后让他们去扫大街。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:376`

### English

Arrest the instigators and put them on waste disposal duty.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:472`

## colony.9040.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9040.name&lang=en)

### 中文

挖掘外壳

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:377`

### English

Exploiting the Shells

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:474`

## colony.9040.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9040.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的工程师和生化学家实现了自我超越。新开发的药物和极度精确的钻头让我们可以在不威胁到我们基础设施的前提下通过钻入这些尼玛的坚硬晶质外壳中获取矿物，同时还能获取生物质。我们所有的殖民地中只有这里可以产出这样的资源。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:378`

### English

Our engineers and biochemists have outdone themselves. Newly developed drugs and extremely precise drills will allow us to harvest the minerals by drilling into the hard crystalline shell of these Nemmas, as well as allowing us to collect biomatter from beneath without jeopardizing our infrastructure. If only all our colonies could yield such bounties.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:475`

## colony.9045.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9045.name&lang=en)

### 中文

消灭这些长寿的爬行动物

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:379`

### English

Exterminate the Perpetual Reptiles

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:477`

## colony.9045.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9045.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对尼玛的净化已经完成了——极度地系统化，因此既没有污染海洋也没有威胁到我们的基础设施。很快我们可以将我们的注意力转移到收获它们的尸骸中，并在[Root.GetName]上建立更多的永久殖民地。首先，我们得先储存好这些残羹剩饭。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:380`

### English

The purging of the Nemmas has been completed - all very systematically, so as not to pollute the oceans or otherwise endanger our infrastructure. Soon we can turn our attention to the task of harvesting their remains, and establishing a more permanent and enduring colony on [Root.GetName]. First, however, we need to store all the leftovers.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:478`

## colony.9045.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9045.a&lang=en)

### 中文

这是对待这些无脑野兽的最佳方法。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:381`

### English

The only way to handle such mindless beasts.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:479`

## colony.9950.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9950.name&lang=en)

### 中文

平流层喷口

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:382`

### English

Stratospheric Vents

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:481`

## colony.9950.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9950.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

现在，§Y[planet.GetName]§!上重要的殖民工作已经完成，[Root.Owner.GetName][Owner.GetScientistPlural]开始将注意力转向这个行星上的高耸尖塔。\n\n这些矿物构成的结构似乎完全中空，将行星地壳下方的气体一路传输到大气热层。并且由于某些未知的原因，这些气体还吸引着缇扬奇以及其他太空生物，让它们长途跋涉来到这颗星球。\n\n我们如果能够对这种星球奇观开展一次彻底的研究，就能加深我们对于这些奇幻现象的了解。当然了，我们也可以想个方法来收集这些稀有的气体。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:383`

### English

Now that crucial colonization efforts of §Y[planet.GetName]§! have been completed, [Root.Owner.GetName] [Owner.GetScientistPlural] have turned their attention towards its lofty spires.\n\nThese mineral formations appear to be entirely hollow, transporting gaseous substances from beneath the planet's crust all the way to the thermosphere. For an as yet unknown reason, these gases also attract Tiyanki and other spaceborne fauna to travel across great distances to this planet.\n\nA more thorough research project would allow us to study this planetary wonder and expand our understanding of these incredible creatures, or perhaps to devise a method of claiming these precious gases for ourselves.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:482`

## colony.9950.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9950.a&lang=en)

### 中文

研究这些气体喷口。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:384`

### English

Study the vents.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:483`

## colony.9950.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9950.b&lang=en)

### 中文

我们可没有时间管这些东西。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:385`

### English

There is no time for this.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:484`

## colony.9955.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9955.name&lang=en)

### 中文

平流层喷口研究完毕

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:386`

### English

Stratospheric Vents Studied

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:486`

## colony.9955.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9955.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经确定，§Y[planet.GetName]§!上的气体排放口是过热气体与其中的颗粒不断冷却沉积，历经数百万年时光形成的。那些没有沉积的气体则在大气热层中聚集成团，并被在其中优雅穿行的缇扬奇吞食。\n\n这是一个罕见的机会，我们不仅可以近距离观察这些生物，还可以吸引外星游客前来观光。或者，我们也可以利用这些气体喷口提炼异星天然气，不过这样就需要把这些生物赶出它们栖息多年的地方。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:387`

### English

We have determined that the vents on §Y[planet.GetName]§! have formed over millions of years as superheated gas and sediments cool down and solidify. The remaining fumes in the lower thermosphere form clouds that the Tiyanki feed on, plowing through with unexpected grace.\n\nThis affords us a rare opportunity not only to observe the creatures in close proximity, but also increase the colony's appeal to extraplanetary visitors. Alternatively, the vents could be harnessed for exotic gas refining at the expense of driving away the fauna from their ancient feeding grounds.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:487`

## colony.9955.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9955.a&lang=en)

### 中文

将其用于观光与研究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:388`

### English

Use for tourism and research.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:488`

## colony.9955.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9955.b&lang=en)

### 中文

将其改造为气体精炼场所。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:389`

### English

Redirect for refining.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:489`

## colony.9960.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9960.name&lang=en)

### 中文

平流层喷口观光平台

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:390`

### English

Stratospheric Vent Platforms

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:491`

## colony.9960.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9960.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在最活跃的气体喷口附近建造豪华的观景平台后，我们可以大幅提升§Y[planet.GetName]§!的独特旅游体验。游客可以与缇扬奇近距离接触，平台上的研究人员也会引导他们进行观光。\n\n更富裕的游客可以直接参加科学采样探险，甚至还能亲身体验踏足活体缇扬奇带来的刺激！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:391`

### English

The unique tourism opportunities on §Y[planet.GetName]§! could be enhanced greatly by building luxurious viewing platforms near the most active vents. This would allow visitors to get up close and personal with Tiyanki as our on-site researchers guide them through this one of a kind experience.\n\nThe more affluent patrons could join scientific sampling expeditions or even experience the thrill of setting foot on a live Tiyanki!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:492`

## colony.9960.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9960.a&lang=en)

### 中文

建造观景平台。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:392`

### English

Build the viewing platforms.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:493`

## colony.9960.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9960.b&lang=en)

### 中文

这些资源还有别的用处。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:393`

### English

The resources are needed elsewhere.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:494`

## colony.9965.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9965.name&lang=en)

### 中文

平流层喷口扩展

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:394`

### English

Stratospheric Vent Expansion

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:496`

## colony.9965.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9965.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的工程师提出，可以利用炸药来扩宽排放口以充分利用§Y[Planet.GetName]§!地下储存的气体。尽管排放口倒塌的风险微乎其微，但是为以防万一还是通知一下当地人口为好。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:395`

### English

Our engineers suggest that the underground gas reserves on §Y[Planet.GetName]§! could be fully harnessed by expanding the vents with mining charges. The dangers of the vents collapsing should be negligible, but the local populace should be notified just in case.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:497`

## colony.9965.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9965.a&lang=en)

### 中文

安放炸药！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:396`

### English

Set the charges!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:498`

## colony.9965.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9965.b&lang=en)

### 中文

不值得冒这个险。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:397`

### English

It is not worth the risk.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:499`

## colony.9965.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9965.c&lang=en)

公会能处理此事。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:398`

### English

The Guild will handle this.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:500`

## colony.9970.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9970.name&lang=en)

### 中文

喷口扩展成功

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:399`

### English

Vent Expansion Success

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:502`

## colony.9970.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9970.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

喷口扩展计划大获成功，我们的工程师汇报星球各处的气体产量均有增长。而不幸的副作用是有一些比较脆弱的排放口发生了史诗级的倒塌，对我们的一些设施造成了轻微损伤。\n\n且不论这些微小的阻碍，我们成功地驾驭了§Y[planet.GetName]§!独特的自然条件并得到了好处。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:400`

### English

The vent expansion project was a resounding success and our engineers are reporting increased gas production across the planet. As an unfortunate side-effect, some of the more fragile vents crashed spectacularly, causing minor damage to our facilities.\n\nDespite this minor setback, we have managed to harness the unique conditions of §Y[planet.GetName]§! to great advantage.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:503`

## colony.9975.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9975.name&lang=en)

### 中文

喷口扩展失败

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:401`

### English

Vent Expansion Failure

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:505`

## colony.9975.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9975.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对炸药的计算失误导致数座排放口碎裂，倒塌到了地上，这对我们的设施造成了可观的损害。尽管有一部分精炼厂现在的产出是提高了，但总体而言得不偿失。令人欣慰的是，崩塌的构造下出现了意想不到的矿藏资源。\n\n这些事件提醒我们，如果不砸碎几个喷口，就无法建立跨越星系的文明。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:402`

### English

Miscalculations with the mining charges caused several vents to fracture and eventually topple to the ground, resulting in considerable damage to our facilities. While some refineries are now producing more gas than before, the total yield has decreased. The silver lining is that the collapsed structures have revealed unexpected sources of minerals.\n\nThese events act as a reminder that galaxy-spanning civilizations cannot be built without cracking a few vents.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:506`

## nemma_world_chain_title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anemma_world_chain_title&lang=en)

### 中文

尼玛星球

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:403`

### English

Nemma World

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:508`

## nemma_world_chain_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anemma_world_chain_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

巨大的尼玛居住在我们的一个星球上，我们已经在他们的背上建立了一个殖民地的基础。我们将决定它们的命运。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:404`

### English

The titanic Nemma inhabit one of our worlds and we have already built the foundation of a colony on their backs. We will need to decide what to do with them.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:509`

## stratovent_world_chain_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astratovent_world_chain_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们可以在其中一个殖民地上获得潜在的丰富资源，自然形成的通风孔将有价值的资源抛到到太空中。现在，我们需要决定如何处理它们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:426`

### English

We have access to a potentially lucrative resource on one of our colonies, naturally formed vents expunging valuable resources into space. Now we need to decide on what to do with them.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:534`

## broken_world_digsite_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abroken_world_digsite_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[From.Planet.GetName]的一处破损的拱顶发现了奇怪的信号。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:429`

### English

We have picked up strange signals from a broken dome on [From.Planet.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:538`

## site_tombworld_weapon_cache

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_tombworld_weapon_cache&lang=en)

### English

Weapons Cache

## site_tombworld_weapon_cache_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_tombworld_weapon_cache_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[From.Planet.GetName]破碎地面的下方发现了一个大型加固贮藏室。考虑到在这样一个充满辐射的废土，还有其被埋葬的年份，我们很惊讶它居然完好无损。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:431`

### English

A large reinforced cache has been found underneath the broken ground of [From.Planet.GetName]. It's surprisingly intact considering the years it spent buried in a radioactive wasteland.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:540`

## MESSAGE_HYPERDRIVE_SECURED_TITLE

## MESSAGE_HYPERDRIVE_SECURED

### English

Our away party managed to pacify the arthropoids and escape with the research.

## the_lonely_bot_welcome_desc

### 中文

生活在这个星球上的小机器人已经竭尽全力帮助我们的殖民者安顿下来并建立自己的家园。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:437`

The little robot who lives on the planet has gone out of its way to help our colonists settle down and establish themselves.

### English
