## nanite_swarm_modifier_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ananite_swarm_modifier_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个行星上到处都是外星纳米机器人，攻击进入大气层的任何东西——无论生物的还是机械。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:443`

### English

This planet is infested with a swarm of alien nanomachines, attacking anything that enters the atmosphere - biological or mechanical.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:557`

## tamed_nanite_swarm_modifier

## tamed_nanite_swarm_modifier_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atamed_nanite_swarm_modifier_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一个自我进化的纳米虫群在这个星球上被驯化，现在正对殖民者的日常生活伸出援助之手。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:445`

### English

A self-evolved nanite swarm was domesticated on this planet and now helps the colonists with their day-to-day life.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:559`

## turtle_world_modifier_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aturtle_world_modifier_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在这颗星球上起初认为是岛屿的东西其实是一些巨大的活着的爬行动物的背部。这迫使定居者们在卷铺盖离开新家的过程显得非常痛苦。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:449`

### English

What were initially regarded as islands on this planet have instead turned out to be the backs of massive, live reptiles. This forces the inhabitants to take greater pains when parceling out housing.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:565`

### English

### English

## turtle_world_slaves_modifier_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aturtle_world_slaves_modifier_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个星球奴役了当地的巨大尼玛，优化了他们对行星的利用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:453`

### English

This world has enslaved the native giant Nemmas living there, optimizing their use of the planet.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:569`

## anomaly.6755.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6755.name&lang=en)

### 中文

真菌发射器

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:488`

### English

Fungal Emitters

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:613`

## anomaly.6755.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6755.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在[From.GetName]的地表之下生活着一种非常奇特的有机体。扫描和取样显示，它是一种散布着孢子的菌丝体。与我们母星上熟悉的孢子的尺寸相比，它们非常巨大。它们的表面闪烁迷人，千变万化，色调似乎随着环境而变化。\n\n其中最奇怪的事情莫过于它们似乎形成了一种可以产生科学家相信是信号的东西的网络。现在的辐射还非常弱，但稍作努力，也许就能找到它们指向何方。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:489`

### English

Below the surface of [From.GetName] lives a most peculiar organism. Scans and samples show that it is some sort of mycelium littered with spores. Compared to the traditional size of spores on our homeworld, these are massive. Their surface is a shimmering kaleidoscope, and the hues seem to change based on their surroundings.\n\nThe strangest thing about them is that they seem to form a network capable of generating what the science crew believes could be a signal. The current emissions are very weak, but with some effort, it might be possible to find out where they lead.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:614`

## anomaly.6755.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6755.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们会试着辨别这些信号的指向。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:490`

### English

We will try to identify the signal destination.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:615`

### English

Bring them back to be sold.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:616`

## anomaly.6755.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6755.c&lang=en)

我们没空管这个。

### English

## anomaly.6760.intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6760.intro&lang=en)

### 中文

在[From.GetName]的地表之下生活着一种非常奇特的有机体。扫描和取样显示，它是一种散布着孢子的菌丝体，并且与“巨大蘑菇”直接关联。这些孢子看起来变化成长成了小的球状物。它们表面变幻莫测的颜色相当瑰丽，但更重要的是它们发出来比一般孢子更强的信号。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:493`

### English

Below the surface of [From.GetName] lives a familiar organism. Scans and samples show that it is some sort of mycelium, littered with enormous spores and directly related to the 'Fumongus.' Its spores seem, however, to have mutated and grown into small beads. Their ever-changing colorful surface is quite the spectacle, but more importantly, they seem to be producing a stronger signal than the regular spores.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:618`

## anomaly.6760.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6760.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$anomaly.6760.intro$\n\n我们的科学家猜测我们也许可以通过部分孢子带回“巨大蘑菇”来增强当前的网络。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:494`

### English

$anomaly.6760.intro$\n\nOur scientists hypothesize that we might be capable of strengthening the current network by bringing some of these spores back to the 'Fumongus'.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:619`

## anomaly.6760.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6760.a&lang=en)

### 中文

准备一个项目照办。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:495`

### English

Prepare a project to do so.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:620`

## anomaly.6760.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6760.b&lang=en)

### 中文

带回数据就行。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:496`

### English

Just bring back the data.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:621`

## anomaly.6765.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6765.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$anomaly.6760.intro$\n\n由于我们拆解了这个网络，这些信息就没有什么用处了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:497`

### English

$anomaly.6760.intro$\n\nSince we dismantled the network, we don't really have a practical use for this information.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:622`

## anomaly.6770.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6770.name&lang=en)

### 中文

跟随真菌

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:498`

### English

Following the Fungi

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:623`

## anomaly.6770.intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6770.intro&lang=en)

### 中文

结果证明追踪菌丝体发出的信号是件棘手的活。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:499`

### English

Tracking the signal emitted by the mycelium proved to be a tricky business.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:624`

## anomaly.6770.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6770.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$anomaly.6770.intro$ 我们迷茫的科学家最终定出了“通过蘑菇来一瞥更高纬度”这种假设。关于会带来什么结果的细节完全没有。不过无论如何：这项操作是成功的，并且确实把我们指向了某个目标。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:500`

### English

$anomaly.6770.intro$ Our puzzled scientist finally managed by 'using mushrooms to peer into additional dimensions.' Details are scarce about what this entails, but in any case: the operation was a success and has pointed us towards a destination.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:625`

## anomaly.6770.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6770.a&lang=en)

### 中文

指条明路！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:501`

### English

Chart a course!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:626`

## anomaly.6780.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6780.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$anomaly.6770.intro$尽管[Root.GetLeaderName]尽了最大努力，仍然无法为这些信号指明目的地，而菌丝体仍然谜团重重。尽管如此，我们在这个过程中也收获颇丰，数据也如之前所说的一样会被带回去。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:502`

### English

$anomaly.6770.intro$ Despite the best efforts of [Root.GetLeaderName], it was impossible to determine a destination for the signal and the mycelium mostly remains a mystery. Despite this, we managed to learn a lot in the process and the data is being brought back as we speak.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:627`

## anomaly.6785.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6785.name&lang=en)

### 中文

蘑菇网络

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:503`

### English

Mushrooming the Network

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:628`

## anomaly.6785.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6785.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

将变异的孢子移植到现有的蘑菇上是一项生物工程壮举。借助详细描述“巨大蘑菇”基因变化的档案，我们的科学家设法在不影响其效率的情况下进一步改变它。结果非常惊人，几个月后，蘑菇长了十倍。反过来这又增加了对水的供给的需求，但附近冰冻的小行星足以为它们提供几个世纪甚至数千年的水分。\n\n更大的蘑菇带来了更好的网络，在对计算设备进行了一些升级之后，我们现在再次准备好听听孢子怎么说。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:504`

### English

Grafting the mutated spores onto the existing mushroom was a feat of bio-engineering. With the help of archives detailing the genetic alterations already made on the 'Fumongus,' our scientists managed to further alter it without compromising its efficiency. The results were spectacular, and in a matter of months, the mushrooms had grown tenfold. This in turn increased the need for water to supply them, but the nearby frozen asteroids hold enough to hydrate them for centuries, if not millennia.\n\nWith bigger mushrooms came a better network, and after some upgrades to the computing equipment, we are now once again ready to listen to what the spores have to say.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:629`

## FOLLOWING_FUNGI_PROJECT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AFOLLOWING_FUNGI_PROJECT&lang=en)

### 中文

真菌航迹

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:505`

### English

The Fungal Trail

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:631`

## FOLLOWING_FUNGI_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AFOLLOWING_FUNGI_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName] 上的一个异常现象揭示了一群奇特的真菌的存在，它发出了一个信号……大概是。通过在源头放大信号的方法，我们的科学家相信我们可以发现该信号的目的地并更多地了解这种菌丝体。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:506`

### English

An anomaly on [From.GetName] has revealed the existence of a colony of strange fungi sending a signal... Somewhere. By amplifying it at the source, our scientists believe we could discover the destination of that signal and understand more about this mycelium.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:632`

## MUSHROOMING_NETWORK_PROJECT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AMUSHROOMING_NETWORK_PROJECT&lang=en)

### 中文

嫁接孢子

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:507`

### English

Grafting the Spores

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:633`

## MUSHROOMING_NETWORK_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AMUSHROOMING_NETWORK_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

突变孢子的发现，为我们开发菌丝体监听阵列提供了一个独特的机会。这些孢子更大，发出更强的信号，可以帮助我们促进“巨大蘑菇”的发育。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:508`

### English

The discovery of mutated spores offers a unique opportunity to develop our Mycelial Listening Array. These spores are bigger, send a stronger signal, and could help us foster the development of the 'Fumongus.'

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:634`

## colony.2060.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2060.name&lang=en)

### 中文

采掘蘑菇

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:515`

### English

Mushroom Picking

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:644`

## colony.2060.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2060.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[Root.GetName]上的殖民地进行的例行生态研究中，偶然发现了一种奇怪的真菌生命形式。科学小组报告说，菌丝体的表面闪闪发光，色彩斑斓，但最让他们感兴趣的是，每当大范围内发生某些事情时，不同的蘑菇群似乎在相互交流。\n\n此外，生物体的生物特征似乎与殖民地的生态系统明显不同。当地科学团队相当困惑，并要求提供专业支持，因为我们可能正在处理一种有感知力的物种。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:516`

### English

A routine ecological study of our colony on [Root.GetName] has stumbled upon a strange fungal lifeform. The science team reports that the surface of the mycelium is shimmering and colorful, but what was most intriguing to them was the fact that separate clusters were seemingly communicating with one another whenever something occurred within a wide radius.\n\nFurthermore, the biology of the organism appears to be clearly alien to the ecosystem of the colony. Puzzled, the local science team has requested specialized support as we might be dealing with a sentient species.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:645`

## colony.2060.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2060.a&lang=en)

处理掉他们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:517`

### English

Dispose of them.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:646`

### English

Take a closer look

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:647`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2060.c&lang=en)

……他们和巨大蘑菇有关！

### English

... They are related to the Fumongus!

## colony.2065.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2065.name&lang=en)

### 中文

孢子有耳

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:520`

### English

The Spores Have Ears

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:649`

## colony.2065.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2065.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们收到了来自[Root.GetName]的报告。经过进一步检查，我们发现的外星真菌似乎正在向我们目前无法确定的星系中的某个位置发出信号。遥感探测和安全分析表明，菌丝体完全有可能是某种高级间谍生物。\n\n然而，值得注意的是，这种生物体已经想当老了。设计这些间谍蘑菇的人很可能已经不在了。但如果他们还在的话，最好不要唤醒这些沉睡的捕食者。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:521`

### English

We have concerning reports coming from [Root.GetName]. Upon further examination, it seems that the alien fungus we found is emitting a signal towards a location in the galaxy that we were unable to pinpoint so far. Telemetry and security analysis indicate that it is completely possible for the mycelium to be some kind of advanced spying organism.\n\nHowever, it is worth noting that this organism is old. There is a fair chance that whomever engineered these spying mushrooms is not around anymore. But if they are, it might be best not to poke the sleeping predator.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:650`

## colony.2065.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2065.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们想知道更多。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:522`

### English

We need to know more.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:651`

## colony.2065.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2065.b&lang=en)

### 中文

这太冒险了。摧毁他们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:523`

### English

This is too risky. Destroy them.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:652`

## colony.2070.intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2070.intro&lang=en)

### 中文

对菌丝体和信号的仔细分析表明，它可能是类似生物网络的一部分，可能跨越整个星系，甚至更远。虽然我们知道的不多，但我们发现，作为网络的一部分，这个菌丝体也在接收来自其他地方的信号。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:524`

### English

Careful analysis of the mycelium and the signals showed that it was likely part of a network of similar organisms, possibly spanning across the galaxy as a whole, and maybe beyond. While we do not know much, we discovered that as part of the network, this mycelium was also receiving signals from other places.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:653`

## colony.2070.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2070.name&lang=en)

### 中文

隔墙有耳

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:525`

### English

Someone is Listening

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:654`

## colony.2070.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2070.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$colony.2070.intro$\n\n我们无法确定，但考虑到包括传输的数据量在内的几个因素，我们认为有人正在积极使用网络并从中收集信息。我们已经在制定虚假信息的反间谍计划，并且正在全帝国范围内更新安全措施以应对这种形式的间谍活动。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:526`

### English

$colony.2070.intro$\n\nWe cannot be certain, but considering several factors, including the volume of data transferred, we believe that someone is actively using the network and collecting information from it. We are already working on a counter-intelligence plan of disinformation, and security measures are being updated empire wide to account for this form of spycraft.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:655`

## colony.2070.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2070.a&lang=en)

### 中文

开始散布假情报！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:527`

### English

Let the disinformation begin!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:656`

## colony.2075.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2075.name&lang=en)

### 中文

无人倾听

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:528`

### English

No One Listens

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:657`

## colony.2075.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2075.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$colony.2070.intro$\n\n考虑到包括传输的数据量在内的几个因素，我们认为该网络并没有仍在使用中。它仍然部分活跃的事实表明它可能只是处于休眠状态而不是损坏状态。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:529`

### English

$colony.2070.intro$\n\nConsidering several factors, including the volume of data transferred, we do not think that the network is still in use. The fact that it is still partially active indicates that it is probably just dormant and not destroyed.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:658`

## colony.2075.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2075.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们可以学到什么？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:530`

### English

What can we learn from it?

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:659`

## colony.2080.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2080.name&lang=en)

### 中文

孢子连接

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:531`

### English

Spore Connection

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:660`

## colony.2080.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2080.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们对这种真菌与菌丝体监听阵列的联系的所有疑虑，在经过简短的比较后很快就被打消了。这是与巨大蘑菇交流的众多后代之一，这可能是我们进一步了解这个生物体的好机会。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:532`

### English

Whatever doubts we had about this fungus' connection to the mycelial listening array were quickly dispelled after a brief comparison. This is one of the many offsprings communicating with the Fumongus, and this could be an opportunity to further our comprehension of the organism.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:661`

## colony.2080.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2080.a&lang=en)

### 中文

试试我们能否增强这个网络。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:533`

### English

See if we can strengthen the network.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:662`

用它来监视殖民地。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:534`

### English

Use it to keep a closer eye on the colony.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:663`

## colony.2080.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2080.c&lang=en)

### 中文

拆解这个网络，但我们还是可以研究它。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:535`

### English

We dismantled the network, but we can still study it.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:664`

## colony.2085.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2085.name&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]的叛乱。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:536`

### English

Unrest on [Root.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:665`

## colony.2085.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2085.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

关于我们使用当地菌丝体密切监测菌落的项目的消息已经传播开来，但并没有受到好评。尽管我们尽最大努力来缓和局势，但当地民众普遍认为这是违背相互信任的原则的，并要求我们立即终止该项目。\n\n局势迅速升级，我们当地的执法人员不堪重负。如果我们选择继续这个项目，他们就需要我们的帮助来维持对殖民地的控制。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:537`

### English

Word about our project to use the local mycelium to closely monitor the colony has spread, and it wasn't well received. Despite our best efforts to defuse the situation, the local population widely considers this as a breach of trust and are demanding that we put an end to this project immediately.\n\nThe situation is escalating quickly, and our local enforcers are overwhelmed. They would need help to maintain control of the colony if we choose to go on with the project.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:666`

## colony.2085.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2085.a&lang=en)

### 中文

人民是对的。放弃。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:538`

### English

The population is right. Abort.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:667`

## colony.2085.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2085.b&lang=en)

### 中文

如果他们没有什么可隐瞒的，那么他们就没有什么可害怕的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:539`

### English

If they have nothing to hide, they have nothing to fear.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:668`

## colony.2090.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2090.name&lang=en)

### 中文

孢子顺风耳

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:540`

### English

The Spores Are Our Ears

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:669`

## colony.2090.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2090.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在 [Root.GetName] 上培养了菌丝体，并微调了本地计算设备以排除干扰。负责团队报告说，该网络现在正在密切监视殖民者。一些小罪犯已经受到了相应的制裁。\n\n不幸的是，在其他地方复制安装的尝试都失败了。很可能是一些我们尚未知晓的因素在起作用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:541`

### English

We have grown the mycelium on [Root.GetName], and fine-tuned the local computing equipment to sort through the interference. The responsible team reports that the network is now closely monitoring the colonists. Already, a number of petty criminals have been sanctioned appropriately.\n\nUnfortunately, attempts at replicating the installation in other places have met with failure. It is likely that some unknown factor is at play here.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:670`

## colony.2095.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2095.name&lang=en)

### 中文

真菌失败

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:542`

### English

Fungal Failure

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:671`

## colony.2095.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2095.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们为促进菌丝体在[Root.GetName]上传播所做的努力导致了更多“传统”蘑菇的生长。一些殖民者的事件表明它们是可食用的。此事件使整个网络在一段时间内失去平衡，但现在它似乎稳定且正常运行。然而，整体上并没有明显的改善。看起来这些蘑菇只是用来储存水的。它们对网络没有贡献，它们的破坏也不会对网络产生影响。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:543`

### English

Our efforts to stimulate the spread of the mycelium on [Root.GetName] have resulted in the growth of more 'traditional' mushrooms. An incident with some colonists has shown them to be edible. This event has thrown the whole network off balance for a while, but it now appears to be stabilized and functional. However, no significant improvement has been noticed overall. It looks like these mushrooms are merely storage for water. They do not contribute to the network and their destruction has no impact on it either.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:672`

## terra.15.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterra.15.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在完成§Y[From.GetName]§!的环境改造之后，我们的[Owner.GetTerraformerPlural]注意到了一个黄金的机遇。剩下的环境改造设备经过调整改造后可以作为这个行星的基础设施。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:547`

### English

After completing the terraforming of §Y[From.GetName]§!, our [Owner.GetTerraformerPlural] have spotted a golden opportunity. The remaining terraforming equipment can potentially be repurposed as infrastructure for the planet.

## terra.20.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterra.20.name&lang=en)

### 中文

改造的美景

### English

Engineered Beauty

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:687`

## terra.20.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterra.20.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

环境改造§Y[From.GetName]§!完工得比原先估计的要快，这给了我们的[Root.GetTerraformerPlural]一些额外的时间开展一些实验，实验结果十分引人注目。这颗行星的自然特质得到了强化，既有美学情操，又保证了实用主义。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:558`

### English

Terraforming on §Y[From.GetName]§! has finished faster than initially estimated, which has left our [Root.GetTerraformerPlural] with time to experiment, and the results are impressive. The planet's natural traits have been enhanced to be both aesthetically pleasing and utilitarian.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:688`

### English

## terra.25.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterra.25.name&lang=en)

### 中文

环境改造难题

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:561`

### English

Terraforming Complications

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:691`

## terra.25.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterra.25.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在§Y[From.GetName]§!上的漫长项目令人沮丧地结束了，只取得了一部分成功。在对最后的几处山谷进行地形变动时，生物质重调器发生了功能异常，导致我们创造出了一大滩§Y环境改造残余物§!。尽管对于殖民者不构成危险，它还是意味着这个星球目前会有一点狭窄。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:562`

### English

Our long project on §Y[From.GetName]§! has frustratingly ended in partial success. As the final valleys were undergoing terramorphing, a malfunction occurred in the biomass realigner, leading to the creation of large swaths of §YTerraforming Residue§!. While not dangerous to our colonists, it means that the planet is a bit more cramped for the time being.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:692`

## terra.25.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterra.25.a&lang=en)

### 中文

别让这件事再次发生。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:563`

### English

Don't let this happen again.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:693`

## terra.30.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterra.30.name&lang=en)

### 中文

以错为鉴

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:564`

### English

Learning From Mistakes

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:694`

## terra.30.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterra.30.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的[Owner.GetTerraformerPlural]现在清除了§Y[Root.GetName]§!上的最后一点§Y地形改造残余物§!。尽管清除工作十分痛苦、还黏黏糊糊，但这一切并非没有价值。对造成这一事故的原因和后果进行分析，使我们对地形学有了更加深远的理解。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:565`

### English

Our [Owner.GetTerraformerPlural] have now cleared the final §YTerraforming Residue§! on §Y[Root.GetName]§!. While the cleanup was painful and quite sticky, it was not all for naught. Analysis of the cause of this issue and the fallout has led to furthering our understanding of terralurgy.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:695`

## terra.30.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterra.30.a&lang=en)

### 中文

好歹还是有点好处的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:566`

### English

At least some good has come of it.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:696`

## terra.35.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterra.35.name&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetHomeWorldName]二号

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:567`

### English

[Root.GetHomeWorldName] II

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:697`

## terra.35.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterra.35.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们不仅成功地对§Y[From.GetName]§!进行了环境改造，我们还做得更加好了。在定稿阶段的最后，我们的[Root.GetTerraformerPlural]设法想让这个星球成为我们本土动物群的完美家园，创造一个同[Root.GetHomeWorldName]及其相似的[From.GetPlanetMoon]。我们的所有本土植物也会移植到那里去，只是地形会不太一样。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:568`

### English

We have not only succeeded in terraforming §Y[From.GetName]§!, but we have done something even better. During the final stretch of the process, our [Root.GetTerraformerPlural] managed to make it a perfect home for our local fauna, creating a [From.GetPlanetMoon] eerily similar to [Root.GetHomeWorldName]. All our local plant life is there but none of the familiar terrain.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:698`

## terra.35.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterra.35.a&lang=en)

### 中文

这个[From.GetPlanetMoon]应该作为[Root.GetHomeWorldName]二号被人所知

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:569`

### English

This [From.GetPlanetMoon] shall be known as [Root.GetHomeWorldName] II

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:699`

一次幸运的发展。

### English

A fortuitous development.

## terra.40.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterra.40.name&lang=en)

### 中文

行星策略

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:572`

### English

A Planetary Gambit

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:702`

## terra.40.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterra.40.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

正当§Y[From.GetName]§!的环境改造工程即将收尾之时，一个独特的情况发生了。我们的[Root.GetTerraformerPlural]发现，我们有可能将这个[From.GetPlanetMoon]变为潮汐锁定。这样的现象如果自然发生了，可能会对殖民地造成危害。然而，如果是在环境改造的过程中这么做，我们就能充分利用昼半球无穷无尽的太阳时长，产出极其丰富的能量。我们当然也要制定一个集中计划，将我们剩余的工业园区和住房布置在夜半球或晨昏线地区。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:573`

### English

A unique situation has occurred on §Y[From.GetName]§! just as the terraforming efforts are winding down. Our [Root.GetTerraformerPlural] have discovered that it's possible to tidally lock the [From.GetPlanetMoon]. This occurring naturally can be quite a detriment to a colony. However, doing it during the terraforming process could allow us to exploit the endless solar hours the dayside will receive and generate an obscene amount of energy. We would still have to make a concentrated effort to set up the rest of our industry and housing on the twilight and night side.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:703`

## terra.40.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterra.40.a&lang=en)

### 中文

让[From.GetPlanetMoon]再次转起来，这么做风险太大了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:574`

### English

Set the [From.GetPlanetMoon] spinning again, the risk is too great.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:704`

### English

### English

Unlocks a special §YColony Decision§! to unlock this [From.GetPlanetMoon]'s full potential.

## terra.45.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterra.45.name&lang=en)

### 中文

成功过头

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:577`

### English

Excess to Success

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:707`

## terra.45.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterra.45.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

随着§Y[From.GetName]§!环境改造完工，我们发现这一工程使我们处在了一个始料未及的情况。我们实际上需要的资源，并没有我们最初立项的时候预计完成项目所需要的那么多，现在我们有机会为这个[From.GetPlanetMoon]增添一项额外的特征。我们的[Root.GetTerraformerPlural]呈递给了我们三个不同的选项。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:578`

### English

As the terraforming of §Y[From.GetName]§! is settling down, we discover that the project has put us in an unanticipated situation. We did not need as many resources as we initially set aside to finish this project, and now we have the chance to add-on one additional feature to the [From.GetPlanetMoon]. Our [Root.GetTerraformerPlural] have presented us with three different options.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:708`

## terra.45.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterra.45.a&lang=en)

我们可以使用更多的$district_generator_plural$。

### English

We could use more $district_generator_plural$.

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterra.45.c&lang=en)

### English

## terra.50.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterra.50.name&lang=en)

### 中文

真一

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:582`

### English

Oneness

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:712`

## terra.50.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterra.50.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在将§Y[From.GetName]§!转变为[This.GetAAnPlanetClass][This.GetClassAdj]星球的过程中，[Root.GetTerraformerPlural]筹划着不仅将当地动物群连接到主脑，还想将这个星球本身也连接到主脑，尽管有点担心暴露在环境改造气体中有可能诱发突变。蜂群现在有机会在更加真实的感知下，变得前所未有地完整。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:583`

### English

While turning §Y[From.GetName]§! into [This.GetAAnPlanetClass] [This.GetClassAdj] world, the [Root.GetTerraformerPlural] managed to link not only the local fauna to the mind but, through freak mutations when exposed to the terraforming gases, link the mind to the world itself. The collective now has the opportunity to become whole in a truer sense than before.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:713`

## terra.50.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterra.50.a&lang=en)

### 中文

是的，这是主脑的意志。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:584`

### English

Yes, the mind wills it.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:714`

## terra.50.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterra.50.b&lang=en)

### 中文

不，这不是这个星球的意图。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:585`

### English

No, this is not the intention for this world.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:715`

## terra.55.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterra.55.name&lang=en)

### 中文

完美的组织

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:586`

### English

Perfect Organization

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:716`

## terra.55.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterra.55.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对于蜂群来说，超越自然不是什么新鲜事。在对§Y[From.GetName]§!进行地形改造的过程中，每一座山峦都经过了考量，每一片动物群的放置都经过了战略推演。这个[From.GetPlanetMoon]现在变成了一处组织完美的后勤天堂，方便我们的[Root.GetUnitDronePlural]为蜂群整体的建设添砖加瓦。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:587`

### English

Surpassing nature is not something new for this collective. During the terraforming process of §Y[From.GetName]§!, every hill was considered, and every piece of fauna was strategically placed. The [From.GetPlanetMoon] has now ended up being a perfectly organized logistical paradise for our [Root.GetUnitDronePlural] to produce for the collective.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:717`

## terra.60.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterra.60.name&lang=en)

### 中文

出土[GetPreFTLUpperPlural]

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:588`

### English

Unearthed [GetPreFTLUpperPlural]

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:718`

## terra.60.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterra.60.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在为§Y[From.GetName]§!土地施肥和导入大气的过程中，我们的[Root.GetTerraformerPlural]有个意外的发现。在[From.GetName]的地表以下存在一个地下外星文明。应该是由于[From.GetPlanetMoon]的矿物密度导致此前我们的扫描仪并未发现他们。\n这些[GetPreFTLLowerPlural]似乎完全没有意识到行星的地表已经改变，这让我们有了决定如何与他们接触的主动权。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:589`

### English

While enriching the soil and adding an atmosphere to §Y[From.GetName]§!, our [Root.GetTerraformerPlural] have made an unexpected find. Beneath the surface of [From.GetName], there exists a civilization of subterranean aliens. It must have been because of the [From.GetPlanetMoon]'s mineral density that our scanners didn't detect them before.\nThe [GetPreFTLLowerPlural] seem unaware that their surface has changed, giving us a chance to do what we will with them.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:719`

## terra.60.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterra.60.a&lang=en)

真是有趣。

### English

How quaint.

就是说地底下有害虫咯。

### English

So the pests scurry underground now.

### English

It's good to see that the universe is not devoid of intelligent life.

## terra.65.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterra.65.name&lang=en)

### 中文

有所帮助的瑕疵

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:594`

### English

A Helpful Blemish

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:724`

## terra.65.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterra.65.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的[Root.GetTerraformerPlural]一开始以为某块地只是§Y[From.GetName]§!上的一个臭气袋子，结果没想到那里竟是一块福地！它喷发出来的有毒气体实际上是未经精炼的§Y异星天然气§!。有了这处全新的自然资源，为这个[From.GetPlanetMoon]引入大气层所需的能源巨幅下降了，我们还可以在接下来的很多年采集这里的天然气！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:595`

### English

What our [Root.GetTerraformerPlural] first took for a nasty gas pocket on §Y[From.GetName]§! has turned out to be a blessing! The toxic fumes it was releasing are an unrefined version of §YExotic Gas§!. With this new natural resource, the energy required to introduce an atmosphere to the [From.GetPlanetMoon] has drastically decreased, and we can harvest the gas for years to come!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:725`

## terra.65.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterra.65.a&lang=en)

### 中文

棒极了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:596`

### English

Excellent.

## terra.70.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterra.70.name&lang=en)

### 中文

未曾预料的突变

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:597`

### English

Unexpected Mutations

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:727`

## terra.70.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterra.70.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的[Root.GetTerraformerPlural]就§Y[From.GetName]§!上的情况即带来了好消息，也带来了坏消息。好消息是环境改造进程十分的顺利，我们有了一处美轮美奂的盖亚星球。坏消息是，所有住在这个[From.GetPlanetMoon]上的人都暴露在了环境改造气体中，已经不能适应非盖亚星球的环境了。这些“囿于盖亚”的居民在盖亚星球上生活时，展现出了惊人的生产力提升。相比于没有受到环境改造气体影响、在改造之后才过去的移民，这些居民的表现要好得不知道到哪里去了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:598`

### English

Our [Root.GetTerraformerPlural] have good news and bad news for us about §Y[From.GetName]§!. The good news is that the terraforming process went swimmingly, and we have a beautiful Gaia World. The bad news is that everyone living on the [From.GetPlanetMoon] was exposed to the terraforming gases and now can't live on non-Gaia Worlds. These Gaia-trapped inhabitants have surprisingly been showing an increase in their productivity while on the Gaia World. Far better than workers that weren't affected by the gas that migrated there later.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:728`

## terra.70.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterra.70.a&lang=en)

### 中文

至少他们看上去没有感到不舒服。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:599`

### English

At least they don't seem to be uncomfortable.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:729`

## terra.75.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterra.75.name&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]此处便是天堂

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:600`

### English

Paradise is a Place on [From.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:730`

## terra.75.desc_regular

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterra.75.desc_regular&lang=en)

### 中文

在我们派往§Y[From.GetName]§!的[Root.GetTerraformerPlural]之中，似乎有部分人是某个[Root.GetHomeWorldName]上古老宗派的追随者。这些[Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]相信寰宇之中有一处留给我们人民的天堂，结果他们选择不去寻找，而是亲自去创造这处天堂。[From.GetName]现在被转变成了一处被赐福的[From.GetPlanetMoon]，宛如从我们古老历史的宗卷中显现出来的图景。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:601`

### English

Amongst our [Root.GetTerraformerPlural] assigned to §Y[From.GetName]§!, there seem to have been followers of an ancient sect from [Root.GetHomeWorldName] among them. These [Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural] believe that there is a paradise for our people, and instead of looking for it, they have now created it. [From.GetName] has now been turned into a [From.GetPlanetMoon] of the blessed, looking like an illustration manifested from the pages of our ancient history.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:731`

## terra.75.desc_spirit

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterra.75.desc_spirit&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的环境改造工作者完成了前所未有的功绩！阅尽整个[Root.GetName]的历史，总有一桩神话或传说流传下来，讲述许诺给我们人民的赐福之地——一处天堂。当我们驶向繁星却无处寻觅这处福地时，我们的[Root.GetTerraformerPlural]启动了一项计划，要亲自将这个传说化为现实。现在，§Y[From.GetName]§!的地平线在我们面前向远方延伸，人人肃然起敬，心知我们亲手缔造了属于自己的天堂。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:602`

### English

Our terraformers have outdone any of their previous work! Throughout [Root.GetName], history there have always been myths and legends about a blessed land for our people, a paradise. When we set out to the stars and didn't find the Land of Bliss, a project to make it a reality was started by our [Root.GetTerraformerPlural]. Now the awe-inspiring horizons of §Y[From.GetName]§! stretch before us, and we know that we have made our own paradise.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:732`

## terra.75.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterra.75.a&lang=en)

### 中文

美不胜收。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:603`

### English

It's beautiful.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:733`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterra.75.b&lang=en)

它只是个普通的盖亚星球吧？

### English

It's just a normal Gaia World?

## terra.80.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterra.80.name&lang=en)

### 中文

辐射植物

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:605`

### English

Radioactive Flora

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:735`

## terra.80.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterra.80.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在清除§Y[From.GetName]§!大气层和土壤中的辐射的过程中，我们的[Root.GetTerraformerPlural]不得不求助于我们的异星生物学家。他们发现了一个物种，依靠辐射繁衍生息，而现在正在枯萎。更令人惊奇的是，这个物种看上去还处于未开智状态，但将将就要进化出智能了。现在，随着最后一处泥土即将冲刷干净，我们必须决定如何处置这个新生命。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:606`

### English

While cleaning out the radiation in §Y[From.GetName]'s§! atmosphere and soil, our [Root.GetTerraformerPlural] had to call in our xenobiologists. They had found a species that seemed to thrive off the radiation and was now wilting. Even more curious, this species appears to be presapient and on the cusp of achieving sentience. Now, as the final soil scrubbers are approaching, we have to decide what to do with this new life.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:736`

## terra.80.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterra.80.a&lang=en)

我们毁掉了它们的栖息地，现在我们应当保护它们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:607`

### English

We have destroyed their habitat, now we have to preserve them.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:737`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterra.85.name&lang=en)

辐光四溢的招待会

### English

## terra.85.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterra.85.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在花费数年调整这些我们在§Y[FromFromFrom.GetName]§!上发现的辐射合成植物，并且使其适应环境之后，我们现在可以将他们纳入我们的社会了。尽管这个物种还出人意料地年轻，但是他们看上去非常擅长收集能量。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:616`

### English

Having spent years acclimating and adjusting the radiotrophic plants found on §Y[FromFromFrom.GetName]§!, we now are ready to allow them into our society. While an incredibly young species, they seem to excel in harvesting energy.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:746`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterra.85.a&lang=en)

## terra.90.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterra.90.name&lang=en)

### 中文

武器贮藏室

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:619`

### English

Weapons Cache

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:749`

## terra.90.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterra.90.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

恢复一个死寂星球是一项艰苦的工作，而且十分单调重复：隔绝一块区域，清理所有的卵石，替换土地，净化任何残留的水源，添加新的动物。随着§Y[From.GetName]§!的环境改造进程逐渐扫尾，我们的[Root.GetTerraformerPlural]发现了一个规模可观的地下金属容器，根据其防御工事，我们假定这是一个武器库。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:620`

### English

Restoring a Tomb World is always hard work and often very repetitive: section off an area, clear out any rubble, replace the soil, purify any remaining water, and add new fauna. As the terraforming process on §Y[From.GetName]§! was winding to a close, our [Root.GetTerraformerPlural] discovered a sizeable underground metal container, and based on its fortifications, it is assumed that this is a weapons cache.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:750`

## terra.90.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterra.90.a&lang=en)

### 中文

令人好奇。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:621`

### English

Curious.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:751`

## terra.95.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterra.95.name&lang=en)

### 中文

揭开尘封的过去

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:622`

### English

Bringing the Past to Light

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:752`

## terra.95.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterra.95.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.Planet.GetName]的贮藏室并不好挖掘，因为它的发现地点被深深地埋在了城市废墟之下。又因为不管容器里面储藏着什么，我们都被要求谨慎行事避免将其误触发，发掘进度被进一步地拖慢了。现在这栋建筑矗立在我们的面前，等待我们随时开始调查。复杂的外星文字被刻在贮藏室的两侧，或许能提示我们里面有什么内容物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:623`

### English

The cache on [From.Planet.GetName] was not easy to unearth, as it was buried deep below the ruined city in which it was discovered. The excavation effort was further slowed by the caution required to avoid setting off whatever is stored in the container. Now the structure stands before us and is ready to for investigation. Complex alien script is carved into the sides of the cache that might hint at its content.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:753`

## terra.100.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterra.100.name&lang=en)

### 中文

军械库

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:624`

### English

The Armory

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:754`

## terra.100.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterra.100.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的[Owner.GetLinguists]完成了翻译外星人的铭文，发现贮藏室外刻着一个警示。§Y“在战争时期，任何情况下，都不允许任何政府进入这座军械库！”§!\n\n无论这里面储存着什么武器，那对于一整个文明都具有足够的毁灭性，能够将他们化成灰。我们剩下的工作就是小心地打开这座军械库，然后拿到我们的奖励。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:625`

### English

Our [Owner.GetLinguists] have finished translating the alien inscriptions and have discovered a warning engraved into the cache. §Y"Do not under any circumstances allow any government to have access to this armory during wartime!"§!\n\nWhatever weapon was stored here was too destructive even for a civilization that was capable of reducing themselves to ash. All that is left is to carefully open the armory and claim our prize.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:755`

翻译错误

### English

Mistranslations

## terra.105.desc.generic

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterra.105.desc.generic&lang=en)

### 中文

似乎是我们的[Owner.GetLinguists]将“图书馆”错误翻译成了“军械库”。我们一直对“权力的来源”一词存在错误的理解，直到我们打开了贮藏室，看到了更多的外星文本。§Y“如果你在读这一段，那说明我们从战争中存活了下来，现在准备开始重建。当我们星球的局势变得愈发恶化的时候，我花费了许多个月份创建这座图书馆，我的朋友和我忠实的同事一直陪伴左右。但既然你在读这一段，那说明我们中的某人幸存了下来，因此请好好接受这一份遗产。我和我的朋友收集到的，所有有关我们人民文化的成果，都集中在这里。你们第一个看见这个的人，将成为我最亲爱的朋友的象征。”§!\n\n在饰板的下面，是一个双足哺乳动物的遗体，以及一个四足长鼻子动物的金属雕像。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:627`

### English

It seems like our [Owner.GetLinguists] mistranslated the word 'library' as 'armory'. We had assumed the incorrect meaning of 'source of power', which was only apparent after opening the cache and being greeted by further xeno script. §Y"If you are reading this, that means that we survived the war and are now ready to rebuild. I spent many long months creating this library while our world's situation worsened, accompanied by my friend and faithful companion. But since you are reading this, that means that someone did survive, so please take part in your heritage. All the cultural works of our people my friend and I could gather are collected here, and let the first you see be a representation of my dearest friend."§!\n\nThe remains of a bipedal mammal and a metal statue of a four-legged long-snouted animal stands under the plaque.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:757`

## terra.105.desc.earth

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterra.105.desc.earth&lang=en)

### 中文

似乎是我们的[Owner.GetLinguists]将“图书馆”错误翻译成了“军械库”。我们一直对“权力的来源”一词存在错误的理解，直到我们打开了贮藏室，看到了更多的外星文本。§Y“如果你在读这一段，那说明我们从战争中存活了下来，现在准备开始重建。当我们星球的局势变得愈发恶化的时候，我花费了许多个月份创建这座图书馆，我的朋友和我忠实的同事——沃尔特——一直陪伴左右。但既然你在读这一段，那说明我们中的某人幸存了下来，因此请好好接受这一份遗产。我和沃尔特收集到的，所有有关我们人民文化的成果，都集中在这里。你们第一个看见这个的人，将成为我最亲爱的朋友的象征。”§!\n\n在饰板的下面，是一个双足哺乳动物的遗体，以及一个四足长鼻子动物的金属雕像。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:628`

### English

It seems like our [Owner.GetLinguists] mistranslated the word 'library' as 'armory'. We had assumed the incorrect meaning of 'source of power', which was only apparent after opening the cache and being greeted by further xeno script. §Y"If you are reading this, that means that we survived the war and are now ready to rebuild. I spent many long months creating this library while our world's situation worsened, accompanied by my friend and faithful companion Walter. But since you are reading this, that means that someone did survive, so please take part in your heritage. All the cultural works of our people Walter and I could gather are collected here, and let the first you see be a representation of my dearest friend."§!\n\nThe remains of a bipedal mammal and a metal statue of a four-legged long-snouted animal stands under the plaque.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:758`

### English

## anomaly.6791.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6791.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们对$NAME_Hillos$残骸场的扫描已经完成。其化学结构同时具备生物和非生物性质，我们从未见过类似的东西。\n\n这片残骸与在此恒星系中巡逻的奇怪船只间有何关联仍然模糊不清。船上没有生命迹象。事实上，我们探测不到任何种类的能量特征。姑且来说就是，我们在幽灵船面前栽跟头了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:648`

### English

Our scans of the debris field in $NAME_Hillos$ are complete. Possessing both organic and abiogenic properties, the chemical structure is unlike anything we've ever seen.\n\nThe connection between the debris and the strange vessel patrolling the system remains unclear. There are no life-signs aboard. In fact, we can detect no power signatures of any kind. For lack of a better descriptor, we have stumbled on a ghost ship.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:784`

### 中文

$NAME_Hillos_ship$的幼体

### English

$NAME_Hillos_ship$'s Baby

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:787`

## anomaly.6792.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6792.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的科研团队得出结论，认为在其命令日志中被称作“$NAME_Hillos_ship$”的、被遗弃的舰船曾在$NAME_Hillos$恒星系中进行实验，实验目的是创造合成人生命。反物质约束装置泄露导致舰上人员连同着新出生的“幼儿”一道丧生。但是，在舰船各系统中探测到的少量灵能表明，那个合成生命体的“鬼魂”在事故发生前几秒钟掌控了船只。\n\n可以想象，新生的意识体辗转花费了数个世纪，徒劳地寻找自己的母亲。不幸的是，此时此刻我们没有办法与该实体交流，也无法帮助其取得安宁。目前来看，$NAME_Hillos$恒星系中大概注定要继续有幽灵船出没了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:651`

### English

Our science team has concluded that the abandoned ship, referred to as '$NAME_Hillos_ship$' in its command logs, was conducting an experiment in the $NAME_Hillos$ system, the goal of which was the creation of synthetic life. An antimatter containment breach resulted in the death of the ship's crew, as well as the newborn 'infant.' However, low-level psionic energy detected throughout the ship's systems suggest that the synthetic lifeform's 'ghost' took possession of the vessel seconds before the accident.\n\nIt is conceivable that the nascent consciousness has spent the intervening centuries in a vain search for its mother. Unfortunately, at this time we have no way of communicating with the entity, nor of helping it to find rest. For now, the haunting of the $NAME_Hillos$ system seems destined to continue.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:788`

## anomaly.6792.alt.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6792.alt.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的科研团队得出结论认为，在其命令日志中被称作“$NAME_Hillos_ship$”的、被遗弃的舰船曾在$NAME_Hillos$恒星系中进行实验，实验目的是创造合成人生命。反物质约束装置泄露导致舰上人员连同着新出生的“幼儿”一道丧生。在舰船的各系统中，我们探测到了灵能的存在。感觉这里还发生了一些别的事情，一些比单纯的事故更诡异的事情。\n\n很有可能这个合成生命体具有某种潜在灵能力量，它的“鬼魂”在事故发生前几秒钟掌控了船只。如果真是如此，那么这个新生的意识体可能辗转花费了数个世纪，徒劳地寻找自己的母亲。不幸的是，此时此刻我们没有办法与该实体交流，也无法帮助其取得安宁。目前来看，$NAME_Hillos$恒星系中大概注定要继续有幽灵船出没了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:652`

### English

Our science team has concluded that the abandoned ship, referred to as '$NAME_Hillos_ship$' in its command logs, was conducting an experiment in the $NAME_Hillos$ system, the goal of which was the creation of synthetic life. An antimatter containment breach resulted in the death of the ship's crew, as well as the newborn 'infant.' Throughout the ship's systems, we have also detected the presence of psionic energy. There is the sense that something more occurred here; something stranger than a simple malfunction.\n\nIt is possible that the synthetic lifeform itself possessed a kind of latent psionic power, and that its 'ghost' took possession of the vessel seconds before the accident. If so, this nascent consciousness may have spent the intervening centuries in a vain search for its mother. Unfortunately, we are unable to make contact at this time. For now, the haunting of the $NAME_Hillos$ system seems destined to continue.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:789`

## anomaly.6792.alt_hive_robot.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6792.alt_hive_robot.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们对$NAME_Hillos$恒星系内的舰船的评估已经完成。据其内部指挥日志识别为“$NAME_Hillos_ship$”，由修复出的舰船记录可以得知其任务目标是创造合成生命体。一个反物质容器的破裂导致了船上的船员和实验“婴儿”的终结。\n\n我们的[Owner.GetTelepathPlural]探测到了游荡在舰船的系统中的痕量灵能能量，我们随之发现那个裂口并不只是简简单单的故障产物。\n\n这些能量信号表明，合成生命形式具有某种潜在的灵能力，并且这些力量在故障之前很可能已经显现过了，这一切都导致一个新生的意识体在寻找母性的输入。由于没有更多的数据，我们无法证实这一理论，仍需要进一步的研究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:653`

### English

Our assessment of the vessel in the $NAME_Hillos$ system is complete. Identified from its command logs as '$NAME_Hillos_ship$', recovered ship records track its mission goal of creating synthetic life. An antimatter containment breach resulted in the termination of the ship's crew as well as the experimental 'infant'.\n\nOur [Owner.GetTelepathPlural] have detected traces of psionic energy lingering throughout the ship's systems, leading us to the conclusion the breach was a result of more than a simple malfunction.\n\nThese energy signatures indicate the synthetic lifeform possessed latent psionic power, and that these powers likely manifested before the accident. Resulting in a nascent consciousness searching for a maternal input. Without more data we are unable to confirm this theory, further study will be required.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:790`

## anomaly.6792.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6792.a&lang=en)

### 中文

悲伤的命运。

A sad fate.

灵能呜咽

### English

Psionic Chatter

## anomaly.6793.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6793.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们探测到有什么东西从$NAME_Hillos$恒星系中传出。那艘叫$NAME_Hillos_ship$的幽灵船使用一种只能说是“灵能频段”的东西在不断地发出悲伤的哭嚎。显然，自从我们第一次遇到这艘船和被困在船上的新生意识以来它就一直如此了。似乎我们最近的灵能飞升是我们能听到这个孩子哭泣的缘由。通过进一步研究，我们也许能够与之联系。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:657`

### English

We have detected something unusual emanating from the $NAME_Hillos$ system. On what can only be described as a 'psionic frequency', the so-called ghost ship $NAME_Hillos_ship$ has been issuing a plaintive and irregular wail. Apparently, this has been going on since our first encounter with the vessel and the nascent consciousness trapped onboard. It seems likely that our recent psionic transcendence is the reason we're now able to perceive the child's cries. With further study, we may even be able to answer them.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:795`

## anomaly.6793.desc.hive_robot

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6793.desc.hive_robot&lang=en)

### 中文

我们探测到有什么东西从$NAME_Hillos$恒星系中传出。我们最近调谐的[Root.GetTelepathPlural]已经开始接收来$NAME_Hillos_ship$舰船的信号。这些通信与生物意义上的年轻人有相似之处，并且显示出自我们最初相遇以来已经传播的迹象，我们最近的灵能发展使最终探测到它们成为可能。\n\n通过进一步研究，我们也许能够与进行更多交流。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:658`

### English

We have detected something unusual emanating from the $NAME_Hillos$ system. Our recently attuned [Root.GetTelepathPlural] have begun to receive signals emanating from the vessel $NAME_Hillos_ship$. These communications share similarities with those of biological young, and show signs of having been broadcast since our initial encounter, with our recent psionic developments making it possible to finally detect them.\n\nWith further study, we may be able to communicate with the entity further.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:796`

## anomaly.6794.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6794.name&lang=en)

### 中文

看看是谁在说话

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:659`

### English

Look Who's Talking

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:798`

## anomaly.6794.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6794.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在经过几个月痛苦的研究后，我们的灵能使终于成功地与占据幽灵船$NAME_Hillos_ship$的那个意识取得联系。尽管它的词汇量非常稀少，但这个合成生命体可以用感受和印象进行表达。这个“孩子”的孤独显而易见，这也可以从它与其他灵魂接触时的欣喜中看出来。它似乎也喜欢我们的科研，在船旁边嬉戏地滑过$NAME_Hillos$星系，就像一只水生鲸类动物的幼崽围绕着它的母亲一样。至少，“闹鬼”的$NAME_Hillos$恒星系最终迎来了令人愉快的结局。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:660`

### English

After months of painful research, our telepaths have succeeded in making contact with the consciousness in possession of the ghost ship $NAME_Hillos_ship$. Possessing only a rudimentary vocabulary, the synthetic lifeform was nevertheless able to express itself through feelings and impressions. The 'child's' loneliness was palpable, as was its joy in finally making contact with other minds. It also seems to have taken a liking to our science vessel, playfully gliding throughout the $NAME_Hillos$ system alongside the ship, much like the calf of a water-born cetacean around its mother. At last, the haunting of the $NAME_Hillos$ system has come to a happy end.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:799`

## anomaly.6794.desc.hive_robot

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6794.desc.hive_robot&lang=en)

### 中文

在数月的高强度研究之后，我们的[Root.GetTelepathPlural]终于能和这个占据着幽灵船$NAME_Hillos_ship$的意识沟通了。尽管它的词汇量非常稀少，但这个合成生命体可以用感受和印象进行表达。\n\n这个“孩子”的孤独显而易见，这也可以从它与其他灵魂接触时的欣喜中看出来。而且，它似乎也喜欢我们的科研，在船旁边嬉戏地滑过$NAME_Hillos$星系，就像一只水生鲸类动物的幼崽围绕着它的母亲一样。至少，诡异的$NAME_Hillos$恒星系最终迎来了令人愉快的结局。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:661`

### English

After months of extensive research, our [Root.GetTelepathPlural] have successfully made contact with the consciousness in possession of the ghost ship $NAME_Hillos_ship$. Possessing only a rudimentary vocabulary, the synthetic lifeform was nevertheless able to express itself through feelings and impressions.\n\nThe 'child's' loneliness was palpable, as was its joy in finally making contact with other minds. Additionally, it seems to have taken a liking to our science vessel, playfully gliding throughout the $NAME_Hillos$ system alongside the ship, much like the calf of a water-born cetacean around its mother. The anomalous behavior it caused in the $NAME_Hillos$ system has ceased.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:800`

### English

### English

A curious entity.

## anomaly.6795.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6795.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们收到了我们的一位首席灵能者的消息。现任，在研究了$NAME_Hillos$星系中的存在之后，他们承担起了尝试与占据着幽灵船$NAME_Hillos_ship$的灵能意识沟通的重任。在几个月艰苦的尝试后，他们终于成功地建立了联系。\n\n这个“孩子”的孤独显而易见，这也可以从它与其他与之相似的灵魂接触时的欣喜中看出来。它现在调皮地滑过$NAME_Hillos$星系，用灵能向每一位愿意聆听它声音的个体倾诉。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:664`

### English

We have received word from one of our foremost psychics. Apparently, after learning of the presence in the $NAME_Hillos$ system, they took it upon themselves to make contact with the psionic consciousness in possession of the ghost ship $NAME_Hillos_ship$. After months of painful trials, they have finally succeeded in making contact.\n\nPossessing only a rudimentary vocabulary, the synthetic lifeform was nevertheless able to express itself through feelings and impressions. The 'child's' loneliness was palpable, as was its joy in finally making contact with a mind like itself. It is now playfully gliding throughout the $NAME_Hillos$ system, chattering psionically away to anyone who cares to listen.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:804`

## anomaly.6795.desc.hive_robot

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6795.desc.hive_robot&lang=en)

### 中文

在数月的后续研究后，我们的[Root.GetTelepathPlural]成功地接触了占据着$NAME_Hillos_ship$舰船的灵能意识。\n\n尽管它的词汇量非常稀少，但这个合成生命体在与其他灵能意识接触时，可以表达出我们通常所认为的“快乐”。由于其目前没有表现出任何可见的威胁，我们就继续留它在$NAME_Hillos$恒星系里的那台装置内了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:665`

### English

After months of continued study, our [Root.GetTelepathPlural] have successfully made contact with the psionic consciousness in possession of the ship $NAME_Hillos_ship$.\n\nDespite possessing only a rudimentary vocabulary, the synthetic lifeform was able to express what we commonly recognize as 'joy' at making contact with another psionic mind. As it currently poses no discernable threat, we have left it to its own devices in the $NAME_Hillos$ system.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:805`

## site_transformation_intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_transformation_intro&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的科学家在[From.Planet.GetName]发现了一些奇怪的东西。作为一颗热带星球，其表面大片区域被茂密的丛林覆盖：巨大的、寿达千年的古树从林冠中突兀而起，它们枝叶茂密的树冠俯瞰着其他陆上景观。\n\n初看起来，这些参天大树的分布似乎杂乱无章。然而，更详细的分析表明这些树的分布具有数学精准性，也许是由某个消失已久的文明所种植的。不仅如此，团队领袖相信，树木的分布方式可能包含着一组坐标，指向丛林深处某个未被探索的区域。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:670`

### English

Our scientists have discovered something unusual on [From.Planet.GetName]. A tropical world, much of the planet's surface is covered with dense jungle: massive, thousand year old trees erupt from the canopy, their leafy crowns towering over the landscape.\n\nAt first glance, the distribution of these giants appears random. However, further analysis indicates that the trees are arranged with mathematical precision, and it seems likely they were planted by some long-vanished civilization. What's more, the team leader believes the distribution pattern may comprise a set of coordinates, one that points to an unexplored location deep within the jungle.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:812`

## ancrel.15010.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.15010.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的科学家在[From.Planet.GetName]发现了一些奇怪的东西。作为一颗热带星球，其表面大片区域被茂密的丛林覆盖：巨大的、寿达千年的古树从林冠中突兀而起，它们枝叶茂密的树冠俯瞰着其他陆上景观。\n\n初看起来，这些参天大树的分布似乎杂乱无章。然而，更详细的分析表明这些树的分布具有数学精准性，也许是由某个消失已久的文明所种植的。不仅如此，团队领袖相信，树木的分布方式可能包含着一组坐标，指向丛林深处某个未被探索的区域。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:672`

### English

Our scientists have discovered something unusual on [From.Planet.GetName]. A tropical world, much of the planet's surface is covered with dense jungle: massive, thousand year old trees erupt from the canopy, their leafy crowns towering over the landscape.\n\nAt first glance, the distribution of these giants appears random. However, further analysis indicates that the trees are arranged with mathematical precision, and it seems likely they were planted by some long-vanished civilization. What's more, the team leader believes the distribution pattern may comprise a set of coordinates, one that points to an unexplored location deep within the jungle.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:815`

## ancrel.15000.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.15000.name&lang=en)

### 中文

欢迎来到丛林

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:673`

### English

Welcome to the Jungle

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:817`

## ancrel.15000.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.15000.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

由于[From.Planet.GetName]的林冠非常茂密难以穿透，从空中对穿越丛林便捷路线的搜索几乎一无所获。林冠之下生机勃勃：有无附肢的掠食者、耷拉脸的灵长类和成群的吸血昆虫。森林的土层非常肥沃，而恶臭的水洼让探索团队难以前行。不仅如此，还有某些东西，不知道是茂密的植被还是未知的电磁干扰，使仪器设备经常发生故障。\n\n项目领队相信他们还是能够在树木间找到通路，只是进程可能会非常缓慢。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:674`

### English

Given the impenetrable canopy on [From.Planet.GetName], aerial surveys have proven useless in determining a convenient route through the jungle. Below, the undergrowth teems with life: limbless predators; droop-faced primates; swarms of hematophagous insects. The forest floor is loamy, and pools of fetid water have caused the team significant delays. What's more, something - either the density of the foliage or an as yet undefined electrical interference - has prevented much of their equipment from functioning properly.\n\nThe project's lead believes they should still be able to forge a path through the trees, though progress is likely to be slow.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:818`

## ancrel.15000.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.15000.a&lang=en)

### English

## ancrel.15001.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.15001.name&lang=en)

### 中文

灵能质变

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:676`

### English

Psionic Metamorphosis

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:821`

## ancrel.15001.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.15001.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对土壤的分析显示，[From.Planet.GetName]在过去的一百个公转周期内进行过一场令人惊奇的气候转变。和今天植被茂密的景象大不相同，这颗星球曾经荒芜又遍地岩石。项目领队相信这些巨大的植物本身就是原因：共振显像扫描发现，它们的树汁中存在灵能。这些能量从地下水中被植被巨大的根系抽出，并经由类似光合作用的过程逐渐散布到整个行星的大气中。这很可能就是团队仪器设备出故障的原因。\n\n有趣的是，月光似乎能促进灵能扩散。在晴朗的夜晚，甚至空气看起来都在震颤，这个现象在团队接近目的地的过程中越来越强烈。这一景象我们前所未见。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:677`

### English

A soil analysis reveals that [From.Planet.GetName] has undergone a startling climatological transformation within its last 100 cycles. Far from the lush jungle world that exists today, the planet was once rocky and barren. The project lead believes the great trees themselves are responsible: a resonance imaging scan detected the presence of psionic energy within their sap. Pulled from groundwater by the trees' vast root systems, this energy is eventually diffused throughout the planet's atmosphere in a process akin to photosynthesis. It is highly likely that this is the cause of the team's malfunctioning equipment.\n\nInterestingly, the presence of moonlight appears to accelerate the psionic diffusion. On clear nights, the air itself seems to vibrate, a phenomenon that has only grown in intensity as the team nears its destination. This is unlike anything we've ever seen before.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:822`

## ancrel.15001.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.15001.a&lang=en)

### 中文

很迷人。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:678`

### English

Fascinating.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:823`

## ancrel.15002.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.15002.name&lang=en)

### 中文

洞穴

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:679`

### English

The Cave

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:825`

## ancrel.15002.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.15002.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在终于抵达他们寻找的坐标后，考古团队发现了一条隐藏在林冠下蜿蜒崎岖的河谷。河水从圆环形的洞口奔流而出，显然，这条河一定发源自某个庞大的地下泉。一条数千年前在岩壁上开凿出来的摇摇欲坠的阶梯将团队带向了一个地下隧道。\n\n洞穴的墙壁和顶上密密麻麻地涂写着一些潦草的象形文字，其中最引人注目的是一个圆形的类人脸的符号。我们的翻译算法成功地解码了许多这类符号，其中这一符号指代这颗星球的卫星。\n\n在洞穴中，灵能的存在已经爆表了。不幸的是，道路的尽头被一处数世纪历史的塌方所封堵，只要稍给一些时间，探险队应该可以设法通过。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:680`

### English

At last reaching their tracked coordinates, the archaeology team discovered a winding river valley hidden beneath the canopy. Rushing forth from a circular cave-mouth, the river apparently stems from some massive, underground spring. A precarious stairway, carved from the rocks several thousand years ago, delivered the team to a subterranean tunnel.\n\nThe cave's walls and ceiling are covered in a compact scrawl of ideographs, the most prominent of which is a round, humanoid face. Our translation algorithms have succeeded in decoding much of the symbology, and this particular image likely represents the planet's moon.\n\nWithin the cave, the presence of psionic energy is off the charts. Unfortunately, the path ahead was blocked by a cave-in several centuries ago, but given time, the expedition should be able to find a way through.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:826`

## ancrel.15002.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.15002.a&lang=en)

### 中文

开挖！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:681`

### English

Get digging!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:827`

## ancrel.15003.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.15003.name&lang=en)

### 中文

石笋支柱

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:682`

### English

The Pillar

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:829`

## ancrel.15003.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.15003.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.Planet.GetName]上的科学团队发来了一封喜报！成功进入洞穴下层后，他们最终抵达了一个开阔的、半球形的石室。在这里，河道围绕着一座小小的岛屿流淌，小岛的中央是一块稀有晶体构成的石笋。这座石笋是由数百万年的水滴逐渐形成的，而它似乎就是整颗行星奇异灵能的源头。研究团队发现了宗教仪式的痕迹，还有若干仪式性的人造小祭器散布在石笋支柱的周围。除此之外，多相位扫描还在支柱的地基中发现了一个圆形的物体。一旦挖掘出来，这个物体有很大希望能够提供一些关于这个神秘地方的本质的线索。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:683`

### English

A note of triumph from our science team on [From.Planet.GetName]! After successfully reaching the lower levels of the cave, they at last arrived in a wide, hemispherical chamber. There, the river parts around a small island, at the center of which is a stalagmite of rare crystals. Formed drop-by-drop over the course of several millennia, the pillar appears to be the source of the planet's strange, psionic energy. The team has discovered evidence of religious ritual, as well as several small artifacts of ceremonial significance scattered around the pillar. What's more, a multiphasic scan revealed a round object embedded in the pillar's base. Hopes are high that, once removed, this artifact will shed some light on the true nature of this mysterious place.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:830`

## ancrel.15003.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.15003.a&lang=en)

### 中文

谨慎行事。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:684`

### English

Proceed with caution.

我们必须揭秘这个力量的源泉。
