### English

We must uncover the source of this power.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:832`

## ancrel.15004.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.15004.name&lang=en)

### 中文

造化面具

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:686`

### English

The Mask of Transformation

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:834`

## ancrel.15004.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.15004.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在[From.Planet.GetName]的科学团队有了新的突破！他们小心地将晶体支柱完整地挖掘了出来。其中保存着一个古老的、类人的面具。它的里侧写着一些象形文字，指明这件文物的名字叫做“造化之面具”。这些字符可能会意自这颗行星的卫星所具有的转变性的力量，即随着月相的变化操控潮涨潮落。\n\n面具本身则具有难以置信的灵能性质，很有可能就是这颗行星发生快速气候变化的原因。无论是谁培养了那座晶体支柱，以及谁精心种植了那些复杂的林冠地图，他们很显然都是出自崇拜并保护这件文物的目的。团队提出的理论是，如果运用得当，这个面具可以协助我们的帝国转变整颗行星。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:687`

### English

A breakthrough from the science team on [From.Planet.GetName]! Their careful excavation of the crystal pillar is complete. Preserved within was an ancient, humanoid mask. Ideographs on the reverse side refer to the artifact as The Mask of Transformation. This is likely a reference to the transformative power of the planet's moon, controlling the tides as it transitions from one phase to the next.\n\nThe Mask itself possesses incredible psionic properties, and is likely responsible for the rapid climatological transformation of the planet. Whoever fostered the crystal pillar and planted the elaborate map of trees in the forest above clearly intended to honor and protect this artifact. The team theorizes that, if properly used, the Mask may enable our empire to transform entire worlds.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:835`

## ancrel.15004.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.15004.a&lang=en)

### 中文

难以置信。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:688`

### English

Incredible.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:836`

## ancrel.15005.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.15005.name&lang=en)

### 中文

支离破碎！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:689`

### English

Shattered!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:838`

## ancrel.15005.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.15005.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.Planet.GetName]上传来了坏消息，我们的科研团队没能成功地把那件文物从晶体石笋中取出。可能出于操作过于暴力，团队把这座支柱彻底砸碎了。更糟糕的是，其中的文物被损坏到无法辨认，而整间石室里充盈的灵能霎时消失无踪。\n\n所幸这些晶体本身还是颇具价值的。即使我们也许永远无法完全了解这个先驱文明修建这个场所的目的，我们的努力至少没有完完全全白费。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:690`

### English

Frustration on [From.Planet.GetName] as our science team has failed to remove the artifact from the crystal stalagmite. Perhaps working with too much vigor, the team's efforts caused the pillar to shatter. Worse, the artifact within was damaged beyond recognition, and all traces of psionic energy have vanished from the chamber.\n\nOne minor consolation is that the crystal itself could prove extremely valuable. While we may never fully understand the intentions of the precursor civilization that built this place, at least our efforts have not entirely been in vain.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:839`

## ancrel.15005.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.15005.a&lang=en)

### 中文

沮丧，但也收获颇丰。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:691`

### English

Frustrating, yet lucrative.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:840`

## ancrel.15006.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.15006.name&lang=en)

### 中文

林间偷袭

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:692`

### English

Bushwhacked!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:842`

## ancrel.15006.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.15006.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.Planet.GetName]上的科研团队发来紧急通讯！他们对丛林的鲁莽涉足似乎严重地激怒了一些当地的种群。\n\n在几个夜晚中，团队成员看到了倏然闪过的阴影，在林下灌木从中时隐时现。然后，在月圆之夜，一支像猿类一样的生物组成的部队从天而降，嚎叫并挥舞着棍子。得益于我们先进的科技，这些袭击者很快被赶回了树上，不过还是有几名探险队的成员被他们掳走了。茂密的树林中还能听到他们哀嚎的回响。\n\n尽管遭遇到了危险，科学家[Root.GetLeaderName]许诺仍会继续探索。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:693`

### English

Emergency transmission from the science team on [From.Planet.GetName]! Their rough foray into the jungle appears to have badly angered some of the local fauna.\n\nOver the course of several nights, the team sighted fleeting shadows, half-glimpsed between the underbrush. Then, on the eve of the full moon, a troop of simian-like creatures descended on the expedition, howling and brandishing sticks. Given our technological advantage, these raiders were quickly driven back into the trees, but not before they had made off with several members of the team. We are told the dense foliage did little to muffle the screams.\n\nDespite the danger, scientist [Root.GetLeaderName] has vowed to continue the expedition.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:843`

## ancrel.15006.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.15006.a&lang=en)

### 中文

令人担忧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:694`

### English

Concerning.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:844`

## ancrel.15007.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.15007.name&lang=en)

### 中文

失物招领

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:695`

### English

Lost and Found

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:846`

## ancrel.15007.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.15007.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们从[From.Planet.GetName]上的探险队获悉了一条新消息。在迷宫般的丛林中探索比起先预期的还要困难。我们的团队每挣扎前进一步都要和意料之外的沼泽、坚硬诡异的藤蔓做斗争，同时还要面对那些出奇智慧的类猿生物。研究团队通过驯服一些温顺的有蹄类生物，终于是穿过了丛林中最困难的地区。它们还可以在巨大树木的树根下找到埋着某种类虫生物肉的埋藏点。因为这些生物看起来非常兴奋，这些虫肉一定是当地的某种美味佳肴。更重要的是，研究团队很快也从树林的地表下发掘出了一些制造物件，这些小发现也进一步表明了这颗行星上曾经存在着先驱文明。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:696`

### English

We have received an update from the expedition on [From.Planet.GetName]. Navigating the labyrinthine jungle has proven more challenging than initially hoped. As the team struggled to push forward, stymied by unexpected marshes and thick, creeping vines, they were approached by an unusually intelligent simian. Picking its way through the foliage on the back of a tamed ungulate, the creature led the team through the worst of the jungle. It also made sure to point out caches of edible insectoid larvae buried in the roots of the great trees. Given the creature's excited gestures, these must be something of a local delicacy. More importantly, the tip resulted in further evidence of the planet's precursor civilization, as the team quickly unearthed several artifacts from the forest floor.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:847`

## ancrel.15007.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.15007.a&lang=en)

### 中文

那些猿类也没什么大不了的！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:697`

### English

That simian is alright!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:848`

## ancrel.15008.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.15008.name&lang=en)

### 中文

灌木派对

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:698`

### English

Bush Party

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:850`

## ancrel.15008a.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.15008a.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.Planet.GetName]上的研究团队遭遇了一些困难。在树林中意外发现了一座蜿蜒崎岖的河谷，他们在丛林地表的探索近乎停滞了。汹涌的河水中尖石穿空，犬牙交错，从现在这条路线几乎无法通过。随之而来的绕道又带来了意想不到的后果：一支当地群居猿类的大队靠近了他们。这些长着斜眉毛的生物以取悦探险队为乐事，每晚他们都满载着大量发酵的浆果而来。尽管士气空前高涨，但进度仍然缓慢，项目领导汇报时警告整支团队可能缺失行动力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:699`

### English

Hard times for the science team on [From.Planet.GetName]. Progress through the jungle ground to a near halt with the unexpected discovery of a river valley winding beneath the trees. The raging waters are stuttered with sharp rocks, and have proven impassable along the team's chosen course. The resulting detour has had surprising consequences: the arrival of a gregarious troop of local simians. These slope-browed creatures have delighted in entertaining the expedition, arriving each night laden with an extremely potent variety of fermented berry. Although morale is higher than ever, progress continues to slow, and the project lead reports an alarming lack of initiative among the team.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:851`

## ancrel.15008b.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.15008b.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.Planet.GetName]上的研究团队遭遇了一些困难。在树林中意外发现了一座蜿蜒崎岖的河谷，他们在丛林地表的探索近乎停滞了。汹涌的河水中尖石穿空，犬牙交错，从现在这条路线几乎无法通过。随之而来的绕道又带来了意想不到的后果：一支当地群居猿类的大队靠近了他们。这些长着斜眉毛的生物以取悦探险队为乐事，每晚他们都满载着大量发酵的浆果而来。尽管我们无法消化他们的礼物，这支团队还是被猿类大队所吸引，沉迷这些生物的有趣行为而荒废了任务。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:700`

### English

Hard times for the science team on [From.Planet.GetName]. Progress through the jungle ground to a near halt with the unexpected discovery of a river valley winding beneath the trees. The raging waters are stuttered with sharp rocks, and have proven impassable along the team's chosen course. The resulting detour has had surprising consequences: the arrival of a gregarious troop of local simians. These slope-browed creatures have delighted in entertaining the expedition, arriving each night laden with an extremely potent variety of fermented berry. Despite our inability to digest their gift, the team has nevertheless taken to the troop, indulging the creatures' antics at the expense of the mission.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:852`

## intentionally_tidally_locked_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Alost_soul_cat&lang=en)

### English

Lost Soul

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:870`

## lost_soul_cat_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Alost_soul_cat_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在太空中我们发现了一个严重损毁的双足机器人。我们尚不清楚它的能源，但我们可以从它的记忆体中获取一些信息。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:716`

### English

We have found a heavily damaged bipedal robot floating in space. We do not understand its energy source, but we may be able to retrieve information from its memory.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:871`

## anomaly.6786.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6786.name&lang=en)

### 中文

迷路灵魂

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:717`

### English

Lost Soul

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:873`

## anomaly.6786.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6786.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在太空中我们发现了一个严重损毁的双足机器人。它的面部毫无特征，而它的金属外构严重损坏了。初步的扫描显示它还有残留的能源，不过它使用的能源和我们的大相径庭。也许它的内部电路还能留存一些过去的数据，不过充能和翻译数据需要花费一些时间。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:718`

### English

We have found a bipedal robot floating in space. Its faceplate is featureless, and its metal exterior is damaged. Preliminary scans indicate it has residual power, but its power source is radically different from our own. Its internal circuitry may hold memories of its previous existence, but recharging it and translating its information will take time.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:874`

## anomaly.6786.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6786.a&lang=en)

### 中文

扫描这个机器人。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:719`

### English

Scan the robot.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:875`

## anomaly.6786.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6786.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

§L让我们看看你的记忆，宇宙旅行者。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:720`

### English

§LLet's see what you remember, cosmic traveler.§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:876`

## anomaly.6786.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6786.b&lang=en)

### 中文

放弃它。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:721`

### English

Abandon it.

## anomaly.6786.b.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6786.b.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

§L再见，一坨垃圾。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:722`

### English

§LSo long, hunk of junk.§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:878`

## anomaly.6787.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6787.name&lang=en)

### 中文

古老灵魂

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:723`

### English

Old Soul

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:880`

## anomaly.6787.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6787.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

通过不懈的努力，我们找到了充能机器人并扫描它记忆体的方法。难过的是，这个机器已经严重劣化，我们难以取回有条理的信息。我们会考虑发起后续研究，可能会研究它的其他同类，但这个机械流浪者的命运永远会是一个谜。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:724`

### English

Through diligent effort, we have found a way to recharge the robot's power supply and scan its memory banks. Sadly, the machine has deteriorated so badly that we cannot retrieve coherent information. We will consider a follow-up study, and we may search for others of its kind, but the fate of this mechanical castaway must remain a mystery.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:881`

## TEMPORAL_PRISM_CAT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATEMPORAL_PRISM_CAT&lang=en)

### 中文

异常时间波动

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:726`

### English

Unusual Temporal Fluctuations

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:885`

## TEMPORAL_PRISM_CAT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATEMPORAL_PRISM_CAT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

在[From.GetName]的周边地区探测到异常的时空波动。需要进一步分析。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:727`

### English

Unusual fluctuations in spacetime have been detected in the vicinity of [From.GetName]. Further analysis is advised.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:886`

## anomaly.6788.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6788.name&lang=en)

### 中文

时间棱镜

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:728`

### English

Temporal Prism

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:888`

## anomaly.6788.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.6788.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对[From.GetStarName]星系内时间波动的分析发现了一些值得注意的东西：一座巨大的、漂浮在太空中的棱镜。这座棱镜不折射光线，而是折射时间。取决于不同的入射角，[Root.GetName]的船员体会到时间在以不同的速率流逝。扫描也表明，他们中有些人快速老化，而另一些人则比预期的要年轻了数天甚至数年。\n\n我们对这座棱镜的研究确乎加深了我们对时间的本质和性质的理解，但是它的起源和作用仍不得而知。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:729`

### English

Our analysis of the temporal fluctuations in the [From.GetStarName] system has revealed something remarkable: a massive prism, floating in space. Rather than light, the prism refracts time. Depending on the angle of incidence, the crew of the [Root.GetName] report experiencing time at different rates. Scans indicate that some have aged rapidly, while others are days, or even years younger than they should be.\n\nWhile our study of the prism has certainly deepened our understanding of the nature and properties of time, its origin and purpose remain a mystery.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:889`

## colony.8099.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.8099.name&lang=en)

### 中文

隐伏的蛇类

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:731`

### English

Insidious Ophidians

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:893`

## colony.8099.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.8099.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们探测到成群的生物正在我国位于[root.GetName]的殖民地之下向上掘进。它们随时可能从冒出地面。我们的科学家提出理论认为，也许是地表振动把它们从某种地下蛰伏状态中唤醒了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:732`

### English

We have detected swarms of creatures burrowing up beneath our colony on [root.GetName]. They could emerge any day now. Our scientists theorize that surface vibrations may have awakened them from some form of subterranean slumber.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:894`

## colony.8099.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.8099.a&lang=en)

### 中文

动员科学家和士兵。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:733`

### English

Mobilize scientists and soldiers.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:895`

## colony.9100.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9100.name&lang=en)

### 中文

蜿蜒的密谋

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:734`

### English

Slithering Subterfuge

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:897`

## colony.9100.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9100.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[root.GetName]上的殖民者报告称一些房屋正受到巨蛇侵扰，且许多建筑物处于停电状态。我们应该弄清两者间有无关联，

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:735`

### English

Colonists on [root.GetName] are reporting that some homes are now infested with serpents, and many structures are experiencing power failures. We should see if there's a connection.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:898`

## colony.9100.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9100.a&lang=en)

### 中文

观察这些奇异的生物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:736`

### English

Observe these curious creatures.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:899`

## colony.9100.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9100.b&lang=en)

### 中文

用火把它们从赶出殖民地。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:737`

### English

Force them from the colony with fire.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:900`

## colony.9101.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9101.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

巨蛇们被电能的源头所吸引，可能是在寻找养分或寻求乐趣。如果我们可以切换自己的能源，也许它们就会觉得这么干不合品味……或是吃到苦头。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:738`

### English

The serpents are being drawn to sources of electrical energy, possibly seeking nourishment or pleasure. If we can alter our energy source, perhaps they'll find it distasteful... or painful.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:902`

## colony.9102.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9102.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[root.GetName]上巨蛇的侵略性如今有所减弱，但它们似乎在专心拆解殖民者的机器设备。其技巧暗示它们起码拥有初步智能。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:739`

### English

The serpents on [root.GetName] are less aggressive now, but they seem intent on dismantling the colonists' technology. Their techniques imply they possess at least a crude intelligence.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:903`

## colony.9103.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9103.name&lang=en)

### 中文

不详嘶语

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:740`

### English

Sinister Sibilance

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:905`

## colony.9103.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9103.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

正如我们观察过[root.GetName]上入侵的巨蛇一般，它们也在注视我们。巨蛇们找到了渗透和胡乱操作我们的载具、计算机以及挖矿设备的方法。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:741`

### English

Just as we have observed the invading serpents on [root.GetName], they are watching us. They have found ways to infiltrate and randomly operate our vehicles, computers, and mining equipment.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:906`

## colony.9103.desc.science

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9103.desc.science&lang=en)

### 中文

正如我们观察过[root.GetName]上入侵的巨蛇一般，它们也在注视我们。巨蛇们找到了渗透和胡乱操作我们的载具、计算机以及挖矿设备的方法。\n\n我们得知这群蛇形生物有一种简单的语言。出于外交目的，我们的科学家如今称它们为“蛇族”。它们则称自己为“嘿嘶嘶”，不过我们读这个名字时往往读错，从而激怒了它们，导致更多的蛇咬人。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:742`

### English

Just as we have observed the invading serpents on [root.GetName], they are watching us. They have found ways to infiltrate and randomly operate our vehicles, computers, and mining equipment.\n\nWe know that the ophidians have a simple language. For the sake of diplomacy, our scientists now refer to them as 'Ophids.' They refer to themselves as the 'Hisssss,' but we often mispronounce that name, which infuriates them and leads to more biting.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:907`

## colony.9103.desc.soldier

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9103.desc.soldier&lang=en)

### 中文

正如我们观察过[root.GetName]上入侵的巨蛇一般，它们也在注视我们。巨蛇们找到了渗透和胡乱操作我们的载具、计算机以及挖矿设备的方法。\n\n据我们的侦察兵所说，这些“蛇种”生活在地下深处。喷火器可以作为有效威慑。它们憎恨我们，不过它们更恨火焰。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:743`

### English

Just as we have observed the invading serpents on [root.GetName], they are watching us. They have found ways to infiltrate and randomly operate our vehicles, computers, and mining equipment.\n\nFrom what our recon soldiers can tell, these 'ophidians' live far underground. Incinerators are an effective deterrent. They hate us, but they hate fire more.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:908`

## colony.9103.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9103.a&lang=en)

### 中文

让它们以我们的能源为食。激活通用翻译器。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:744`

### English

Let them feed on our power sources. Activate the universal translator.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:909`

## colony.9103.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9103.b&lang=en)

### 中文

动员突击部队。用喷火器进行攻击。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:745`

### English

Mobilize shock troops. Attack with incinerators.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:910`

## colony.9104.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9104.name&lang=en)

### 中文

地下世界

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:746`

### English

Underworld

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:912`

## colony.9104.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9104.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[root.GetName]上的巨蛇把我们的一些采矿设备转变成了交通工具，用来在它们的地底世界与我们的地表世界间旅行。这显著破坏了我们的生态系统。\n\n我们殖民地的科学家开始称这些蛇形入侵者为“嘿嘶”。士兵们为其起了另外的若干名字，大部分这些名字还是不提为好。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:747`

### English

The serpents on [root.GetName] have converted some of our mining equipment into transports for traveling between their subterranean world and our surface world. This has damaged our ecology significantly.\n\nThe scientists in our colony have started referring to these serpentine invaders as the 'Hiss'. The soldiers have other names for them, most of which are not worth repeating.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:913`

## colony.9104.desc.science

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9104.desc.science&lang=en)

### 中文

[root.GetName]上的巨蛇把我们的一些采矿设备转变成了交通工具，用来在它们的地底世界与我们的地表世界间旅行。这显著破坏了我们的生态系统。\n\n尽管“嘿嘶”们造成了混乱，我们的科学家怀疑它们可能是在消遣。这些蛇形生物尚未带走我们的全部采矿设备，所以我们也许会想停用我们的一些机器，以便维修。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:748`

### English

The serpents on [root.GetName] have converted some of our mining equipment into transports for traveling between their subterranean world and our surface world. They have damaged our ecology significantly.\n\nAlthough the 'Hiss' are unleashing chaos, our scientists suspect this might be a diversion. The ophidians haven't taken all of our mining equipment yet, so we may want to take some of our machines offline for repairs.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:914`

## colony.9104.desc.soldier

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9104.desc.soldier&lang=en)

### 中文

[root.GetName]上的巨蛇把我们的一些采矿设备转变成了交通工具，用来在它们的地底世界与我们的地表世界间旅行。这显著破坏了我们的生态系统。\n\n不论蛇种的动机为何，我们的士兵坚持认为采矿必须继续下去。我们也许会损失保安和士兵，但绝不能让一条蛇溜进我们的家园……或是接触我们的技术。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:749`

### English

The serpents on [root.GetName] have converted some of our mining equipment into transports for traveling between their subterranean world and our surface world. They have damaged our ecology significantly.\n\nWhatever the ophidians' motivations might be, our soldiers insist that mining must continue. We may lose guards and soldiers, but we can't let a single serpent into our homes... or our tech.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:915`

## colony.9104.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9104.a&lang=en)

### 中文

停用剩余的采矿设备。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:750`

### English

Take the remaining mining equipment offline.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:916`

## colony.9104.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9104.b&lang=en)

### 中文

还是用火烧的不够。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:751`

### English

This is what happens when you don't use enough fire.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:917`

## colony.9105.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9105.name&lang=en)

### 中文

横行的灾祸

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:752`

### English

Infestation Devastation

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:919`

## colony.9105.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9105.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

蛇族入侵者冷酷无情的破坏行动，还有在[root.GetName]上进行的复杂挖掘行动，彻底破坏了这颗星球的生态。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:753`

### English

Relentless destruction from serpentine invaders and elaborate excavation on [root.GetName] have devastated the world's ecology.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:920`

## colony.9106.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9106.name&lang=en)

### 中文

提升与馈赠

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:754`

### English

Uplift and a Gift

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:922`

## colony.9106.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9106.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[root.GetName]上的巨蛇把我们的一些采矿设备转变成了交通工具，用来在它们的地底世界与我们的地表世界间旅行。我们的科技是不是在用新的方式让它们变得强大了？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:755`

### English

The serpents on [root.GetName] are now using mining equipment to travel freely between their subterranean nests and our surface world. Has our technology empowered them in new ways?

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:923`

## colony.9106.desc.science

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9106.desc.science&lang=en)

### 中文

[root.GetName]上的巨蛇把我们的一些采矿设备转变成了交通工具，用来在它们的地底世界与我们的地表世界间旅行。我们的科技是不是在用新的方式让它们变得强大了？\n\n我们的科学家报告道，这些“嘿嘶”在每一个交通工具旁都涂画了相同的符号。我们怀疑它们还拥有一套简陋的书写语言系统。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:756`

### English

The serpents on [root.GetName] are now using mining equipment to travel freely between their subterranean nests and our surface world. Has our technology empowered them in new ways?\n\nOur scientists are reporting that the 'Hiss' have scrawled the same symbol near each of the transports. We suspect they also have a crude written language.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:924`

## colony.9106.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9106.a&lang=en)

### 中文

这个全新的蛇族甬道发掘出了……矿藏！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:757`

### English

These new serpent tunnels have unearthed... minerals!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:925`

## colony.9107.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9107.name&lang=en)

### 中文

蛇族飞船

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:758`

### English

Serpent Ship

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:927`

## colony.9107.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9107.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[root.GetName]上的蛇族正耐心地等待一艘舰船进入这个星系。看来，某个倒霉蛋觉得在我们的殖民地上降落是个好主意，现在他的星舰上面装满了蛇。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:759`

### English

The serpents on [root.GetName] have patiently waited for a ship to enter the system. Someone thought it would be a good idea to land on our colony, so their starship is now full of serpents.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:928`

## colony.9107.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9107.a&lang=en)

### 中文

就让那些逃跑的蛇族拥有这艘船吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:760`

### English

Let the fleeing ophidians have the ship.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:929`

## colony.9107.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9107.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

当这些高智商的蛇形生物闯入无边的宇宙时，我们也帮不上什么忙，只能好奇它们的最终命运如何。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:761`

### English

When these hyperintelligent ophidians venture forth into the cosmos, one cannot help but wonder what their ultimate fate will be.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:930`

## colony.9107.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9107.b&lang=en)

### 中文

摧毁这艘蛇船。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:762`

### English

Destroy the serpent ship.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:931`

## colony.9107.b.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9107.b.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

这些高智商的蛇形生物将他们的族人转移到了我们的一艘诱饵船上。我们应该让他们上船……然后把反应堆过载。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:763`

### English

These hyperintelligent ophidians have relocated their population aboard our tempting starship. We should let them on board... and overload the reactor.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:932`

## colony.9108.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9108.name&lang=en)

### 中文

嘶嘶星辰

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:764`

### English

To the Starsss

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:934`

## colony.9108.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9108.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一艘孤独的星舰悄悄地绕过了我们的所有探测装备，前往周边的恒星系。这些蛇族在[root.GetName]上留下了一大堆地底起重机和一片富饶的矿藏。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:765`

### English

A lone starship has slipped past all our detection equipment and slithered into the surrounding star system. The ophidians have left [root.GetName] with a series of subterranean lifts and a wealth of minerals.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:935`

## colony.9108.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9108.a&lang=en)

### 中文

再会，蛇。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:766`

### English

So long, serpents.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:936`

## colony.9109.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9109.name&lang=en)

### 中文

嘶嘶轶事

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:768`

### English

Hiss Story

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:939`

## colony.9109.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9109.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在位于[root.GetName]的最大规模殖民地的外围，发生了一起爆炸。我们会保持警惕，但就目前情况来说，我们已经烧光了最后一波蛇族入侵者。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:769`

### English

A massive explosion has detonated outside our largest colony on [root.GetName]. We will remain vigilant, but as far as we can tell, we have incinerated the last of our ophidian invaders.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:940`

## colony.9109.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9109.a&lang=en)

### 中文

再会，蛇。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:770`

### English

So long, serpents.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:941`

## VENDING_MACHINE_CAT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AVENDING_MACHINE_CAT&lang=en)

### 中文

一份邀请

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:773`

### English

An Invitation

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:946`

## VENDING_MACHINE_CAT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AVENDING_MACHINE_CAT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

一座信标围绕着[From.GetName]，召唤着路过的人靠近。在这个小行星上，有一座巨大的金属碑塔赫然耸立，它的作用尚不明确。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:774`

### English

A signal beacon orbits [From.GetName], beckoning travelers nearer. On the asteroid, a giant metal monolith looms, its purpose unknown.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:947`

## anomaly.7000.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7000.name&lang=en)

### 中文

神秘碑塔

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:775`

### English

Mysterious Monolith

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:949`

## anomaly.7000.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7000.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]上发现的这座巨大的碑塔让我们的科研船派过去都微如蝼蚁。在这块宏伟的、光滑的板状金属表面，曾经炫目的彩色图案如今已变得斑驳。\n\n机器的侧面一处小的能量出口似乎暗示着只要以适量的能量币作为交换，它的秘密就能悉数揭示，

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:776`

### English

The monolith we found on [From.GetName] dwarfs the science ship we sent to investigate it. A majestic, smooth slab of metal, the once-garish pigment patterns on the front have been reduced to mere flecks.\n\nA small power outlet on the machine's side promises its secrets will be unveiled in exchange for a modest sum of energy credits.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:950`

## anomaly.7000.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7000.a&lang=en)

### 中文

激活机器。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:777`

### English

Activate the machine.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:952`

## anomaly.7000.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7000.b&lang=en)

### 中文

最好不要理它。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:778`

### English

Best to leave it alone.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:953`

## anomaly.7010.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7010.name&lang=en)

### 中文

机械复苏

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:779`

### English

The Machine Awakens

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:955`

## anomaly.7010.desc.base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7010.desc.base&lang=en)

### 中文

机械活动了起来，远古的传动机构在光滑的表面底下叮当作响。在一阵呼呼、嗡嗡声之后噪音戛然而止，一块金属挡板划开，从机器中滚出了一个有舰船一般大小的圆柱状罐体……\n\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:780`

### English

The machine stirs, ancient mechanisms clanking behind the smooth facade. After much whirring, buzzing, and a dramatic pause, a metallic panel slides back, and a ship-sized cylinder rolls out of the machine...\n\n

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:956`

## anomaly.7010.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7010.a&lang=en)

### 中文

给罐里头的饮料注册专利，然后把它卖给全星系。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:781`

### English

Patent the drink. Sell it to the whole galaxy.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:957`

## anomaly.7010.desc.full

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7010.desc.full&lang=en)

### 中文

圆柱状罐体中保存着某种远古的饮料，奇迹般地抵御了时间的侵蚀。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:782`

### English

The cylinder contains an ancient beverage, still miraculously preserved against the ravages of time.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:958`

## anomaly.7010.desc.full.megacorp

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7010.desc.full.megacorp&lang=en)

### 中文

$anomaly.7010.desc.base$$anomaly.7010.desc.full$依据市场数据，这种失传已久的饮品比我们目前能为生物体提供的最好的饮料还要好喝20%。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:783`

### English

$anomaly.7010.desc.base$$anomaly.7010.desc.full$ According to our market data, the long-forgotten drink is 20% more delicious than our current range of drinkable products for biological entities.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:959`

## anomaly.7010.desc.full.nonbio

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7010.desc.full.nonbio&lang=en)

### 中文

$anomaly.7010.desc.base$ $anomaly.7010.desc.full$尽管这种饮品对我们没有直接用处，我们的[Owner.GetScientistPlural]已可以把它们转化成更有用的资源：§Y异星天然气§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:784`

### English

$anomaly.7010.desc.base$$anomaly.7010.desc.full$ While the liquid provides no substance to us, our [Owner.GetScientistPlural] have been able to convert it into a much more valuable resource: §YExotic Gas§!.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:960`

## anomaly.7010.desc.full.bio

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7010.desc.full.bio&lang=en)

### 中文

$anomaly.7010.desc.base$$anomaly.7010.desc.full$这种饮品已经轻易地被我们的[Owner.GetScientistPlural]复制出来了，经过了大量缜密的测试后确认，这种液体干净又卫生，富有营养，而且关键的是还很好喝。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:785`

### English

$anomaly.7010.desc.base$$anomaly.7010.desc.full$ The drink has been easily replicated by our [Owner.GetScientistPlural], who, after conducting rigorous testing, have ascertained it to be safe, nutritious and - most crucially - delicious.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:961`

## anomaly.7010.desc.empty

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7010.desc.empty&lang=en)

### 中文

$anomaly.7010.desc.base$这个圆柱状罐体内部是中空的，其内容物早就已经从腐蚀掉的封盖中蒸发殆尽。这个罐体自身状态良好，构成其的未知合金与我们用于建造舰船的合金水平相当。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:786`

### English

$anomaly.7010.desc.base$The cylinder is empty, its content long evaporated through a corroded sealing cap. The container itself is still in good shape, though, and the unknown alloy is on par with the metal we use to make our ships.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:962`

## anomaly.7010.desc.rot.bio

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7010.desc.rot.bio&lang=en)

### 中文

$anomaly.7010.desc.base$其中包含的液体恶臭不堪；这种曾经可口的营养物质被岁月的流逝所腐坏。通过研究这些物质，我们的[Owner.GetScientistPlural]期望可以获悉一些关于曾经喜爱这种饮品的外星人的生物学信息，假定我们能先搞定它的臭味的话。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:787`

### English

$anomaly.7010.desc.base$The cylinder contains a liquid most foul; a once-delicious nutrient long spoiled by time. By studying the substance, our [Owner.GetScientistPlural] hope to learn about the biology of the aliens who once delighted in it - assuming we can do something about the smell first.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:963`

## anomaly.7010.desc.rot.nonbio

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7010.desc.rot.nonbio&lang=en)

### 中文

$anomaly.7010.desc.base$圆柱状罐体内部包含的液体混合物含有蔗糖、柠檬酸和其他生物性物质。我们的[Owner.GetScientistPlural]相信它是一种由碳基生命制造并饮用的富有营养的物质。进一步的研究有可能能提供关于这种已消亡物种的生物学线索。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:788`

### English

$anomaly.7010.desc.base$The cylinder contains a liquid mixture of sucrose, citric acid, and other biological compounds. Our [Owner.GetScientistPlural] believe it to be a nutrient substance made by and for carbon-based beings. Further studies will hopefully provide more clues pertaining to the biology of this lost race.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:964`

## colony.9050.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9050.name&lang=en)

### 中文

意外矿层

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:790`

### English

Unexpected Mineral Seams

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:967`

## colony.9050.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9050.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在[Root.GetName]上的地质勘探揭示了这颗行星远比我们之前预想的更加富有矿藏。在调节了我们的传感器以后它便可以扫描更深的地下，发现了许多大有前景的矿场。我们应该委派更多的人力物力进行勘探，或许可以发现更深处的有用矿脉。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:791`

### English

Geological surveys on [Root.GetName] have revealed that the planet is richer in minerals than we thought. After calibrating our sensors to penetrate deeper into the ground, we have discovered several promising mining sites. Should we direct additional resources to probing efforts, we might yet uncover deeper veins of useful ore.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:968`

## colony.9050.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9050.a&lang=en)

### 中文

没钱，直接开挖吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:792`

### English

No funding. Start digging.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:969`

## colony.9050.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9050.b&lang=en)

### 中文

批准经费，进行深层勘探。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:793`

### English

Funding granted. Probe deeper.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:970`

## colony.9055.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9055.name&lang=en)

### 中文

深层矿脉

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:794`

### English

Deep Veins

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:971`

## colony.9055.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9055.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

探测[Root.GetName]的地下是一个冗长的过程：我们的探测器对地下构成的响应不佳，地质构造中的断层又使得我们需要频繁地调整仪器。然而，我们还是发现了更多值得开发的高价值矿石。它们的深度使得开采作业的后勤工作远复杂于往常，但无论如何这应该都是值得的。没有迹象表明进一步的了勘探作业可以让我们找到更多矿脉了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:795`

### English

Probing the underground of [Root.GetName] is a tedious process: our scanners do not react well to the ground composition, and shifts in the geological layers mean that we have to recalibrate them every time. Still, we have discovered more exploitable veins of high quality ore. Their depths makes the logistics of the mining operation more complex than usual, but it will take more than that to stop us. There is no assurance that additional probing operations will lead us to more mineral veins.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:972`

### 中文

开始采矿作业。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:796`

### English

Begin mining operations.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:973`

## colony.9055.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9055.b&lang=en)

### English

There has to be more.

## colony.9060.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9060.name&lang=en)

### 中文

为“石”过早

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:798`

### English

Too Little, Too Deep

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:975`

## colony.9060.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9060.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

最近在[Root.GetName]的勘探作业几乎一无所获。我们所能找到的其他的矿藏全部都埋藏过深、储量不大，而且处于不稳定的地层当中。开采这些矿物的成本将会高于开采所得的利润。尽管如此，我们此前的发现已经表明此处的资源极其丰富，我们需要数十年乃至上百年的时间才能开发完毕。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:799`

### English

The latest prospecting efforts on [Root.GetName] were inconclusive. Whatever minerals we could detect were buried too deep, in too small quantities, and through unstable ground. The projected costs of exploiting these veins would be higher than the profits. Despite this, our previous findings represents an enormous source of resources that should keep us busy for decades if not centuries.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:976`

## colony.9060.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9060.a&lang=en)

### 中文

很好了。撸起袖子干吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:800`

### English

Very Well. Get to work.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:977`

## colony.9065.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9065.name&lang=en)

### 中文

更多矿物

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:801`

### English

More Ore

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:978`

## colony.9065.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9065.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

无论在[Root.GetName]上的矿物藏得多深，我们都要找到它们，开采它们。我们一直就是这样的。其他人也许面对不稳定地层、令人望而却步的深度和采矿运输的后勤工作面前退缩，而我们绝不。底下世界就是我们的家园，只要找准方向定能有所收获。往下挖得越深，矿物的品质和储量就越高。我们最新的采样甚至表明了深层中稀有水晶的存在。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:802`

### English

No matter how deep the minerals on [Root.GetName] are hidden, we will find and extract them. It is what we do. Others might give up when confronted with unstable grounds, unfathomable depths or the logistics of maintaining transport tunnels, but we do not. The underground is our home, and if we know where to look, it will provide. The lower we go, the higher the quality and quantity of the ore. Our latest samples even hinted at the presence of rare crystals deeper.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:979`

## colony.9065.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9065.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经找到够多了，开始开采吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:803`

### English

We found enough. Commence extraction.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:980`

## colony.9065.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9065.b&lang=en)

### 中文

这些水晶是我们的！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:804`

### English

These crystals shall be ours!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:981`

## colony.9070.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9070.name&lang=en)

### 中文

炽热水晶

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:805`

### English

Fiery Crystals

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:982`

## colony.9070.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9070.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[Root.GetName]上的勘探队已经抵达了最新的深度。如预期一样，灯光所及之处，我们果然找到了一丛丛闪耀着橙色、黄色和红色的灵动光芒的晶簇。它们有的看起来如同照亮洞穴的火盆，在岩壁上投下斑驳的光影。我们还有一个谜一样的发现：如果以千年尺度来观测，这些晶体似乎在缓慢地向行星的地心移动。我们相信，如果向地心挖得更深的话我们一定会有更多发现。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:806`

### English

A new depth has been reached by our surveying expeditions on [Root.GetName]. As predicted, we are encountering clusters of crystals that shine in vivid hues of oranges, yellows, and reds when light passes through them. At times, it looks as if a brazier is illuminating the caves where we find them, casting shifting shadows on the walls. We also made a puzzling observation: it looks as if over the millennia, these crystals are slowly migrating towards the center of the planet. We are confident that there will be more to find if only we dig deeper towards the core.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:983`

## colony.9070.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9070.a&lang=en)

### 中文

这些勘探工作花销过高了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:807`

### English

These probing works are too expensive.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:984`

## colony.9070.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9070.b&lang=en)

我们该继续挖下去。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:808`

### English

Then a digging we shall go.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:985`

## colony.9075.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9075.name&lang=en)

### 中文

贪婪的代价

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:809`

### English

The Price of Greed

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:986`

## colony.9075.intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9075.intro&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的勘探队往[Root.GetName]的深处进一步寻找矿石的过程中发现越来越多的水晶散布在他们的路径上。最终，他们抵达了接近[Root.GetName]核心的一个巨大洞穴，随后便失去了联系。他们最后的消息是正在搭建灯光和传感器以研究洞穴，然后就听到痛苦的尖叫，死亡和毁灭随之而来。\n\n事故的原因很快被确定了，其他的地址勘探者探测到了多重的隧道回声正高速向我们的殖民地袭来。没过多久，恐怖的事情发生了，我们被一个硕大无比的晶态实体袭击。它看起来太空中的晶态实体别无二致，但更加具有侵略性，并且可以使用重构能力在一瞬间改变它们的身体形状。\n\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:810`

### English

As our surveyors delved ever deeper to find ore on [Root.GetName], more and more crystals littered their paths. Finally, they reached a gigantic cavern located close to the core of [Root.GetName], and we lost communications. The last thing we know is that they were in the process of setting up lights and various sensors to study the cave. Then screams. Pain. Death. Destruction.\n\nThe cause was soon revealed when other geological surveyors detected multiple echoes tunneling at tremendous speed toward our colonies. Not long after, amid quakes and tremors, we were attacked by a crystalline entity of tremendous proportions. It is not unlike the spaceborne crystals, but seems to be highly aggressive, and capable of using the reconstructive capabilities of its kind to reshape its body in an instant.\n\n

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:987`

## colony.9075.outro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9075.outro&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管晶态实体的核心还深埋于地下，但它分裂出许多触手一般的附肢正在破土而出，准备攻击我们同时吸收更多晶体生长。预期前景十分不妙，我们很快就能看到到[Root.GetName]上的殖民活动要到头了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:811`

### English

While the core of the crystalline entity remain buried deep, it spawned tentacle-like appendages that dug through the ground looking to attack us or to absorb more crystals to grow. Projections are grim and we might be looking at the end of our colony on [Root.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:988`

## colony.9075.desc.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9075.desc.a&lang=en)

### 中文

$colony.9075.intro$以前我们用来安抚太空晶体的无线电信号都没有起作用。$colony.9075.outro$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:812`

### English

$colony.9075.intro$ All attempts to deploy the radio signals that we use to pacify spaceborne crystals have failed. $colony.9075.outro$

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:989`

## colony.9075.desc.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9075.desc.b&lang=en)

### 中文

$colony.9075.intro$在太空中我们的舰队可以以高机动性和火力来取胜于这些晶体，但在行星上我们对它束手无策。$colony.9075.outro$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:813`

### English

$colony.9075.intro$ It is one thing to face such an entity in space where our ships can bring their speed and weapons to bear, but another entirely to fight them planetside. $colony.9075.outro$

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:990`

## colony.9080.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9080.name&lang=en)

### 中文

击败晶态实体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:819`

### English

Crystalline Entity Defeated

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:996`

## colony.9080.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9080.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

经过精疲力竭的斗争，盘踞于行星核心的晶态实体终于被击败了。最终的决战发生在深层地下，这只晶体巨兽核心的巢穴当中，只有将其击碎才能彻底停止它的活动。它的诸多附肢瓦解了，在分子层面发生了分解，在整个行星上散播着晶体尘埃。晶体的触手钻出的无数隧道将让我们在挖取构成这颗行星深层的富矿获得巨大的便利。至于这个晶态实体的起源我们仍还有诸多未解之谜，但我们也对太空中的晶体是如何创造出来的获得了一些见解。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:820`

### English

After a grueling campaign, the crystalline entity dwelling at the heart of a planet has finally been defeated. The final battle took place deep underground, in the cave housing the core of the crystal behemoth, and only after we managed to shatter it did the beast stop fighting. Its various appendages disintegrated, losing cohesion at a molecular level and sprinkling crystal dust across the planet. The innumerable tunnels left in the wake of crystal tentacles are going to help us a lot in our efforts to mine the exceptionally rich minerals composing this world. As for the origin of the entity itself, we are left with many questions, but we might have just gained some insight on the way that spacefaring crystalline entities are created.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:997`

### English

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:998`

## colony.9085.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9085.name&lang=en)

### 中文

异常事件

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:826`

### English

Unusual Happenings

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1003`

## colony.9085.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9085.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[Root.GetName]上的开采作业遇到了意外的困难，包括一些小规模的地震和晶体矿脉的位移，总之发生了一些什么奇怪的事情。穷尽我们所有关于开采的经验都无法解释到底是怎么回事。这些情况使得我们很难保证开采作业的安全，我们已经在一些无法预测的事故和塌方中损失了一些设备、人员和矿石。也许我们应该适可而止，在一定的深度停止采矿。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:827`

### English

Our mining operations on [Root.GetName] have encountered unexpected difficulties. Ranging from mild tremors to shifting crystal veins, something is going on. Despite all our experience in tunneling, we have no idea what it is. These circumstances make it hard to work in secure conditions, and we have already lost gear, lives and ore in unpredictable accidents and collapses. Perhaps it would be best to halt mining operations under a certain depth?

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1004`

## colony.9085.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9085.a&lang=en)

### 中文

没事，接着采。

### English

It's probably nothing.

### English

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1006`

## seeds_of_destruction_intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aseeds_of_destruction_intro&lang=en)

### 中文

我们发现了一艘古老的外星人星舰，它被半掩埋在一个小行星撞击坑中间。冰晶布满了舰身的大部分，但我们还是很顺利地发现了气阀门……并开始搜索这艘船暗藏的秘密。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:832`

### English

We have discovered an ancient alien starship lying half-buried in an asteroid crater. Glacial ice has inexorably spread across most of the ship, but we can easily access the airlock... and search for any secrets hidden behind it.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1011`

## ancrel.14001.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.14001.name&lang=en)

### 中文

笼中怒

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:833`

### English

Rage in a Cage

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1013`

## ancrel.14001.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.14001.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

当我们打开这艘外星人星舰的气阀门时，等待在另一边的是一片修罗地狱。我们初步调查发现，这里简直就是一片残杀现场。在检查支离破碎的船员尸体之后，我们很确定他们生前正互相撕扯对方的四肢。我们可以转身就走……但那样我们就永远没法发现这场悲剧是怎么发生的了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:834`

### English

When we opened the airlock of the alien starship, putrefaction and decay awaited us on the other side. Our preliminary investigation has uncovered a scene of carnage. After examining the mutilated bodies of the crew, we are certain they tore each other limb from limb. We could just leave... but then we'd never know why this tragedy happened.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1014`

## ancrel.14002.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.14002.name&lang=en)

### 中文

精密实验室

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:835`

### English

Elaborate Laboratory

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1015`

## ancrel.14002.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.14002.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

随着进一步的发掘，我们找到了这艘船的科学实验室。微弱的能量布满这片区域，泛出轻柔的紫外光。一片凌乱的紫色灌木正在厚重的孢子云。看来，这艘船的医生利用实验室和通风闸将一片有毒的云雾排放到了飞船的每一个角落。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:836`

### English

Digging deeper, we have uncovered the ship's science lab. Minimal power saturates the scene in ultraviolent illumination. A riot of purple shrubbery is releasing thick clouds of spores. The ship's doctor used a laboratory and ventilation shafts to flood the ship with an intoxicating mist.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1016`

## ancrel.14003.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.14003.name&lang=en)

### 中文

丢掉开关！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:837`

### English

Throw the Switch!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1017`

## ancrel.14003.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.14003.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们认为自己能免疫这片有毒云雾带来的危害。如果我们能让飞船的引擎运作起来，我们就能启动这艘船并将其送回母星研究。这需要时间，但这座精密的药学实验室能为我们帝国的士兵们提供有用的生物增强剂。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:838`

### English

We think we are immune to the full effects of the intoxicating mist. If we can get the ship's engines online, we can launch the ship and send it back home for study. This will take time, but the elaborate pharmaceutical laboratory may provide useful biochemical augmentation for our empire's soldiers.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1018`

## ancrel.14003.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.14003.a&lang=en)

### 中文

让反应堆重新上线！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:839`

### English

Bring the reactor online now!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1019`

## ancrel.14003.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.14003.b&lang=en)

### 中文

运行诊断程序，并开始修复。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:840`

### English

Run diagnostics and make repairs.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1020`

## ancrel.14004.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.14004.name&lang=en)

### 中文

极乐爆发

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:841`

### English

Bliss Outbreak

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1021`

## ancrel.14004.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.14004.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们愚蠢地以为我们能够免疫这片云雾，但是我们的工程师们已经陷入了销魂极乐的状态。他们在飞船的过道跳舞，大声地唱歌。我们需要时间对空气进行净化，抓住这帮子工程师，然后才能发动飞船。这些耽搁会花费我们的时间。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:842`

### English

We foolishly thought we were immune to the mist, but our engineers are experiencing a state of rapturous bliss. They dance in the ship's corridors, singing atonally. We will need to clear the atmosphere and capture our engineers before we can launch the ship. This delay will cost us time.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1022`

## ancrel.14004.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.14004.a&lang=en)

### 中文

抓住已经陷入“极乐”的工程师。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:843`

### English

Capture the blissed-out engineers.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1023`

## ancrel.14005.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.14005.name&lang=en)

### 中文

狂怒爆发

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:844`

### English

Rage Outbreak

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1024`

## ancrel.14005.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.14005.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们愚蠢地以为自己能够免疫这片云雾，但是我们的科学家们已经在经受心理狂躁的苦果。在飞船的每一个角落都有打斗发生。我们需要时间对空气进行净化，镇压住这群云雾的受害者，然后才能发动飞船。这些耽搁会花费我们的时间。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:845`

### English

We foolishly thought that we were immune to the mist, but our scientists are experiencing fits of psychotic rage. Fighting has broken out shipwide. We will need to clear the atmosphere and subdue the mist's victims before we can launch the ship. This delay will cost us time.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1025`

## ancrel.14005.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.14005.a&lang=en)

### 中文

制服癫疯的科学家。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:846`

### English

Subdue the maniacal scientists.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1026`

## ancrel.14006.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.14006.name&lang=en)

### 中文

技术的诱惑

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:847`

### English

Temptations of Technology

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1027`

## ancrel.14006.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.14006.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们起飞了！尽管很危险，但外星人飞船的有毒迷雾确实能够增强我们帝国士兵的战斗力和忠诚心。然而，问题还是存在：我们应该让自己的士兵去接触这种有害的物质吗？或者我们从这场悲剧的错误中学得教训，放弃这一切？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:848`

### English

We have liftoff! Though dangerous, the alien ship's intoxicating mist could augment the effectiveness and loyalty of our empire's soldiers. However, the question remains: should we subject our own soldiers to this hazardous substance? Or should we say no and learn from their tragic mistakes?

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1028`

## ancrel.14006.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.14006.a&lang=en)

### 中文

好好利用这个令人惊叹的科技。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:849`

### English

Exploit this promising technology.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1029`

## ancrel.14006.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.14006.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

胜利在等着！我们的士兵应该愿意冒这个风险。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:850`

### English

Victory awaits! Our soldiers should be willing to take this risk.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1030`

## ancrel.14006.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.14006.b&lang=en)

放弃这个危险的科技。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:851`

### English

Forsake this dangerous technology.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1031`

## ancrel.14006.b.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.14006.b.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

有些秘密我们还是别知道为好。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:852`

### English

There are some secrets we were not meant to know.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1032`

## on_crashed_ship_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aon_crashed_ship_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们发现了一艘古老的外星人星舰，它被半掩埋在一个小行星撞击坑中间。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:854`

### English

We have discovered an ancient alien starship lying half-buried in an asteroid crater.
