## anomaly.7030.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7030.name&lang=en)

### 中文

失事地点

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:855`

### English

Crash Site

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1037`

## anomaly.7030.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7030.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们发现了一艘古老的外星人星舰，它被半掩埋在一个小行星撞击坑中间。这艘科研船的外壳搭载了一个扩增的探测阵列，没有配备武器。船体外部在撞击之前没有受到任何伤害。我们可以假设，这场灾难之所以发生，是因为船员酿成的某种致命错误。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:856`

### English

We have discovered an ancient alien starship half-buried in an asteroid crater. This science ship's exterior has an extensive sensory array and no weaponry. The exterior was not damaged in any way before it crashed. We can assume this catastrophe was the result of a fatal error caused by the crew.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1038`

## anomaly.7030.desc.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7030.desc.a&lang=en)

### 中文

太悲剧了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:857`

### English

How tragic.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1039`

## site_space_shanty_dig

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_space_shanty_dig&lang=en)

### 中文

瓶中信

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:859`

### English

Message in a Bottle

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1043`

## space_shanty_intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aspace_shanty_intro&lang=en)

### 中文

我们找到了一艘设计独特的古代太空舰船的残骸。它使用的推进方式与现代舰船并不相同，而是依赖于在真空中展开的巨大的帆来进行。也许这艘船可以捕获恒星能量，并在星光和恒星风的推动下前进。我们的考古学家迫切希望揭开隐藏在这艘古老舰船中的秘密。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:860`

### English

We have found the wreckage of an archaic spaceship of unusual design. Instead of using propulsion similar to that of modern ships, the craft relied on large sails unfurled in the vacuum of space. Presumably, the ship harvested solar energy and drifted on the solar winds created by starlight. Our archaeologists are eager to uncover the secrets concealed within this spacecraft.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1044`

## space_shanty_intro_bio

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aspace_shanty_intro_bio&lang=en)

### 中文

深空中出现了一具远古实体的尸骸。它的外形与任何已知的活体舰船都不同，具有巨大的膜状帆，可以在宇宙风中徐徐涨开，乘着遥远繁星的辐射，化身在不可见的洋流中翱翔的星际巨兽。\n\n如今，它不过是一句干枯的尸体，骨架破裂裸露在外，曾经光彩熠熠的帆膜破烂不堪。目前尚不确定这个生物是被设计出来的还是进化而来，亦或是介于两者之间。生物考古学家们渴望研究这具遗骸，去倾听它终末旅程的回响，也许，只是也许，学习它曾经对着群星所唱出的歌谣。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:861`

### English

The drifting remains of an ancient entity have been discovered in the depths of space. Its form is unlike any living vessel we have known, it possesses vast, membranous sails, once capable of unfurling in the cosmic winds, basking in the radiation of distant stars like a great celestial leviathan gliding on unseen currents.\n\nNow, it is little more than a withered husk, its frame cracked and exposed, its once-lustrous sails reduced to tattered remnants. Whether this being was engineered, evolved, or something in between remains uncertain. Our bio-archeologists are eager to study its remains, to listen for echoes of its final journey, and perhaps, just perhaps, to learn the song it once sang to the stars.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1045`

## ancrel.15021.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.15021.name&lang=en)

### 中文

瓶中信

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:862`

### English

Message in a Bottle

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1047`

## ancrel.15021.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.15021.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

正如我们预期，这艘“恒星帆船”的内部相当古色古香。它的舰桥甚至仍是仿照古时候在行星液态海面上航行的那种帆船进行设计的。\n\n甲板的材料似乎是金属，但大部分表面又以雕刻的木材覆盖，而观察窗和数据显示屏又是以类似玻璃的材料制成。\n\n将舰船的计算机通电后，我们发现了一首老式的航海家之歌的数据文件。据我们的考古学家称，这种旋律曾一度被称为“船歌”或者“太空船歌”。我们目前正在尝试翻译歌词。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:863`

### English

As we expected, the interior of this 'solar sailer' is appropriately archaic. The bridge resembles the deck of an old planetbound sailing ship designed to sail on watery seas.\n\nThe deck plating appears to be metal, but most surfaces are covered with carved wood, a contrast to the glass-like substance on viewports and datascreens.\n\nPowering up the ship's computers, we have found a datafile with an old-fashioned seafarers' song. According to our archaeologist, this melody was once known as a 'sea chanty' or 'space shanty.' We are attempting to transcribe the lyrics.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1048`

## ancrel.15022.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.15022.name&lang=en)

### 中文

太空船歌

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:864`

### English

Space Shanty

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1049`

## ancrel.15022.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.15022.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在我们完成对数据文件的修复和重新激活后，很快一首敲击节拍的音乐旋律充盈起了整艘舰船，伴随着一个自动调音过后的古代航海家的歌声。歌词大意：\n\n“来啊，航行太空的年轻小伙子们，请听吧！\n我要唱一首畅游于大海的鱼之歌！\n\n小伙子们，狂风将起，风暴将临\n当大风吹起，我们就出航吧！\n大风向远方吹吧，嘿！快吹吧！\n向着星海，她缓缓启航。\n\n恒星风骤起，\n带我向远方。\n穿过那太空航道，\n我们驶向星光！\n\n小伙子们，狂风将起，风暴将临\n当大风吹起，我们就出航吧！\n大风向远方吹吧，嘿！快吹吧！\n向着星海，她缓缓启航。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:865`

### English

Immediately after we completed the restoration and reactivation of the datafiles, a thrumming musical cadence surged throughout the ship, accompanied by the auto-tuned voices of ancient mariners. Transcription follows:\n\n'Come all you young spacefarers, listen to me!\nI'll sing you a song of our fish from the sea!\n\nAnd it's windy weather, boys, stormy weather, boys\nWhen the wind blows, then we're all together, boys\nBlow ye winds fringeward, hey! Blow ye winds, blow!\nOut to the galaxy, steady she goes.\n\nUp come the solar winds\nTaking us far\nAll through the space lanes\nAnd out to the stars!\n\nAnd it's windy weather, boys, stormy weather, boys\nWhen the wind blows, then we're all together, boys\nBlow ye winds fringeward, hey! Blow ye winds, blow!\nOut to the galaxy, steady she goes!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1050`

## ancrel.15023.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.15023.name&lang=en)

### 中文

深空猛兽

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:866`

### English

Cosmic Beasties

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1051`

## ancrel.15023.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.15023.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在我们探索这艘舰船的过程中，进一步的重新充能和对数据文件的恢复解锁了这首远古太空船歌的更多章节。这艘船的全息投影显示出水手、航海家们一起载歌载舞的扭曲抽动的影像：\n\n“巨龙忽降临\n寻找着我们的家乡\n越过茫茫太空\n找到波涛下的那个地方！\n\n小伙子们，狂风将起，风暴将临\n当大风吹起，我们就出航吧！\n大风向远方吹吧，嘿！快吹吧！\n向着星海，她缓缓启航。\n\n缇扬奇跃起\n它是深空之王\n在这超空间竞速中\n与我们的舰船互不相让\n\n（合唱重复）\n\n星神兽出现了！\n它伸出触手\n扼住了我们的舰船\n我们惊恐万状！”\n\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:867`

### English

As we explored the ship, further attempts to power it up and recover its datafiles unlocked more stanzas of the ancient space shanty. The ship's holographic display vibrantly projected images of sailors and spacefarers singing and dancing in accompaniment:\n\n'Up comes the Great Dragon\nIn search of our home\nThrough space to a place that's\nBeneath waves and foam!\n\n'And it's windy weather, boys, stormy weather, boys\nWhen the wind blows, then we're all together, boys\nBlow ye winds fringeward, hey! Blow ye winds, blow!\nOut to the galaxy, steady she goes.\n\n'Up comes the Tiyanki\nThe king of deep space\nRacing our ships\nIn a hyperspace chase!\n\n(chorus repeats)\n\n'Up comes Leviathan\nTentacles grasp\nChoking our ship\nAs we shiver and gasp!\n\n(chorus repeats)

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1052`

## ancrel.15024.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.15024.name&lang=en)

### 中文

或许是饮料

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:868`

### English

Potent Potables

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1053`

## ancrel.15024.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.15024.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的考古学家发现了一处可能是饮料的贮藏点。那些水手们喝的就是这种臭不可闻的液体么？我们肯定是不会喝这些奇怪的液体的。但我们对这艘船的探索成果着实令人沉醉！\n\n“石质人来了\n他们从巨岩中诞生\n当他们聚到一起\n这些巨砾将强壮无比！\n\n（合唱）\n\n死灵族来了\n恐怖而又高大\n你怎么都杀不死他们，伙计\n他们还能复活！\n\n（合唱）\n\n机器蜂群来了\n大家快跑吧\n你得打一个、十个、一百个\n1101！\n\n（合唱）”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:869`

### English

Our archaeologists have unearthed of a stash of potent potables. Were the sailors consuming these foul fluids? We will not ingest these strange liquids. Our exploration of the ship is already sufficiently intoxicating!\n\n'Up come the Lithoids\nAll hewn from their stone\nThese boulders are bolder\nWhen they're not alone!\n\n(chorus)\n\n'Up come the Necroids\nAll fearsome and great\nYou can't kill them, mateys,\nThey'll reanimate!\n\n(chorus)\n\n'Up come swarms of robots\nGet ready to run\nFight one, ten, a dozen,\n1101!\n\n(chorus)

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1054`

## ancrel.15025.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.15025.name&lang=en)

### 中文

抛开锚链！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:870`

### English

Cast Off the Hawser!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1055`

## ancrel.15025.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.15025.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的探索人员发现了远古历史的一隅，向世人展现了古代探险家们的热血和勇敢。显然，船长使用这首“太空船歌”来激励船员、协调行动。甚至他们独处的时候都喜欢唱这首歌！我们的考古学家认为，这部作品应当保存到博物馆当中。\n\n至于这支古老的歌曲，我们已经翻译了能找到的全部歌词，并存入了我们的数据库。我们的“水手”甚至从中获取灵感，唱起了我们自己版本的太空船歌，这也能大大激发他们的士气和效率。\n\n“我们生于深海\n直抵苍穹\n我们建设家园\n直到永久！\n\n小伙子们，狂风将起，风暴将临\n当大风吹起，我们就出航吧！\n大风向远方吹吧，嘿！快吹吧！\n向着星海，她缓缓启航。”\n\n现在是时候从这艘古老舰船中离开了，“抛开锚链”，借助恒星风扬帆起航。我们的大船将向着星海深处稳稳航行！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:871`

### English

Our exploration has revealed a glance of ancient history, displaying the zeal and courage of ancient explorers. Clearly the captain used this 'space shanty' to motivate and coordinate the crew. Perhaps they even sang along! Our archaeologists are suggesting this performance should be preserved in a museum installation.\n\nAs for the ancient melody, we've transcribed all the lyrics we could find and added them to our databanks. Our own 'sailors' now have a habit of singing versions of their own space shanties, which should improve their morale and efficiency.\n\n'We rose from the ocean\nAnd reached for the sky\nWe're building an empire\nThat never will die!\n\n'And it's windy weather, boys, stormy weather, boys\nWhen the wind blows, then we're all together, boys\nBlow ye winds fringeward, hey! Blow ye winds, blow\nOut to the galaxy, steady she goes!'\n\nIt's time to untether from this ancient vessel, 'casting off the hawser' as we set sail on solar winds. Steady as she goes!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1056`

## anomaly.7020.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7020.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们找到了一艘设计独特的古代太空舰船。它使用的推进方式与现代舰船并不相同，而是依赖于在真空中展开的巨大的帆来进行。也许这艘船可以捕获恒星能量，并在星光和恒星风的推动下前进。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:879`

### English

We have found the wreckage of an archaic spaceship of unusual design. Instead of using propulsion similar to that of modern ships, the craft relied on large sails unfurled in the vacuum of space. Presumably, the ship harvested solar energy and drifted on the solar winds created by starlight.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1066`

## colony.9800.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9800.name&lang=en)

### 中文

虚无次元

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:898`

### English

A Dimension of Static

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1091`

## colony.9800.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9800.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]上的次元传送门自发关闭了。在我们稳定传送门后，看到它通向一个延绵不断的白色、无构造的虚空，仅有黑色噪点的随机条带穿插其中。这个奇怪的现象立刻开始渗入我们的宇宙并吞噬我们的稳定器，使得传送门发生了坍缩。\n\n传送门、虚空和次元稳定器在同一个瞬间消失了，什么都没有留下，甚至连痕迹粒子都没有。若不是连传送门都没有稳定下来，谁知道这里将会发生何等灾难。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:899`

### English

The Dimensional Portal on [Root.GetName] shut itself. When we stabilized the portal, we saw that it led to a dimension consisting solely of a white, formless void interrupted only by random streaks of black noise. The strange phenomenon instantly started to seep into our universe and consumed our dimensional stabilizers, collapsing the gateway.\n\nThe portal, static, and dimensional stabilizers have all winked out of existence simultaneously. Nothing was left behind; not even trace particles. If the portal hadn't been unstable, who knows what kind of disaster might have occurred here.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1092`

## colony.9805.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9805.name&lang=en)

### 中文

扰动

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:900`

### English

Disruptions

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1094`

## colony.9805.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9805.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

最近，我们位于§Y[Root.GetHomeWorldName]§!主要物理实验室的[Root.GetScientistPlural]在工作中发现了怪异的波动。与超空间有关的实验受到的干扰最为严重，但这些干扰仅仅持续了一周左右就彻底消失了。与此同时，[From.GetName]在那处已经关闭的传送门发现了一些波动，幸好那种不可能的虚空没有重现。希望这不会引起进一步的后果。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:901`

### English

Recently our [Root.GetScientistPlural] working in our primary physics lab on §Y[Root.GetHomeWorldName]§! have noticed strange fluctuations in their work. Our hyperdrive-related tests suffered most from these interferences, yet these interruptions only went on for about a week before stopping entirely. At the same time [From.GetName] noticed fluctuations from the now closed dimensional portal, thankfully the impossible static did not return. Hopefully, nothing will come of this.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1095`

## colony.9810.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9810.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

今天，[Root.GetHomeWorldName]上的科学界举行了一场充满了渴求与焦虑的学术报告，[cloned_physicist.GetName]发表了[cloned_physicist.GetHerHis]关于§Y[Root.Capital.GetStarName]§!附近自发生成的负相物质离子的最新发现。\n\n“这些发现可以促进我们对人工虫洞等其他诸多课题的理解，”[cloned_physicist.GetName]声称！\n\n我们的海军部则对这些课题抱有不同的看法，并建议我们立刻让舰队返回母星系，直到这种全新的未知物质的来源被探明为止。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:905`

### English

Eager and anxious discourse started in the scientific circles of [Root.GetHomeWorldName] today as [cloned_physicist.GetName] published [cloned_physicist.GetHerHis] recent findings of spontaneously appearing negative mass particles close to §Y[Root.Capital.GetStarName]§!.\n\n"These findings could advance our understanding of artificial wormholes and a host of other subjects," proclaims [cloned_physicist.GetName]!\n\nOur Admiralty holds a different view on the subject and suggests we bring our fleets home until the source of this new and untested matter can be determined.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1100`

## colony.9810.desc.robot

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9810.desc.robot&lang=en)

### 中文

我们收到了一份尖端科学单元[cloned_physicist.GetName]发来的新报告。报告中阐述了关于§Y[Root.Capital.GetStarName]§!附近自发生成的负相物质粒子的最新发现。\n\n“这些发现可以促进[Root.GetName]对人工虫洞等其他诸多课题的理解，”[cloned_physicist.GetName]在报告中称。\n\n威胁分析子例程则建议在这一全新的不明物质被鉴定完毕之前先让舰队返回母星系。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:906`

### English

A new report from lead physics unit [cloned_physicist.GetName] was received today. The report included recent findings of the spontaneously appearing negative mass particles close to §Y[Root.Capital.GetStarName]§!.\n\n"These findings could advance the [Root.GetName] understanding of artificial wormholes immensely as well as related subjects," reports science unit [cloned_physicist.GetName].\n\nThreat analysis subroutines suggest bringing fleets home until the source of this new and untested material can be determined.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1101`

## colony.9810.desc.hive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9810.desc.hive&lang=en)

### 中文

我们收到了物理特化工蜂[cloned_physicist.GetName]的新观测结果，内容是关于§Y[Root.Capital.GetStarName]§!附近自发生成的负相物质离子的最新发现。\n\n“这些发现可以促进[Root.GetName]对人工虫洞等其他诸多课题的理解，”工蜂反馈称。\n\n我们的意识不信任这一突然的发展和独特的感觉，敦促部分舰队返回母星系。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:907`

### English

New observations from physics specialized drone [cloned_physicist.GetName] have been disseminated regarding the recent findings of spontaneously appearing negative mass particles close to §Y[Root.Capital.GetStarName]§!.\n\n"These findings could advance the [Root.GetName] understanding of artificial wormholes and additional related subjects," reports the drone.\n\nThe Mind is untrusting of this sudden development and instinctively feels an urge to return some fleets to the spawning system.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1102`

## colony.9815.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9815.name&lang=en)

### 中文

天裂之夜

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:908`

### English

The Night the Sky Split

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1104`

## colony.9815.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9815.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]最伟大的头脑汇聚一堂，倾听[cloned_physicist.GetName]关于利用这些负向物质离子的最新想法，没想到报告被突然打断了。刺耳的警报声响彻恒星系中的每一座观测站和空间站，§Y[Root.GetHomeWorldName]§!的夜空中产生了前所未见的异象。地平线上，一道与我们的恒星一般大小的裂隙撕开了夜空，一支小型武装舰队从裂缝中鱼贯而出。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:909`

### English

The greatest minds of the [Root.GetName] gathered today to listen to [cloned_physicist.GetName]'s latest ideas for using the negative mass particles, only to be interrupted. Emergency sirens screeched in every observatory and space station across the system as §Y[Root.GetHomeWorldName]§! got a new fixture in the night sky. A rip in space the size of our star stretches over horizons while a small fleet of fighters pours through the tear.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1105`

## colony.9815.desc.robot

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9815.desc.robot&lang=en)

### 中文

为了研究[cloned_physicist.GetName]关于负相物质粒子的最新发现，我们部署了额外的科学单元，但不想这一过程被打断了。刺耳的警报声响彻恒星系中的每一座观测站和空间站，§Y[Root.GetHomeWorldName]§!的夜空中产生了前所未见的异象。一道与我们的恒星一般大小的裂隙撕开了夜空，一支小型武装舰队从裂缝中鱼贯而出。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:910`

### English

Additional science units had been deployed to study [cloned_physicist.GetName]'s latest findings and the use of the negative mass particles only to be interrupted. Emergency sirens screeched in every observatory and space station across the system as §Y[Root.GetHomeWorldName]§! got a new fixture in the night sky. A rip in space the size of our star stretches over the sky as a small fleet of fighters pours out of the tear.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1106`

## colony.9815.desc.hive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9815.desc.hive&lang=en)

### 中文

为了研究[cloned_physicist.GetName]关于负相物质粒子的最新发现，我们部署了额外的工蜂，但不想这一过程被打断了。刺耳的警报声响彻恒星系中的每一座观测站和空间站，§Y[Root.GetHomeWorldName]§!的夜空中产生了前所未见的异象。一道与我们的恒星一般大小的裂隙撕开了夜空，一支小型武装舰队从裂缝中鱼贯而出。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:911`

### English

Additional drones had been deployed to study [cloned_physicist.GetName]'s latest findings and the use of the negative mass particles only to be interrupted. Emergency sirens screeched in every observatory and space station across the system as §Y[Root.GetHomeWorldName]§! got a new fixture in the night sky. A rip in space the size of our star stretches over the sky as a small fleet of fighters pours out of the tear.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1107`

## colony.9820.name

### 中文

旧版编码

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:912`

### English

An Old Code

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1109`

## colony.9820.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9820.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

随着最后一艘敌舰被击毁，在我们恒星基地内一位操作探测器的尉官汇报了一件怪事。这些舰船的外观与[Root.GetName]在多年前已经停用的型号别无二致。同时这些舰船也不具有超光速引擎，武器也相对老旧。汇报尚未结束时，一道通讯请求信号从怪异的裂口中发出，响彻了整个星系。这个信号使用的竟是我们最高级别的编码……不过是10年前的版本。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:913`

### English

As the last corvette falls, an ensign manning the sensors of our starbase reports something curious. These ships are very similar to outdated vessels that the [Root.GetName] stopped using years ago. The ships are lacking FTL drives and are equipped with obsolete weaponry. While the report is coming in, a communication request originating from the strange tear reverberates throughout the system. It's using our highest clearance codes... from 10 years ago.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1110`

## colony.9820.desc.robot

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9820.desc.robot&lang=en)

### 中文

随着最后一艘敌舰停止运转，观测分析子例程汇报了一件怪事。这些入侵者的舰船外观与[Root.GetName]在多年前已经停止生产的型号别无二致。而且，这些舰船也不具有超光速引擎，武器也相对老旧。汇报尚未结束时，一道通讯请求信号从怪异的裂口中发出，响彻了整个星系。这个信号使用的竟是我们最高级别的信号交换协议……不过是10年前的版本。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:914`

### English

As the last corvette is rendered inoperable, observation analysis subroutines report something abnormal. The invader ships are similar to outdated vessels that the [Root.GetName] stopped producing years ago. Additionally, the ships lack FTL drives and are equipped with obsolete weaponry. While the report is coming in, a communication request originating from the strange tear reverberates throughout the system. It's using our most complex handshaking protocols... from 10 years ago.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1111`

## colony.9820.desc.hive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9820.desc.hive&lang=en)

### 中文

随着最后一艘敌舰被击毁，一个运转传感器的工蜂汇报了一件怪事。这些舰船的外观与[Root.GetName]在多年前已经停用的型号别无二致。同时这些舰船也不具有超光速引擎，武器也相对老旧。汇报尚未结束时，一道通讯请求信号从怪异的裂口中发出，响彻了整个星系。它是一个主脑不熟悉的、另一个[Root.GetSpeciesName]意识。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:915`

### English

As the last corvette falls, a drone manning the sensors reports something curious. These ships are very similar to outdated vessels that the [Root.GetName] stopped using years ago. The ships are lacking FTL drives and are equipped with obsolete weaponry. While the report is coming in, a communication request originating from the strange tear reverberates throughout the system. It's something this Mind is unfamiliar with, another [Root.GetSpeciesName] intelligence.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1112`

## colony.9820.a.robot

跨次元呼叫

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:917`

### English

A Trans-Dimensional Call

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1115`

## colony.9821.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9821.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

随着通讯画面一同闪烁显现那张面孔在[Root.GetSpeciesName]看来是前所未见地疲惫、欣喜而又惶恐。[cloned_physicist.GetHeShe]看起来太眼熟了，简直就是[cloned_physicist.GetName]老了20岁的翻版。§B“呃……你好[Root.GetHomeWorldName]-阿尔法，我是[cloned_physicist.GetName]-贝塔”§!他停顿了一下，回了回神。§B“我，唔，我们……我们对刚才发生的意外非常抱歉。希望你们能理解，我们星球的防御集体中有一小撮冥顽不灵分子认为他们可以通过武力来夺取[Root.GetHomeWorldName]。我知道我的解释听起来像是狡辩，但请相信我，我们真的没有恶意。”§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:918`

### English

As the comms screen flickers on, what looks to be the most exhausted, elated, and simultaneously terrified [Root.GetSpeciesName] imaginable appears. They look familiar, a dead ringer for [cloned_physicist.GetName] if [cloned_physicist.GetHeShe] had aged 20 years. §B"Ah... hello [Root.GetHomeWorldName]-Alpha, this is [cloned_physicist.GetName]-Beta"§! they stop to gather themselves. §B"I, uh, we... We are terribly sorry for what just transpired. You have to understand, that was a rogue and defunct element of our world's defense collective thinking they could seize [Root.GetHomeWorldName] by force. I know the words might sound empty, but please believe me when I say that we have no ill intention towards you."§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1116`

## colony.9821.desc.robot

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9821.desc.robot&lang=en)

### 中文

随着通讯画面闪烁显现，一串身份识别码被传来。§R校验和错误：§!这是[cloned_physicist.GetName]的专属ID！\n§B“你好[Root.GetName]-阿尔法，很抱歉：此前的敌意行为是由[Root.GetName]-贝塔系统中冗余失效的分支军事机构导致的。它已被处理并删除。陈述：我们没有进一步敌意行动的意图。”§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:919`

### English

As the comms screen flickers on, an identification code is sent through. §RChecksum error:§! This is [cloned_physicist.GetName]s unique ID! \n§B"Greetings [Root.GetName]-Alpha and apologies: Previous hostile action was caused by a redundant and deprecated military branch of [Root.GetName]-Beta systems. It has since been dealt with and deleted. Statement: We intend no further hostile interactions."§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1117`

## colony.9821.desc.hive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9821.desc.hive&lang=en)

### 中文

随着通讯画面闪烁显现，另一个意识的通讯工蜂拥有与[cloned_physicist.GetName]相同的意识结构。怪异。§B“你好[Root.GetName]-阿尔法，很抱歉。此前对你们发起攻击的工蜂是一些托管于独立设备过久的反常子个体。对于你们将其清除干净，[Root.GetName]-贝塔表示非常感谢。我们没有进一步负面行动的意图，愿意提出一些对我们意识双方均有利的提案。”§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:920`

### English

As the comms screen flickers on the other Mind communication drone has an identical structure as [cloned_physicist.GetName]. Curious. §B"Greetings [Root.GetName]-Alpha and apologies. The drones that attacked you were deviants left to their own devices too long. [Root.GetName]-Beta appreciates that you exterminated them. We desire no more negative interactions and want to propose something beneficial for both our minds."§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1118`

## colony.9821.a.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9821.a.response&lang=en)

### 中文

§B我以及这边的所有人来自一个与你们宇宙相似的平行宇宙。在我们也不清楚的过去某个时刻，我们两个现实发生了分支，我们的宇宙从那时起就一直在逐渐崩溃中。我们首先是从遥远的星系开始消失发现这件事的，并非常努力地研究以尝试避免这种末日的降临。在最近的几十年内，我们的一切资源和[Root.GetAdjective]时间都被投入到打开这个跨次元传送门上。只有通过这种最为试验性的技术和最尖端的物理学我们才能与你们会面，而现在离我们这边的现实毁灭已经不剩几年了。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:923`

### English

§BI and everyone else here are from a parallel universe to yours. We don't know when, but we believe that our two realities split some time ago, and our universe has been unraveling ever since. We first noticed when remote systems started disappearing and have been hard at work trying to avoid certain doom. For the last few decades, all our resources and [Root.GetAdjective]-hours have been invested in opening this trans-dimensional gateway. It's only with our most experimental technology and advanced physics that we have managed to reach you, just a few years before our reality ceases to be.§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1121`

## colony.9821.a.response.robot

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9821.a.response.robot&lang=en)

### 中文

§B[cloned_physicist.GetName]-贝塔以及目前其他的所有单位都是来自平行宇宙。我们首次观测到贝塔宇宙的崩塌是在数十年之前，而首次发现遥远星系开始消失则是在55.136年之前。从那时起，我们调动了一切可用的资源与计算能力来打开这个跨次元传送门。据测算，[Root.GetHomeWorldName]-贝塔只剩下25.3333年寿命了。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:924`

### English

§B[cloned_physicist.GetName]-Beta and all other present units are from a parallel universe. Deterioration of universe Beta was first observed decades ago. Remote systems were first observed to disappear 55.136 years ago. Since then, all available resources and computation power have been put into opening this trans-dimensional gateway. Estimated time left until [Root.GetHomeWorldName]-Betas erasure: 25.3333 years.§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1122`

## colony.9821.a.response.hive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9821.a.response.hive&lang=en)

### 中文

§B我，[cloned_physicist.GetName]-贝塔，以及目前其他所有属于蜂巢意识的成员都是来自一个与你们相似的平行宇宙。虽然不知道是何时开始，但我们确信很久以前我们共处的现实被一分为二，而归属于我们的那一半从那时起便逐渐走向崩溃。侦察工蜂在第一次发现遥远星系开始消失后发现了这件事，之后蜂巢意识便竭尽全力阻止末日的降临。在过去的数十年中，我们投入了所有的资源与研究力量，动员了全部工蜂来打开这个跨次元传送门。只有通过这种最为试验性的技术和最尖端的物理学我们才能与你们会面，这离我们这边的现实毁灭已经不剩几年了。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:925`

### English

§BThis one, [cloned_physicist.GetName]-Beta, and all other members of the Mind present are from a parallel universe to yours. We don't know when, but we believe that some time ago our two realities split and our universe has been unraveling ever since. Observation drones first noticed when remote systems started disappearing, and this Mind has been hard at work trying to avoid certain doom ever since. For the last few decades, all drones, resources, and study has been invested in opening this trans-dimensional gateway. It's only with our most experimental technology and advanced physics that we have managed to connect with you, a few years before this reality ceases to be.§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1123`

## colony.9821.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9821.b&lang=en)

### 中文

为什么你们好像没有超光速能力？

### English

Why do you seem to be lacking FTL capabilities?

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1124`

## colony.9821.b.robot

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9821.b.robot&lang=en)

### 中文

阐明缺失超光速系统原因！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:927`

### English

Explain lack of FTL systems!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1125`

## colony.9821.b.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9821.b.response&lang=en)

### 中文

[cloned_physicist.GetName]-贝塔看起来被问住了，§B“你们成功地突破了光速？我的天啊这真是个天大的好消息！那么那些傻瓜真的没有理由进攻你们——整个星系我们都触手可及！唔……当然，前提是你们乐意帮助我们。”§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:928`

### English

[cloned_physicist.GetName]-Beta seems at a loss for words, §B"You managed to crack FTL? Oh, what glorious news! Then those fools had no reason to attack - the whole galaxy is available to us! Um... assuming that you would be willing to help us, of course."§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1126`

## colony.9821.b.response.robot

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9821.b.response.robot&lang=en)

### 中文

§B用于通过超空间航道的超光速系统在理论上是可行的。不过我们认为该项目并非必要，而且所有资源都被投入到了传送门的研究之中，因此许多研究的优先级都被降至0。通讯结束后向[Root.GetName]-阿尔法查询结果？§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:929`

### English

§BFTL systems capable of accessing hyperlanes were calculated to be possible but the project was declared non-essential and the resources were absorbed into the Gateway Research Collective. Many avenues of research had their priority set to 0. Request for [Root.GetName]-Alpha's results after communication is over?§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1127`

## colony.9821.b.response.hive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9821.b.response.hive&lang=en)

### 中文

孪生意识蜂巢意识停顿了一会，才开始回答：§B“蜂巢意识不愿意在宇宙行将灭亡之时在超光速领域投入研究资源。请问能允许我们加入您的意识吗？”§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:930`

### English

The other mind seems to need a moment before answering §B"This Mind was unwilling to invest resources into developing FTL systems when the universe was ceasing to be. Please allow us to partake in this understanding?"§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1128`

## colony.9822.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9822.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§B我们的宇宙岌岌可危。而我们对此束手无策。我们为了这一天已经建造了巨型的运输船，恳求你们接纳我们的难民。只有最聪明和最优秀的领袖才有资格登上这艘船。请救救我的……§!§Y我们的§!§B文明。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:933`

### English

§BOur universe is doomed. There is no way to get around that. We have been building giant transport ships for this day and implore you to let us send over refugees. Only our brightest and most capable leaders will fit on these ships. Please save my-§! §YOUR§! §Bcivilization.§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1132`

## colony.9822.desc.robot

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9822.desc.robot&lang=en)

### 中文

§B我们已经建成了运输船，请求转移所有[Root.GetName]-贝塔之中的单位，并保全我们的资源与数据库，希望我们对浩瀚星宇的征程不会就此止步。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:934`

### English

§BTransportation ships have been built. Request to send over [Root.GetName]-Beta units to preserve resources and databanks. We do not wish to end our exploration of the universe here.§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1133`

## colony.9822.desc.hive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9822.desc.hive&lang=en)

### 中文

§B[Root.GetName]-贝塔岌岌可危，而我们对此束手无策。我们为了这一天已经建造了巨型的运输船，并会尽可能输送我们的子个体。\n此外我们还希望能够融入你们的蜂巢意识。尽管诞生之初你我并无不同，但是随着时间推移，我们的经历与见闻也成为了无法割舍的东西。请让我们一同共存吧。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:935`

### English

§B[Root.GetName]-Beta's universe is doomed. There is no way to get around that. We have been building giant transport ships for this very day and would offer any drones we could send.\nAdditionally we wish to merge hiveminds. While identical at first this collective has had experiences and insights we don't want to lose. Please let us live on.§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1134`

## colony.9840.name

### 中文

峰会

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:939`

### English

The Summit

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1139`

## colony.9840.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9840.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

各领域的杰出人才在§Y[Root.GetName]§!上汇聚一堂，决定我们的异次元孪生文明的终极命运。在[Root.GetHomeWorldName]上发生了无数激烈而艰难的论战。有的人建议不要干涉，研究一个宇宙的消亡一定能收获颇丰。也有说法称应该接受[cloned_physicist.GetName]-贝塔的请求，尽可能多地撤离那颗行星上的人们。还有人提到趁机将他们留在星球上的有价值的资源及能量储存搜刮一空简直易如反掌。\n\n随着峰会议论氛围的逐渐缓和，[cloned_physicist.GetName]走上讲台，[cloned_physicist.GetHeShe]宣称使用贝塔宇宙带来的新科技，应该有机会将整个[Root.GetHomeWorldName]-贝塔带到我们的现实中。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:940`

### English

A summit of the top minds from every field gathered on §Y[Root.GetName]§! to discuss the fate of our dimensional twins. Harsh and difficult debates were had throughout [Root.GetHomeWorldName]. Some suggested a hands-off approach. Studying the end of a universe could be very insightful. There was talk about granting [cloned_physicist.GetName]-Beta's request to evacuate as much of the planet as possible while some mentioned how easy it would be to take whatever precious resources and energy stores the planet had left.\n\nJust as the summit was winding down, [cloned_physicist.GetName] took center stage and declared that [cloned_physicist.GetHeShe] believed that using the new technologies brought from the beta universe, there was a chance to bring the entirety of [Root.GetHomeWorldName]-Beta into our reality.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1140`

## colony.9840.desc.robot

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9840.desc.robot&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]控制下所有的超级计算机一齐运转，试图计算出关于当前[Root.GetName]-贝塔两难处境的最佳解决方案。最终，4个选项被认定可行。\n\n1：研究贝塔宇宙的崩塌。\n2：进入传送门并夺取一切有价值的矿物与能源储备。\n3：通过科学单元：[cloned_physicist.GetName]-贝塔的提议。\n4：想办法利用高度试验性的贝塔世界科学技术，将[Root.GetHomeWorldName]-贝塔带入阿尔法宇宙。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:941`

### English

Every supercomputer under the control of [Root.GetName] was put on calculating the most optimal solution to the current dilemma regarding [Root.GetName]-Beta. In the end, 4 potential options were declared viable.\n\n1: Study collapse of Beta universe.\n2: Enter gateway and acquire all relevant stockpiles of minerals and energy.\n3: Allow science unit: [cloned_physicist.GetName]-Beta's proposal.\n4: Attempt to use highly experimental Beta science and technology to bring [Root.GetHomeWorldName]-Beta into Alpha reality.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1141`

## colony.9840.desc.hive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9840.desc.hive&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetSpeciesName]全力思考如何决定[Root.GetName]-贝塔的命运后，4个潜在选线被给出。\n\n1：研究贝塔宇宙的崩塌。\n2：进入传送门并夺取一切有价值的矿物与能源储备。\n3：接受[Root.GetName]-贝塔的请求，接纳孪生意识。\n4：想办法利用高度试验性的贝塔世界科学技术，将[Root.GetHomeWorldName]-贝塔带入阿尔法宇宙，合并孪生意识。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:942`

### English

As the full might of [Root.GetSpeciesName]'s intelligence was put on the task of deciding the fate of [Root.GetName]-Beta 4 main potential solutions were proposed.\n\n1: Study collapse of the Beta universe.\n2: Enter the gateway and acquire all relevant stockpiles of minerals and energy.\n3: Accept [Root.GetName]-Beta request and subsume the other Mind.\n4: Attempt to use highly experimental Beta science and technology to bring [Root.GetHomeWorldName]-Beta into Alpha reality merging Minds.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1142`

## colony.9850.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9850.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一道无形的虚无裂隙撕裂了§B[Root.GetHomeWorldName]-贝塔§!的天空，就在[cloned_physicist.GetName]-阿尔法和贝塔正在通力合作完成他们艰难的任务的过程中，那道虚无也正在步步紧逼。负相物质稳定器激活后，有如虚空裂可能将其吞噬一般，世界间的缝隙拂过[Root.GetHomeWorldName]-贝塔。缝隙很快化作一缕涟漪消逝了，负向物质逐渐不再在原有的位置显现。\n\n终于完成了。在数个月对全新重力井和宜居带的的学习和研究之后，[Root.Capital.GetStarName]现在拥有了另一颗环绕着它的全新宜居星球。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:958`

### English

A formless static covers a large swath of §B[Root.GetHomeWorldName]-Beta's§! sky and is closing in rapidly as [cloned_physicist.GetName]-Alpha and Beta, working side by side, finish their arduous task. The negative mass stabilizers activate, and the tear between worlds moves over [Root.GetHomeWorldName]-Beta just as the static would smother it. The rift winks out moments later, and negative mass stops appearing at its former location.\n\nIt is done. After months of study and research accounting for new gravitational wells and habitable zones, [Root.Capital.GetStarName] now has a new inhabited planet orbiting it.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1159`

## colony.9850.desc.robot

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9850.desc.robot&lang=en)

### 中文

一道无形的虚无裂隙撕裂了§B[Root.GetHomeWorldName]-贝塔§!的天空，就在[cloned_physicist.GetName]-阿尔法和贝塔的科学单元正在通力合作的过程中，那道虚无也正在步步紧逼。负相物质稳定器激活后，有如虚空裂可能将其吞噬一般，世界间的缝隙拂过[Root.GetHomeWorldName]-贝塔。缝隙很快化作一缕涟漪消逝了，负向物质逐渐不再在原有的位置显现。\n\n终于完成了。在数个月对全新重力井和宜居带的的学习和研究之后，[Root.Capital.GetStarName]现在拥有了另一颗环绕着它的全新宜居星球。 

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:959`

### English

A formless static covers a large swath of §B[Root.GetHomeWorldName]-Beta's§! sky and is closing in rapidly as [cloned_physicist.GetName]-Alpha and Beta and all other available science units work side by side. The negative mass stabilizers activate, and the tear between worlds moves over [Root.GetHomeWorldName]-Beta just as the static would smother it. The rift winks out moments later, and negative mass stops being produced at its former location.\n\nIt is done. After months of study and research accounting for new gravitational wells and habitable zones, [Root.Capital.GetStarName] now has a new inhabited planet orbiting it.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1160`

## colony.9850.desc.hive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9850.desc.hive&lang=en)

### 中文

一道无形的虚无裂隙撕裂了§B[Root.GetHomeWorldName]-贝塔§!的天空，就在[cloned_physicist.GetName]-阿尔法和贝塔的工蜂正在通力合作的过程中，那道虚无也正在步步紧逼。负相物质稳定器激活后，有如虚空裂可能将其吞噬一般，世界间的缝隙拂过[Root.GetHomeWorldName]-贝塔。缝隙很快化作一缕涟漪消逝了，负向物质逐渐不再在原有的位置显现。\n\n终于完成了。新的视点和意识加入了[Root.GetName]，并在整个集体意识中激起涟漪。在数个月对全新重力井和宜居带的的学习和研究之后，[Root.Capital.GetStarName]现在拥有了另一颗环绕着它的全新宜居星球。 

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:960`

### English

A formless static covers a large swath of §B[Root.GetHomeWorldName]-Beta's§! sky and is closing in rapidly as [cloned_physicist.GetName]-Alpha and Beta and all other available drones work side by side. The negative mass stabilizers activate, and the tear between worlds moves over [Root.GetHomeWorldName]-Beta just as the static would smother it. The rift winks out moments later, and negative mass stops being produced at its former location.\n\nIt is done. The new perspectives and minds joining the [Root.GetName] send a ripple through the Collective. After months of study and research accounting for new gravitational wells and habitable zones, [Root.Capital.GetStarName] now has a new inhabited planet orbiting it.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1161`

就这一次，大家都活下来了！

### English

Just this once, everybody lives!

## colony.9851.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9851.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

停留在贝塔宇宙的探测器记录着那个世界最后的时刻，一道无形的虚无之壁吞噬了[Root.Capital.GetStarName]-贝塔，随后§B[Root.GetHomeWorldName]-贝塔§!也难逃厄运。裂隙开合之前的瞬间，探测器就突然内失去了联系，而裂隙产生的负相粒子也一齐消失了。\n\n另一次元的一切都不复存在了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:963`

### English

Probes left in the Beta universe record the final moments of reality, as a formless static wall absorbs first [Root.Capital.GetStarName]-Beta and then §B[Root.GetHomeWorldName]-Beta§!. The feed is abruptly cut off shortly before the rift winks out of existence. The negative mass particles it was producing are likewise gone.\n\nAnyone left on that side is no more.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1165`

### English

穿越次元的边界

### English

## colony.9855.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9855.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

自从裂隙出现之后，一种现象开始蔓延。处在我们次元的人们跑到他们在贝塔宇宙对应的孪生家庭那里，然后带他们偷渡到我们这个稳定的次元来。而偷渡很多时候就是由负责阻隔两个宇宙的边防哨卫进行的，毕竟[Owner.GetName]上谁在那边没有孪生家庭呢？如果只有一小撮人偷渡也就算了，但要是不加以制止，事情就会演变成恐慌性的逃跑。如果§B[Owner.GetHomeWorldName]-贝塔§!的居民流失过多，那边的基础设施就会随之崩溃，那样的话想要将他们的星球转移过来就更难了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:966`

### English

A common issue has started occurring ever since the rift appeared. Families in our dimension are reaching out to their Beta counterparts and smuggling them into our stable universe. This is often done by the border guards assigned to keeping the universes separate, as basically everyone in the [Owner.GetName] has family on the other side. A few groups doing this wouldn't matter, but we would quickly have a stampede on our hands if the practice spreads. If §B[Owner.GetHomeWorldName]-Beta§! loses too many inhabitants, their infrastructure will collapse, and it will be even harder to get the world through.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1169`

### English

## colony.9860.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9860.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在这么近的地方有一个镜像世界的好处就在于，如果[Owner.GetSpeciesName]的文化遗产被破坏或遗失，你或许可以在这个世界中找到备份，这样大伙们又可以重新瞻仰这些瑰宝了。不过一些可恶的家伙却以此为契机偷偷潜入§B[Target.GetName]-贝塔§!，然后将这些宝物窃为己有。结果我们现在有了整整两份由我们历史中某位伟大创作者缔造的杰作。[Target.GetName]-贝塔的政府现在向我们发来一份措辞严厉而正式的通告，要求我们归还被窃的作品。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:971`

### English

One of the great things about having a mirror world so near is that [Owner.GetSpeciesName] cultural pillars that might have been destroyed or lost in the last few years might still exist on the other planet, so everyone can enjoy them again. Yet unsavory characters have taken this as an opportunity to infiltrate §B[Target.GetName]-Beta§! and secure these great works for themselves. We now possess not one but two masterworks made by a great inventor from our past. [Target.GetName]-Beta's government has already sent an angry yet formal request for us to return the plunder.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1175`

最后的请求

### English

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1180`

## colony.9870.desc.helped

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9870.desc.helped&lang=en)

### 中文

贝塔宇宙的情况十万火急、每况愈下。今天上午传送门里又传来了联合呼救信号。即便我们尽了最大的努力，但可能我们的异次元亲戚们最终还是会被猎杀着他们宇宙的虚无所吞噬。如果我们想要拯救另一个版本的自己，那现在就是最后的机会了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:976`

### English

The situation in the Beta-Universe is dire, and it's only getting worse. A unified cry for salvation was heard from the portal earlier this morning. Even with our best efforts, it seems like our kin will become one with the nothingness haunting their universe. If we want to save our alter selves, the time is now.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1181`

## colony.9870.desc.ignored

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9870.desc.ignored&lang=en)

### 中文

今天，贝塔宇宙的传送门中再次传出了可怜的呼救声，啜泣着请求着我们的救援。我们从他们的惨境中获利已经有些时日了，因为无法在那个宇宙中与任何东西取得联系，他们还在尝试着向我们大声呼救。不可挽回的分水岭正在靠近，而很快，贝塔[Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural]就将不复存在了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:977`

### English

A pitiful cry for help was heard from the Beta-Universe portal today, a final whimper for salvation. We have been benefiting from their situation for a while, yet they still call out to us since there is no one to talk to in their lonely universe. We are approaching the point of no return. Soon, the Beta-[Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural] will be no more.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1182`

## colony.9875.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9875.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

拥有第二个如假包换的[Owner.GetGovernmentName]给我们带来了一个意料之外的机遇。用来让我们的意识保持日常组织化的计算需要占用许多[Owner.GetTeamMemberPlural]，但这个任务现在可以分流到第二个[Root.GetName]上。这样做的价值是显而易见的，尽管可能会拖慢§B[Target.GetName]贝塔§!的拯救工作，但我们相信一切仍在掌控之中。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:983`

### English

Having a second [Owner.GetGovernmentName] which is so similar provides us with an unprecedented opportunity. The calculations required to keep our consciousness organized every day take up many [Owner.GetTeamMemberPlural], but this task can now be offloaded to the second [Root.GetName]. The value of this is apparent, and while it might slow down the recovery effort of §B[Target.GetName]-Beta§!, we are sure we will still manage.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1189`

### 中文

在持续的困境下，另一个[Owner.GetGovernmentName]终于失去理智，狂躁地想利用[Owner.GetSoldierPlural]夺取[Target.GetName]！我们必须在防线被冲垮前组织好自己的防御子个体。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:988`

### English

In their continued distress, the other [Owner.GetGovernmentName] has lashed out and manically taken a hold of the [Owner.GetSoldierPlural] on [Target.GetName]! We must organize our defense drones before we are overrun.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1195`

## colony.9885.desc.hive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9885.desc.hive&lang=en)

### 中文

最后一只变异工蜂被击败了。我们的通讯工蜂再一次探测到了另一个意识的存在，但现在它变得更加地温顺，等待着我们启动通讯。\n\n“§B我们对此表示歉意：随着§R虚无§!的逼近，本能反应已经越来越难以压制了。我们保证在未来会严加管束自己。§!”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:993`

### English

The final divergent drone has been defeated. Our communication drones are once again detecting the presence of the other Overmind, but it's acting a lot submissively this time, waiting for us to initiate communication.\n\n"§BThis Mind apologises - instinctive reactions are getting harder to suppress as §RThe Static§! nears. We will ensure that we restrain ourselves in the future.§!"

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1201`

## colony.9885.desc.machine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9885.desc.machine&lang=en)

### 中文

叛乱的繁育单元被击败了。我们的观测分析子例程再次探测到了来自[FromFrom.GetName]贝塔的通讯单元。他们只留下了一条简单的讯息。\n\n“§B多余的防御单元再一次以非最佳方式行动。随着§R虚无§!的迫近，它们成功地突破了我们的隔离措施。这个进程已经被删除了，不会再有进一步的行为。§!”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:994`

### English

The rebelling defense unit has been defeated. Our observation analysis subroutines are once again detecting the presence of [FromFrom.GetName]-Beta's communication units. They left us with a simple message.\n\n"§BOnce again redundant defense units have acted in sub-optimal ways. As the §RThe Static§! approaches, they managed to get through our quarantining efforts. The processes have since been deleted and should not take any further action.§!"

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1202`

### English

### English
