## negative_mass_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anegative_mass_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的母星周围最近出现了负相物质。这种奇怪的现象肯定不是动摇我们社会根基的事情的预兆。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1034`

### English

Negative Mass has recently appeared around our homeworld. The strange phenomenon surely won't be a herald of something that will shake our society's very core.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1249`

## SINGING_PLANET_CAT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASINGING_PLANET_CAT&lang=en)

### 中文

怪异读数

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1036`

### English

Strange Readings

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1253`

## SINGING_PLANET_CAT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASINGING_PLANET_CAT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

这颗行星发射出怪异的磁力波，与我们目前为止所知的一切均大不相同，几乎可以说它就像想要与我们沟通一般。唯有对这一不同寻常的现象进行调查方才符合逻辑——我们可能遇上了一个行星大小生物形态的绝佳个例。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1037`

### English

This planet emits strange magnetic waves, quite unlike anything we have seen so far, almost as if it is trying to communicate with us. It is only logical to investigate this unusual phenomenon - we may have just encountered an extraordinary example of life on a planetary scale.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1254`

## anomaly.7101.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7101.name&lang=en)

### 中文

咏唱的行星

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1038`

### English

Singing Planet

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1256`

## anomaly.7101.standard.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7101.standard.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们起先认为这是一种刻意的交流尝试，结果发现它不过是一种不常见的自然现象。这颗行星产生的磁力波只要进行合适的调整和编辑，就可以听起来像歌唱一般，但这肯定不是由任何有意识的个体产生的。然而我们的一些科学家也发现，这“歌声”相当地吸引人。\n\n也许我们可以利用这一现象，比如招待艺术家或VIP到空间站中，然后捕获这些信号，把它们作为一种放松的音乐播放给他们听。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1039`

### English

What we first assumed to be a deliberate attempt at communication has turned out to be an unusual but naturally occurring phenomenon. The magnetic waves produced by the planet, given proper handling and editing, may sound a bit like talking or even singing, but they are in no way created by a thinking being. Some of our scientists, however, find it quite catchy.\n\nIt might be possible to make use of this phenomenon, possibly by hosting artists or VIPs at a station created solely to catch these signals and play them as relaxing music.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1257`

## anomaly.7101.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7101.a&lang=en)

### 中文

令人着迷。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1040`

### English

Fascinating.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1258`

## anomaly.7101.machines.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7101.machines.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这颗行星发射出了一系列信号，我们的探索无人机认为它们应该是人造的。但在进一步的检测后，我们发现这些信号不过是一些自然发生的不寻常但无害的磁力波。这一发现并没有产生即时有效的收益，但由于实在是太特别，在长期的研究中它可以提供一些研究数据。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1041`

### English

The planet is emitting a series of signals that our exploratory drones speculated might be of artificial origin. Upon closer examination, the signals were revealed to be merely a natural phenomenon caused by unusual but harmless magnetic waves. This discovery does not provide any immediate benefit to the collective, but given their uniqueness, studying them may provide us with some research data in the long run.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1259`

## anomaly.7101.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7101.b&lang=en)

### 中文

记录它。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1042`

### English

Catalog it.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1260`

## anomaly.7101.hive.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7101.hive.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这颗行星发射出了一系列信号，我们的探索工蜂认为它们应该是人造的。但在进一步的检测后，我们发现这些信号不过是一些自然现象，它的独特性质可以用于增强我们的工蜂与主脑的连接。用这颗行星产生的磁力波照射工蜂后它们可以更好地响应主脑的意志。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1043`

### English

The planet is emitting a series of signals that our exploratory drones speculated might be of artificial origin. Upon closer examination, they turned out to be nothing more than a natural phenomenon, one with the unusual property of strengthening the connection of our drones to the collective mind. It seems that drones exposed to the planet's magnetic waves respond better to the will of the hive.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1261`

## anomaly.7101.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7101.c&lang=en)

### 中文

一切为了蜂巢。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1044`

### English

Everything for the hive.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1262`

## anomaly.7101.c.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7101.c.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

蜂巢可以复制这些磁力波，用于增强自己的意志。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1045`

### English

The hive can replicate these magnetic waves and use them to strengthen its will.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1263`

## colony.9200.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9200.name&lang=en)

### 中文

隐藏地库

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1047`

### English

Hidden Vault

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1267`

## colony.9200.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9200.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在一次对[Root.GetName]的例行探索中，行星探索单位发现了一座巨大的大门，其后很可能具有整座大山规模的内部空间。看来是其中隐形装置发出的某种强磁场导致飞船的探测器没有发现这里，而伪装过的大门也让使其对地表测绘探测器隐形了。\n\n另一方面，更专门的测试显示，向其输入稳定的能量流应该可以让大门重新激活，最终也让我们得以一窥其后的秘密。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1048`

### English

During a standard reconnaissance of [Root.GetName], the planetary exploration unit came across a massive door that apparently leads directly into the interior of a mountain range. It appears that the strong magnetic field produced by some sort of cloaking device hid it from our ship's sensors, and the camouflaged entrance remained invisible to the autonomous research probes mapping the surface.\n\nOn the other hand, more specialized tests have shown that delivering a steady stream of energy should make the door operational again, eventually allowing us to open it and see what's inside.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1268`

## colony.9200.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9200.a&lang=en)

### 中文

提供能量。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1049`

### English

Provide the energy.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1269`

## colony.9200.a.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9200.a.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

建起供电网络需要花费一些时间，并且会在探索地下舱室的过程中持续地消耗能量。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1050`

### English

Setting up the grid might take some time, and will continue to sip the energy during the exploration of the vault.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1270`

## colony.9201.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9201.name&lang=en)

### 中文

黑暗等待

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1051`

### English

Darkness Awaits

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1272`

## colony.9201.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9201.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

能量从电路中流过，大门再一次被激活，经过简单的修修补补我们很快明白了大门的工作原理。进一步的拓扑研究揭示了其内部是一个巨大的地下舱室。墙上的每一寸都覆盖着外星基础设施，甚至包括支撑天花板的巨大支柱也释放出若隐若现的能量信号。由于能量读数和与我们科技的模糊相似性，这似乎是一座地下的生产设施。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1052`

### English

As energy flowed through the circuits, the entrance came online, and with additional tinkering, we quickly discovered how the door worked. Additional topographical research revealed the interior to be a large underground vault. Alien infrastructure covered every square centimeter of the walls - even the huge pillars supporting the ceiling showed faint energy signatures. Given the energy readings and vague similarities to our technology, it appears to be an underground manufacturing facility.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1273`

## colony.9201.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9201.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们会研究其中的秘密。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1053`

### English

We will learn it secrets.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1274`

## colony.9201.a.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9201.a.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

由于该工程规模浩大，彻底研究它将花费许多资源、人力以及不可预估的时间。预计会就这项工程的进展进行周期性汇报。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1054`

### English

Given the scope of this operation, it will take a lot of resources, workforce, and an unknown amount of time to study it thoroughly. Regular updates and reports on their work are somewhat to be expected.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1275`

## colony.9201.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9201.b&lang=en)

### 中文

我们不能冒这个险，安放炸药。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1055`

### English

We can't risk it, place explosives.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1276`

## colony.9201.b.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9201.b.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们将封闭入口，并炸毁这个地方。没人知道在地下舱室里到底是会有什么东西在等着我们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1056`

### English

We will seal the entrance and demolish this place. Who knows what might lurk inside this vault.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1277`

## colony.9202.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9202.name&lang=en)

### 中文

山炸了！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1057`

### English

Mountain Blast!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1279`

## colony.9202.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9202.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

距离地下舱室很远的人也能感受到大地在震颤，而近一些的人甚至可以看到硕大的山体碎块飞向空中，在地上产生了一个巨大无比的凹坑，其中散布着岩石、碎片和扭曲的机械部件。关于到底发生了什么事情，有一个很简单的猜测，在试图通过爆破炸毁大门的时候，设施内发生了一系列连锁反应。\n\n不论如何，这座地下舱室最终只剩下这个冒着烟的大弹坑了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1058`

### English

Those far from the vault could feel the ground shake. Those closer could see a large chunk of the mountainside fly off into the air, creating a huge crater filled with rocks, debris, and twisted machinery. A quick guess as to what could have gone wrong led to the conclusion that a chain reaction was set in motion inside the vault when the initial explosion breached the door.\n\nRegardless, a smoking crater is all that is left of the underground vault.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1280`

## colony.9202.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9202.a&lang=en)

### 中文

至少这样更安全了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1059`

### English

It's safer this way.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1281`

## colony.9210.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9210.name&lang=en)

### 中文

失踪！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1060`

### English

Disappearances!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1283`

## colony.9210.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9210.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

研究[Root.GetName]的第一阶段主要关注对入口的测绘。地下舱室的第一个房间是一个巨大的直深入山体的大厅，分支的走廊则又通向更深处。石雕的柱子上装饰着许多华丽的牌匾，每一块上面都描绘着可能是建造这座设施的外星种族。我们无法破译他们的语言，但由这些图画记叙的故事都以战争、纷争和奇迹般的工程为主体。\n\n更恼人的是有一整个建设单位失踪了；他们在汇报了黑暗中发出的某种声响之后就消失得无影无踪。局势有一发不可收拾的风险，需要更多果断举措。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1061`

### English

The initial phase of research on [Root.GetName] was largely focused on mapping the entrance. The first room within the vault is a giant hall, stretching deep into the mountain, with several branching corridors pushing even further into the depths. Various ornate plaques decorate stone chiseled pillars, each depicting the alien race that presumably built this place. We can't decipher their language, but the story told by those art pieces are centered on warfare, struggle, and grandiose projects.\n\nMore disturbing is the loss of an entire construction unit; after reporting the presence of strange sounds in the dark, the unit simply vanished. The situation is in danger of slipping out of control and calls for more decisive action.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1284`

## colony.9210.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9210.a&lang=en)

### 中文

增派安保力量并搜救他们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1062`

### English

Double the security forces and search for them.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1285`

## colony.9210.a.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9210.a.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们会组建一支搜救团队，但许多居民已经料到了最坏的可能。我们还会增派安保人手以避免这种情况再次发生。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1063`

### English

We will form a search team, but a lot of citizens already anticipate the worst. We will also double the guards to prevent further incidents.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1286`

## colony.9210.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9210.b&lang=en)

### 中文

不惜一切代价找到他们！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1064`

### English

Send in everyone, find them!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1287`

## colony.9210.b.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9210.b.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们不能放任他们不管！我们要组建一支志愿队搜寻失踪者！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1065`

### English

We can't leave them to die! We will make a volunteer squad to look for the missing!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1288`

## colony.9210.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9210.c&lang=en)

### 中文

组建搜寻队，让其他人继续工作。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1066`

### English

Form a search party, and put the rest back to work.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1289`

## colony.9210.c.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9210.c.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

可以预见这样的事情可能还会再次发生。我们会尝试找到失踪的工人，但是行动不能拖延，我们已经做好了牺牲的准备。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1067`

### English

It was expected that something like this might have happened. We will try to find the missing workers, but we can't delay our operations - we are ready to pay the price.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1290`

## colony.9210.d

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9210.d&lang=en)

### 中文

令人不安。派遣更多安保子个体。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1068`

### English

Disturbing. Send more guard drones.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1291`

## colony.9210.d.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9210.d.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

失踪的单位会有新的顶替上去，研究要继续。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1069`

### English

Missing units will be replaced with new ones. The research has to continue.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1292`

## colony.9211.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9211.name&lang=en)

### 中文

找到工人

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1070`

### English

Workers Found

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1294`

## colony.9211.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9211.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的一支安保队伍汇报他们找到了失踪的工人。看来这个地下舱室中用于将不速之客关进“小盒子”的陷阱和装置还在运作中，这些设施由可以自行移动的墙体组成。\n\n那些不走运的工人被陷阱杀害了。我们的民众对结果非常愤怒，但至少我们知道该留心什么东西了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1071`

### English

One of our security teams reports that the missing workers have been found. It turns out that this vault still has operational traps and devices that were programmed to trap unwanted visitors within "boxes" comprised of dislocated and moving walls.\n\nThe unfortunate workers were all killed within the trap. Our citizenry is furious at the result, but at least now we know what to look for.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1295`

## colony.9211.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9211.a&lang=en)

### 中文

真恐怖。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1072`

### English

That's terrible.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1296`

## colony.9212.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9212.name&lang=en)

### 中文

救出工人

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1073`

### English

Workers Rescued

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1298`

## colony.9212.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9212.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在派出了大量的搜救团队之后，我们很快就找到了失踪的工人。发现他们的时候，他们正被关在一个立方体牢房一样的结构中，这似乎是由自由移动的墙体构成的。看来地下舱室中的安保系统还在运作，因此，我们之后会更小心地应对这些陷阱装置。尽管如此，人们还是对工人起初没有得到妥善保护而感到愤怒。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1074`

### English

Given the enormous search team, it didn't take long to find the missing workers. The last of them were discovered trapped in a square prison-like structure made of articulated and self-moving walls. It appears that the security systems within the vault are still operational. Given this, we will be more prepared to counter such contraptions in the future. Nonetheless, the population is angry that the workers were not provided with proper safety measures in the first place.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1299`

## colony.9212.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9212.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们挺走运的了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1075`

### English

We got lucky.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1300`

## colony.9213.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9213.name&lang=en)

### 中文

找到工人

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1076`

### English

Workers Found

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1302`

## colony.9213.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9213.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

失踪的工人在一处怪异的陷阱装置中找到了，他们被四面可移动的墙体困在了里面。不幸的是这些个体已经不再对帝国有什么用处了，但至少他们的牺牲揭示了陷阱的存在。现在工作可以继续畅通无阻地进行，而在这里工作的人们心里的石头也落地了，他们知道自己至少现在是安全了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1077`

### English

The missing workers were found in a grotesque contraption that imprisoned them between four movable walls. Unfortunately, these individuals are no longer of any use to the empire, but at least their sacrifice revealed the existence of the trap. The work now continues undisturbed, and the population working on the site feels some relief, knowing that they should be safe - for now.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1303`

## colony.9213.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9213.a&lang=en)

### 中文

他们早就知道这其中的危险。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1078`

### English

They knew the danger.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1304`

## colony.9214.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9214.name&lang=en)

### 中文

找到子个体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1079`

### English

Drones Found

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1306`

## colony.9214.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9214.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

失踪的子个体在一处由可动墙体构成的精巧的陷阱装置中找到了，它们可以互相勾连形成密闭空间。不幸的是这些子个体都已经过度损坏、不堪再用。之后我们的调查子个体就可以利用这些知识来对抗同类陷阱了。工作得以不受干扰地继续进行下去。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1080`

### English

The missing drones were found in an ingenious contraption of moving walls that linked together to create enclosed prison spaces. Unfortunately, all of our drones are damaged beyond use. From now on, our surveying drones will use this knowledge to neutralize traps of this kind. Work continues without any further disturbances.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1307`

## colony.9214.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9214.a&lang=en)

### 中文

不幸。继续。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1081`

### English

Unfortunate. Continue.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1308`

## colony.9220.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9220.name&lang=en)

### 中文

纪念堂

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1082`

### English

Remembrance Hall

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1310`

## colony.9220.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9220.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们对[Root.GetName]通道的探索发现了一座大堂，奇怪的是这个房间并不像所有其他的一样密布着机械和管道。看起来建造者对这里特别用心，其中安放着许多保存良好的双足动物雕塑。这些雕塑具有一定的细节，但大多具有统一的外观。不幸的是，除了对可能是历史人物的一些纪念性致敬之外，没有证据表明这个大厅有什么作用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1083`

### English

We delved into the corridors at [Root.GetName] and discovered a large hall, which is strangely devoid of the pipe machines covering every other hallway and wall. It looks like whoever built this place took special care of it - there are many statues here in excellent condition, depicting bipedal individuals. With some traces of detail they have mostly a uniform appearance. Unfortunately, there is no evidence of what this hall was, other than some monumental tribute to probably historical figures.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1311`

## colony.9220.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9220.a&lang=en)

### 中文

归档这些发现。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1084`

### English

Catalog the finding.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1312`

## colony.9220.a.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9220.a.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这就是一些没用的陈旧艺术作品，没什么价值。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1085`

### English

It's just a useless piece of old art, with no real value.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1313`

## colony.9220.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9220.b&lang=en)

### 中文

真是技艺精湛。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1086`

### English

This is fine craftsmanship.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1314`

## colony.9220.b.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9220.b.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的一些工匠对这些外星人的作品赞不绝口。他们在这些艺术中找到了远古之美与和谐的启迪。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1087`

### English

Some of our craftsmen are astonished by the work created by those aliens. They find a hint of old beauty and harmony in their art.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1315`

## colony.9220.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9220.c&lang=en)

### 中文

真是壮丽的石雕。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1088`

### English

What a magnificent stonework.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1316`

## colony.9220.c.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9220.c.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的人民发现这些石雕实在是精美绝伦，在这样一个陌生的地方发现如此绝品真是令人欣慰。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1089`

### English

Our people find the stonework to be of absolutely extraordinary quality, which is very soothing to find in such an unfamiliar place.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1317`

## colony.9220.d

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9220.d&lang=en)

### 中文

真好看！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1090`

### English

They are beautiful!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1318`

## colony.9220.d.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9220.d.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个地方仿佛就是由我们自己最好的石匠和艺术家打造而成的，难道仅仅是巧合吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1091`

### English

This place looks almost like the work made by our greatest stonemasons and artificers. Is this a mere coincidence?

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1319`

## colony.9230.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9230.name&lang=en)

### 中文

发生地震！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1092`

### English

Earthquake Incoming!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1321`

## colony.9230.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9230.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[Root.GetName]的地震探测器发现了一场即将到来的地震。虽然大部分工人安全无虞，但其中一组在深处舱室的工人可能遭遇塌方的危险。他们汇报称有两台不一样的远古机器，一台是连接到地面的笨重装置，另一台则通过一系列管道连接到墙体上。我们暂不清楚哪一台对这处设施更加重要。工人们估计他们可以拆解其中一台机器用于加固墙体和天花板以应对地震。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1093`

### English

Our seismographs have detected an impending earthquake on [Root.GetName]. Most of the workers have found safety, but one group is in a remote hall that could be at risk of collapse. They reported two distinct, ancient machines there - one being a bulky contraption connected to the ground, and the other attached to the walls via a series of pipes. We can't say if they are anything important for the facility. The workers have estimated that may be possible to use one of those machines to reinforce the ceiling and walls against the quake.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1322`

## colony.9230.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9230.a&lang=en)

### 中文

那就用墙上的管道。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1094`

### English

Use wall pipes.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1323`

## colony.9230.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9230.b&lang=en)

### 中文

那就用那台笨重的东西。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1095`

### English

Use the bulky one.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1324`

## colony.9230.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9230.c&lang=en)

### 中文

依靠他们的专业知识。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1096`

### English

Rely on their expertise.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1325`

## colony.9230.d

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9230.d&lang=en)

### 中文

蜂巢会找到办法的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1097`

### English

The hive will find solution.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1326`

## colony.9231.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9231.name&lang=en)

### 中文

安然无恙

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1098`

### English

Safe and Sound

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1328`

## colony.9231.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9231.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的工人拆解了机器，并成功地巩固了承重结构和天花板。然而，在这之后墙体暴露出了更多的管道，从中逸出嘶嘶声。目前我们无法评估潜在的损失。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1099`

### English

Our workers managed to disassemble the machinery and have successfully bolstered the strength of the supports and ceiling. However, this effort exposed extensive piping in the walls, from which a hissing noise escapes. Right now, it's impossible to assess the potential losses.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1329`

## colony.9231.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9231.a&lang=en)

### 中文

成功了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1100`

### English

Success.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1330`

## colony.9232.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9232.name&lang=en)

### 中文

确实安全

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1101`

### English

Proper Safety

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1332`

## colony.9232.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9232.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的工人拆解了机器，并成功地巩固了承重结构和天花板。不过代价到底是什么呢？将那台沉重的机器移除后，地面出现的巨大的空洞把我们引向了机械的更深处。这一行为的潜在损失到目前为止完全不能预估。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1102`

### English

Our workers managed to disassemble the machinery and used it with success to bolster the strength of the old supports and the ceiling - but what was the cost really? Removing the bulky machine resulted in a gaping hole in the ground leading deeper inside the machinery. Potential losses to this action are, for now, completely unknown.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1333`

## colony.9232.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9232.a&lang=en)

### 中文

成功了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1103`

### English

Success.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1334`

## colony.9233.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9233.name&lang=en)

### 中文

匆忙建设

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1104`

### English

Hasty Construction

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1336`

## colony.9233.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9233.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的工人能够正确评估局势，并设法在不破坏古老机械的前提下修复并加强支撑。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1105`

### English

Our workers were able to properly assess the situation and managed to repair and strengthen the supports without destroying the ancient machinery.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1337`

## colony.9233.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9233.a&lang=en)

### 中文

很好。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1106`

### English

Good.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1338`

## colony.9234.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9234.name&lang=en)

### 中文

新应对方案

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1107`

### English

New Approach

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1340`

## colony.9234.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9234.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

收集了许多子个体的感官印象后，我们找到了更好的方案：我们以少量低级子个体为代价巩固大厅确保了安全。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1108`

### English

Gathering the sense impressions of various drones, a better solution was found: the hall has been strengthened to a safe degree at the cost of a few menial drones.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1341`

## colony.9234.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9234.a&lang=en)

### 中文

学习，适应，克服困难。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1109`

### English

Learn. Adapt. Overcome.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1342`

## colony.9240.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9240.name&lang=en)

### 中文

洞见

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1110`

### English

Insight

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1344`

## colony.9240.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9240.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[Root.GetName]上的探索终于让我们获得了一些洞见。我们最初的预计是正确的：该地下设施事实上是一座巨大的工厂。对外星语言的进一步的分析显示，它被称为“国王锻造厂”。然而，这座锻造厂其实并没有被启用，它的建造者显然缺乏完成这项工程的技术。\n\n只要我们能深入到锻造厂的核心，我们应该就能完成他们未竟的工作。尽管这条通道被厚重的大门阻挡，我们的工程师坚信他们一定能解决这个问题。由于这扇大门并没有任何显而易见的闭锁或开启机制，显然我们可能需要对其施加一些强力手段才能继续推进。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1111`

### English

Our exploration on [Root.GetName] finally yielded some insights. Our first assessment turned out to be correct. The vault is in fact a giant foundry. Further analysis of the alien language reveals that it was referred to as a King-Forge. However, the Forge was never operational - its builders apparently lacked the technology to see the project through to completion.\n\nIt should be possible for us to finish their work as long as we can get into the heart of the foundry. Although the way is blocked by heavy doors, our engineers insist they can be overcome. However, as the doors don't have any obvious locking or opening mechanisms, a certain application of force will be required before we can proceed.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1345`

## colony.9240.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9240.a&lang=en)

### 中文

试着温和地打开大门。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1112`

### English

Open the doors gently.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1346`

## colony.9240.a.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9240.a.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们会试着用动力工具和重型机械撬开大门。如果运气好的话，我们应该不会碰上别的问题就能打开它。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1113`

### English

We will try to pry open the doors with power tools and heavy machinery. With some luck, we will be inside with no further problems.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1347`

## colony.9240.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9240.b&lang=en)

### 中文

爆破大门。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1114`

### English

Blast the doors.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1348`

## colony.9240.b.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9240.b.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

有时候暴力就是最好的解决办法。安置炸药尽快炸开它。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1115`

### English

Sometimes the violent approach is the best approach. Set explosives and be done with it.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1349`

## colony.9240.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9240.c&lang=en)

### 中文

再多研究一下大门。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1116`

### English

Examine the doors more.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1350`

## colony.9240.c.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9240.c.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

心急吃不了热豆腐。毕竟这里已经发现了那么多陷阱，有时候还是别匆忙用直接手段解决。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1117`

### English

Let's not be hasty. We encountered various traps before. Sometimes it's better not to rush with obvious solutions.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1351`

## colony.9240.d

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9240.d&lang=en)

### 中文

找找别的办法。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1118`

### English

Look for other routes.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1352`

## colony.9240.d.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9240.d.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们擅长用不寻常的手段解决各种各样的问题，我们一定能找到其他进入的办法。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1119`

### English

We are skilled in overcoming problems with unusual solutions, so we might be able to find other way inside.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1353`

## colony.9240.e

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9240.e&lang=en)

### 中文

钻穿大门。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1120`

### English

Drill through the doors.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1354`

## colony.9240.e.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9240.e.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的子个体可以轻松地切割大门的材料。虽然需要花费一些时间，但是我们有的是时间。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1121`

### English

Our drones will cut through the doors with ease. It will take some time, but we have all the time in the cosmos.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1355`

## colony.9241.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9241.name&lang=en)

### 中文

气体泄露！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1122`

### English

Gas!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1357`

## colony.9241.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9241.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在强行突破大门后，覆盖墙面的机械开始泄露有害气体。不幸的是这一切发生得太突然，造成了大量的伤亡。整座设施的工作人员需要紧急撤离，需要等到气体散去之后才能重新安全地进入设施。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1123`

### English

After forcing the doors, the machinery covering the walls started to leak hazardous gas. Unfortunately, this happened too quickly, and the result was further casualties. The entire facility needed to be evacuated, and will have to be left for some time before we can safely reenter.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1358`

## colony.9242.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9242.name&lang=en)

### 中文

大爆炸！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1124`

### English

Explosion!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1360`

## colony.9242.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9242.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

炸开大门是很容易，但我们没有料到的是爆炸后有气体开始涌入通道。这种气体高度易燃，并且摧毁了一部分原有的机器。幸运的是所有人都已经撤离了爆炸波及的区域，没有人员伤亡。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1125`

### English

Opening the door with explosives was easy, but what we didn't anticipate was the gas that poured into the hallway after the explosion. It turned out that the gas was highly flammable and destroyed some of the ever-present machinery. Fortunately, there was no one in the blast zone, so there were no casualties.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1361`

## colony.9243.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9243.name&lang=en)

### 中文

冥想

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1126`

### English

Meditation

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1363`

## colony.9243.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9243.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

无论是运气好还是圣灵眷顾，这一座之前阻碍我们去路的大门对我们今早的仪式产生了反应。它发出微微的共振，并且我们可以试着操纵它。在一处小标记隐藏着的摄像机镜头前做出正确的一套手势可以解开门锁让我们畅通无阻地进入。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1127`

### English

Whether by luck or divine intervention, the same door that blocked the way forward responded to our morning rituals: a faint harmonic resonance was detected, a resonance we were eventually able to manipulate. The correct set of gestures made in front of a small marking that concealed the lens of a camera ensured that we gained unobstructed entry.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1364`

## colony.9243.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9243.a&lang=en)

### 中文

来自神祇的礼物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1128`

### English

A gift from the divine.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1365`

## colony.9244.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9244.name&lang=en)

### 中文

第三条道路

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1129`

### English

Third Way

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1367`

## colony.9244.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9244.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在进一步的研究后，我们很快发现了门的后面隐藏着一个安保相机或扫描仪。骇入相机是一个更困难的任务，但我们能够解码它的程序和基础功能，然后就能简单地获取通过大门的方法了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1130`

### English

After additional research, we quickly recognized something in the door that served as a security camera or scanner. Hacking into it was a more difficult task, but we were able to decipher its programming and basic functions - thus gaining access to the door without further complication.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1368`

## colony.9244.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9244.a&lang=en)

### 中文

干得不错。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1131`

### English

Good job.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1369`

## colony.9245.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9245.name&lang=en)

### 中文

气体泄露

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1132`

### English

Gas

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1371`

## colony.9245.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9245.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

看来钻穿大门的过程中我们的子个体激活了某种防御机制，过道的内部释放出了某种化学气体武器。幸运的是我们的集体意识能够采取恰当的应对措施，很快这些气体就散去了。这些子个体已经回去继续工作了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1133`

### English

It appears in drilling through the door, our drones activated some defensive mechanism that unleashed weaponized gas inside the corridor. Fortunately, the Collective was able to take proper precautions, and soon enough the gas had dissipated. The drones have already returned to work.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1372`

## colony.9245.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9245.a&lang=en)

### 中文

继续。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1134`

### English

Proceed.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1373`

## colony.9250.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9250.name&lang=en)

### 中文

锻造厂核心

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1135`

### English

Foundry Heart

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1375`

## colony.9250.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9250.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]上的锻造厂的核心是一台遍布管道、传送带的扭曲网络，显然是某种工作站。通过快速调查我们发现了其缺失能源、维护和若干关键系统。此外还有充足的证据显示，我们探索这座地下掩体对这座锻造厂的内部工作过程造成了影响。我们可以试着修复它，虽然可能要花费一些时间。作为替代方案，我们也可以选择设置一个回收厂来拆解整个机械系统。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1136`

### English

The heart of the foundry on [Root.GetName] is a twisted network of pipes, conveyor belts, and what appear to be workstations. A quick survey revealed that power, maintenance, and key systems are missing. There is also reason to believe that our exploration of the vault has had some effect on the internal workings of the foundry. We can try to repair it, though it will be time consuming. Alternatively, we can always set up a scrapyard and dismantle the entire machine.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1376`

## colony.9250.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9250.a&lang=en)

### 中文

修复它。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1137`

### English

Repair it.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1377`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9250.a.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们会把机器接入行星电网并且修复它。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1138`

### English

We will plug the machinery into the planetary power grid and fix it.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1378`

## colony.9250.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9250.b&lang=en)

我们不仅要修复机器，还要研究它的工作原理。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1140`

### English

Fixing the machinery is not enough - we need to learn how it works.

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9250.c&lang=en)

### 中文

这座锻造厂可以为帝国做出贡献，并为我们提供关于原住民的潜在知识。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1142`

### English

This foundry will serve the empire and provide us with potential knowledge about its original inhabitants.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1382`

### 中文

### English

The scrapyard will begin its slow process of dismantling the machinery.

## colony.9251.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9251.name&lang=en)

### 中文

通电运行

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1149`

### English

Powered Up

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1390`

## colony.9251.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9251.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

能源一下子灌进[Root.GetName]上饥渴的机器里，它发出了震耳欲聋的咆哮，管道开始输送熔融的矿物，照亮了整个工厂，让温度不断上升。这些原材料输送到了工作站，操作员将其转变为所需形状和密度的合金。看起来整个锻造厂的每个部分都是为了这个目的所建造的，甚至连支柱都不止有支撑屋顶的作用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1150`

### English

Energy flows through the hungry machines on [Root.GetName]. With a deafening roar, the pipes began to transport the liquefied minerals, brightening and heating the entire facility. The raw materials were delivered to the workstations where operators can form them into alloys of the desired shape and density. It looks like every part of the foundry was made with just this one purpose in mind. Even the pillars have more to do than support the ceiling.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1391`

## colony.9251.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9251.a&lang=en)

### 中文

继续。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1151`

### English

Go on.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1392`

## colony.9260.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9260.name&lang=en)

### 中文

工作环境

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1152`

### English

Work Conditions

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1394`

## colony.9260.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9260.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

有人担心[Root.GetName]地下室的主舱室中有一些有毒气体正在积累。这些气体并不致命，但对在工作站中的操作员来说有一定的危害。如果我们想要充分利用这一设施，必须采取适当的安全举措。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1153`

### English

There is some concern that toxic gases are building up in the main chamber of the [Root.GetName] vault. The gas is non-lethal, but it can be hazardous to anything that operates workstations. If we want to make full use of this place, we should put some safety measures in place.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1395`

## colony.9260.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9260.a&lang=en)

### 中文

那就轮换操作员。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1154`

### English

Just rotate the operators.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1396`

## colony.9260.a.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9260.a.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们会让操作员在感受到不适之前采取轮换，也许会有偶发的头疼。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1155`

### English

We will rotate the operators before they get sick. An occasional headache might occur.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1397`

## colony.9260.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9260.b&lang=en)

### 中文

规制工作时长。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1156`

### English

Regulate the work time.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1398`

## colony.9260.b.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9260.b.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们会设置休息时间，让气体得以在运作间隙消散以提供更安全的工作环境。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1157`

### English

We will issue breaks, allowing the gas to dissipate for safer workplace.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1399`

## colony.9260.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9260.c&lang=en)

### 中文

让奴隶上。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1158`

### English

Use slaves.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1400`

## colony.9260.c.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9260.c.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

区区几个奴隶而已，只要给他们更好的衣服他们就会心甘情愿去出卖健康。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1159`

### English

Who cares about slaves? We could always give them better clothes, and they will be happy to risk their health.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1401`

## colony.9260.d

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9260.d&lang=en)

### 中文

实施严格工作规范。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1160`

### English

Issue strict work regulations.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1402`

## colony.9260.d.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9260.d.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

工人轮换、时长规制、在岗保护……我们必须在不妨碍生产的同时遏制危险因素。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1161`

### English

Worker rotation, time regulations, and protections on-site - we must mitigate the hazards without hampering our productivity.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1403`

## colony.9260.e

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9260.e&lang=en)

### 中文

轮换子个体。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1162`

### English

Rotate drones.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1404`

## colony.9260.e.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9260.e.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

每个子个体都会为集体意识工作，直至完成它们的使命。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1163`

### English

Every drone works for the consciousness till they fill its purpose.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1405`

## colony.9270.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9270.name&lang=en)

### 中文

回收中心

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1164`

### English

Recycling Center

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1407`

## colony.9270.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9270.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]的铸造厂具有一个专门的回收处理舱室，用产生的废弃物来提高产出率。如果能让它重新运作起来对我们来说是件好事。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1165`

### English

The [Root.GetName] foundry has a dedicated recyclable processing chamber that uses post-production waste to increase production efficiency. It might be beneficial for us to get it up and running again.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1408`

## colony.9270.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9270.a&lang=en)

### 中文

那就让它重新运作起来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1166`

### English

Make it operational.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1409`

## colony.9270.a.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9270.a.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们会让回收中心重新上线。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1167`

### English

We will bring the recycling center online.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1410`

## colony.9270.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9270.b&lang=en)

### 中文

用我们自己的方案。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1168`

### English

Use our designs.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1411`

## colony.9270.b.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9270.b.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们自己的回收方案更加先进。我们可以将其整合进整个机器系统里。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1169`

### English

Our recycling patterns are far superior. We could implement them into the machinery.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1412`

## colony.9270.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9270.c&lang=en)

### 中文

增强合金产出。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1170`

### English

Enhance alloy production.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1413`

## colony.9270.c.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9270.c.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们可以将整个回收舱室改造成矿物纯化中心，以提高整体的产出效率。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1171`

### English

We can repurpose the recycling chamber into a mineral purifying center. It will enhance our production.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1414`

## colony.9280.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9280.name&lang=en)

### 中文

或多或少？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1172`

### English

More or Less?

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1416`

## colony.9280.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9280.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在[Root.GetName]锻造厂的工作基本完成了，但其中仍有一些对原有设计和工作方式的改进空间。据估计如果提升矿物输入，我们还能进一步提高合金的产出。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1173`

### English

Work on the [Root.GetName] foundry is almost complete, but there are still places where we could improve on the original design and work. It is estimated that by increasing the amount of minerals input, we could increase the production of the alloy.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1417`

## colony.9280.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9280.a&lang=en)

### 中文

别压榨这个外星机器。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1174`

### English

Don't stress alien machinery.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1418`

## colony.9280.a.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9280.a.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们不会利用这个机会。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1175`

### English

We won't use the opportunity.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1419`

## colony.9280.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9280.b&lang=en)

### 中文

就这么做。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1176`

### English

Do it.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1420`

## colony.9280.b.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9280.b.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们会提升产出，但也会提升矿物维护费。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1177`

### English

We will increase the production, but also mineral upkeep.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1421`

## colony.9280.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9280.c&lang=en)

### 中文

我们能不能试试有机材料？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1178`

### English

Can't we use organic matter?

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1422`

## colony.9280.c.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9280.c.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们会把一部分矿物替换成有机质，但不可能完全奏效。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1179`

### English

We could supplant some part of the mineral intake with organic matter, but it will be impossible to fully implement it.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1423`

## colony.9280.d

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9280.d&lang=en)

### 中文

这看起来像我们自己的老旧科技。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1180`

### English

It's like our old technology.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1424`

## colony.9280.d.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9280.d.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们曾统治一片广阔的疆域，拥有无与伦比的科技。有些新近重新发现的科技可以用来提高这座铸造厂的产出能力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1181`

### English

We once ruled a vast empire and had technology beyond comparison. Some recently rediscovered technology could use us to enhance the production capacity of the foundry.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1425`

## colony.9290.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9290.name&lang=en)

### 中文

隆重开业

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1182`

### English

Grand Opening

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1427`

## colony.9290.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9290.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

成功了！在[Root.GetName]上的铸造厂正在全速运转，尽管和它建造者的原始设计不尽相同，但确实也是一个壮观的设施，它能以超高的效率产出合金。而它的产出就是我们的决心和独创性获得的回报。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1183`

### English

It's finished! The foundry on [Root.GetName] is fully operational, perhaps not like its original Builders designed it, but it's still a sight to behold, and a more-than-efficient way to produce alloys. All that's left is to reap the rewards of our determination and ingenuity.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1428`

## colony.9290.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9290.a&lang=en)

### 中文

好日子！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1184`

### English

Great day!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1429`

## colony.9290.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9290.b&lang=en)

### 中文

建造者又是什么情况？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1185`

### English

What about the Builders?

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1430`

## colony.9290.b.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9290.b.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们其实至今没有关于建造者下落的线索。他们看起来好像就这么消失了，留下的关于他们自己的信息也很模糊。我们需要收集起所有能找到的信息，把它们记录下来并保存好。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1186`

### English

We never really found out what happened to the builders. They seem to have simply disappeared, leaving behind only vague information about themselves. We need to gather everything we can and write it down - preserve it.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1431`

## colony.9290.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9290.c&lang=en)

### 中文

我们需要一位监工。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1187`

### English

We need an overseer.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1432`

## colony.9290.c.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9290.c.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们有让精英监管此类大型工程的传统，我们应当任命合适的贵族来关照我们新的铸造厂。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1188`

### English

It's customary for our elites to oversee such enterprise - we should appoint prominent noble to take care of our new foundry.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1433`

## colony.9290.d

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9290.d&lang=en)

### 中文

任命执行官。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1189`

### English

Appoint an executive.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1434`

## colony.9290.d.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9290.d.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

让一位总经理监管此类大型工程非常合理，我们应当任命一位$job_executive_with_icon$来关照我们新的铸造厂。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1190`

### English

It's logical for our managers to oversee such an enterprise - we should appoint an $job_executive_with_icon$ to take care of our new foundry.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1435`

## colony.9290.e

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9290.e&lang=en)

### 中文

很好。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1191`

### English

Good.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1436`

## colony.9295.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9295.name&lang=en)

### 中文

金属泥潭

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1192`

### English

Metallic Puddles

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1438`

## colony.9295.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9295.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在最近的一场降雨中，殖民者注意到了[Root.GetName]的表面出现了一些金属泥潭。这些泥潭具有很大的黏度，如同液态金属一般。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1193`

### English

After a recent rainfall, colonists have noticed metallic puddles appearing on the surface of [Root.GetName]. These puddles have a viscous consistency, like liquid metal.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1439`

## colony.9295.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9295.a&lang=en)

### 中文

禁止接触泥潭。可能有毒！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1194`

### English

Leave them alone. They may be toxic!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1440`

## colony.9295.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9295.b&lang=en)

### 中文

进一步调查！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1195`

### English

Investigate further!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1441`

## colony.9296.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9296.name&lang=en)

### 中文

白银史莱姆

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1196`

### English

Silver Slime

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1442`

## colony.9296.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9296.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]上的这些黏性金属泥潭正在扩张。陆地上巨大的条带被银色的黏液覆盖。他们似乎在暴雨之后开始增殖了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1197`

### English

The viscous metallic puddles on [Root.GetName] are growing. Huge swathes of land are now covered in silver slime. They seem to grow following heavy rainfall.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1443`

## colony.9296.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9296.a&lang=en)

### 中文

穿好防护服开始调查！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1198`

### English

Suit up and investigate!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1444`

## colony.9299.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9299.name&lang=en)

### 中文

难以捉摸的黏液

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1199`

### English

Elusive Viscosity

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1445`

## colony.9299.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9299.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]上的这些黏性金属泥潭在躲避着殖民地科学家。目前其根源仍未探明，并且还一直在抗拒我们的探察。我们的仪器正在费劲地解码它们的化学组成。如果想要获得答案我们还有许多测试要做。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1200`

### English

The viscous metallic puddles on [Root.GetName] are eluding colony scientists. So far their origin is undetermined, and they have been resistant to our probing. Our instruments are struggling to decipher their chemical makeup. We have more tests to run if we want answers.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1446`

## colony.9299.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9299.a&lang=en)

### 中文

继续尝试！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1201`

### English

Keep trying!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1447`

## colony.9297.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9297.name&lang=en)

### 中文

消失的泥潭

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1202`

### English

Disappearing Puddles

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1448`

## colony.9297.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9297.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

消失了！[Root.GetName]上的那些金属泥潭在一夜之间消失得一干二净，没有留下半点痕迹。我们不知道它们还会不会再出现……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1203`

### English

They're gone! The viscous metallic puddles on [Root.GetName] have disappeared. It's as if they've just evaporated. We don't know if or when they'll be back again...

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1449`

## colony.9297.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9297.a&lang=en)

### 中文

有时候事情会自己解决自己。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1204`

### English

Sometimes these things just resolve themselves...

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1450`

## colony.9298.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9298.name&lang=en)

### 中文

银泥归来

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1205`

### English

Return of the Silver Slime

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1451`

## colony.9298.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9298.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

回来了！在[Root.GetName]上的黏性金属泥潭又回来了。他们仍在躲避[Root.GetName]上的殖民者们对其本质的探察。我们获得了第二次机会，抓住这次机会好好研究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1206`

### English

They're back! The viscous metallic puddles on [Root.GetName] have returned. Their nature continues to elude the colonists on [Root.GetName]. We've been handed another opportunity. We should investigate them while we can.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1452`

## colony.9298.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9298.a&lang=en)

### 中文

第二次机会……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1207`

### English

A second chance...

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1453`

## colony.9300.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9300.name&lang=en)

### 中文

是新元素，老板！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1208`

### English

Elementary, Dear Leader!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1454`

## colony.9300.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9300.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]上发现了一种新的单质！以黏性金属泥潭为契机，我们获得了一个突破性的发现，这些泥潭是由一种单一的金属单质构成的。这种元素目前被我们命名为[Root.GetName]钖，存在于[Root.GetName]的大气中，但当混入大量降雨中时就会呈现出在行星表面发现的那种黏性。其存在形式呈现循环性，与基于一氧化二氢构成的泥潭相似。\n\n真是个绝妙的发现。我们对元素的认知拓展了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1209`

### English

A new element has been discovered on [Root.GetName]! After a breakthrough in the case of the viscous metallic puddles, we have discovered the puddles comprise a unique chemical element. The element, which we're calling [Root.GetName]onium, is present in the air on [Root.GetName], but when introduced to high precipitation, takes on the viscous consistency we have seen on the planet's surface. Their appearances are cyclical, much like dihydrogen-monoxide based puddles.\n\nA fascinating discovery. Our understanding of the elements continues to grow.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1455`

## colony.9300.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9300.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们还有许多要学习的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1210`

### English

We still have much to learn.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1457`

## FASCINATING_METEOROLOGY_PROJECT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AFASCINATING_METEOROLOGY_PROJECT&lang=en)

### 中文

神奇的气象学

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1211`

### English

Fascinating Meteorology

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1458`

## FASCINATING_METEOROLOGY_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AFASCINATING_METEOROLOGY_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

对[Root.GetName]神奇的气象学进行深入研究对我们的科学发展的帮助无可估量。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1212`

### English

## colony.9301.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9301.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经获得了对[Root.GetName]独特天气系统的意义非凡的洞见。在拓展了我们对化学元素的理解以外，我们最近的研究还对我们的食物生产带来了积极的影响。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1214`

### English

We have gained invaluable insight into the unique weather systems of [Root.GetName]. In addition to expanding our understanding of chemical elements, our recent research has had positive implications for food production.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1461`

## INTEMPORAL_ORB_CAT_DESC

### 中文

我们发现了外形类似球体的高密度能量信号。建议进行更深入的调查。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1277`

## anomaly.7105.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7105.name&lang=en)

### 中文

高密度能量

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1279`

### English

Concentrated Energy

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1537`

## anomaly.7105.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7105.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]的异常能量读数的来源确定是一个蓝色的小球。[Owner.GetScientistPlural]汇报，传感器从中发现了相反极性的快子。\n\n该物体周围的地面上刻着符号，也许是由某个更古老的文明留下的。目前不清楚是符号的含义是指引还是警示。\n\n完整的文本还远不能翻译，但部分符号似乎与时间测量和[Solar_System.GetName]恒星系中天体相对位置有关。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1280`

### English

The source of the unusual energy readings noted on [From.GetName] is confirmed to be a small, blue orb. The [Owner.GetScientistPlural] report that sensors pick up tachyons of reverse polarity.\n\nSymbols are carved into the ground around the object, perhaps placed there by an older civilization. It is unclear whether they are meant as an instruction, or a warning.\n\nThe full inscription is far from translated, but individual symbols seem related to the measuring of time and the relative positions of celestial objects in the [Solar_System.GetName] system.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1538`

## anomaly.7105.a.standard

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7105.a.standard&lang=en)

研究球体。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1281`

### English

Study the orb.
