## anomaly.7110.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7110.name&lang=en)

### 中文

局部时间反转

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1285`

### English

Local Time Reversal

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1544`

## anomaly.7110.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7110.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在[FromFrom.GetName]发现的蓝色能量球体消失了。对当地的扫描完全没有发现该球体的任何迹象。更令人困惑的是，地上的符号也一同消失得无影无踪，如同从未存在过一般。\n\n基于在此处发现的异常的快子信号，我们的[Owner.GetScientistPlural]推断，球体和符号一同发生了时间回溯。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1286`

### English

The orb of blue energy first detected on [FromFrom.GetName] has disappeared. A scan of the location show no traces of the strange sphere. Even more baffling is the erasure of the markings on the ground: it is as if they were never there.\n\nGiven the unusual tachyon readings we detected at the site, our [Owner.GetScientistPlural] postulate that both the orb and the writing were moving backwards through time.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1545`

## anomaly.7115.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7115.name&lang=en)

### 中文

时光如流水

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1288`

### English

Time Flows Like a River

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1548`

## anomaly.7115.desc.regular

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7115.desc.regular&lang=en)

### 中文

在[FromFrom.GetName]上的站点进行工作时，[Root.GetLeaderName]无意间擦到了蓝色球体。\n\n[leader.GetHeSheCap]之后称看到了时间如同一条河流一般延展，前后连接着无尽的过去与未来。在[leader.GetHimHer]的面前，蓝色能量从时间流中蜿蜒涌出，被吸入了[leader.GetHisHer]的身体里。尽管该意外在[Owner.GetScientistPlural]精确测量中持续了不到一秒钟，但[leader.GetHeShe]感觉仿佛度过了一个多小时。\n\n在幻觉结束后，[Root.GetLeaderName]确信时间不会再影响[leader.GetHimHer]了。[leader.GetHeShe]现在拥有了无限的时间，于是想利用无尽的寿命自行探索宇宙。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1290`

### English

While working at the site on [FromFrom.GetName], [Root.GetLeaderName] brushed against the blue orb.\n\n[leader.GetHeSheCap] later described a vision of time stretching out like a river into the infinite future and past. In front of [leader.GetHimHer], blue energy arced out of the timestream and was absorbed by [leader.GetHisHer] body. Although the incident was accurately measured by the other [Owner.GetScientistPlural] to span less than a second, [leader.GetHeShe] experienced it for more than a hour.\n\nAfter the vision ended, [Root.GetLeaderName] was certain that time would no longer affect [leader.GetHimHer]. With an infinite lifetime ahead, [leader.GetHeShe] want[leader.GetSXX] to use [leader.GetHisHer] endless time to explore the universe on [leader.GetHisHer] own.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1550`

## anomaly.7115.desc.immortal

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7115.desc.immortal&lang=en)

### 中文

在[FromFrom.GetName]上的站点进行工作时，实习[Owner.GetScientist][intemporal_leader.GetName]无意间擦到了蓝色球体。\n\n[intemporal_leader.GetHeSheCap]之后称看到了时间如同一条河流一般延展，前后连接着无尽的过去与未来。在[intemporal_leader.GetHimHer]的面前，蓝色能量从时间流中蜿蜒涌出，被被吸入了[intemporal_leader.GetHisHer]的身体里。尽管该意外在[Owner.GetScientistPlural]精确测量中持续了不到一秒钟，但[intemporal_leader.GetHeShe]感觉仿佛度过了一个多小时。\n\n在幻觉结束后，[intemporal_leader.GetName]确信时间不会再影响[intemporal_leader.GetHimHer]了。[intemporal_leader.GetHeShe]现在拥有了无限的时间，于是想利用无尽的寿命自行探索宇宙。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1292`

### English

While working at the site on [FromFrom.GetName], junior [Owner.GetScientist] [intemporal_leader.GetName] brushed against the blue orb.\n\n[intemporal_leader.GetHeSheCap] later described a vision of time stretching out like a river into the infinite future and past. In front of [intemporal_leader.GetHimHer], blue energy arced out of the timestream and was absorbed by [intemporal_leader.GetHisHer] body. Although the incident was accurately measured by the other [Owner.GetScientistPlural] to span less than a second, [intemporal_leader.GetHeShe] experienced it for more than a hour.\n\nAfter the vision ended, [intemporal_leader.GetName] was certain that time would no longer affect [intemporal_leader.GetHimHer]. With an infinite lifetime ahead, [intemporal_leader.GetHeShe] want[intemporal_leader.GetSXX] to use [intemporal_leader.GetHisHer] endless time to explore the universe on [intemporal_leader.GetHisHer] own.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1552`

## anomaly.7115.desc.hive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7115.desc.hive&lang=en)

### 中文

在[FromFrom.GetName]上的站点进行工作时，[Root.GetLeaderName]无意间擦到了蓝色球体。\n\n[leader.GetHeSheCap]接收到了一份图像，显示时间如同一条河流一般延展，前后连接着无尽的过去与未来。在[leader.GetHimHer]的面前，蓝色能量从时间流中蜿蜒涌出，被被吸入了[leader.GetHisHer]的身体里。尽管该意外在其他[Owner.GetScientistPlural]的精确测量中持续了不到一秒钟，但[leader.GetHeShe]感觉仿佛度过了一个多小时。\n\n在幻觉结束后，我们确信时间不会再影响[Root.GetLeaderName]了。由于现在拥有了无限的时间，我们可以利用[leader.GetHisHer]无尽的寿命令其自行探索宇宙。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1294`

### English

While working at the site on [FromFrom.GetName], [Root.GetLeaderName] brushed against the blue orb.\n\n[leader.GetHeSheCap] received a vision of time stretching out like a river into the infinite future and past. In front of [leader.GetHimHer], blue energy arced out of the timestream and was absorbed by [leader.GetHisHer] body. Although the incident was accurately measured by the other [Owner.GetScientistPlural] to span less than a second, [leader.GetHeShe] experienced it for more than a hour.\n\nAfter the vision ended, we were certain that time would no longer affect [Root.GetLeaderName]. With an infinite lifetime ahead, we could use [leader.GetHisHer] endless time to explore the universe on [leader.GetHisHer] own.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1554`

## anomaly.7115.desc.devouring

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7115.desc.devouring&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetLeaderName]吃掉了[FromFrom.GetName]上的球体。\n\n[leader.GetHeSheCap]接收到了一份图像，显示时间如同一条河流一般延展，前后连接着无尽的过去与未来。在[leader.GetHimHer]的面前，蓝色能量从时间流中蜿蜒涌出，被被吸入了[leader.GetHisHer]的身体里。尽管该意外在其他[Owner.GetScientistPlural]的精确测量中持续了不到一秒钟，但[leader.GetHeShe]感觉仿佛度过了一个多小时。\n\n在幻觉结束后，我们确信时间不会再影响[Root.GetLeaderName]了。由于现在拥有了无限的时间，我们可以利用[leader.GetHisHer]无尽的寿命令其自行探索宇宙。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1296`

### English

[Root.GetLeaderName] ate the blue orb on [FromFrom.GetName].\n\n[leader.GetHeSheCap] received a vision of time stretching out like a river into the infinite future and past. In front of [leader.GetHimHer], blue energy arced out of the timestream and was absorbed by [leader.GetHisHer] body. Although the incident was accurately measured by the other [Owner.GetScientistPlural] to span less than a second, [leader.GetHeShe] experienced it for more than a hour.\n\nAfter the vision ended, we were certain that time would no longer affect [Root.GetLeaderName]. With an infinite lifetime ahead, we could use [leader.GetHisHer] endless time to explore the universe on [leader.GetHisHer] own.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1556`

## anomaly.7120.name

### 中文

返航

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1305`

### English

The Return

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1567`

## anomaly.7120.desc.regular

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7120.desc.regular&lang=en)

### 中文

长程扫描发现了一艘舰船正在驶向[Root.Capital.GetName]。随着它越来越近，我们发现那艘船的设计与我们一世纪前的如出一辙。\n\n在打开通讯之后，我们发现那艘舰船是[intemporal_explorer.GetName]驾驶的，就是那位被蓝色球体从时间流中带走的[Owner.GetScientist]。\n\n[intemporal_explorer.GetHeSheCap]在对撞的中子星和逆转的时空引力井周围漫步，又乘坐飞船返回虚空。值得一提的是，我们的物理学家证实，他带回来的信息不仅大部分是可信的，而且甚至是开创性的。\n\n[intemporal_explorer.GetName]的身体似乎完全不随着时间的流逝而改变，即便我们最近的科技也无法解释那颗球体的功能。[intemporal_explorer.GetHeShe]得以逃脱终极灭亡，将会继续探索宇宙，直到时间的终结。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1306`

### English

Long-range scans picked up an approaching vessel near [Root.Capital.GetName]. As it moved closer, it became clear that the ship's design matched our own from a century ago.\n\nAfter opening comms, we discovered that the ship was piloted by [intemporal_explorer.GetName], the [Owner.GetScientist] who [intemporal_explorer.GetWasWere] detached from the timestream by a blue energy orb.\n\n[intemporal_explorer.GetHeSheCap] rambled about colliding neutron stars and inverse space-time gravitational wells, before taking the ship back into the dark. Remarkably, our physicists have confirmed that much of this information is not only verifiable, but indeed groundbreaking.\n\n[intemporal_explorer.GetName] appeared physically unchanged by the passage of time, but even our current technology could not explain the functionality of the orb. Barring their own fatal destruction, [intemporal_explorer.GetHeShe] will continue to explore the universe until the end of time.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1568`

## anomaly.7120.desc.hive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7120.desc.hive&lang=en)

### 中文

长程扫描发现了一艘舰船正在驶向[Root.Capital.GetName]。随着它越来越近，我们发现那艘船的设计与我们一世纪前的如出一辙。我们发现那艘舰船是[intemporal_explorer.GetName]驾驶的，就是那位被蓝色球体从时间流中带走的[Owner.GetScientist]。\n\n这位[Owner.GetScientist]的再次连入了我们，知识涌入集体思维中：那是关于对撞的中子星和逆转的时空引力井的观测记忆。传输一结束，这艘船又启程返回了虚空。值得一提的是，我们的[Owner.GetScientistPlural]证实，他带回来的信息不仅大部分是可信的，而且甚至是开创性的。\n\n[intemporal_explorer.GetName]的身体似乎完全不随着时间的流逝而改变，即便我们最近的科技也无法解释那颗球体的功能。[intemporal_explorer.GetHeShe]得以逃脱终极灭亡，将会继续探索宇宙，直到时间的终结。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1307`

### English

Long-range scans picked up an approaching vessel near [Root.Capital.GetName]. As it moved closer, it became clear that the ship's design matched our own from a century ago. We discovered that the ship was piloted by [intemporal_explorer.GetName], the [Owner.GetScientist] who [intemporal_explorer.GetWasWere] detached from the timestream by a blue energy orb.\n\nAgain with us, knowledge flowed from the [Owner.GetScientist] into the collective: sense-impressions of colliding neutron stars and inverse space-time gravitational wells. Once the transfer was complete, the ship continued on into the dark. Remarkably, our physics [Owner.GetScientistPlural] have confirmed that much of this data is not only verifiable, but indeed groundbreaking.\n\n[intemporal_explorer.GetName] appeared physically unchanged by the passage of time, but even our current technology could not explain the functionality of the orb. Barring their own fatal destruction, [intemporal_explorer.GetHeShe] will continue to explore the universe until the end of time.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1569`

## DATA_DIAMOND_CAT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ADATA_DIAMOND_CAT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]地表冰层的最上层覆盖着规模异常巨大的晶体沉积。我们还检测到了一个微弱的信号存在。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1309`

### English

The superficial layer of surface ice on [From.GetName] covers an abnormally large crystal deposit. We are also detecting the presence of a faint signal.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1572`

### English

[From.GetName] Crystal Deposit Study

## DATA_DIAMOND_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ADATA_DIAMOND_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]上的水晶发出的令人迷惑的信号引人好奇。建议进行进一步研究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1311`

### English

The disorientating signals emanating from the crystal on [From.GetName] are a curiosity. Further study is advised.

## anomaly.3145.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3145.name&lang=en)

### 中文

钻取幻觉

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1312`

### English

Drilling Delusions

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1575`

## anomaly.3145.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3145.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在[From.GetName]上正要着手开展钻取作业之后，[Root.GetLeaderName]产生了强烈的幻觉。虽然没有受到身体伤害，但他们报告说，他们起先是被破碎不连贯的视觉和嗅觉幻觉所淹没，这些幻觉展现了某种未知的外星物种。\n\n已经准备了一项特殊项目来进一步调查这些怪异的晶体贮藏。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1313`

### English

After setting up to drill into the ice layer on [From.GetName], [Root.GetLeaderName] experienced intense sensations. While not physically harmed, they reported being initially overwhelmed by disjointed visual and olfactory hallucinations depicting an unknown alien species.\n\nA special project has been prepared to investigate this strange crystal deposit further.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1576`

## anomaly.3145.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3145.a&lang=en)

### 中文

令人不安。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1314`

### English

Disconcerting.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1577`

## anomaly.3150.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3150.name&lang=en)

### 中文

数据钻石

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1315`

### English

Data Diamond

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1578`

## anomaly.3150.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3150.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在冰面以下，晶体自身几乎构成了行星的大部分物质。取样分析表明有一处用于数据存储的人工高密度晶格。估测表明这些存储的数据远比我们所有的集体数据库都要庞大。\n\n似乎这个晶体的建造者使用灵能共振来写入数据。[Root.GetLeaderName]非常好奇，并申请在表面建造一座自动研究站。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1316`

### English

Below the ice, the crystal itself makes up the majority of the planet's mass. Samples suggest an artificially dense lattice is used for data storage. Estimates indicate the volume of stored knowledge is magnitudes beyond everything in our collective database.\n\nIt is likely that the builders of this crystal used a psionic resonance to imprint data. [Root.GetLeaderName] is so intrigued, that they have requested permission to oversee the creation of an automated research station on the surface

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1579`

## anomaly.3150.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3150.a&lang=en)

### 中文

在轨道上研究就行了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1317`

### English

Studying it from orbit will do.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1580`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3150.b&lang=en)

祝你在地上好运，[Root.GetLeaderName]。

### English

Good luck down there, [Root.GetLeaderName].

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1581`

## anomaly.3150.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3150.c&lang=en)

### 中文

开采晶体作为资源。

### English

Mine the crystal for resources.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1582`

## anomaly.3165.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3165.name&lang=en)

### 中文

机不可失

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1320`

### English

A Terrible Thing To Waste

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1583`

## anomaly.3165.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3165.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在自动研究站即将竣工时，晶体突然释放出了一阵极强的能量活动，[Root.GetLeaderName]不幸当场毙命。不一会儿之后，我们收到了一份自称来自[leader.GetHimHer]的自动通讯。\n\n尽管其身体的神经系统已经无法修复，但其意识被困在了水晶中。由于身处[leader.GetHisHer]这种新的境地，[leader.GetSheHe]可以帮助我们深入晶体数据库在未来进行进一步的研究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1321`

### English

Nearing the completion of the automated research station, there was a sudden spike in activity from the crystal which left [Root.GetLeaderName] dead. Soon after, we received an autonomous communique claiming to be from [leader.GetHimHer].\n\nWhile their body's nervous system is beyond repair, their mind has been trapped within the crystal. Making the best of [leader.GetHisHer] new situation, [leader.GetSheHe] has offered to assist with any future research into the crystal database.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1584`

## anomaly.3165.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3165.a&lang=en)

### 中文

喜忧参半的结果。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1322`

### English

A bittersweet outcome.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1585`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3150.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### English

A small research deposit will be added to [FromFromFrom.GetName].

## anomaly.3150.b.tooltip

[FromFromFrom.GetName]上将新增一处大型研究矿藏，但可能对[Root.GetLeaderName]存在危险。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1324`

### English

A large research deposit will be added to [FromFromFrom.GetName], but it could be dangerous for [Root.GetLeaderName].

## anomaly.3150.c.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3150.c.tooltip&lang=en)

[FromFromFrom.GetName]上将新增一处稀有水晶矿藏。在获得了相应的科技后就可以进行开采。

A rare crystal deposit will be added to [FromFromFrom.GetName]. We will be able to mine it if we have the appropriate technology.

## reassured_tracking

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Areassured_tracking&lang=en)

### 中文

监控结果令人欣慰

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1328`

### English

Thankful Tracking

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1593`

## reassured_tracking_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Areassured_tracking_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

集成跟踪系统正在对人口进行监控。尽管集体焦虑依旧存在，但是知道至少有人在想办法解决问题，这个殖民地也稳定了下来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1329`

### English

A centralized tracking system is monitoring the population. While collective anxiety persists, the knowledge that something is being done is keeping the colony stable.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1594`

## collective_autophobia

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acollective_autophobia&lang=en)

### 中文

集体孤独症

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1330`

### English

Collective Autophobia

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1595`

## collective_autophobia_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acollective_autophobia_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一种对失踪的集体恐惧笼罩着这个殖民地。人们互相紧密依靠的渴望已经影响了日常的工作。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1331`

### English

A collective fear of being disappeared has gripped this colony. The urge to be in close proximity to one another has slowed day-to-day operations.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1596`

## freed_stasis_criminals

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afreed_stasis_criminals&lang=en)

### 中文

顽固罪犯获释

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1332`

### English

Freed Stasis Criminals

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1597`

## freed_stasis_criminals_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afreed_stasis_criminals_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

大多数获释囚犯都是惯犯。他们遭受着的化冲击使得他们难以融入社会，犯罪率也随之飙升。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1333`

### English

The majority of the freed prisoners were hardened criminals. The culture shock they are now experiencing has made reintegration difficult and crime rates have spiked.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1598`

## freed_stasis_dissidents

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afreed_stasis_dissidents&lang=en)

### 中文

顽固异见者获释

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1334`

### English

Freed Stasis Dissidents

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1599`

## freed_stasis_dissidents_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afreed_stasis_dissidents_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

大多数囚犯都是政治异见者，他们积极地在殖民地上传播自己的艺术与文化。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1335`

### English

The majority of the prisoners were political dissidents. They are enthusiastically sharing their art and culture with the colony.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1600`

## colony.9900.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9900.name&lang=en)

### 中文

晨间打卡缺席

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1337`

### English

Missed The Morning Check-in

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1603`

## colony.9900.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9900.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

日前，[Root.GetName]上涌现出大量的[Owner.GetColonistPlural]失踪事件。这些人唯一的共同点就是他们在失踪时都孤身一人。随着消息散播开来，人们对于失踪的恐惧随之降临。民众的惶恐不安对殖民地的日常运作带来了极大的麻烦。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1338`

### English

Recently, numerous [Owner.GetColonistPlural] have gone missing on [Root.GetName]. The only discernible pattern is that each was alone at the time of their disappearance. As news began to spread, a collective fear of going unescorted has gripped the colony. This state of unrest is causing considerable problems for our day-to-day operations.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1604`

## colony.9900.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9900.a&lang=en)

### 中文

开展跟踪器植入项目。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1339`

### English

Implement tracking procedures.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1605`

## colony.9900.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9900.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]上§Y100§!£pop£§H人口§!失踪。我们将部署一套昂贵的跟踪监控系统来帮助我们解决这个谜团。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1340`

### English

§Y100§! £pop£ §HPops§! have gone missing from [Root.GetName]. An expensive tracking system will be used to help us solve this mystery.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1606`

## colony.9900.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9900.b&lang=en)

### 中文

没有必要对此恐慌。

## colony.9905.name

### 中文

扫描成功

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1343`

### English

Lucky Scan

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1609`

## colony.9905.desc.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9905.desc.a&lang=en)

### 中文

得益于在[Root.GetName]上部署的追踪系统，我们获得了重要的线索。\n\n在近期[Owner.GetColonistPlural]失踪案例中，我们都在事件发生后迅速进行了扫描，进而得到了他们最后出现的位置。而在每个案例中，我们都发现了一种衰变的带电粒子残留物。\n\n我们已经成立了一个特殊项目，以确定这些粒子的真实性质及其与我们失踪[Owner.GetColonistPlural]之间的关系。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1344`

### English

Thanks to the implementation of the tracking system on [Root.GetName] we have a substantial lead.\n\nAs the most recent of our [Owner.GetColonistPlural] disappeared, and we quickly followed up with a scan of their last location ping. A decaying residue of charged particles was found in each instance.\n\nWe've established a special project to determine the true nature of the particles and their involvement in our missing [Owner.GetColonistPlural].

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1610`

## colony.9905.desc.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9905.desc.b&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[Root.GetName]上取得了幸运的突破！我们发现了最近失踪的[Owner.GetColonistPlural]的去处。对这块区域的详细扫描发现了一些带电粒子的衰变痕迹。\n\n我们已经成立了一个特殊项目，以确定这些粒子的真实性质及其与我们失踪[Owner.GetColonistPlural]之间的关系。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1345`

### English

We've had a lucky break on [Root.GetName]! We have uncovered the location of the most recent [Owner.GetColonistPlural] to go missing. A detailed scan of the area revealed a decaying residue of charged particles.\n\nWe've established a special project to determine the true nature of the particles and their involvement in our missing [Owner.GetColonistPlural].

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1611`

### English

### English

## colony.9910.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9910.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

参照残留痕迹，我们通过全恒星系的扫描，在[Root.GetName]的卫星上发现了那些粒子的存在证据。\n\n通过搜索卫星表面，我们找到了一坐被沙尘掩埋了数百年的小型气闸。其中是一座被遗弃的行政办公复合体、一台远距离物质传送机和一个连接在极先进静滞仓网络的力场操纵系统。\n\n静滞仓中冷冻着许多个体，其中也包括我们失踪的殖民者。几乎可以肯定这座设施就是一座监狱。我们还需要弄清楚他们被抓进来的原因以及解救的方法。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1349`

### English

Using the residue as a reference, a system-wide scan found evidence of the particles within [Root.GetName]'s moon.\n\nA search of the lunar surface revealed a few small airlocks buried under centuries of dust. Inside was an abandoned complex of admin offices, long-range matter transporters, and field manipulation systems connected to a network of highly advanced stasis pods.\n\nFrozen in the pods were many individuals, including our missing colonists. There is little doubt that this facility functioned as a prison. We have yet to determine why they were taken and how we can free them.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1615`

## colony.9910.a

棒极了

### English

## colony.9915.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9915.name&lang=en)

### 中文

逃离卫星

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1351`

### English

Escape From The Moon

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1617`

## colony.9915.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9915.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

真相和阴谋相去甚远，我们失踪的[Owner.GetColonistPlural]是由于系统故障而遭到关押。\n\n计算机的一个故障激活了监狱老旧的传送系统，随机地将几位我们的[Owner.GetColonistPlural]定罪。这座设施的建造者将这些静滞仓兼用于囚禁犯人和再教育功能。将我们的人口释放出来相对容易，但许多这些囚犯仍被困在静滞场中。尽管我们可以将他们释放，但由于没有犯罪记录，我们无法完整得知这些人的犯罪过往。\n\n或者，我们可以修复这座监狱，让专职的监狱长控制它的传送系统。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1352`

### English

Far from a conspiracy, our missing [Owner.GetColonistPlural] were imprisoned because of a glitch.\n\nA computer error reactivated the prison's aging transporter system and randomly identified our [Owner.GetColonistPlural] as guilty. The facility's architects used the pods for both prisoner storage and re-education. Freeing our people was relatively easy, but many original prisoners are still stuck in stasis fields. It should be possible to release them, although the lack of prison records means we cannot know the full extent of their criminal pasts.\n\nAlternatively, we could restore the prison, giving special wardens control of its transporter system.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1618`

## colony.9915.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9915.a&lang=en)

这可是不规矩的[Owner.GetColonistPlural]们的好去处。

### English

A useful device for wayward [Owner.GetColonistPlural].

## colony.9925.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9925.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]上释放的囚犯既有顽固的犯罪分子也有政治意见者：艺术家、作家和音乐家等等。\n\n虽然这些文化囚犯很高兴与我们分享他们的知识，但许多罪犯走投无路又开始重操旧业。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1365`

### English

The prisoners released on [Root.GetName] were a mix of hardened criminals and political dissidents: artists, writers, and musicians.\n\nWhile the cultural prisoners are happy to share their knowledge with us, lacking any other option, many of the criminals have reverted to their old ways.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1631`

## colony.9930.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9930.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]上释放的囚犯大多数都是政治异见者：艺术家、作家和音乐家等等。\n\n在重获自由之后他们很乐意与我们分享他们的文化和知识。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1368`

### English

## d_stasis_prison_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_stasis_prison_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在这颗行星的卫星里面，有一座连接在高度先进的静滞仓网络上的废弃的监狱复合体。被冷冻在其中的囚犯无法逃脱。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1375`

### English

Inside this planet's moon, an abandoned prison complex is connected to a network of highly advanced stasis pods. Prisoners frozen within are unable to escape.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1643`

## d_prisoner_intergration

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_prisoner_intergration&lang=en)

### 中文

结构性整合

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1376`

### English

Structured Integration

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1644`

## d_prisoner_intergration_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_prisoner_intergration_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

将这些静滞的囚犯一次性释放是个错误的决定。通过对案例的逐一分析我们才能更好地确保每一个个体可以完全整合进我们的社会中。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1377`

### English

Freeing the stasis prisoners all at once would have been a mistake. By examining them on a case by case basis, we can better ensure that each individual can fully integrate into our society.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1645`

## d_meals_in_pods

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_meals_in_pods&lang=en)

### English

## d_meals_in_pods_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_meals_in_pods_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些冷冻静滞的东西就是绝佳的预制肉。

### English

Those frozen in stasis make for excellent pre-packed meals.

## STASIS_PRISON_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASTASIS_PRISON_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]上的[Owner.GetColonistPluralCap]最近开始一个接一个地失踪。调查发现了一些独特的带电粒子残留。我们会利用所有已知的信息来寻找失踪的[Owner.GetColonistPlural]。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1382`

### English

[Owner.GetColonistPluralCap] on [Root.GetName] have been disappearing. A break in the investigation indicated the presence of a uniquely charged particle residue. We will use all available information to locate the missing [Owner.GetColonistPlural].

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1651`

静滞看守

## colony.9310.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9310.name&lang=en)

### 中文

轨道垃圾场

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1397`

### English

Junkyard Orbit

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1668`

## colony.9310.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9310.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[planet.GetName]上传回的最新消息称我们对行星废弃物管理能力的计算发生了错误。大量的垃圾场融合在一起，形成了一片无法通行的废土，一直延伸到行星人口中心的边缘。\n\n当地人开始大规模地将这些垃圾发射到轨道上，不过这也只是缓兵之计。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1398`

### English

The latest report from [planet.GetName] informs us that we have miscalculated the planet's waste management capabilities. Massive dumpsites have merged to form a single, impassable wasteland, one that stretches to the very edge of the planet's population centers.\n\nThe locals have begun to deploy large shipments of garbage into orbit, but only as a 'temporary measure.'

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1669`

## colony.9310.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9310.a&lang=en)

### 中文

令人担忧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1399`

### English

Alarming.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1670`

## colony.9310.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9310.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

马上把这个烂摊子收拾一下。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1400`

### English

Clean up their mess. Immediately.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1671`

## colony.9315.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9315.name&lang=en)

### 中文

碎片笼罩

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1401`

### English

Veiled in Debris

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1672`

## colony.9315.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9315.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

事故已经发生了。\n\n由于一个严重的故障，许多环绕[planet.GetName]垃圾仓发生了碰撞，大量的碎片进入了底层大气。这一事故已经限制了行星的航天能力，据估计，随着这些太空垃圾的持续瓦解，情况只会越来越糟。然而，收集这些垃圾又是一项费时费力的大工程。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1402`

### English

There has been an accident.\n\nDue to a critical error, several trash pods orbiting [planet.GetName] collided, causing large amounts of debris to enter the lower atmosphere. This has already limited the planet's spacefaring capabilities, and according to estimates, the situation could further worsen as the space trash continues to break down. However, launching an operation to collect the debris might prove long and costly.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1673`

## colony.9315.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9315.a&lang=en)

### 中文

打扫轨道。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1403`

### English

Clean the orbit.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1674`

## colony.9315.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9315.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

部署特殊单位。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1404`

### English

Deploy the special units.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1675`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9315.b&lang=en)

### 中文

太空这么大。这点儿垃圾算什么。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1405`

### English

Space is big. A little garbage is no concern.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1676`

### English

There are more pressing issues in the galaxy.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1677`

## colony.9315.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9315.c&lang=en)

宣告紧急事态。

### English

Declare a state of crisis.

## colony.9315.c.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9315.c.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

我们必须竭尽所能清理这些碎片。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1408`

### English

We MUST do everything in our power to collect the debris.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1679`

## colony.9320.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9320.name&lang=en)

### 中文

轨道清理完成

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1409`

### English

Orbital Clean-Up Complete

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1680`

## colony.9320.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9320.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这是[planet.GetName]上里程碑式打得一天，太空垃圾收集工完成了最后的一个轮班。环绕着[planet.GetName]的垃圾环带的规模已经缩小到不再对通过的舰船构成主要威胁了。\n\n在未来的日子里，仍会有小规模的清理工作继续进行。完全把轨道清理干净也许是不可能的，但至少最坏的情况已经过去了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1410`

### English

A momentous day on [planet.GetName] as the astrogarbage collectors have completed their final shift. As of this day, the trash rings surrounding [planet.GetName] have been reduced by such a scale that they no longer pose a major threat to passing ships.\n\nIn the coming days, the work will continue on a smaller scale. It might be impossible to clean the orbit entirely, but at least the worst is over.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1681`

## colony.9320.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9320.a&lang=en)

### 中文

终于一切可以恢复正常了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1411`

### English

Finally things can return to normal.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1682`

## colony.9325.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9325.name&lang=en)

### 中文

封锁之地

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1412`

### English

Ground Locked

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1683`

## colony.9325.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9325.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

环绕[Planet.GetName]的失控垃圾环带的事态趋于平缓之后，局势却突然急转直下。\n\n殖民地的科学家表示，一块块太空垃圾的碎片互相碰撞，形成了一个微碎片构成的星环，连带损害的风险严重阻碍了进出殖民地的资源和人口的转运。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1413`

### English

After dismissing concerns over some runaway space garbage in orbit around [Planet.GetName], the situation has drastically escalated.\n\nAs the colonial scientists suggested, individual pieces of space trash have collided to create a halo of microtrash around the planet. This has made it extremely hard to transport resources and people in or out of the colony without risking collateral damage.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1684`

## colony.9325.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9325.a&lang=en)

### 中文

糟糕。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1414`

### English

Oops.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1685`

## colony.9330.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9330.name&lang=en)

### 中文

流星雨

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1415`

### English

Shooting Stars

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1686`

## colony.9330.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9330.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

环绕[Planet.GetName]的失控垃圾环带的事态趋于平缓之后，事态似乎小事化了了。\n\n在行星的强引力场作用下，无数的流星开始大规模地坠落，在大气层内开始燃烧。每到夜幕降临，人们开始习惯于聚在一起驻足观看这一独特而壮丽的流星雨。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1416`

### English

After dismissing concerns over some runaway space garbage in orbit around [Planet.GetName], the issue seems to have resolved itself.\n\nThousands of 'shooting stars' have begun to fall en masse as the planet's strong gravitational pull brings the trash close enough to burn in the atmosphere. Nightly gatherings have become commonplace as people arrive to witness the unique display.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1687`

## colony.9330.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9330.a&lang=en)

### 中文

创意观星时间到。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1417`

### English

Time for some recreational stargazing.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1688`

## colony.9335.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9335.name&lang=en)

### 中文

天清空净

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1418`

### English

Clear Skies

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1689`

## colony.9335.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9335.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

随着太空垃圾收集工完成了最后一个轮班，[planet.GetName]上发生了一场历史性的庆祝活动。\n\n从今天开始，一度萦绕在行星轨道上的垃圾环带已减少到无法探测的程度。由于成效显著，人们轰动般地支持着收集工们的进一步行动，确保子孙后代能见到一片晴空。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1419`

### English

A historic celebration has erupted on [planet.GetName] as the astrogarbage collectors' final shift has ended.\n\nAs of this day, the trash rings that once surrounded the planet are no longer detectable. Due to this success, the collectors have received resounding support to continue their operation, ensuring clear skies for generations to come.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1690`

## colony.9335.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9335.a&lang=en)

### 中文

值得纪念的一天。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1420`

### English

A momentous day.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1691`

## orbital_cleanup

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorbital_cleanup&lang=en)

### 中文

轨道净空

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1421`

### English

Orbital Clean-Up

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1692`

## orbital_cleanup_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorbital_cleanup_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

在一低层大气碰撞事故后，[Root.GetName]的轨道布满了太空碎片。如果不尽快清理，次生灾害可能导致这颗星球的交通中断。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1422`

### English

The orbit of [Root.GetName] is littered with space debris after a collision in the lower atmosphere. It must be cleared before a cascading effect renders the planet inaccessible.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1693`

## orbital_cleanup_intense

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorbital_cleanup_intense&lang=en)

### 中文

强力轨道净空

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1423`

### English

Intense Orbital Clean-Up

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1694`

## orbital_cleanup_intense_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorbital_cleanup_intense_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

在一低层大气碰撞事故后，[Root.GetName]的轨道布满了太空碎片。必须不惜一切代价进行彻底的清理才能保证定居者未来的安全。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1424`

### English

The orbit of [Root.GetName] is littered with space debris after a collision in the lower atmosphere. It must be thoroughly cleaned to ensure a secure future for the inhabitants, regardless of the cost.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1695`

## ground_locked_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aground_locked_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在一次严重的行星低轨太空碎片碰撞后，直到进一步通知前，再也没有舰船允许通过。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1448`

### English

After serious collisions of space debris in the planet's lower orbit, no vessels can pass through until further notice.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1719`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aclear_skies_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

该星球的天空中最近刚刚清理掉了危险的太空碎片，人们得以再度自由地进出这颗星球。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1450`

### English

The skies of this world have recently been cleared from hazardous space debris, allowing people to travel in and out as they please.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1721`

密弥库蚁活动

### English

Myrmeku Activity

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1760`

## colony.9350.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9350.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们一在§Y[Root.GetName]§!安顿下来就发现了大量的节肢类活动。具体来说，有一种叫做“密弥库蚁”的会建造聚群的微小昆虫出乎意料地数量众多。\n\n它们的地下群落数量众多，遍布整片大陆，但目前为止还没有造成过什么问题。目前尚不清楚它们是在寻找着地表上的什么东西，或是为了躲避地下的什么东西而逃出。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1488`

### English

Since settling on §Y[Root.GetName]§!, we have noted increased arthropod activity. Specifically, tiny colony-building insects designated "Myrmeku" have become unexpectedly productive.\n\nTheir underground colonies are spread across the continent in great numbers, but until now they have never caused an issue. It is unknown if they are searching for something above ground or fleeing from something beneath.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1761`

## colony.9350.a.mem_vault

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9350.a.mem_vault&lang=en)

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1489`

### English

Update "Myrmeku" in the Vaults of Knowledge.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1762`

### English

A fascinating species.

## colony.9355.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9355.name&lang=en)

### 中文

[Owner.GetColonistPluralCapital]被攻击

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1491`

### English

[Owner.GetColonistPluralCapital] Attacked

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1765`

## colony.9355.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9355.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在§Y[Root.GetName]§!上的[Owner.GetColonistPlural]遭受到了残暴的攻击！由于太过靠近密弥库蚁的群落，它们被蚁拥而上。这些微小的昆虫钻进了[Owner.GetColonistPlural]的框架内，这些受害者很快就被击溃了。\n\n落败的[Owner.GetColonistPlural]在一瞬间就被肢解了，但关于这种昆虫的行为目的没有记录下任何有意义的信息。的磁性外骨骼凹凸不平的表面会破坏我们的内部电路，当两只密弥库蚁接触时，它们会产生微小的电火花；这两种情况都可能导致灾难性的系统故障。\n\n以这种方式损失[Owner.GetColonistPlural]拖慢了这颗[Root.GetPlanetMoon]的发展速度。我们必须采取些行动。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1492`

### English

Our [Owner.GetColonistPlural] on §Y[Root.GetName]§! have been viscously attacked! Venturing too close to a Myrmeku colony, they were swarmed. As the tiny insects entered openings in the [Owner.GetColonistPlural]' frames, the victims were quickly overwhelmed.\n\nThe fallen [Owner.GetColonistPlural] were immediately disassembled, but no conclusive data was collected as to the insects' purpose. The craggy surface of the Myrmeku's magnetic exoskeletons damage our internal circuitry, and when two Myrmeku touch, they create a tiny electrical spark; both of which may cause catastrophic failure.\n\nLosing [Owner.GetColonistPlural] this way has slowed down the [Root.GetPlanetMoon]'s development rate. Something must be done.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1766`

### English

## colony.9360.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9360.name&lang=en)

### 中文

记忆吞食者

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1494`

### English

Memory Eaters

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1769`

## colony.9360.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9360.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

密弥库蚁以迅雷不及掩耳之势在我们的定居点附近建造了数座大型建筑。他们的攻击已经不再局限于周边地区了，而是开始出现在居住区域中。\n\n大量可供解剖的对象揭示了密弥库蚁对我们感兴趣的令人不安的原因：这种昆虫吃我们§Y存储芯片§!的绝缘材料。\n\n这种致命性对受害者只是暂时的，因为记忆电路可以替换并恢复备份。然而，替换这些部件就会让新[Owner.GetColonistPlural]的建造放慢。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1495`

### English

With astounding speed, the Myrmeku have built several sprawling structures close to our settlement. Their attacks are no longer limited to the perimeter, but have also begun inside housing structures.\n\nThe large number of subjects available for autopsy has revealed the disturbing reason why the Myrmeku are interested in us: The insects consume the insulation material of our §Ymemory chips§!.\n\nThis is only fatal for the victim in the short term, as memory circuits can be replaced and backups restored. However, replacing the parts means delays for the construction of new [Owner.GetColonistPlural].

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1770`

## colony.9365.desc.seal_colony

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9365.desc.seal_colony&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[Root.GetName]§!上的殖民建筑进行了完全的密封以避免密弥库蚁进入。\n\n所有新旧建筑的入口都增加可以磁化的金属条带来捕获密弥库蚁，但在野外[Owner.GetColonistPlural]仍会遭受攻击而损失。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1507`

### English

The colony buildings on §Y[Root.GetName]§! were completely sealed off to keep the Myrmeku insects out.\n\nThe entrances of both new and existing buildings were outfitted with metallic strips that can be magnetized to trap the Myrmeku, but [Owner.GetColonistPlural] are still being lost to attacks in the field.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1783`

## colony.9365.desc.upgrade_robots

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9365.desc.upgrade_robots&lang=en)

### 中文

将我们的记忆芯片封装在额外保护的外壳中的升级套件已经在进行开发和现场测试。\n\n不幸的是，密弥库蚁的下颚足够强壮，只要有足够的时间和数量，它们可以吃掉任何可用的材料。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1508`

### English

Upgrade kits to house our memory chips in an additional protective shell have been developed and field tested.\n\nUnfortunately, the Myrmeku mandibles are strong enough to eat through any available material, given enough time and numbers.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1784`

## colony.9365.desc.pest_control

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9365.desc.pest_control&lang=en)

### 中文

为了杀光密弥库蚁，我们派出了巡逻队寻找它们的巢穴。地表的结构很容易通过燃烧和爆破来摧毁，但地下的群落实在过深，难以进行完全的清理。\n\n而在行动过程中我们甚至失去了更多的[Owner.GetColonistPlural]。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1509`

### English

To kill off the Myrmeku, patrols were sent to scan for nests. It was easy to destroy the structures above ground with fire and explosives, but the underground colonies were buried deep enough that they were unfeasible to completely eradicate.\n\nWe lost even more [Owner.GetColonistPlural] during the operation.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1785`

## colony.9365.desc.harvest

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9365.desc.harvest&lang=en)

### 中文

为了控制密弥库蚁，我们在它们的巢穴附近建造了生物捕获器。地表的结构很容易进行收获，但地下的群落实在过深，难以进行完全的清理。\n\n而在行动过程中我们甚至失去了更多的[Owner.GetColonistPlural]。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1510`

### English

To control the Myrmeku, bio harvesters were established near their colonies. The structures above ground were easily harvested, but the underground colonies were buried too deep to completely eradicate.\n\nWe lost even more [Owner.GetColonistPlural] during the operation.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1786`

## colony.9370.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9370.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

杀死§Y[Root.GetName]§!上所有有机生物的准备已经完成。\n\n行动过程中，在轨道上观看[Root.GetPlanetMoon]让我们有机会得以欣赏地表有机复杂性的降低。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1513`

### English

Everything has been prepared for the cleansing of biological life on §Y[Root.GetName]§!.\n\nObserving the [Root.GetPlanetMoon] from orbit during the event will give us an opportunity to appreciate the reduction in organic complexity on the surface.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1790`

## colony.9375.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9375.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

至今为止，为解决密弥库蚁问题的努力尚未得到成功。这些昆虫依旧数量惊人，袭扰着[Root.GetName]各地，殖民地的交通各种活动深受其害。我们分析子例程通过对未来预期的模拟得出了以下方案。\n\n应对方案A：通过向远离殖民地的区域空投记忆体绝缘材料来将密弥库蚁引走，并回收利用损坏的[Owner.GetColonistPlural]。\n\n应对方案B：研究这些密弥库蚁，寻找到他们残暴本性的可能利用方式。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1516`

### English

Efforts so far have not put an end to the Myrmeku problem. The insects still crawl across [Root.GetName] in immense numbers, making travel and off-colony operations difficult. Our analysis subroutine has concluded its simulation of potential futures with the following approaches.\n\nApproach A: Distract the Myrmeku by airdropping memory insulation material far off from our colony and recycle the fallen [Owner.GetColonistPlural].\n\nApproach B: Study the Myrmeku to see if we can find a way to turn their vigorous activity into something useful.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1794`

## colony.9380.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9380.name&lang=en)

### 中文

强力结果

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1520`

### English

Powerful Results

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1799`

## colony.9380.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9380.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们对密弥库蚁的研究完成了。把校准过的磁体放置在它们的聚落入口附近时，这些昆虫的磁性外骨骼就会释放出我们能够利用的电能。\n\n这些研究还表明，密弥库蚁会依照当地磁场移动，因此在我们的记忆芯片加装去磁保护壳就可以避免受其攻击。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1521`

### English

Our study of the Myrmeku has concluded. When calibrated magnets were placed around the entrances of their colonies, the magnetism in the insect exoskeletons generated electricity that we could harness.\n\nThe study also showed that the Myrmeku move according to local magnetic fields, so installing a demagnetized protective shell around our memory chips should be enough to protect them.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1800`

## colony.9380.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.9380.a&lang=en)

### 中文

棒极了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1522`

### English

Brilliant.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1801`

## colony.7230.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.7230.name&lang=en)

### 中文

冰川危机

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1524`

### English

Glacial Crisis

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1804`

## colony.7230.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.7230.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[planet.GetName]的最新气候报告带来了一项令人担忧的结论。奇怪的是，冷冻行星上的表面温度一直以惊人的速度上升。\n\n如果我们无法确定生态系统迅速恶化的原因，这种情况可能会导致行星广阔冰川的灾难性融化。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1525`

### English

The latest climate report from [planet.GetName] has come to a concerning conclusion. Inexplicably, the surface temperature on the frozen planet has been rising at an alarming rate.\n\nIf we fail to identify the reason for the ecosystem's rapid deterioration, the situation could lead to catastrophic melting of the planet's vast glaciers.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1805`

## colony.7230.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.7230.a&lang=en)

### 中文

找出行星升温的原因。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1526`

### English

Find out why the planet is heating.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1806`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.7230.a.tooltip&lang=en)

这能是什么原因引起的呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1527`

### English

What could be the cause?

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1807`

## colony.7230.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.7230.b&lang=en)

没什么可担心的。

### English

It's nothing to worry about.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1808`

## colony.7235.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.7235.name&lang=en)

### 中文

冰中裂痕

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1529`

### English

Cracks in the Ice

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1809`

## colony.7235.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.7235.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[flooding_planet.GetName]上温度的升级正在急剧加速。这引起了冰冷的表层之下出现了巨大裂缝，揭开了广阔的地下海洋。\n\n幸运的是，我们的调查已经确定了这种现象的潜在原因。由于我们的存在，保护行星的独特大气盾已经开始变得出极其脆弱，并随时可能崩溃。采取严格的措施来遏制我们的生产力将最大程度地减少我们对生态的影响。这样做应该足够解决这个问题。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1530`

### English

The escalation of temperatures on [flooding_planet.GetName] is accelerating dramatically. This has caused substantial fractures within the icy surface layers, unveiling a vast subsurface ocean.\n\nFortunately, our investigations have identified a potential cause for this phenomenon. Due to our presence, the unique atmospheric shield safeguarding the planet has begun to exhibit critical fragility and could potentially collapse. Implementing stringent measures to curb our production would minimize our ecological impact. It might be enough to solve the issue.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1810`

## colony.7235.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.7235.a&lang=en)

### 中文

修改我们的生产指标。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1531`

### English

Revise our production targets.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1811`

冰川期到此为止。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1532`

### English

It is about time the ice age ended.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1812`

## colony.7240.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.7240.name&lang=en)

### 中文

地核融化

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1534`

### English

Core Meltdown

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1814`

## colony.7240.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.7240.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[flooding_planet.GetName]上排放量做出限制的尝试并未产生理想的的结果。我们在解决持续性生态危机方面的仅仅略有成功，由于海平面上升，地理意义上的低地正受到威胁。\n\n目前，我们的人口中心仍然免受直接影响，但几乎可以肯定的是，行星范围的全面融化不可避免。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1535`

### English

Our attempt to limit our emissions on [flooding_planet.GetName] has not yielded promising results. We have been only marginally successful in addressing the ongoing ecological crisis, and geographical lowlands are coming under threat due to the rising sea levels.\n\nAt present, our population centers remain shielded from immediate impact, but it is almost certain planet-wide meltdown cannot be avoided.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1815`

## colony.7240.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.7240.a&lang=en)

### 中文

尝试减轻损失。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1536`

### English

Try to mitigate the damages.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1816`

### English

停止全部生产活动直到找到解决方案。

Halt all production until a solution is found.

## colony.7245.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.7245.name&lang=en)

### 中文

巨大的洪泛区

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1539`

### English

Vast Floodplains

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1819`

## colony.7245.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.7245.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在我们忽视迅速恶化的[flooding_planet.GetName]生态状况后，局势已逐渐陷入前所未有的灾难。\n\n巨大的冰盖的瓦解导致海洋蔓延，带来了巨大的破坏和动荡。建造防洪大坝，我们可以保护其余的城市，但更多的不可预见的后果将会接踵而至。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1540`

### English

In the wake of our neglect to address the rapidly deteriorating ecological state of [flooding_planet.GetName], the situation has spiraled into an unprecedented cataclysm.\n\nThe disintegration of vast ice sheets has given rise to sprawling oceans, leaving behind immense devastation and upheaval. By building flood barriers we can protect our remaining cities, but more unforeseeable consequences still linger on the horizon.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1820`

## colony.7245.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.7245.a&lang=en)

### 中文

行星正在发大水！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1541`

### English

The planet is flooding!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1821`

## colony.7247.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.7247.name&lang=en)

### 中文

永冻之秘

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1542`

### English

Secrets of the Permafrost

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1822`

## colony.7247.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.7247.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

随着[flooding_planet.GetName]行星的持续融化，在永霜冻土下有一个有趣的发现。无人知晓，无人开发且惊人丰富的矿物和稀有资源的极地宝库整静静地等待着我们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1543`

### English

As the meltdown of the planet [flooding_planet.GetName] continues, a fascinating discovery was made beneath the permafrost. An astonishingly abundant polar reservoir of minerals and rare resources lies waiting, beckoning us with untold wealth and untapped potential.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1823`

## colony.7247.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.7247.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们中头奖了，赶快开采！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1544`

### English

Jackpot. Start drilling!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1824`

## colony.7247.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.7247.b&lang=en)

### 中文

永霜冻土已经遭受了太多的痛苦了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1545`

### English

The permafrost has suffered enough.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1825`

## colony.7247.b.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.7247.b.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

我们不能冒着恶化局势的危险。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1546`

### English

We cannot risk worsening the situation.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1826`

## colony.7250.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.7250.name&lang=en)

### 中文

解冻的病原体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1547`

### English

Unfrozen Pathogen

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1827`

## colony.7250.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.7250.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[flooding_planet.GetName]上的极地开采站出现了令人不安的进展。工人们突然失去意识，为了安全起见，必须建立严格的禁止通行区域。\n\n最初的调查表明，由于冰冷的环境被改变，休眠病毒的苏醒过来。这种苏醒的病原体的真实本性和特征仍然未知，这给我们带来了许多未解难题。它的传播性及对人民构成的潜在危险程度仍然不确定。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1548`

### English

A disconcerting development has unfolded at our polar mining station on [flooding_planet.GetName]. Workers have succumbed to sudden bouts of unconsciousness, necessitating the implementation of a stringent no-pass zone for their safety.\n\nInitial investigations suggest the emergence of a dormant virus, awoken as its icy confines were disturbed. The true nature and characteristics of this awakened pathogen remain uncertain, leaving us grappling with a multitude of unanswered questions. The extent of its transmissibility and the potential danger it poses to the people remains undetermined.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1828`

## colony.7250.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.7250.a&lang=en)

### 中文

不要冒险。立刻给该区域消毒。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1549`

### English

Take no chances. Sanitize the area.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1829`

## colony.7250.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.7250.b&lang=en)

### 中文

保持隔离。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1550`

### English

Keep the quarantine up.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1830`

## colony.7255.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.7255.name&lang=en)

### 中文

极地财富

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1551`

### English

Polar Riches

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1831`

## colony.7255.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.7255.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在[flooding_planet.GetName]极地地区的空洞深处，一个采矿团队发现了巨大的财富。在冰雪层的面纱之下发现了像冰川一样雄伟的巨大晶体。\n\n这些非凡的结构，是在无数的岁月中在巨大的压力下铸造的，这证明了这里存在我们尚未发现的无限潜力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1552`

### English

In the hollowed depths of the polar regions of [flooding_planet.GetName], a mining team has made a discovery of monumental proportions. Veiled beneath layers of ice and snow, colossal crystals reminiscent of majestic glaciers were found.\n\nThese extraordinary formations, forged under immense pressure over untold ages, stand as a testament to the unlimited potential we have yet to uncover.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1832`

## colony.7255.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.7255.a&lang=en)

### 中文

真是令人难以置信的发现。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1553`

### English

What an incredible find.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1833`

## colony.7260.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.7260.name&lang=en)

### 中文

气候稳定

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1554`

### English

Climate Stabilized

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1834`

## colony.7260.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.7260.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经达到了环境突破。\n\n燃料燃烧产生的无害副产物，与[flooding_planet.GetName]的细菌云接触时产生了奇特的副作用。我们现在知道，这些云通过遮蔽恒星光芒来保留行星的自然生物节律，这点至关重要。随着细菌逐渐开始减少，我们无意中加剧了排放的影响。\n\n凭借这种新发现的知识，我们已迅速制定了措施以恢复平衡。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1555`

### English

We have reached an environmental breakthrough.\n\nThe innocuous byproducts generated through the combustion of our fuels harbor an insidious side-effect when coming to contact with [flooding_planet.GetName]'s bacterial clouds. We now know that these clouds play a vital role in retaining the planet's natural biorhythm by working as a solar shield. As the bacteria slowly begun to diminish, we inadvertently exacerbated the effect of our emissions.\n\nArmed with this newfound knowledge, resolute measures have been swiftly enacted to restore the equilibrium.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1835`

## colony.7260.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.7260.a&lang=en)

### 中文

终于，气候再次稳定了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1556`

### English

At last, the climate is stable again.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1836`

## colony.7265.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.7265.name&lang=en)

### 中文

冰川期的终结

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1557`

### English

End of an Ice Age

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1837`

## colony.7265.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.7265.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[flooding_planet.GetName]最后一个冰川沉入海洋，新时代随即开始。永霜冻土的消失揭露出一个宽敞的水下领域，那里面充满了未开发的资源。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1558`

### English

A new climatic era begins as the last vestiges of [flooding_planet.GetName]'s glaciers sink into the ocean. The disappearance of the permafrost has uncovered an expansive underwater realm, filled with untapped resources.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1838`

## colony.7265.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.7265.a&lang=en)

### 中文

海滩度假时间到。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1559`

### English

Time to hit the beach.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1839`

### English

## PLANET_MELTDOWN_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3APLANET_MELTDOWN_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

[flooding_planet.GetName]上的气候迅速恶化。希望我们可以赶在局面无法扭转之前弄清楚原因。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1561`

### English

The climate on [flooding_planet.GetName] is rapidly deteriorating. Hopefully we can figure out why before it is too late to reverse the process.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1841`

## MELTING_RESEARCH_PROJECT

## MELTING_RESEARCH_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AMELTING_RESEARCH_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

[flooding_planet.GetName]上的温度迅速升高。我们的[Root.GetScientistPlural]需要扩大他们的研究范围，以解决正在进行的局势所造成的新问题。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1563`

### English

The temperature on [flooding_planet.GetName] has risen alarmingly quickly. Our [Root.GetScientistPlural] need to expand the scope of their studies to address the new issues created by the ongoing situation.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1843`
