## RAPID_DESERTIFICATION_CAT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ARAPID_DESERTIFICATION_CAT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.Planet.GetName]的航拍图像显示，其迅速减少的植被可能表明，该行星表面存在一种未知的生命形式栖息在此。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1603`

### English

Aerial images taken of [From.Planet.GetName]'s rapidly diminishing vegetation could indicate the presence of an unidentified life form habiting its surface.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1889`

## anomaly.7135.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7135.name&lang=en)

### 中文

已识别外星动物群

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1604`

### English

Alien Fauna Identified

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1890`

## anomaly.7135.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7135.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[desertling_planet.GetName]上发现了生命。没有智慧的小型哺乳动物群遍布在整个行星表面。\n\n我们的科学家将它们标记为“沙生兽”，它们的数量正以惊人的速度增加。这个行星已经超出了它的承载能力，生态系统正在迅速恶化。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1605`

### English

We have found life on [desertling_planet.GetName]. Vast non-sapient herds of small mammals roam across the entire planetary surface.\n\nTheir population, labeled 'desertlings' by our scientists, is increasing at an alarming rate. The planet is already beyond its capacity, and the ecosystem is deteriorating rapidly.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1891`

## anomaly.7135.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7135.a&lang=en)

### 中文

调查为什么现在会发生这种情况。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1606`

### English

Investigate why this is happening now.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1892`

## anomaly.7135.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7135.b&lang=en)

### 中文

最好不要干预。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1607`

### English

Best not intervene.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1893`

## anomaly.7135.b.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7135.b.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

我们将从远处监控局势。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1608`

### English

We'll monitor the situation from afar.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1894`

## RAPID_DESERTIFICATION_PROJECT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ARAPID_DESERTIFICATION_PROJECT&lang=en)

### 中文

生态系统被蚕食

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1609`

### English

Encumbered Ecosystem

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1895`

## RAPID_DESERTIFICATION_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ARAPID_DESERTIFICATION_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

一群迅速蔓延的哺乳动物在行星[desertling_planet.Planet.GetName]上过度繁殖。凭借适当的资源，我们的科学家可以收集更多关于这种非智慧物种的起源和能力的线索。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1610`

### English

The planet [desertling_planet.Planet.GetName] is overpopulated by a herd of rapidly spreading mammals. With the proper resources, our scientists could gather more insights about the origins and capabilities of this non-sapient species.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1896`

## anomaly.7140.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7140.name&lang=en)

### 中文

入侵物种

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1611`

### English

Invasive Species

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1897`

## anomaly.7140.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7140.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在研究[desertling_planet.GetName]上的沙生兽时，我们的科学家观察到了一个关键的现象。尽管这个物种到处都是，但却没有相应的化石记录。这些生态入侵者在相对短的时间内造成了极端的环境破坏，很可能是被某个外来者带到这里的。\n\n形势已经十分清楚，迫切需要我们进行干预。如果不采取行动，这些生物将摧毁这个星球自然生态系统中剩下的一切。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1612`

### English

While studying the desertlings on [desertling_planet.GetName] our scientists have made a pivotal observation. Despite the ubiquitous nature of the species, there is a striking absence of matching fossil records. These ecological interlopers have caused extreme environmental damage over a relatively short time period, and were most likely transported here by an outside actor.\n\nThe pressing need for intervention has become abundantly clear. Without action, these creatures will destroy what remains of this planet's natural ecosystem.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1898`

## anomaly.7140.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7140.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们将尽可能多收养这些小东西。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1613`

### English

We will adopt as many critters as possible.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1899`

## anomaly.7140.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7140.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

我们将对他们的行为负责。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1614`

### English

We will be responsible for their actions.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1900`

## anomaly.7140.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7140.b&lang=en)

### 中文

制定动物数量控制政策。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1615`

### English

Enact animal population controls.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1901`

## anomaly.7140.b.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7140.b.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

选择遗传修饰可用于遏制生长…

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1616`

### English

Select genetic modifications could be used to curb the growth...

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1902`

## anomaly.7140.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7140.c&lang=en)

### 中文

消灭这些野兽。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1617`

### English

Exterminate the beasts.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1903`

## anomaly.7140.c.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7140.c.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

必须不惜一切代价保护行星。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1618`

### English

The planet must be protected at all cost.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1904`

## anomaly.7140.d

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7140.d&lang=en)

### 中文

我们将一些动物当作牲口。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1619`

### English

We'll take some animals as livestock.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1905`

## anomaly.7140.d.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7140.d.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

我们将向每个殖民地带去两个奴隶。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1620`

### English

We'll take two slaves for every colony.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1906`

## POPULATION_CONTROL_PROJECT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3APOPULATION_CONTROL_PROJECT&lang=en)

### 中文

人口控制

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1621`

### English

Population Control

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1907`

## POPULATION_CONTROL_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3APOPULATION_CONTROL_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

为了拯救[desertling_planet.GetName]的生态状态，必须抑制入侵动物群的指数级数量增长。我们的科学家需要时间和资源来找到可行的解决方案。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1622`

### English

To save the ecological state of [desertling_planet.GetName] the exponential population growth of the invading fauna's population must be suppressed. Our scientists need time and resources to find an executable solution.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1908`

## anomaly.7145.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7145.name&lang=en)

### 中文

灭绝事件

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1623`

### English

Extinction Event

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1909`

## anomaly.7145.base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7145.base&lang=en)

### 中文

已经屈服于指数繁殖的哺乳动物的侵袭。深深的寂静笼罩着现在贫瘠的大地，所有存留的植被都被吞噬到根部。\n\n杂食性的哺乳动物奄奄一息，要么直接饿死，要么在愈演愈烈的饥荒中被同类吃掉。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1624`

### English

has succumbed to the infestation of the exponentially breeding mammals. Deep silence envelops the now barren landscape, where all traces of vegetation have been devoured down to their roots.\n\nThe omnivorous mammals themselves lie still, either perished from starvation, or eaten by their kin as the famine progressed.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1910`

## anomaly.7145.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7145.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们收到消息，[desertling_planet.GetName]$anomaly.7145.base$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1625`

### English

We have received news that [desertling_planet.GetName], $anomaly.7145.base$

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1911`

## anomaly.7145.b.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7145.b.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们收到消息称[desertling_planet.GetName]$anomaly.7145.base$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1626`

### English

We have received news that [desertling_planet.GetName] $anomaly.7145.base$

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1912`

## anomaly.7145.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7145.a&lang=en)

### 中文

至少苦难现在结束了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1627`

### English

At least the suffering is now over.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1913`

## anomaly.7145.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7145.b&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的殖民地被毁。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1628`

### English

Our colony is destroyed.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1914`

## anomaly.7150.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7150.name&lang=en)

### 中文

自然的新生

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1629`

### English

Nature's Rebirth

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1915`

## anomaly.7150.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7150.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

经过艰苦的努力，我们对[desertling_planet.GetName]上的沙漠生物进行扑杀和基因改造，它们的数量终于降低到了一个可持续的水平。\n\n没有了过多的食草动物，一个微妙的平衡出现了。大自然正在迅速恢复，新鲜的草地开始在曾经荒凉的沙漠上生长。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1630`

### English

After an arduous effort to sterilize and genetically alter the desertlings on [desertling_planet.GetName], their population has finally been reduced to a sustainable level.\n\nWithout the overwhelming amount of grazers, a delicate equilibrium has emerged. Nature is rapidly restoring itself, as fresh patches of grass begin to sprout over the once barren desert.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1916`

## anomaly.7150.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7150.a&lang=en)

### 中文

一处隐蔽的天堂。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1631`

### English

A hidden paradise.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1917`

## anomaly.7155.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7155.name&lang=en)

### 中文

红色沙漠

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1632`

### English

Red Desert

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1918`

## anomaly.7155.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7155.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

最终，最后一只啮齿动物在[desertling_planet.GetName]上死去。沙子本身已经被染成了红色，这是因为我们为拯救星球免于侵染所付出的巨大努力，但是新的绿芽已经开始萌发。我们出手的时候刚刚好。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1633`

### English

Finally, the last of the rodents lie dead on [desertling_planet.GetName]. The sand itself has been tinted red by our massive effort to save the planet from its infestation, but already new green buds have begun to sprout. We were just in time.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1919`

## anomaly.7155.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7155.a&lang=en)

### 中文

这是必须要做的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1634`

### English

It had to be done.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1920`

## colony.7160.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.7160.name&lang=en)

### 中文

国家宝藏

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1635`

### English

National Treasures

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1921`

## colony.7160.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.7160.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

当我们向这些沙生兽开放我们的殖民地时，没有人能够预料到这样的结果。这些小动物已经完美地适应了我们的生活方式，在我们的领土上，成为了家庭生活中的可爱伴侣。\n\n它们的快速融入引发了一种猜测，即这些生物一定曾经被驯化过。无论是什么原因导致了它们与生俱来的友好，这个传言只增加了人们对这些非凡生物的更进一步兴趣。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1636`

### English

When we opened our colonies to the desertlings, no one could have anticipated the results. The critters have adapted seamlessly into our way of life, becoming beloved companions in households all across our territory.\n\nTheir rapid integration has led to speculation that these creatures must at once time have been domesticated. Whatever the cause of their innate friendliness, the rumor only adds to the fascination surrounding these extraordinary creatures.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1922`

## colony.7160.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.7160.a&lang=en)

### 中文

某种程度上，它们拯救了我们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1637`

### English

In a way, they saved us.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1923`

## colony.7165.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.7165.name&lang=en)

### 中文

麻烦的小动物

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1638`

### English

Troublesome Critters

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1924`

## colony.7165.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.7165.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

可怕的消息传来。几只沙生兽在运输过程中逃脱，形成了一个迅速扩大的野生种群。我们必须迅速处理这种情况，以避免环境崩溃。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1639`

### English

Dire news has reached us. A few desertlings have broken free during transportation, forming a rapidly expanding community in the wild. The situation must be handled swiftly to avoid an environmental collapse.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1925`

## colony.7165.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.7165.a&lang=en)

### 中文

猎杀那些禽兽！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1640`

### English

Hunt the beasts down!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1926`

## colony.7165.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.7165.b&lang=en)

### 中文

用围墙建立一个保留地。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1641`

### English

Build a walled reserve.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1927`

## colony.7170.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.7170.name&lang=en)

### 中文

哺乳生物数量稳定

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1642`

### English

Mammalian Population Stabilized

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1928`

## colony.7170.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.7170.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在遏制猖獗的沙生兽侵袭方面取得了一定的成功。遗憾的是，我们无法根除这些难以捉摸的野兽。继续监测情况至关重要，因为它可能随时达到临界点。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1643`

### English

We have been moderately successful in curbing the rampant desertling infestation. Regrettably, we have been unable to eradicate the elusive beasts. It is crucial to continue monitoring the situation, as it could reach a tipping point at any moment.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1929`

## colony.7170.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.7170.a&lang=en)

### 中文

狩猎继续。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1644`

### English

The hunt continues.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1930`

## colony.7175.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.7175.name&lang=en)

### 中文

失控的侵染

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1645`

### English

Uncontrollable Infestation

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1931`

## colony.7175.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.7175.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们尝试控制沙生兽，结果是徒劳无功。这些贪婪的生物深挖洞穴，它们扩大它们殖民地的速度比我们消灭它们的速度还要快。除非我们允许我们的军队使用其庞大的爆炸物和有毒气体武器库，否则这场战斗就会失败，并将危及地面上每个人的安危。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1646`

### English

Our attempt to control the infestation of desertlings has been rendered futile. The voracious creatures have already burrowed deep, expanding their large colonies faster than we can exterminate them. The battle is lost unless we allow our military to use their vast arsenal of explosives and noxious gases, a course of action which would risk the well-being of everyone on the surface.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1932`

### English

## BREACHSEALER_CAT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ABREACHSEALER_CAT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

暗淡的瑟顿星系潜藏着一处宇宙织构的裂痕。更不同寻常的是这个裂口本身正在收缩，似乎有什么东西或是什么人正在修复它。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1677`

### English

The dim Seddom system houses a tear in the fabric of the universe. Even more unusual, the tear itself is gradually shrinking, as if something or someone is working to repair it.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1969`

## anomaly.7200.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7200.name&lang=en)

### 中文

缝合裂口

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1678`

### English

Closing Aperture

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1970`

## anomaly.7200.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7200.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

瑟顿之中的景象光怪陆离。涡旋的行星在黑暗中若隐若现；附近是我们宇宙本身织构的一个缺口，也是一个吞噬着其中恒星光芒的宇宙巨胃。\n\n尽管行星体的排列看似正常，但进一步的研究发现它们是以一种非常精确的数学关系安排到一起的。数百万年前，某种难以置信的力量将这个恒星系的轨道天体编排成了一种独特的图样，这种图案不仅影响着裂隙，还避免了它裂向更广阔的星系。\n\n我们似乎来得正好——这项史诗级的工程即将竣工。这个星系距离恢复正常近在咫尺。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1679`

### English

Seddom is an eerie spectacle. The swirling planets are wreathed in darkness; nearby is a breach in the very fabric of our universe, a cosmic maw that devours the very radiance of the system's star.\n\nDespite the seemingly natural arrangement of planetoids, further analysis has revealed that they are arranged with mathematical precision. Millennia ago, some incredible force orchestrated the orbital bodies of this system into a specialized pattern, one which has not only mitigated the effects of the tear, but prevented it from spreading into the wider galaxy.\n\nWe arrive here at an auspicious moment - a monumental work nears completion. This system's restoration is imminent.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1971`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.7200.a&lang=en)

### English

## GEOCENTRIC_EXPERIMENT_CAT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AGEOCENTRIC_EXPERIMENT_CAT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

在[from.Solar_System.GetName]恒星系中探测到了许多怪异的重力读数，从内小行星带到恒星系中心的荒芜行星四处闪烁着这些信号。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1684`

### English

Strange gravimetric readings have been detected throughout the [from.Solar_System.GetName] system, pulsing from the inner astroid belt to the desolate planet at the heart of the system.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1977`

## anomaly.8000.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.8000.name&lang=en)

### 中文

$GEOCENTRIC_EXPERIMENT_CAT$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1685`

### English

$GEOCENTRIC_EXPERIMENT_CAT$

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1978`

## anomaly.8000.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.8000.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[from.Solar_System.GetName]恒星系的重力稳定是由小行星带中释放出来的§Y$sr_dark_matter$§!维系起来的。这些发射源被一台位于中心行星上的超级电脑控制着。中控电脑的记录表明，整个恒星系是一处由某个远古文明建设的重力操控实验设施。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1686`

### English

The gravitational stability of the [from.Solar_System.GetName] system is maintained by §Y$sr_dark_matter$§! emitters positioned within its asteroid belt. These emitters are controlled by a supercomputer located on the central planet. Records from this central computer suggest the entire system was an experimental site for gravity manipulation, established by an ancient civilization.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1979`

## anomaly.8000.tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.8000.tt&lang=en)

### 中文

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1687`

### English

Adds §Y$d_physics_1$§! and §Y$d_dark_matter_deposit_1$§! to every §Y$GRAVITY_STABILIZER$ Asteroid§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1980`

## MYSTERIOUS_ROCK_CAT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AMYSTERIOUS_ROCK_CAT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

在调查[From.GetName]的时候，我们发现了一种怪异的岩石形态，矗立在这颗本以为空无一物的荒凉行星上。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1699`

### English

While surveying [From.GetName], we uncovered a grotesque rock formation from what has been thought to be nothing but a barren planet.

## anomaly.2000.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.2000.name&lang=en)

### 中文

怪异巨石

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1700`

### English

Grotesque Rocks

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1997`

## anomaly.2000.start.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.2000.start.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对[From.GetName]的进一步探索非但没解开多少谜题，反而让它更加疑点重重。这里的地表到处都散落着栩栩如生的雕塑，脸上无声地凝固着痛苦的神情。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1701`

### English

Continued exploration of [From.GetName] has yielded more questions than answers. The surface is littered with eerily lifelike statues, their expressions petrified in silent agony.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1998`

## anomaly.2000.default.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.2000.default.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$anomaly.2000.start.desc$\n\n科学家们的意见发生了分歧；有些认为这些东西曾经是生物，是被一种未知的力量变成了石头。而另一些则认为它们代表了一个失落已久的文明所崇拜的神灵。更谨慎的少数人认为，这些雕像不是一种致敬，而是一种警告：对任何胆敢骚扰这颗行星的人发出严厉的警告。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1702`

### English

$anomaly.2000.start.desc$\n\nOur scientists are divided; some believe that these forms were once living beings, turned to stone by an unknown force. Others suggest they represent deities, worshipped by a long-forgotten civilization. A more cautious few believe the statues are not a tribute, but a warning: a grim message to any who would dare to disturb the planet.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:1999`

## anomaly.2000.hive.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.2000.hive.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$anomaly.2000.start.desc$\n\n我们的集体意识中涌出了许多关于这些雕像的不同想法，比如它们可能是被未知力量变成石头的活物，或者代表了某种古代失落文明所崇拜的神祇。亦或许这些雕像并不是致敬，而是一种警告：对任何胆敢骚扰这颗行星的人发出严厉的警告。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1703`

### English

$anomaly.2000.start.desc$\n\nMany different thoughts flood our collective consciousness some of these forms could have been living beings, turned to stone by an unknown force. Or perhaps they represent deities, worshipped by a long-forgotten civilization. Maybe these statues are not a tribute, but a warning: a grim message to any who would dare to disturb the planet.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2000`

## anomaly.2000.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.2000.a&lang=en)

### 中文

继续研究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1704`

### English

Continue the research.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2001`

## anomaly.2000.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.2000.b&lang=en)

停止调查。

### English

Discontinue the investigation.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2002`

## anomaly.2005.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.2005.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]船员的进一步调查发现，这些雕像是某种与石质类高度相似的古代物种的遗骸。曾经维持其形态的某种力量早已消散了。现在，它们暴露在自然环境中，被逐渐侵蚀成细小的灰尘，将它们的历史残余散进了[From.GetName]无情的风中。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1706`

### English

Further investigation by the crew of [From.GetName] has identified the statues as remnants of an ancient species bearing strong resemblance to the Lithoids. Whatever essence once held their forms together has long since deteriorated. Now, exposed to the elements, they gradually erode into fine dust scattering the remains of its history into the relentless winds of [From.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2003`

## anomaly.2005.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.2005.a&lang=en)

### 中文

很迷人。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1707`

### English

Fascinating.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2004`

## anomaly.2006.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.2006.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]号船员们的进一步尝试被一阵突如其来的狂风打断，这股风横扫了整个研究现场。那些脆弱的石像瞬间化为齑粉，它们曾承载的谜题也随之湮灭无痕。由于缺乏可供分析的残留证据，也无法得出明确结论，我们别无选择，只能终止此次调查。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1708`

### English

Further attempts by the crew of [From.GetName] were abruptly halted when a sudden gust of wind swept across the research site. The brittle stone figures crumbled into dust, erasing any trace of the riddle they once posed. With no remaining evidence to analyze and no clear conclusions to be drawn, we are left with little choice but to discontinue the investigation.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2005`

## anomaly.2006.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.2006.a&lang=en)

### 中文

真不幸。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1709`

### English

Unfortunate.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2006`

探索怪异岩石形态

### English

Explore the Grotesque Rock Formation

## GROTESQUE_ROCK_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AGROTESQUE_ROCK_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[From.GetName]的地表偶然发现了一种怪异的岩石形态。需要进一步研究才能确定为何它会存在于这颗其他方面都贫瘠的星球上。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1711`

### English

We have stumbled upon a grotesque rock formation on the surface of [From.GetName]. Further study is needed to determine why it remains on this otherwise barren planet.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2008`

## ICY_FLOWERS_CAT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AICY_FLOWERS_CAT&lang=en)

### 中文

寒冷的发现

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1713`

### English

A Cold Discovery

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2011`

## ICY_FLOWERS_CAT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AICY_FLOWERS_CAT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

对[From.GetName]地表厚重冰层多次进行扫描，仪器均产生了杂乱数据及异常干扰模式。冰层之下存在某种持续干扰探测设备的物体，我们建议进一步调查。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1714`

### English

Repeated attempts to scan the thick glacial layer covering [From.GetName] resulted in erratic data and unexpected interference patterns. Something beneath the ice keeps disrupting our equipment. Further investigation is advised.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2012`

## anomaly.2010.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.2010.name&lang=en)

### 中文

植物乐园

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1715`

### English

Botanical Paradise

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2013`

## anomaly.2010.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.2010.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在[From.GetName]冰壳之下的勘探，我们发现了一片广阔的地下洞穴。令人惊叹的是，这座隐蔽的温室中存在着一个郁郁葱葱的生态系统——茂密的动植物在隔绝环境中蓬勃生长，受到冰层保护而远离星球表面恶劣的环境。洞穴内保持着稳定的微气候，很可能是由地热喷口维持的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1716`

### English

Excavation beneath the icy crust of [From.GetName] has revealed a vast subterranean cavern. Remarkably, this hidden greenhouses is a thriving ecosystem, a lush flora and fauna flourish in isolation, protected by the ice from the planet's surface otherwise hostile environment. The cave maintains a stable microclimate, likely sustained by geothermal vents.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2014`

## anomaly.2010.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.2010.a&lang=en)

### 中文

要进一步进行探索。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1717`

### English

There is more to explore here.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2015`

## anomaly.2010.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.2010.b&lang=en)

中止项目。

### English

Abandon the project.

## anomaly.2015.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.2015.name&lang=en)

### 中文

亿载难逢

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1719`

### English

Defying All Probabilities

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2017`

## anomaly.2015.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.2015.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在洞穴的更深处，我们遇到了一片广袤的花田。当主动观测开始时，整片花田瞬间绽放，整个洞窟被绚丽的生物荧光所淹没。植物学分析表明，该物种每一亿年才开花一次，需要环境因素达到精确的排列组合才能触发。\n\n我们的到来竟与如此罕见的事件同时发生，这概率微乎其微。但无论纯属巧合还是另有玄机，这无疑是一次非凡的体验。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1720`

### English

Deeper within the cavern, we encountered a sprawling field of flowers. The moment active observation began, the entire field erupted into bloom, flooding the chamber in radiant bioluminescence. Botanical analysis suggests this species only blooms once every 100 million years, triggered by a precise alignment of environmental factors.\n\nThat our arrival coincided with such an event defies all probability, but whether mere coincidence or something more, it was surely a remarkable experience.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2018`

## anomaly.2015.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.2015.a&lang=en)

### 中文

令人惊叹的发现。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1721`

### English

A stunning discovery.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2019`

## anomaly.2020.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.2020.name&lang=en)

### 中文

无法探索的秘密

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1722`

### English

Unexplorable secrets

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2020`

## anomaly.2020.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.2020.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

随着进一步深入洞穴，地震活动突然导致结构失稳。突如其来的构造震颤在岩壁间传导，随时可能引发坍塌。面对迅速恶化的环境，我们立即启动了紧急撤离程序。洞穴中的秘密只能徒留遗憾，无法探索了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1723`

### English

Upon further descent into the cavern, seismic activity abruptly destabilized the structure. A sudden tectonic tremor cascaded through the walls, threatening to trigger an imminent collapse. Faced with the rapidly deteriorating conditions, we initiated immediate evacuation protocols. The secrets within the cave remain unexplorable.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2021`

## anomaly.2020.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.2020.a&lang=en)

### 中文

真是不幸。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1724`

### English

Unfortunate

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2022`

### English

## BOTANICAL_GARDEN_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ABOTANICAL_GARDEN_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

在[From.GetName]的冰封地壳之下，存在一片广阔的地下洞穴。我们拟派遣一支考察队，对这一特殊环境展开进一步研究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1726`

### English

There is a vast subterranean cavern underneath the icy crust of [From.GetName]. We would like to send an expedition to study this extraordinary environment further.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2024`

## colony.10000.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10000.name&lang=en)

### 中文

怪异脚印

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1728`

### English

Strange Footprints

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2027`

## colony.10000.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10000.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[Root.GetName]的边远农业站上发回了报告。许多[Owner.GetFarmerPlural]无缘无故失踪了，除了周围的土壤上留下了巨大的脚印外，他们的家园没有受到任何影响。\n\n这些痕迹不符合任何在此前行星调查中发现的东西：四足、宽趾、有爪的脚印，其深度足以说明来自一种相当大的生物。殖民者担心我们可能遇到了未知的捕食者。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1729`

### English

Reports have come in from our outlying agricultural stations on [Root.GetName]. Several [Owner.GetFarmerPlural] have vanished without a trace, their homesteads eerily undisturbed, save for the appearance of immense footprints in the surrounding soil.\n\nThe tracks are unlike anything examined in the planetary survey, quadrupedal, broad-toed with claws, and deep enough to suggest a creature of considerable mass. The colonists worry that we may be dealing with some unknown predator.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2028`

## colony.10000.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10000.a&lang=en)

进一步调查！

### English

Investigate further!

### English

Idle speculations. Back to work.

## colony.10005.name

### 中文

死亡的怪兽

### English

## colony.10005.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10005.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

深入荒野的探险艰苦卓绝，但最终我们获得了累累硕果。经过数日追踪那生物的踪迹，我们的团队抵达了一处满目疮痍的现场：巨兽的尸体——摆在我们眼前的是厚重的几丁质外骨骼、棘刺状的脊椎隆起，以及末端带弯曲毒刺的分节尾巴。尸身上布满多处贯穿伤。\n\n在其后方，仪器探测到一座被高效伪装的光藓藤蔓遮蔽的古老花园，里面满是缠绕常春藤的大理石柱，光滑的黑曜石小径。团队谨慎前行，直至瞥见花园的守护者——一个举止优雅的类人种族，肌肤泛着微光。尽管景象祥和，那些发光的花藤却构成了有效屏障。我们必须穿透这层闪烁的遮蔽，才能接近生活在屏障彼端的存在。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1733`

### English

The expedition into the wilderness proved taxing, but ultimately fruitful. After days of following the creature's trail, our team arrived at a site of devastation: the corpse of the beast displays a heavily chitinous exoskeleton, thorned spinal ridges, and a segmented tail ending in a single curved stinger. The body is perforated by multiple punctures. \n\nBeyond it, concealed beneath a highly effective cloaking canopy of bioluminescent vines, our instruments detect an ancient garden. Marble columns entwined with ivy, smooth obsidian paths. Our team approached carefully until we caught a glimpse of the garden's caretakers: a humanoid species, elegant in bearing, their skin subtly luminous. Despite the harmonious appearance, the flowers and vines offered an effective veil. We would need to penetrate this glinting cover in order to approach the ones who lived beyond.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2034`

尝试穿透这层伪装。

## colony.10010.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10010.name&lang=en)

### 中文

持续失踪事件

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1735`

### English

Continued Disappearances

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2038`

## colony.10010.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10010.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

事件发生的频率已经升级。这一次，那些足迹不再只是从农舍旁经过，而是径直撕裂了房屋。整栋建筑都以骇人的力量被扯碎。挣扎的痕迹表明受害者是被活捉的，而且并未束手就擒。\n\n殖民者们不再只是感到不安，而是陷入了恐慌。已有数户人家放弃了自己的土地，逃往殖民地的中心地带。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1736`

### English

The frequency of the incidents has escalated. This time, the footprints did not merely pass by the homesteads; they tore straight through them. Entire structures have been ripped apart with alarming force. Traces of struggle suggest the victims were taken alive and did not go quietly.\n\nThe colonists are no longer merely unsettled; they are terrified. Several families have already abandoned their holdings for the colony center.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2039`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10010.a&lang=en)

### 中文

是时候找出凶手了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1737`

### English

Time to find the perpetrator.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2041`

暴力是生活的一部分。

### English

Violence is a part of life.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2042`

## colony.10015.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10015.name&lang=en)

### 中文

鲜花之庭

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1739`

### English

A Court of Blossoms

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2044`

## colony.10015.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10015.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我以花庭之名，欢迎各位旅者。我是坦佩里翁国王，或者像我的族人所称呼的“金心者”，我并非以武力统治，而是通过先辈遗训和仪式来执掌权柄。我既是这个世界的守护者，也是我的人民——阿纳萨里——的引路人。听到你们自己的语言是否感到惊讶？不必害怕。自从你们决定将[Root.GetName]视为你们的家园以来，我们一直在研究你们。你们的人民一定非常强大，才能穿透我们的屏障。我们很荣幸能与你们相见。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1740`

### English

I welcome you, travelers, in the name of the Court of Blossoms. I am King Tamperion, or as my kinship calls me, the 'Goldenhearted', sovereign not by rule of force, but by legacy and ritual. I serve as custodian of the this world and shepherd of my people, the Anathari. Are you alarmed to hear your own language? Never fear. We have been studying you ever since you decided to call [Root.GetName] your home. You must be a powerful people indeed, if you have managed to penetrate our cloak. We are honored to meet you.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2045`

## colony.10015.a.question

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10015.a.question&lang=en)

### 中文

鲜花之庭是什么？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1741`

### English

What is the Court of Blossoms?

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2047`

## colony.10015.a.answer

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10015.a.answer&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的花庭代表着永恒的繁荣。我们的文化引以为傲的是，它并非以武器或暴力统治为定义，而是以和谐为定义，而和谐是通过我们每个人内心的相互理解和善意来实现的。在鲜花之庭中，传统不是旧日的残余，而是我们的法则。与我们分享你们的故事、歌曲和作物，便能一窥我们的生活方式，通过彼此理解进行交流。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1742`

### English

We are the court of eternal bloom and prosperity. Our culture prides itself by not being defined by weapon or dominion, but by harmony, achieved through mutual understanding and kindheartedness within every single one of us. In the Court of Blossoms, tradition is not a remnant, but our law. To share our stories, songs, and crops with us is to have a glimpse at our way of living in a mutual exchange of understanding.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2048`

## colony.10015.b.question

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10015.b.question&lang=en)

### 中文

你能告诉我们关于那个怪物的一些信息吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1743`

### English

What can you tell us about the monster?

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2050`

## colony.10015.b.answer

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10015.b.answer&lang=en)

### 中文

你遇到的生物，大守护者，曾经是我们的保护者。但随着时间推移，它被内部的疫病吞噬，一种寄生在它大脑内的神经型虫草菌株播下了祸根，掏空了它的思想，腐化了它的目的。我们结束了它的痛苦，并非出于愤怒，而是出于悲痛。它不是外来的野兽，而是我们的一部分。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1744`

### English

The creature you encountered, the great guardian, was once our protector. But in time, it was consumed by a blight from within, seeded by a neural-cordyceps strain nested inside its brain, hollowing its mind and corrupted its purpose. We ended its suffering not in anger, but in grief. It was not an alien beast, but a part of us.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2051`

## colony.10015.c.question

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10015.c.question&lang=en)

### 中文

你们想从我们这里得到什么？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1745`

### English

What do you want from us?

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2053`

## colony.10015.c.answer

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10015.c.answer&lang=en)

### 中文

你们或许会觉得我们的行事之道很奇怪。仪式、庆典、吟唱，与会思考和梦想的植物共生。但请理解：与我们共存并非无偿给予。那些拒绝学习的人将会承担后果。这是我们的提议。既非臣服，亦非控制，而是相互理解和接纳。如果你愿意接受这样的提议，那我们庆贺。这正是我们所定义的，我们族群之间和谐相处的纯粹本质。如果你们选择与我们同行，你们将不会孤身一人。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1746`

### English

You may find our ways strange. Rituals, ceremonies, chants, symbiosis with flora that thinks and dreams. But understand this: coexistence with us is not granted freely. Those who refuse to learn will experience consequences. This is our offer. It is neither submission nor control, but mutual understanding and acceptance. Celebration, if you will. It is the purest essence of what we define as harmony among our folk. Should you walk that path with us, you will not walk alone.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2054`

## colony.10016.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10016.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

谨代表鲜花之庭全员，衷心感谢您的合作。请放心，您先前派遣来的人员，我们将其视作贵客，使其宾至如归。绝不会让他们有任何需求上的短缺。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1750`

### English

On behalf of the entire Court of Blossoms, I extend our sincere gratitude for your cooperation. Rest assured, the one you send to us will be welcomed as one of our own, and there shall be nothing they want for.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2060`

## colony.10017.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10017.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

既如此，随你便吧。你已做了决定，我们亦然。我已尽了我的劝告之责，现在你必须承担你选择的后果。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1753`

### English

So be it. Your decision is made, and so is ours. I have offered my counsel, yet now you must bear the fruits of your choice.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2064`

突然袭击

## colony.10020.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10020.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

毫无预兆。坦佩里翁国王从花园的中心现身，身上披着由活体藤蔓和发光花朵编织而成的仪式盔甲。他沿着殖民地边缘，每一步都走得缓慢而庄重，整个世界也在苏醒。藤蔓从土壤中蜿蜒而出，花瓣如刀锋般绽放。空气中弥漫着金色的花粉，细微而闪耀。它渗透进通风口和肺部。几个小时之内，它夺走了许多人的生命，引发癫痫、疯狂，最终导致死亡。\n\n最终，依靠殖民地的自动化防御系统的介入才将他击倒。坦佩里翁国王一言不发地倒下了；目击者说他最后的目光中没有仇恨，只有深深的、庄严的失望。\n\n我们侦察兵发现，曾经的鲜花之庭所在之处，只剩下了森林。仿佛这个王国已经把自己折叠回了土壤之中，带走了它的秘密和它的悲伤。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1756`

### English

There was no warning. King Tamperion emerged from the heart of the gardens, draped in ceremonial armor spun from living vines and bioluminescent blossoms. With each deliberate step along the colony's edge, the world itself awakened. Vines slithered from the soil, while petals unfurled like blades. The air grew thick with a golden pollen, fine and luminescent. It seeped into vents and lungs. Within hours, it had claimed the life of many, inducing seizures, madness, and finally, death.\n\nOnly the intervention of automated colony defenses eventually brought him down. King Tamperion fell without a word; witnesses say his final gaze held not hatred, but a deep and solemn disappointment.\n\nWhere the Court of Blossoms once was, our scouts found only forest. It is as though the kingdom had folded itself back into the soil, taking its secrets and its sorrow with it.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2068`

苦涩的结局。

### English

A bitter ending.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2070`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10025.name&lang=en)

### 中文

其中一员

### English

One of Them

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2072`

## colony.10025.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10025.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们全身心投入了阿纳萨里的怀抱。我们参与他们的仪式并与他们独特的礼仪相协调，模式出现了，意义下也扎了根。\n\n在以长寿周期和繁育为重点的夜间公众仪式中，阿纳萨里开始揭示被压抑的历史片段。他们谈到一种古老而持久的枯萎病，它不但曾经笼罩着这个星球，而且现在仍然威胁着阿纳萨里。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1759`

### English

We immersed ourselves in the ways of the Anathari. As we participated in ceremonial rites and attuned with their peculiar rituals, patterns emerged while meaning took root.\n\nDuring shared nocturnal ceremonies focused on longevity cycles and fertility, the Anathari began to reveal fragments of suppressed history. The folk spoke of an ancient and enduring blight that once blanketed the planet and still has control over the Anathari now.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2073`

## colony.10025.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10025.a&lang=en)

### 中文

有太多可聊的了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1760`

### English

There is much to discuss.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2075`

## colony.10026.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10026.name&lang=en)

### 中文

坦佩里翁国王

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1761`

### English

King Tamperion

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2077`

## colony.10026.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10026.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

曾几何时，远在你们踏足这片土地之前，我们阿纳萨里曾经不过是卑微的管事。我们按照它的呼吸节奏塑造这颗星球，引导生态系统，平衡水流，像无限耐心的园丁照顾花园一般照料这颗有生命的星球。但这种平衡很快遭受到了挑战。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1762`

### English

There was a time, long before your kind set foot upon this soil, when we, the Anathari, served as noble stewards. We shaped this world in rhythm with its breath, guiding ecosystems, balancing currents, tending to the living world as one tends to a garden with infinite patience. Yet balance does not persist unchallenged for long.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2078`

## colony.10026.a.question

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10026.a.question&lang=en)

### 中文

是谁打破了这般平静？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1763`

### English

Who or what disturbed this balance?

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2080`

## colony.10026.a.answer

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10026.a.answer&lang=en)

### 中文

远在历史的根系之下，一个叫做“永朽之暗”的实体破土而出，它的力量无源又无束。我们起先以为它没有恶意，所以就打开了大门，邀请它加入我们的互惠交换中。但它一进入我们的家门，就向我们释放出了全部的力量，把我们全部都奴役了。我们的内心正在渐渐腐朽，慢慢地，我们将会枯萎。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1764`

### English

From beneath the roots of history rose an entity we call 'The Dark Immortal', a force neither born nor bound. We believed it came in peace at first, so we opened our doors to it, inviting it in for mutual exchange. But once inside our borders, it unleashed its full power on us, enslaving us in an instant. Now we are rotting from within. Slowly, we are withering away.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2081`

## colony.10026.b.question

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10026.b.question&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在其中扮演何种角色？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1765`

### English

What part do we play in this?

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2083`

## colony.10026.b.answer

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10026.b.answer&lang=en)

### 中文

你们[Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural]身上有一种我们早已遗失的坚强，一种不受我们历史约束的活力。所以我以这一位遗民而非君主的身份向你请求帮助？这个存在的来源随着每一个周期的流逝而增长，我不确定阿纳萨里还能抵御这种枯萎多长时间。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1766`

### English

You, [Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural], carry a strength we have long since lost. A vitality unbound by our history. And so I ask not as a sovereign, but as a remnant of all that once was, will you help us? The source of this presence grows with each passing cycle, and I am unsure how long the Anathari can still endure this blight.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2084`

## colony.10026.c.question

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10026.c.question&lang=en)

### 中文

你们需要而我们怎么做？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1767`

### English

What do you need us to do?

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2086`

## colony.10026.c.answer

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10026.c.answer&lang=en)

### 中文

必须直面永朽之暗。很久以前，曾有一位勇者尝试过，但她失败了。在弥留之际，她仍扯下了它魂魄的一部分。也许你们可以将其解码，并在[Root.GetName]上找到它的原主。你们的科技应该已经足以识破它的伪装。\n\n我们的存活只能寄希望于团结。记住：一旦失败，面临终结的可不仅仅是我们阿纳萨里和你们的殖民地。如果不加以约束，它甚至可能会吞没整颗行星。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1768`

### English

The Dark Immortal must be faced. Long ago, a great hero tried and perished. Yet in dying, she tore away a fragment of its essence. Perhaps you can decipher it and locate its counterpart somewhere on [Root.GetName]. Your technology may be strong enough to break its cloaking.\n\nOnly united can we hope to survive. Know this: failure means more than the fall of the Anathari, or the end of your colony. If left unchecked, this presence could devour the planet itself.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2087`

### English

## colony.10027.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10027.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

起初的和平交流开始呈现出更令人不安的微妙一面。参加阿纳萨里仪式会导致报告延迟、记忆碎片化以及睡眠改变。坦佩里翁国王仍然彬彬有礼，但当被问及这些仪式的深层目的时，他却讳莫如深。他的解释固然富有诗意，却也闪烁其词。甚至他的那个“金心者”头衔，也没有出现在阿纳萨里教义的记录中。一些人现在认为，这些仪式不仅是文化纽带，也是心理校准的工具。\n\n不管方式如何，结果都是明确的：我们正在与坦佩里翁和他的家族结盟。当他谈到永朽之暗的时候，感觉不像是外来的恳求，而更像是一种内在的神圣责任，帮助我们最亲爱的盟友，阿纳萨里。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1772`

### English

What began as a peaceful exchange has taken on a subtler, more troubling dimensions. Participation in Anathari rites has resulted in delayed reports, fragmented memories, and altered sleep. King Tamperion remains courteous, but increasingly evasive when asked about the deeper purpose of these rites. His explanations, though poetic, often deflect direct inquiry. Even his title, 'Goldenhearted', appears in no recorded Anathari doctrine. Some now suggest the rituals serve not just as cultural bonding, but as tools of psychological calibration.\n\nWhatever the method, the result is unmistakable: we are becoming aligned with Tamperion and his kin. When he speaks of the Dark Immortal, it feels less like foreign entreaty and more like a sacred duty to help our dearest allies, the Anathari.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2093`

### English

## colony.10030.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10030.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们迅速采取行动。一支民兵突击队渗透了鲜花之庭。我们袭击时，坦佩里翁国王在花园中冥想，但他也不是善茬。目击者的描述宛如一场植物的死亡之舞，藤蔓穿石而出，花茎在钢铁挠出道道抓痕。当国王终于被打倒时，整座花园已经被明亮的鲜血和灰烬浸透。\n\n花园之下埋藏的东西让我们大吃一惊，这简直是一座生物宝库：古老的知识、植物工程和无数收获周期的遗传储备。这里的土壤比星球上其他地方都肥沃，如今也不再被古老的看护者所约束。我们可能正要进入一个物质丰富的黄金时代。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1776`

### English

Our action was swift. A militia strike team infiltrated the Court of Blossoms. King Tamperion was meditating in the garden when we struck. But the king did not fall easily. Witnesses describe a terrifying display of botanical wrath, vines erupting from stone, floral constructs clawing through steel. By the time the king was brought down, the garden was soaked in luminous blood and ash.\n\nWhat remained beneath the garden astonished us, as it was a vault of biological treasures: ancient knowledge, botanical engineering, and stores of genetic stock from countless harvest cycles. The soil, now unbound from its ancient caretakers, was more fertile than any yet found on the planet. We may be entering a golden age of abundance.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2099`

### English

## A_CRYPTIC_ESSENCE_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AA_CRYPTIC_ESSENCE_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

坦佩里翁国王把永朽之暗的碎片托付给了我们。通过研究它的灵魂碎片，我们可能就能发现它的巢穴位置，找到打破它对阿纳萨里的控制的方法。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1779`

### English

King Tamperion has entrusted us with a fragment of the Dark Immortal. By studying its essence, we may uncover the location of its lair and the means to break its hold over the Anathari.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2104`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10035.name&lang=en)

### 中文

永朽之暗

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1782`

### English

The Dark Immortal

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2109`

## colony.10035.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10035.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

科学家们成功地解密了魂魄，将永朽之暗的位置锁定在一处荒凉的山顶上。很快，一支探险队就到达了山上的高原，找到了它的老巢。\n\n在中心隐约出现了这场试炼的构筑者：永朽之暗。这个实体来源不明，其形态由等离子体、烟雾、骨骼和具有灵能活性的物质不稳定地融合而成。它的话语绕过声音，直接刺入现场团队每一个成员的神经结构中。\n\n“你们是来归降于我的吗，小[Owner.GetSpeciesNamePluralInsult]们？”它抑扬顿挫地说道，灵能传音中夹杂着嘲讽的意味。“阿纳萨里是我最珍爱的财产。但我给你一个机会。参加试炼，证明你们值得我正眼相看。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1783`

### English

The scientists deciphered the essence successfully and located the Dark Immortal at a forlorn mountain top. Soon after, an expedition team arrived at the mountain's plateau, finding the site occupied.\n\nAt the epicenter loomed the architect of the trials: The Dark Immortal. An entity of incomprehensible origin, its form an unstable fusion of plasma, smoke, bone, and psionically active matter. Its voice bypassed sound, manifesting directly in the neural architecture of every team member present.\n\n'Have you come to subordinate yourselves, little [Owner.GetSpeciesNamePluralInsult]?' it intoned slowly, its thought-voice laced with mockery. 'The Anathari are my dearest possession. But I offer you a chance. Enter the trials and prove yourself worthy of my regard.'

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2110`

## colony.10040.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10040.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在永朽之暗的带领下，一位殖民者被传送到了深山之中的洞窟里。在阴暗的角落里，一只凶猛的怪虫等待着。那东西很像一只体型过于巨大的甲壳动物，外壳上闪烁着奇怪的矿物和古老的合金。它就像是在保卫地盘一般，动作协调得令人毛骨悚然，暗示着它具有颇高的智力水平。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1788`

### English

A single colonist was transported to a cave deep within the mountain, The Dark Immortal leading the way. A monstrous beast awaited in the darkest corner. The entity resembled a crustacean of impossible scale, its shell glittering with strange minerals and ancient alloys. It acted like it was guarding its turf, moving with an eerie coordination that hinted at a surprising level of intelligence.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2117`

## colony.10045.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10045.name&lang=en)

### 中文

通过勇者试炼

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1792`

### English

Succeeding the Trial of Bravery

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2123`

## colony.10045.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10045.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

被选中的殖民者向深渊冲锋，钢铁与甲壳在漆黑的洞窟中相撞。\n\n巨大的甲壳怪虫瞬间作出反应，它的巨钳劈开岩石，庞大的身躯撼动着山体。但殖民者早有准备。他将怪物引诱至更狭窄的通道，预判着它的动作，精准打击事先标记的关节与感知节点。\n\n当尘埃落定，这头巨虫终于倒下了，它最后的抽搐如雷鸣般在山体中回荡。殖民者浑身是血与尘土，却活着走了出来。\n\n永朽之暗缓缓点头，似乎对殖民者这鲁莽之举表示赞许。勇气的试炼，就此完成。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1793`

### English

Steel met shell in the pitch-dark cavern as the chosen colonist charged into the abyss.\n\nThe titanic Crustacean reacted instantly, its claws slicing through stone, its bulk shaking the mountain. But the colonist was ready. Luring it into tighter corridors, they baited the creature's movements, striking at joints and sensory nodes identified in advance.\n\nWhen the dust settled, the creature lay still, its final convulsion echoing through the mountain like thunder. The colonist emerged, covered in blood and dust, but alive.\n\nThe Dark Immortal gave a slow nod, as if in appreciation of the colonist's reckless actions. The trial of bravery was completed.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2124`

### English

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2129`

## colony.10046.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10046.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

正面战斗绝对不是可以进行的策略。殖民者观察着甲壳怪虫的移动，它对光的厌恶，对震动的超敏反应，以及最关键的，它的失明。它只对声音做出反应。每一次移动都是对回声和打击的反应。殖民者制定了一个计划，不是对抗，而是误导。\n\n殖民者向石室的中央石柱投掷了一系列石块。随着声音的回荡，野兽立刻做出了反应，向着噪声冲去。\n\n中央石柱破碎，导致石室的屋顶坍塌。殖民者在一个狭窄的凹陷处找到了庇护，免受部分落石的伤害。甲壳怪虫被埋在数吨重的石头下，被困住，最后永远地咽了气。\n\n永朽之暗似乎很欣赏殖民者的聪明才智。勇气的试炼，就此完成。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1797`

### English

Combat was never a viable strategy. The colonist observed the crustacean's movements, its aversion to light, its hypersensitivity to vibration, and most critically, its blindness. It responded only to sound. Every movement was an echo and strike reaction. The colonist formed a plan, not of confrontation, but misdirection.\n\nThe colonist hurled a series of stones against the chamber's central column. As the sound reverberated, the beast responded instantly, charging at the noise.\n\nThe central column shattered, bringing down the roof of the chamber. The colonist found refuge in a narrow recess, shielded from a partial rockfall. The crustacean was buried beneath tons of stone, trapped, and silenced.\n\nThe Dark Immortal seemed to appreciate the colonist's cleverness and wits. The trial of bravery was completed successfully.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2130`

## colony.10047.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10047.name&lang=en)

### 中文

勇气试炼失败

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1798`

### English

Failing the Trial of Bravery

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2132`

## colony.10047.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10047.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

野兽的利爪快如闪电，在洞穴的黑暗中发出微弱的嗡鸣，每一个动作都模糊不清，每一次攻击都伴随着低频脉冲，震得石头都在颤抖。\n\n殖民者小心翼翼地移动着，考虑着自己所能采取的对策。 虚晃一招，引诱，试图攻击，或者也许是分散注意力。 但这生物适应得比预期的更快。 它的下一次攻击准确命中，这生物因为愤怒身体发光，将整个洞穴都点亮了。\n\n当尘埃落定，殖民者已经死了。 我们未能通过试炼。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1799`

### English

The beast's claws were lightning-fast and hummed faintly in the cave's darkness, each movement a blur, each strike accompanied by a low-frequency pulse that set stone trembling.\n\nThe colonist moved carefully, considering their options. A feint, a lure, an attempted blow, or perhaps a distraction. But the creature adapted faster than expected. Its next strikes landed true, and the cavern lit up in a burst of bioluminescent rage.\n\nWhen the dust cleared, the colonist lay dead. We had failed the trial.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2133`

英勇之举

### English

A valiant effort.

智慧试炼

### English

Trial of Wisdom

## colony.10050.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10050.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一个声音开始在房间里渐渐响起。起初颤抖而犹豫，但很快就洪亮得如同岩壁都在吟诵一般：\n\n§H“不倚兵律出寂寥，\n缄口结契古恨消。\n意志机关不得使，\n潜移默化星河调。\n大象希形造化至，\n太平归境混沌消。”§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1802`

### English

A sound, at first trembling and uncertain, began to form within the chamber. Soon, it was as if the rocks themselves were speaking:\n\n§H'I am forged in silence, not conquest or codes,\nA pact unspoken, that ancient grudge erodes.\nNo engine drives me, no mind commands,\nYet I steady the stars with invisible hands.\nFew see me come, but all feel the change\nA stillness where chaos once held the range.'§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2138`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10050.a&lang=en)

### English

智慧试炼失败

Failing the Trial of Wisdom

## colony.10055.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10055.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这位殖民者的话刚一离开[Owner.Species.GetMouthName]，光亮就退却了。永朽之暗的声音切开了沉寂：“[Owner.GetSpeciesNameInsult]，”它说，“你以为对力量的渴望能解释一切吗？”\n\n就在那一刻，这位殖民者被击中了。吞没它的不是火焰或者武器，只有压力和沉默。他悄无声息地倒下了，山洞把尸体整个吞下。我们的试炼失败了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1806`

### English

As the words left the colonist's [Owner.Species.GetMouthName], the light dimmed. The Dark Immortal's voice cut through the stillness:'[Owner.GetSpeciesNameInsult],' it said, 'not everything can be answered by the sheer lust for power.'\n\nIn that instant, the colonist was struck. No weapon or flame engulfed them, just pressure and silence. They collapsed without a sound, and the chamber swallowed the body whole. We have failed the trial.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2144`

## colony.10060.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10060.name&lang=en)

### 中文

智慧试炼通过

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1807`

### English

Succeeding the Trial of Wisdom

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2146`

## colony.10060.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10060.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在说出答案之后，殖民者屏息凝神，希望自己能够答对。岩室里响起了赞许的隆隆声，永朽之暗看起来似乎很高兴。\n\n“你懂了，”它说道。“不是所有试炼都在考验力量。也有的只需要你拥有打开心扉的勇气。”\n\n谜语渐渐消失，柔和的光线充满了山洞。试炼获得了成功，前路还在等待着我们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1808`

### English

The colonist paused after speaking up, hoping to have presented the right answer. The chamber rumbled in approval, and the Dark Immortal seemed almost pleased.\n\n'You understood,' came its words. 'Not all trials are of strength or power. Some require only the courage to open one's mind.'\n\nA soft light filled the room as the riddle faded away. The trial was a success, and the path forward awaits.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2147`

### English

### 中文

牺牲试炼

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1811`

### English

Trial of Sacrifice

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2152`

## colony.10065.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10065.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一位殖民者踏前一步迎接挑战。他被抬升到一个漆黑的巨大祭坛上，中间摆着两把黑曜石椅子和一张矮长桌。上面只放了一件武器，一把优雅的弯刀。殖民者用[Owner.Species.GetHandName]把刀拾起，感受到了里面的力量正在微微震颤着。\n\n坦佩里翁王被捆在其中一张椅子上，捆住他的不是一般的绳索，而是某种基本力构成的发光丝线。他的表情难以捉摸，既不恐惧也不平均，只有死一般的沉默。而另一张椅子是空的。\n\n随后那个声音又一次进入了殖民者的脑海。\n\n“在这把刀面前，无论国王或是懦夫都是平等的。”永朽之暗喃喃说道，“想要通过这次审判，就必须夺去一条生命。要么剜下坦佩里翁王的心脏，要么就牺牲你自己；自己选吧。选错的话……试炼就结束了。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1812`

### English

A colonist stepped forward to face the trial, and was swept up to stand on a vast, darkened dais. In the center: two obsidian chairs and a long, low table. A single weapon was placed upon it, an elegant, curved blade. As the colonist picked it up with one [Owner.Species.GetHandName], it hummed faintly with power.\n\nIn one chair sat King Tamperion, bound not by rope, but by luminous cords of elemental force. His expression was unreadable, showing neither fear nor peace, only stillness. The other chair was empty.\n\nThen the voice spoke into the colonist's mind once more.\n\n'The blade has tasted kings and cowards alike,' the Dark Immortal whispered. 'To pass this trial, one life must be taken. Take the Anathari leader's heart, or sacrifice yourself; the choice is yours. Choose wrongly... and the trial will end.'

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2153`

## colony.10065.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10065.a&lang=en)

再见了，国王。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1813`

### English

Goodbye, king.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2155`

我们会做出牺牲的。

### English

We will make the sacrifice.

### English

## colony.10070.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10070.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这位殖民者站在坦佩里翁王的身前，静静地站着。弯刀躺在黑曜石桌光滑的台面上。永朽之暗没有做出任何引导。\n\n殖民者拿起弯刀，利落地刺进坦佩里翁王的胸膛，动作迅速而果断。他没有叫喊，只有柔和的金属鸣响，有如远方的钟声。\n\n坦佩里翁王抬起头来，眼神平静。刀刃完全没入，刺穿的东西不是肉体而是某种截然不同的物质。他的胸膛微微发光，闪烁着金色的光芒。坦佩里翁王确实是“金心者”……\n\n国王周围的束缚消失，祭坛也褪色了。永朽之暗又一次对我们说：“你们可以继续了。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1816`

### English

The colonist stood before the bound form of King Tamperion, silent, unmoving. Upon the obsidian table, the blade awaited. The Dark Immortal offered no guidance.\n\nThe colonist took the blade and drove it cleanly into Tamperion's chest, swift and decisive. He made no cry. There was only a soft metallic chime, like the distant clanging of bells.\n\nKing Tamperion looked up, eyes calm. The blade, buried to the hilt, had not pierced flesh, but something else entirely. His chest glowed faintly, pulsing with a golden light. King Tamperion the 'Goldenhearted' indeed...\n\nThe bindings around the king dissolved and the dais faded. The Dark Immortal spoke once more into our minds: 'You may proceed.'

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2159`

## colony.10070.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10070.a&lang=en)

### 中文

真是令人惊讶的结果。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1817`

### English

It is an astonishing outcome.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2161`

### 中文

牺牲试炼失败

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1818`

### English

Failing the Trial of Sacrifice

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2163`

## colony.10075.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10075.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

殖民者看着坦佩里翁王，手里攥着弯刀。坦佩里翁王静静地坐着，他温柔的目光既没有恳求，也没有准许。殖民者犹豫了一下，然后把刀刃对准了自己。\n\n殖民者猛地吸了一口气，倒在地上，响起了弯刀落在祭坛上的声音。选择很明确：放过阿纳萨里的领袖，独自承担重任。\n\n但永朽之暗的话既没有愤怒也没有喜悦：“同情不是宽恕。这把刀不该对自己用。”\n\n一滴眼泪无声地从坦佩里翁王的脸上滚落下来。我们以最大的好意决定了他的子民们的命运。\n试炼结束了，没有荣耀，只有悲哀。那位殖民者死去了，前方的道路没有打开。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1819`

### English

The colonist looked upon King Tamperion, blade in hand. King Tamperion sat still, his soft gaze offering neither plea nor permission. The colonist hesitated, then turned the blade on themself.\n\nA sharp intake of breath, and the colonist collapsed, the blade clattering against the obsidian floor. The choice was clear: to spare the Anathari leader and to carry the burden alone.\n\nBut the Dark Immortal's words arrived with neither anger nor triumph: 'Compassion is not absolution. The blade was never meant for the self.'\n\nA silent tear rolled down King Tamperion's face. With the best intentions, we had sealed his people's fate...\nThe trial ended, not in glory, but sorrow. The colonist was dead, and the path forward was sealed.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2164`

## colony.10080.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10080.name&lang=en)

### 中文

内心的渴望

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1820`

### English

Innermost Desire

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2166`

## colony.10080.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10080.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

永朽之暗站在我们面前，依旧是那么不可捉摸。它的形态只是映衬在石头上的一个巨大而又模糊的剪影，仿佛属于一个只与本维度部分相交的现实。它没有发出声音，而是在我们的脑海中直接绽放它的存在，每个念头都不言而喻，也无可逃避。\n\n在寂静中，黑暗的不朽者传递了一条温柔而近乎欣喜的思绪：“你们得到了我的青睐，小小的[Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural]们。我对你们印象深刻。说吧，你们想要什么？”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1821`

### English

The Dark Immortal stood before us, unchanged, unknowable. Its form was a silhouette against the stone, vast yet indistinct, as though it belonged to a reality only half-intersecting with our own. It made no sound. Instead, its presence bloomed within our minds, each thought delivered not in words but as inevitabilities.\n\nInto the stillness, the Dark immortal transmitted a thought, soft and almost pleased: 'You have gained my favor, little [Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural]. You have impressed me. Now speak, what is it that you desire?'

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2167`

## colony.10080.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10080.a&lang=en)

滚出这颗行星永远别回来。

### English

Leave this planet. Forever.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2169`

消灭阿纳萨里。
