### English

Eradicate the Anathari.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2170`

## colony.10081.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10081.name&lang=en)

### 中文

盛大的成功

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1824`

### English

A Grandiose Success

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2172`

## colony.10081.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10081.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一阵沉默。一阵跨越思想和时间的沉默。随之而来的则是一种无声的允诺。“我不用再盯着阿纳萨里了。”永朽之暗说，“现在归你管了。”语罢，它便消失了。没有光，也没有声音，只有空气中细微的变化让人察觉到某种巨大的东西离开了。\n\n在第一次接触之后的那个周期中，殖民者和阿纳萨里之间的物种间融合更加顺畅了。农作物丰收，城市与自然和谐发展，植物和科技并肩而行。几个世纪以来，[Root.GetName]第一次繁荣起来，充盈着一种不受冲突重压、只为和平调谐的空气。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1825`

### English

There was a pause. A silence that stretched across thought and time, but then a hushed agreement. 'The Anathari are no longer mine to watch,' the Dark Immortal spoke. 'You are bound now.' With that, it vanished. No light or sound, only a change in the air, and the sense that something vast has departed.\n\nIn the cycles that followed first contact, the interspecies convergence between colonists and the Anathari intensified. Crops flourished extensively, and cities rose in harmony with the land. Flora and technology walked side by side. For the first time in centuries, [Root.GetName] flourished, its atmosphere no longer burdened by conflict, but recalibrated for peace.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2173`

## colony.10081.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10081.a&lang=en)

### 中文

巨大的成功。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1826`

### English

A great success.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2175`

## colony.10082.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10082.name&lang=en)

### 中文

金色花瓣雨

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1827`

### English

A Rain of Golden Petals

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2177`

## colony.10082.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10082.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

“如你所愿。”\n\n永朽之暗从我们的脑中退去，也把某种很基本的东西顺手改变了。殖民者们感受到了一种深入骨髓的不安和反胃。整个星球上，阿纳萨里开始崩溃。没有惨叫，没有挣扎，只有华服化为尘土的沙沙声，如同花瓣为微风中飘零的呓语。\n\n在石室内，坦佩里翁王弥弥留尚在。他难以置信地眨了眨眼，里面充满了对[Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural]突然背叛的失望。他既没有抬手，也没有恳请。一朵金色的花从他的袖间滑落到地上。随后他也逝去了。没有哀痛，也没有宽慰，只有曾经存在的那一群人、一首歌、一个文明从[Root.GetName]上消失余下的巨大空虚。\n\n永朽之暗看起来很快乐。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1828`

### English

'As you wish.'\n\nThe Dark Immortal withdrew from our thoughts, and with it, something fundamental shifted. The colonists felt a deep, unsettling churning in their spines. All across the planet, the Anathari began to collapse. No scream was reported, no struggle, only the soft rustle of robes folding into dust, like petals falling in the whisper of a breeze.\n\nIn the chamber, King Tamperion lingered a moment longer. His eyes blinked in utter betrayal and disappointment for the [Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural] species. He raised no hand, spoke no plea. A single golden bloom fell from his sleeve to the cavern floor. And then he, too, was gone. There was no mourning, nor relief. Only the absence of what once was, a people, a song, a civilization, swept from [Root.GetName].\n\nThe Dark Immortal seemed pleased.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2178`

## colony.10082.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10082.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们本就不需要他们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1829`

### English

We never needed them.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2180`

## colony.10085.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10085.name&lang=en)

### 中文

盛大的失败

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1830`

### English

A Grandiose Failure

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2182`

## colony.10085.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10085.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管殖民者们已经踏上了通往阿纳萨里的道路，但最终这还不够。试炼失败了。永朽之暗会继续存在，像枯萎病一般继续笼罩着这片土地。\n\n坦佩里翁王也需要回到鲜花之庭，然而不是作为一位朋友，而是再次成为永朽之暗的仆人，一个破败之国的凋敝之王。我们的殖民者还被允许留在这颗行星上，单不会再获得任何信任。\n\n“你本想给予他们自由，[Owner.GetSpeciesNamePluralInsult]，”永朽之暗在我们脑中说道，“可是你们不配。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1831`

### English

The colonists had walked the path to the Anathari, but in the end, it was not enough. The trials failed. The Dark Immortal would remain, its presence looming across the land like a blight.\n\nKing Tamperion would need to return to the Court of Blossoms, not as a friend, but as a servant once more to The Dark Immortal. A broken king in a broken kingdom. Our colonists would be permitted to remain on the planet, tolerated, but never trusted again.\n\n'You sought to free them, [Owner.GetSpeciesNamePluralInsult],' the Dark Immortal sounded in our heads. 'But you were not worthy.'

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2183`

## colony.10085.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10085.a&lang=en)

### 中文

退无可退了。准备背水一战。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1832`

### English

There is nothing left to lose. Ready our attack.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2185`

### English

Eradicate the Anathari. We will become your loyal thralls.

## colony.10086.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10086.name&lang=en)

### 中文

战争是唯一答案

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1835`

### English

War is the only Answer

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2189`

## colony.10086.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10086.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们知道永朽之暗的力量无比巨大。我们的攻击果断而强力。永朽之暗发出一声翻腾的尖叫，整个洞窟都为之颤抖起来。\n\n尽管军队遭到了重创，但我们还是抓住了永朽之暗。洞穴嚎叫着，空气骨头被碾碎一般裂开。永朽之暗消逝了。\n\n在这之后，坦佩里翁王接见了这些幸存者。他走近殖民者，深深地鞠了一躬。尽管损失惨重，但噩梦终于醒来了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1836`

### English

We knew the power of the Dark Immortal was immense. Our assault was immediate and violent. The cavern trembled as the Dark Immortal let out a churning screech.\n\nWhile our forces took heavy damage, we managed to bind the Dark Immortal. The cavern howled, and the air cracked like splitting bone. Then the Dark Immortal was gone.\n\nAfterward, King Tamperion met the survivors. He approached the colonists and bowed deeply. For all that had been lost, the nightmare was over.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2190`

## colony.10086.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10086.a&lang=en)

### 中文

不管怎么样我们还是保住了星球。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1837`

### English

We saved the planet after all.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2192`

## colony.10087.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10087.name&lang=en)

### 中文

仆从之路

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1838`

### English

The Way of the Thralls

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2194`

## colony.10087.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10087.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

行星各地的阿纳萨里风雨飘摇。骨肉和记忆，它们曾经拥有的一切都开始消失。坦佩里翁王是最后一个。他死的时候一言不发。殖民者说，他在最后一刻化为一道闪光和一圈金色的花瓣。\n\n现在就只剩下永朽之暗了。我们感到它在心灵的空洞中沉淀下来。某种黑暗而古老的东西在我们体内蠕动，缓慢而坚定地从永朽之暗到我们、从过去到现在流动着。\n\n我们的殖民者不再只是[Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural]，而是某种……新的存在。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1839`

### English

Across the planet, the Anathari faltered. Flesh, bone, memory: all they had once been began to fade away. King Tamperion was the last. When his time came, he did not speak. The colonists reported his end as a flash of light and a halo of golden petals.\n\nAll that remained was the Dark Immortal. We felt it settling into the hollow spaces in our minds. Something dark and ancient wormed its way within us. A transference, slow and absolute. From the Dark Immortal to us, from what had been to what now was.\n\nOur colonists are no longer just [Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural], but something else now. Something ... new.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2195`

## colony.10087.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10087.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们接受命运。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1840`

### English

We accept our fate.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2197`

## colony.10090.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10090.name&lang=en)

### 中文

万物当有终

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1841`

### English

All Life Must End

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2199`

## colony.10090.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10090.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一个巨大的实体从暗影中浮现，不是野兽也不是机械，而是某种更诡异的东西。它的外形在熔化的集合体和沸腾的黑影之间变换。它没有任何沟通的打算，只是张开了巨口。\n\n建筑倒塌一地，殖民者被撕成碎末抛洒向空中，被无形的压力碾碎。数个小时内，整片平原荡然无存，只余下死寂遍野。\n\n然后，它就像来时一般倏然而去，在这颗行星上只留下死亡和破坏的痕迹。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1842`

### English

A colossal entity emerged from the shadows, neither beast nor machine, but something stranger, its form shifting between molten geometry and seething shadow. It did not attempt to communicate. It simply devoured.\n\nBuildings crumbled into the ground. Colonists were torn apart, vanishing into the air or crushed under invisible pressure. Within hours, nothing remained but a low hum echoing across the ruined plain.\n\nThen, as soon as it came, the beast vanished, leaving behind a trail of death and devastation on a scorched planet.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2200`

## colony.10090.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10090.a&lang=en)

### 中文

真恐怖的力量……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1843`

### English

What horrifying power...

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2202`

### English

50% of the population on [Root.GetName] has died, and the other half is devastated.

巧合还是命运？

## colony.10095.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10095.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

巡逻队报告在[Root.GetName]周围的森林里发现了奇形怪状的尸体。它们皮肤被烧成焦炭，骨骼也被熏黑，一种原始恐惧的表情凝固在脸上。周围没有发现任何武器，也没有任何冲突的迹象。附近的植被上残留着极端高温暴露和短暂的局部重力异常的迹象。\n\n不管被猎杀的是什么东西，它都已经死了。枯萎的植物灿烂地绽放，当地的动物群也带着谨慎的和谐回归原处。肥沃的土壤似乎预兆着前所未有的丰收。\n\n目前，殖民地安全了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1846`

### English

Patrols reported finding grotesque bodies in the forests surrounding [Root.GetName]. Charred skin, blackened bones, and expressions frozen in primal terror. No weapons were to be found, and no signs of any conflict. Nearby vegetation evidenced signs of extreme heat exposure and brief, localized gravitational anomalies.\n\nBut whatever hunted there is gone, and the planet is responding. Withered flora bloom in radiant abundance, and the local fauna have returned with cautious harmony. The rich soil appears ready to yield harvests unlike any seen before.\n\nFor now, the colony is safe.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2206`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.10095.a&lang=en)

### 中文

幸运的结果。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1847`

### English

A fortunate outcome.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2208`

## anomaly.9000.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.9000.name&lang=en)

### 中文

神秘舰船

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1856`

### English

Mysterious Ship

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2222`

## anomaly.9000.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.9000.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[From.GetName]的调查目前取得了一些有趣的成果，这颗小行星的中心是一个完美的球形空腔，光滑的墙壁似乎是由原子级别的精度雕琢而成。空腔的中央悬浮着一艘小型舰船，它似乎在向[Root.GetName]发出问候，并且也只和[Root.GetLeaderName]对话。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1857`

### English

Our study of [From.GetName] has yielded interesting results. Within the core of the asteroid is the negative space of a perfect sphere. The walls are so smooth as to have been carved with atomic precision, and hovering in the center is a small vessel. It appears to be hailing [Root.GetName] and will only speak to [Root.GetLeaderName].

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2223`

## anomaly.9000.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.9000.a&lang=en)

### 中文

将信号接入主屏幕

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1858`

### English

Put that on the main viewer.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2224`

## anomaly.9001.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.9001.name&lang=en)

### 中文

裁决官

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1859`

### English

The Adjudicator

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2226`

## anomaly.9001.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.9001.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

经统计学计算，你已经荒废了自身63%的潜能。\n在接下来的47秒内向我证明你存在的价值，不然的话我就将你从因果中移除。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1860`

### English

You have squandered 63% of your statistically allotted potential.\nJustify your existence in the next 47 seconds... or be excised from causality.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2227`

## anomaly.9001.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.9001.a&lang=en)

### 中文

你是谁？你想干什么？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1861`

### English

Who are you? What do you want?

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2228`

## anomaly.9001.a.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.9001.a.response&lang=en)

### 中文

我是裁决官。生命是只有少数才能得到的珍贵礼物，而你却将其挥霍。\n\n剩余38秒……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1862`

### English

I am the Adjudicator. The chance at life is a precious gift few get to receive, and you have wasted yours.\n\n38 seconds...

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2229`

## anomaly.9001.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.9001.b&lang=en)

### 中文

你说的“移除”是什么意思？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1863`

### English

What do you mean 'excised'?

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2230`

## anomaly.9001.b.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.9001.b.response&lang=en)

### 中文

我会把你在这条时间线上占据的位置让给另一位更有资格的人。他本来从没有机会能够拥有生命。而你，则会被彻底遗忘，你毫无价值的生命将永远无人知晓。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1864`

### English

I will give the place you hold in this timeline to one who may prove more worthy. One who never had the chance to become. You will be more than forgotten. Your worthless existence will have never been.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2231`

## anomaly.9001.c.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.9001.c.response&lang=en)

### 中文

我以行为论是非，以取舍决对错。\n\n你空耗这么多空气，占用如此多的空间与带宽，除了熵，你什么也没有创造。多元宇宙不会为失去无用的压舱物而哀悼。\n\n剩余0秒……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1866`

### English

I judge by doing. I have the right by taking.\n\nYou have consumed breath, space, and bandwidth, yet contributed nothing but entropy. The multiverse does not mourn lost ballast.\n\n0 seconds...

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2233`

## anomaly.9002.name

### 中文

放射性脉冲

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1867`

### English

Radioactive Pulse

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2235`

## anomaly.9002.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.9002.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[From.GetName]的研究目前取得了一些有趣的成果。这颗小行星的核心含有放射性物质，在其衰变过程中会发出周期性脉冲。虽然科学家[Root.GetLeaderName]可能永远无法从大剂量的伽马辐射中恢复过来了，但这种脉冲是可以预测的，之后的研究人员不会受到伤害。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_6_l_simp_chinese.yml:1868`

### English

Our study of [From.GetName] has yielded interesting results. The asteroid possesses a core of radioactive material that emits a periodic pulse as it deteriorates. While our scientist, [Root.GetLeaderName], may never recover from the large dose of gamma radiation, the pulsing is predictable and poses no risk to future researchers.

Source: `localisation/english/events_6_l_english.yml:2236`
